### [1] [A Geometric Algebra-Informed 3D Gaussian Splatting Framework for Wireless Scene Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19065)
*Jingzhou Shen,Tianya Zhao,Xuyu Wang*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了GAI-GS框架，将3D高斯溅射与几何代数注意力机制结合，在统一架构中显式建模无线传播环境中的射线-物体相互作用，显著提升了多项无线任务的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂传播环境中的无线信道建模需要显式捕捉射线与物体的相互作用（如多径、衰减、反射/衍射），现有方法难以在统一框架中有效建模这些物理过程与空间-电磁联合关系。

Method: 提出几何代数感知的3D高斯溅射（GAI-GS）框架，利用几何代数注意力机制编码空间-电磁联合关系到token表示中，实现场景级聚合；通过token交互建模多径、衰减、反射/衍射等关键电磁效应，形成端到端神经架构。

Result: 在多个真实室内数据集上的广泛评估表明，GAI-GS在各种无线任务中持续超越当前基线方法。

Conclusion: GAI-GS成功将电磁原理融入神经架构，实现了对无线射线传播的物理可解释建模，为复杂环境下的无线建模提供了统一有效的解决方案。

Abstract: In this paper, we introduce Geometric Algebra-Informed 3D Gaussian Splatting (GAI-GS), a framework for wireless modeling that couples 3D Gaussian splatting with a geometric algebra-based attention mechanism to explicitly model ray-object interactions in complex propagation environments. GAI-GS encodes joint spatial-electromagnetic (EM) relations into token representations, enabling scene-level aggregation within a unified, end-to-end neural architecture. This design grounds wireless ray propagation in electromagnetic principles, allowing token interactions to model key effects such as multipath, attenuation, and reflection/diffraction. Through extensive evaluations on multiple real-world indoor datasets, GAI-GS consistently surpasses current baselines across various wireless tasks.

</details>


### [2] [UAV-Assisted Cooperative Edge Inference for Low-Altitude Economy via MoE-based Hierarchical Deep Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19290)
*Wenhao Zhuang,Yuyi Mao,Ivan Wang-Hei Ho,Xianghao Yu*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了HDRL-MoE框架，通过分层深度强化学习和混合专家架构，在满足无人机（UAV）主任务约束的同时，协同优化轨迹、任务卸载和特征压缩，以提升低空经济中的边缘推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低空经济（LAE）通过部署无人机实现灵活的空中机动性，将边缘人工智能集成到LAE平台中可实现实时AI分析。然而，由于无人机主任务带来的严格约束以及无线链路的吞吐量瓶颈，实现这一范式面临挑战。现有的研究难以在满足任务约束的同时高效支持地面设备的边缘推理。

Method: 提出了一个UAV辅助的协同边缘推理框架，将UAV轨迹、推理任务卸载决策和特征压缩比进行联合优化。将该优化问题建模为约束部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程（POMDP）。为高效求解，提出了HDRL-MoE，一种分层深度强化学习框架，将缓慢变化的推理决策与快速变化的UAV轨迹控制解耦。同时集成了混合专家（MoE）架构，其中路由网络协调离散卸载决策，专家网络独立优化特征压缩比。

Result: 大量仿真实验表明，HDRL-MoE相比基线方法取得了显著的推理精度提升。MoE设计使得该框架表现出高可扩展性和高效率。

Conclusion: HDRL-MoE框架有效解决了低空经济中无人机在执行主任务的同时支持边缘推理的挑战，通过分层架构和混合专家设计实现了性能、可扩展性和效率的提升。

Abstract: The low-altitude economy (LAE) is reshaping the industrial landscape by deploying unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) to facilitate a wide range of applications demanding flexible aerial mobility. Integrating edge artificial intelligence (AI) into LAE platforms creates a compelling paradigm where UAVs provide real-time AI-driven analysis while simultaneously executing their primary aerial mission duties. However, realizing this paradigm remains challenging due to the strict mission constraints imposed by these primary duties and the throughput bottlenecks of wireless links. To bridge this gap, we propose a UAV-assisted cooperative edge inference framework where UAVs execute mission-critical LAE duties, quantified by trajectory deviations from reference paths, while concurrently supporting ground devices via intermediate feature offloading. Within this framework, UAV trajectories, inference task offloading decisions, and feature compression ratios are jointly optimized to maximize the system performance. We cast this joint optimization task into a constrained partially observable Markov decision process (POMDP) framework. To efficiently solve it, we propose HDRL-MoE, a novel hierarchical deep reinforcement learning framework that decouples the optimization of slow-varying inference decisions from rapidly changing UAV trajectory control. Furthermore, HDRL-MoE integrates a mixture-of-experts (MoE) architecture, where a router network orchestrates discrete offloading decisions while expert networks independently optimize the feature compression ratios. Extensive simulations show that HDRL-MoE achieves significant inference accuracy gains over baselines and exhibits high scalability and efficiency through its MoE design.

</details>


### [3] [Sample-Efficient Misconfiguration Classification for Network Resilience in Wireless Communications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19303)
*Xin Hao,Chenhan Zhang,Massimo Piccardi,Vijaya Durga Chemalamarri,Qiwen Jiang,Wei Ni,Raymond Owen*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了EtaGATv2算法，一种边缘类型感知的图注意力网络，用于解决无线通信网络中因动态拓扑与协议配置不一致导致的网络中断问题，在仅使用50%训练样本的情况下达到了最先进的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代无线通信网络日益复杂，动态拓扑与协议配置之间的不一致性引发的网络中断已成为关键问题，迫切需要有效的协议误配置分类方法。

Method: 将协议误配置分类问题数学形式化为图学习任务，提出EtaGATv2算法（边缘类型感知的图注意力网络）。该方法通过动态注意力机制捕获非均匀症状传播，并利用边缘类型感知变换从具有不同消息传递行为的异构路由协议中提取协议特定特征。

Result: 在多种真实世界拓扑上的实验表明，EtaGATv2在仅使用50%训练样本的情况下达到了最先进的性能，特别适用于具有动态拓扑和有限负标签数据的网络环境。

Conclusion: EtaGATv2有效解决了协议误配置分类任务中的关键挑战，包括非均匀症状传播捕获和异构路由协议特征提取，为动态拓扑网络中的故障诊断提供了高效解决方案。

Abstract: As modern wireless communication networks grow increasingly complex, network outages driven by the inconsistency between dynamic topologies and protocol configurations have become a critical concern. To solve this issue, we mathematically formulate a protocol misconfiguration classification problem as a graph-based learning task and solve it with our proposed EtaGATv2 algorithm, an edge-type-aware graph attention network with dynamic attention. EtaGATv2 addresses two critical challenges: i) it captures non-uniform symptom propagation for protocol misconfiguration classification tasks, where certain network paths and nodes become critical for diagnosis, and ii) it extracts protocol-specific features from heterogeneous routing protocols with distinct message-passing behaviors by utilizing edge-type-aware transformations. Experiments across diverse and real-world topologies demonstrate that EtaGATv2 reaches state-of-the-art performance with 50% of the training samples, making it particularly suitable for networks with dynamic topologies and limited negative-labeled data.

</details>


### [4] [Fifty Shades of Darknet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19437)
*Siddique Abubakr Muntaka,Jacques Bou Abdo*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文发现了I2P匿名网络中存在一个未被文献描述的“独占网络”隐蔽子层，该子层中的节点可利用网络路由资源但不出现在网络数据库中，能被恶意软件利用作为持久的命令与控制基础设施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的I2P网络安全文献尚未刻画其架构中存在的一个结构独特的子层。该子层允许节点托管服务并利用路由资源，但不在分布式数据库（NetDB）中发布RouterInfo记录，这种特性可能被基于I2P的恶意软件（如I2PRAT）利用，用于针对国家或企业资产的持久命令与控制操作，类似国家级运营中继盒（ORB）基础设施。因此，揭示该子层的存在与特性对于理解I2P内部隐蔽网络的行为至关重要。

Method: 作者通过构建一个受控的三节点测试床进行实验验证。在该测试环境中，演示了一个独占网络节点在面对来自路由器池的顺序Floodfill查询时，NetDB命中数为零，但其托管的服务仍能持续被授权对等节点访问。同时，通过类比分析和文献调研，将该结构与已知的恶意软件I2PRAT及国家级ORB基础设施联系起来。

Result: 实验证实了独占网络节点的隐蔽性与可用性：此类节点在NetDB中无记录，能规避自顶向下的经验性网络映射，但其所托管服务仍对授权节点保持可达。这一特性使其极易被恶意软件用于构建持久、隐蔽的命令与控制通道。

Conclusion: 独占网络子层的存在及其无法通过传统自顶向下映射方法进行刻画，凸显了现有安全研究的盲区。这推动了研究者转向形式化分析方法，以理解I2P内部隐蔽网络的出现机制与行为特征，从而更好地应对潜在的安全威胁。

Abstract: The Invisible Internet Project (I2P) is a peer-to-peer anonymous overlay network whose architecture includes a structurally distinct sublayer not characterized in existing security literature. We term this sublayer the Exclusive Network: nodes here host operational services and draw on I2P's routing resources, but publish no RouterInfo record to the network's distributed database (NetDB). In a controlled three-node testbed, we demonstrate that an Exclusive Network node survives sequential floodfill queries from a pool of routers with zero NetDB hits, while its hosted service remains continuously accessible to authorized peers. This property is exploitable by documented I2P-based malware, for example, I2PRAT (RATatouille), for persistent command-and-control operations against national assets or corporate networks. The structure is analogous to nation-state Operational Relay Box (ORB) infrastructure. The existence of this sublayer, together with the inability of top-down empirical mapping to characterize it, motivates a move toward formal analytical methods to understand the emergence and behavior of covert networks within I2P.

</details>


### [5] [A Hybrid Cluster-Based Classification Model for Anomaly Detection in Unbalanced IoT Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19451)
*Hossein Shaemi Barzoki,Amir Hossein Fathi Hafshejani,Ahmadreza Montazerolghaem*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对IoT网络异常检测的混合模型，通过K-Means聚类将数据划分为不同流量特征簇，并为每个簇独立训练最优分类器，有效解决了数据不平衡和多样性问题，提升了检测准确性和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IoT网络异常检测面临高度不平衡且多样的网络流量数据集这一关键安全挑战，标准分类器难以在所有流量类型上保持良好性能，亟需一种能有效处理数据多样性和不平衡性的检测方法。

Method: 提出一种混合检测模型，首先利用K-Means聚类将训练数据分割为三个不同的流量特征簇，然后针对每个簇独立训练并评估决策树、KNN和XGBoost等多个基线机器学习模型，为每个特定数据特征选择最优分类器。

Result: 实验结果表明，这种针对特定簇的混合方法（即为不同簇分配不同的简单模型）提高了检测准确性，并提供了处理多样IoT攻击流量的更鲁棒、更高效的框架。

Conclusion: 该研究证明，相较于单一复杂分类器，基于聚类分组的混合检测模型能更好地应对IoT网络流量的多样性和不平衡性，为IoT网络安全提供了一种更有效、更鲁棒的异常检测解决方案。

Abstract: Detecting anomalies in Internet of Things (IoT) networks is a critical security challenge, often hampered by highly imbalanced and diverse network traffic datasets. Standard classifiers struggle to perform well across all traffic types. This paper proposes a hybrid detection model to address this challenge using the Bot-IoT dataset. Instead of a single complex classifier, we first employ K-Means clustering to segment the training data into three distinct traffic profile clusters. We then train and evaluate multiple baseline machine learning models, including Decision Tree, KNN, and XGBoost, on each cluster independently to identify the optimal classifier for that specific data profile. Our results show that this clusterspecific, hybrid approach, which assigns different simple models to different clusters, improves detection accuracy and provides a more robust and efficient framework for handling diverse IoT attack traffic.

</details>


### [6] [How Helpful is LLM Assistance in Network Operations? A Case Study at a Large Demonstration Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19627)
*Ryo Nakamura,Koshi Eguchi*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本研究通过105名网络工程师在实际大型网络环境中测试LLM聊天机器人，发现68.1%的评估结果为正面，定量验证了LLM在网络运维中的有效性，并强调了理解模型能力对获取更好响应的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM作为概率模型与传统确定性网络协议和配置存在本质差异，其能力边界尚不明确。为推动LLM在网络运维领域的实际应用，亟需通过真实场景的大规模实验澄清LLM能够以何种方式、在何种程度上协助网络运维工作。

Method: 在一个为公开展览构建的大型示范网络（包含21个机架和异构网络设备）上进行实验，开发了集成三种外部功能的LLM聊天机器人：面向领域知识的检索增强生成（RAG）、网络设备的CLI控制、以及工单系统访问。邀请105名网络工程师在网络构建和运维过程中使用该聊天机器人，并对响应质量进行评价。研究通过分析聊天记录和用户评估来量化模型表现。

Result: 用户评估中68.1%为正面评价，建立了LLM在网络运维中帮助程度的定量基线。分析表明，用户对聊天机器人能力的理解程度与获取优质响应之间存在显著相关性——更好地理解模型能力有助于引出更有效的回答。研究还提供了详细的用例分析和真实的用户-聊天机器人交互案例。

Conclusion: 本研究首次通过大规模真实场景实验定量验证了LLM在网络运维中的实际效用，为LLM在网络领域的应用提供了实证基础和实用参考。结果表明LLM具备成为网络运维有效辅助工具的潜力，同时强调了用户培训和人机协作策略的重要性，为未来LLM辅助网络系统的设计和部署提供了有价值的见解。

Abstract: This paper reports on a real-world case study in which over 100 network engineers assessed how a Large Language Model (LLM) can assist in building and operating a network. The versatility of LLMs has accelerated their adoption across a wide range of domains, and assisting network operations is one such promising application. LLMs are probabilistic models, unlike deterministic protocols and configurations; therefore, clarifying their capabilities -- how and to what extent LLMs can help in network operations -- is a crucial step toward adopting LLMs. To offer practical insights into this issue, we conducted an extensive experiment on a large demonstration network built for a public exhibition, consisting of 21 racks with heterogeneous network devices. In the experiment, a total of 105 network engineers used an LLM-based chatbot while building and operating the network. The chatbot was equipped with three external functions: retrieval-augmented generation for domain-specific knowledge, CLI control of network devices running on the network, and access to a ticket system. The participants gave evaluations for the chatbot's responses on a best-effort basis. Analysis of the chat histories shows that 68.1% of the evaluations were positive, indicating a quantitative baseline of the LLM's helpfulness in network operations. Our results also demonstrate that understanding the capabilities of the chatbot is important for eliciting better responses. Moreover, we provide detailed use case analyses while sharing actual user--chatbot interactions.

</details>


### [7] [Motion-Coupled Sensing: When the State Change Powers Its Own Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19793)
*Muhammad Tahir,Muhammad Mubbashar Baig,Umer Irfan,Muhammad Ahad,Naveed Anwar Bhatti*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出“运动耦合感知”原理，利用铰链运动的机械能为单次“唤醒-感知-传输”事务供电；通过开源电磁能量采集器在垃圾桶、门和柜子上部署，实现超99%（垃圾桶）和约92-94%（门/柜子）的事件传输成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无电池物联网系统主要分为环境能量感知（能量到达与事件无关）和动能事件电报（用户动作驱动短暂上报），但缺少第三类场景：开合动作不仅是待上报的事件，更是潜在物理状态发生改变并需测量的时刻。常规铰链运动可为一次有限的有界感知+长距离传输提供足够能量。

Method: 提出运动耦合感知原则，利用铰链运动的机械能供电一次“唤醒-感知-传输”事务，包括超声波传感和远距离LoRa上行。设计开源紧凑型电磁能量采集器，可无结构改造地加装于垃圾桶盖、门、柜子。以最苛刻的垃圾桶监测场景为目标进行平台 sizing，并在真实环境中大规模部署测试。

Result: 垃圾桶场景：5个校园地点、5945次盖子开合，单事件传输可靠性99.3%。房间门：1870次动作，传输成功率92%。办公室柜子：1636次动作，传输成功率94%。证明同一能量包络可在不同铰链几何间迁移，无需硬件重设计。

Conclusion: 机械开合动作可视为自供能的感知事务，消除周期轮询和定期电池维护，为物联网部署提供免电池、按需感知的新范式。

Abstract: Batteryless IoT systems have largely followed two paths: ambient-energy sensing, where energy arrival is decoupled from the event being monitored, and kinetic event telegrams, where a user actuation powers a short report of the actuation itself. Mechanically gated states expose a third case: the access motion is not only an event to report, but the moment at which a latent physical state may have changed and must be measured. We show that routine hinge motion can supply enough energy for one bounded wake-sense-transmit transaction, including ultrasonic sensing and a long-range LoRa uplink. We call this principle motion-coupled sensing and instantiate it with an open-source compact electromagnetic harvester that retrofits to bins, doors, and cabinets with no structural modification. We size the platform for the most demanding workload, waste-bin monitoring, where each actuation must power both an ultrasonic measurement and a long-range LoRa uplink. Across five campus locations and 5,945 lid actuations, the bin deployment achieves 99.3% per-event transmission reliability. Field deployments on room doors with 1,870 actuations and office cabinets with 1,636 actuations achieve 92% and 94% transmission success respectively, demonstrating that the same energy envelope transfers across hinge geometries without hardware redesign. These results show that mechanical access can be treated as a self-powered sensing transaction, removing periodic polling and scheduled battery maintenance for IoT deployments.

</details>


### [8] [Fair-Aurora: Comparing Fairness Strategies for Reinforcement Learning-Based Congestion Control in Multi-Flow Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19909)
*Thomas Mbrice,Yuyu Liu*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文研究了深度强化学习拥塞控制算法Aurora在多流环境下的公平性问题，评估了三种后处理公平性策略，发现适度的奖励整形能在保持总吞吐量的同时实现最佳公平性，而不同策略在不同场景下各有优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于强化学习的拥塞控制算法在单流环境中表现优异，但在多流网络部署时无法保证带宽的公平共享，亟需研究其公平性特性并提出解决方案。

Method: 构建自定义共享瓶颈模拟器，以Jain公平性指数为主要评估指标，设计了三种保持Aurora架构的后处理策略：奖励整形（策略A）、观测增强（策略B）和丢包敏感度调节（策略C）。

Result: 适度的奖励整形（策略A）在保持总吞吐量的同时实现了最佳公平性；所有策略都通过带宽重分配而非总量减少来实现公平；策略C在混合Aurora-CUBIC竞争场景中表现出最佳的TCP友好性；策略B在动态流变化场景中稳定性最好。

Conclusion: 通过后处理策略可以有效改善RL拥塞控制算法的多流公平性，不同策略适用于不同场景，为RL拥塞控制的实际部署提供了可行的公平性保障方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has emerged as a promising paradigm for Internet congestion control, achieving higher link utilization than classical heuristics. However, RL-based controllers trained in single-flow environments are not guaranteed to share bandwidth equitably when deployed in multi-flow networks. This paper investigates the fairness properties of Aurora~\cite{jay2019aurora}, a state-of-the-art deep RL congestion controller, and evaluates three post-hoc fairness strategies that preserve Aurora's RL architecture: \emph{reward shaping} (Strategy~A), \emph{observation augmentation} (Strategy~B), and \emph{loss-sensitivity tuning} (Strategy~C). Using a custom shared-bottleneck simulator and Jain's fairness index as the primary metric, we find that modest reward shaping achieves the best fairness while preserving aggregate throughput. All strategies maintain the total bandwidth budget with fairness being achieved through redistribution, not reduction. Beyond the 2-flow homogeneous setting, an extended evaluation across mixed Aurora--CUBIC competition and dynamic flow entry/exit scenarios shows that Strategy~C's loss-sensitivity emerges as the most TCP-friendly mechanism, while Strategy~B is the most stable through dynamic flow-set changes.

</details>

### [9] [Operational Memory Architecture for Kubernetes:Preserving Causal Context Across the Evidence Horizon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18755)
*Shamsher Khan*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出运维记忆架构（OMA），通过捕获Kubernetes原生事件轮换前因果链，解决容器崩溃循环导致的关键诊断证据永久丢失问题，实现毫秒级因果边构建与轻量级开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kubernetes原生事件保留机制存在"证据视界"问题：LastTerminationState字段记录的容器失败信息在Pod重启后迅速被覆盖。在高频崩溃循环场景下，关键诊断证据可能在运维人员介入前被多次覆盖并永久丢失。

Method: 提出运维记忆架构（OMA），将证据保留与因果重构编码为显式架构需求。系统基于Go语言实现Kubernetes监控器、SQLite运维存储及查询接口，通过三种模式（P001 OOMKill链、P002 ConfigMap变量误配置、P003 ConfigMap卷挂载传播）捕获运维事件并构建因果链。

Result: 在Minikube和AKS上的30次实验及20个崩溃循环Pod压力测试表明：因果边构建平均延迟低于1ms，采集器处理速率约2.8事件/秒，内存占用低于10MB，实现了极低开销与有效的证据保留。

Conclusion: OMA成功在证据轮换前保存因果故障证据，以轻量级开销解决了Kubernetes诊断证据丢失问题，为高频崩溃场景下的故障诊断提供了可行的架构方案。

Abstract: Kubernetes clusters generate rich operational events during pod lifecycle transitions, yet the platform's native event retention model discards the most diagnostically valuable context. The LastTerminationState field, which records a container's last failure, is overwritten shortly after a pod restart. We define this as the evidence horizon. During high-frequency crash loops, this horizon may be crossed multiple times before inspection, permanently losing critical evidence.
  This paper introduces the Operational Memory Architecture (OMA) to preserve causal failure evidence before event rotation. OMA encodes evidence retention and causal reconstruction as explicit architectural requirements. It captures operational events into causal chains using three patterns: P001 (OOMKill chain), P002 (ConfigMap variable misconfiguration), and P003 (ConfigMap volume mount propagation).
  We implement OMA as an open-source system with a Go-based Kubernetes watcher, SQLite operational memory store, and a simple query interface. Experiments on Minikube and AKS include a 30-run latency analysis and stress tests with up to 20 crash-looping pods. Causal edges are built with mean latency below 1 ms. The collector processes ~2.8 events/sec while using under 10 MB memory, showing minimal overhead and effective evidence preservation.

</details>

*Zhang Liu,Shanhao Zhan,Shaowei Shen,Lianfen Huang,Qiao Xiang,Ying-Jun Angela Zhang,Dusit Niyato*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于扩散模型的软演员-评论家（DSAC）算法，用于优化移动边缘计算环境下的思维树（ToT）推理任务分配，在满足用户质量约束的前提下显著降低生成延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 思维树（ToT）提示通过探索多条推理路径显著提升生成式AI服务质量，但生成多样化推理路径需要对计算密集型的GenAI模型进行多次调用，这对资源受限的移动设备构成重大挑战。现有的AIGC服务供给方案缺乏对ToT推理过程中资源分配与延迟优化问题的研究。

Method: 1. 以创意写作任务为例，通过Qwen 2.5-7B-Instruct实验建立输出token数量与生成延迟及质量的关系模型；2. 引入有向无环图（DAG）建模ToT推理过程，其中顶点表示思维节点，有向边表示思维间的转换关系；3. 建立DAG约束下的思维分配优化问题，目标是在用户可调节的质量约束下最小化生成延迟；4. 提出创新性融合扩散模型的软演员-评论家（DSAC）算法求解最优思维分配决策。

Result: 仿真实验表明，所提DSAC算法在各种仿真场景下相比PPO降低总生成延迟最高8.32%，相比SAC降低11.57%，相比DDQN降低36.09%；即使在严格质量要求下，相比完全本地生成基线方案延迟降低超过80%。

Conclusion: 本文成功解决了移动边缘计算环境下ToT提示AIGC服务的资源优化配置问题，所提出的DSAC算法通过创新性融合扩散模型与强化学习，实现了显著的计算延迟优化效果，为资源受限设备上的高质量AIGC服务供给提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Delivering AI-generated content (AIGC) services fundamentally relies on the reasoning capabilities of generative AI (GenAI) models. Chain-of-Thought (CoT) enhances such reasoning by guiding models through intermediate steps, while Tree-of-Thoughts (ToT) further extends CoT by exploring multiple candidate reasoning paths simultaneously, thereby greatly improving AIGC service quality. However, generating diverse reasoning paths requires separate calls to computationally intensive GenAI models, posing significant challenges for resource constrained user devices. In this paper, we investigate mobile edge computing-enabled AIGC service provisioning with ToT prompting. Specifically, using creative writing AIGC tasks as a case study, we first characterize the number of output tokens as a measure of computational resources in GenAI models and establish its relationship with generation delay and quality through experiments with Qwen 2.5-7B-Instruct. Afterward, we introduce a directed acyclic graph (DAG) model to accurately characterize the reasoning process of ToT prompting, where each vertex represents a thought and each directed edge denotes a transition between consecutive thoughts. We then formulate a DAG-based thought assignment problem aimed at minimizing generation delay subject to a user-adjustable quality constraint. To address this problem, we propose a diffusion-based soft actor-critic (DSAC) algorithm that innovatively integrates diffusion models to determine optimal thought assignment decisions. Through extensive simulations, we demonstrate that the proposed DSAC achieves total generation delay reductions of up to 8.32% over PPO, 11.57% over SAC, and 36.09% over DDQN across various simulation settings, while reducing latency by over 80% compared to the fully local generation baseline even under stringent quality requirements.

</details>


### [11] [Meta-Theorems for Cuttable Distributed Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19157)
*Marthe Bonamy,Avinandan Das,Cyril Gavoille,Timothé Picavet,Jukka Suomela,Alexandra Wesolek*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了从平面图到亏格有界曲面图的近似算法转换方法，将MDS问题的近似比从O(g)改进到常数34+ε，并推广至其他支配集问题和更广泛的局部良好图类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对有界亏格图的最小支配集(MDS)问题的分布式近似算法近似比较差（为24g+O(1)），且缺乏针对更广泛图类和其他支配集变体问题的通用方法。

Method: 提出了一系列关于可切割最小化问题的元定理。对于任意的平面图MDS问题的α-近似LOCAL算法，构造出亏格g曲面图上f(g)轮(3α+1)-近似算法。通过系统性地提取小子图，使其解与全局解在子图邻域内的限制成比例。

Result: 1）针对亏格g曲面图的MDS问题，得到了(34+ε)-近似算法，优于现有的24g+O(1)近似比；2）将结果推广到最小k-元组支配集等其他图问题；3）将结果推广到基于渐近维数的"局部良好"图类，超越了有界亏格图的范畴。

Conclusion: 证明了从平面图算法到有界亏格图算法的系统转换方法是可行的，且通过元定理的建立，该转换框架具有良好的通用性，可应用于多种支配集问题及更广泛的图类，为分布式图算法的设计提供了统一的的理论工具。

Abstract: We prove that given any $α$-approximation LOCAL algorithm for Minimum Dominating Set (MDS) on planar graphs, we can construct an $f(g)$-round $(3α+1)$-approximation LOCAL algorithm for MDS on graphs embeddable in a given Euler genus-$g$ surface. Heydt et al. [European Journal of Combinatorics (2025)] gave an algorithm with $α=11+\varepsilon$, from which we derive a $(34 +\varepsilon)$-approximation algorithm for graphs of genus $g$, therefore improving upon the current state of the art of $24g+O(1)$ due to Amiri et al. [ACM Transactions on Algorithms (2019)]. It also improves the approximation ratio of $91+\varepsilon$ due to Czygrinow et al. [Theoretical Computer Science (2019)] in the particular case of orientable surfaces.
  We generalize this result into two directions: (1) by considering other graph problems studied in Distributed Computing such as Minimum $k$-Tuple Dominating Set, for which constant-round approximation algorithms were known for planar graphs, but not for graphs of bounded genus; and (2) by considering graph classes beyond bounded genus graphs, called locally nice, and relying on the asymptotic dimension of the class. We prove these results by a series of meta-theorems about cuttable minimization problems with constant-round approximation LOCAL algorithms. Roughly speaking, in cuttable problems, one can systematically extract small subgraphs whose solutions are in proportion to the global solution restricted to the neighbourhood of the subgraph.

</details>


### [12] [Generalized Compare-and-Swap and Space-Efficient Universal Constructions for the Infinite-Arrival Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19237)
*Vassos Hadzilacos,Myles Thiessen,Sam Toueg*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了GCAS对象（比较器的广义CAS），并基于此设计了两种空间高效的无等待通用构造，首次在无限到达模型中实现了线性空间复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了在参与进程数量未知且可能无限的系统（无限到达模型）中实现空间高效的无等待通用构造，需要一种比传统CAS更灵活的同步原语。

Method: 引入GCAS对象，将CAS的固定相等测试推广为可参数化的比较器（<、=、>）；基于GCAS设计了两种通用构造算法，第二种构造采用了一种适用于有界并发无限到达模型的新型内存回收机制。

Result: 第一种构造的空间复杂度与当前参与进程数呈线性关系；第二种构造在假设有界并发的前提下，空间复杂度与点竞争呈线性关系。这是首个在无限到达模型中达到该空间复杂度的无等待通用构造。

Conclusion: GCAS对象及其衍生的通用构造证明了在无限到达模型中实现高效空间复杂度的可行性，其中提出的新型内存回收方案具有更广泛的潜在应用价值。

Abstract: We introduce GCAS, a natural generalization of the well-known compare-and-swap (CAS) object. Intuitively, GCAS just replaces the fixed equality test of CAS with a parametrized comparator chosen from $\{<, =, >\}$. To showcase the utility of GCAS, we present two space-efficient wait-free universal constructions for systems where the number of participating processes is unknown and may be infinite (the infinite-arrival model). The first has space-complexity linear in the number of processes that have participated so far, while the second has space-complexity linear in the point contention but assumes bounded concurrency. To the best of our knowledge, these are the first wait-free universal constructions that achieve this space complexity in the infinite-arrival model. To achieve space complexity linear in the point contention, our second universal construction uses a novel memory recycling scheme that works in the infinite-arrival model with bounded concurrency. The ideas behind this recycling scheme could be of more general use.

</details>


### [13] [Quantum-Enhanced Distributed Sensor Fusion: Lower Bounds on Aggregation from Projection Noise to Heisenberg-Limited Byzantine-Tolerant Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19327)
*Vasanth Iyer,S. S. Iyengar*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文推导了存在拜占庭故障和退相干情况下的分布式量子传感器融合均方误差（MSE）统一下界，建立了由纠缠可见度V和故障比例f/M索引的两参数界族。该界限在标准量子极限和海森堡极限之间连续插值，蒙特卡洛仿真和Intel Berkeley Lab数据集验证了理论缩放定律，并发现经典传感器数据缺失与量子退相干以相同模式降低融合一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在实际分布式量子传感系统中，存在两类关键挑战：一是节点可能发生拜占庭故障（恶意或随机错误行为），二是量子纠缠态受到退相干影响。现有研究缺乏对这两种因素同时存在时的性能极限分析。本文旨在建立统一的理论框架，推导MSE下界，以桥接量子计量学与经典流处理架构。

Method: 1. 基于经典Brooks-Iyengar重叠函数及其向量扩展，量化拜占庭故障容错能力；
2. 引入预测异常值模型进行虚拟传感器跟踪；
3. 结合SPOTLESS时空验证方法；
4. 建立两参数（纠缠可见度V、故障比例f/M）索引的MSE下界解析表达式；
5. 通过蒙特卡洛仿真（多达64个传感器）验证理论缩放定律；
6. 在Intel Berkeley Lab 54节点数据集上进行实验验证，采用空间聚类方法。

Result: 1. 推导出MSE统一下界：MSE >= (1-V²)/(4*N*η²*M_eff) + V²/(4*N*η²*M_eff²)，其中M_eff为有效传感器数量；
2. 在Brooks-Iyengar拜占庭容错下M_eff = M-2f，在预测异常检测成功识别故障传感器时M_eff = M-f；
3. 该界限在V=0（标准量子极限，按1/√M_eff缩放）和V=1（海森堡极限，按1/M_eff缩放）之间连续插值；
4. Monte Carlo仿真验证了理论缩放定律；
5. 实验验证表明纠缠使每个簇获得20-27 dB信噪比改善；
6. 发现经典传感器数据缺失以与量子退相干相同的模式降低融合一致性。

Conclusion: 本文成功建立了分布式量子传感器融合在拜占庭故障和退相干条件下的统一MSE下界理论框架，实现了量子计量学与经典流处理架构（包括数据清洗树和80-20幂律）之间的桥梁。该工作为实际量子传感系统的设计和性能评估提供了理论基础，揭示了量子退相干与经典数据缺失的等效性，对分布式量子传感系统的鲁棒性设计具有重要指导意义。

Abstract: We derive unified lower bounds on the mean squared error (MSE) of distributed quantum sensor fusion under Byzantine faults and decoherence. Building on the classical Brooks-Iyengar overlap function and its vector extension, the predictive outlier model for virtual sensor tracking, and SPOTLESS spatial-temporal verification, we establish a two-parameter family of bounds indexed by entanglement visibility V and fault fraction f/M. For M quantum sensors with N atoms each and sensitivity eta, the MSE of any estimator satisfies MSE >= (1-V^2)/(4*N*eta^2*M_eff) + V^2/(4*N*eta^2*M_eff^2), where M_eff = M-2f under Brooks-Iyengar Byzantine fault tolerance and M_eff = M-f when predictive outlier detection successfully identifies faulty sensors. The bound interpolates continuously between the standard quantum limit (V=0, scaling as 1/sqrt(M_eff)) and the Heisenberg limit (V=1, scaling as 1/M_eff). Monte Carlo simulations with up to 64 sensors validate the theoretical scaling laws. Validation on the Intel Berkeley Lab 54-mote dataset with spatial clustering demonstrates 20-27 dB SNR improvement from entanglement per cluster, and reveals that missing classical sensor data degrades fusion agreement in the same pattern as quantum decoherence. The framework bridges quantum metrology with classical stream-processing architectures including Data-Cleaning Trees and the 80-20 Power Law for scale-invariant clustering.

</details>


### [14] [Conflict-Free Replicated Data Types for Neural Network Model Merging: A Two-Layer Architecture Enabling CRDT-Compliant Model Merging Across 26 Strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19373)
*Ryan Gillespie*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出CRDTMergeState双层架构，通过将神经网络合并策略包装在CRDT合规层中，解决了现有合并方法无法满足交换律、结合律和幂等性的结构性问题，从而实现了无冲突的分布式神经网络合并。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有26种神经网络合并策略（包括权重平均、SLERP、TIES、DARE、Fisher合并和进化方法等）都无法满足分布式操作所需的代数性质（交换律、结合律、幂等性）。作者证明这种失败是结构性的：基于归一化的合并方法无法同时满足这三个性质，这阻碍了无冲突分布式操作的实现。

Method: 提出CRDTMergeState双层架构：第一层使用OR-Set CRDT语义管理贡献，通过集合并操作实现天然的交换律、结合律和幂等性；第二层将合并策略作为确定性纯函数应用于规范排序的贡献集，随机性由Merkle根种子决定。这种分离确保了强最终一致性。

Result: 在三个层面验证有效性：(1) 受控4x4张量测试104项全部通过；(2) 生产规模模型（高达7.24B参数）测试208项策略级测试和43,368项层级属性检查；(3) 多节点收敛测试（100节点，20种排序），CRDT开销低于0.5毫秒。包装器透明性通过字节级相同输出验证确认。

Conclusion: CRDTMergeState成功解决了神经网络合并在分布式环境中的结构性问题，为任何合并策略提供了CRDT合规的包装方案，保证所有接收相同贡献的副本计算出相同的合并模型，与消息顺序无关，同时保持原有性能。

Abstract: All 26 neural network merge strategies we tested including weight averaging, SLERP, TIES, DARE, Fisher merging, and evolutionary approaches -- fail the algebraic properties (commutativity, associativity, idempotency) required for conflict-free distributed operation. We prove that this failure is structural: normalisation-based merges cannot simultaneously satisfy all three properties. To resolve this, we present a two-layer architecture -- CRDTMergeState -- that wraps any merge strategy in a CRDT-compliant (Conflict-Free Replicated Data Type) layer. Layer 1 manages contributions via OR-Set CRDT semantics, where the merge operation is set union -- trivially commutative, associative, and idempotent. Layer 2 applies merge strategies as deterministic pure functions over a canonically-ordered contribution set, with randomness seeded from the Merkle root. We prove that this separation guarantees Strong Eventual Consistency: all replicas receiving the same contributions compute identical merged models, regardless of message ordering. Empirical validation spans three tiers: controlled 4x4 tensors (104/104 tests pass), production-scale models up to 7.24B parameters (208 strategy-level tests, 43,368 layer-level property checks at capped tensor resolution), and multi-node convergence under gossip and partition healing (100 nodes, 20 orderings), with CRDT overhead below 0.5 ms. Because the wrapper is transparent, downstream performance is identical by construction, confirmed via byte-identical output verification. The reference implementation is available as crdt-merge v0.9.4.

</details>


### [15] [LatentBox: An Efficient Latent-First Storage System for AI-Generated Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19385)
*Zirui Wang,Yunjia Zheng,Tingfeng Lan,Zhaoyuan Su,Haoran Ni,Juncheng Yang,Yue Cheng*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了LatentBox，一种针对AI生成图像的潜变量优先存储系统。该系统通过将压缩潜变量作为持久存储对象，并利用按需GPU重构来换取存储节省，在保持竞争力的访问延迟的同时，将持久存储需求降低了78.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI生成图像的爆炸式增长给存储基础设施带来了可持续性挑战。传统对象存储将图像作为全分辨率像素块存储，消耗大量存储容量和带宽。然而，AI生成图像可以从紧凑的模型原生潜变量张量确定性重构，使得持久化图像存储本质上是冗余的。

Method: LatentBox将压缩潜变量视为持久存储对象，并在读取路径上使用按需GPU重构，以廉价的计算换取大量持久存储节省。基于对35个月、20亿次请求的生产轨迹的大规模分析，LatentBox将频繁访问的图像保留为解码像素格式以实现快速命中，将不太活跃的对象存储为压缩潜变量以扩展有效缓存容量，并持续调整图像缓存和潜变量缓存之间的分配以优化用户感知的访问延迟。

Result: 基于生产轨迹的原型评估表明，与纯图像存储相比，LatentBox在保持相当甚至更低的平均和尾部延迟的同时，减少了78.7%的持久存储。

Conclusion: LatentBox证明了潜变量优先存储架构在AI生成图像场景下的有效性，为解决AI生成内容的存储可持续性挑战提供了一种可行的解决方案，能够在大幅降低存储成本的同时维持良好的用户体验。

Abstract: The explosive growth of AI-generated images has created a sustainability challenge for storage infrastructure. Platforms like Midjourney and Adobe Firefly already host billions of generative images, yet conventional object stores persist them as blobs with full-resolution pixels, consuming huge amounts of storage capacity and bandwidth. Unlike natural photos, however, AI-generated images can be deterministically reconstructed from compact, model-native latent tensors, making persistent image storage fundamentally redundant.
  This paper presents LatentBox, a latent-first storage system for AI-generated images. LatentBox treats compressed latents as durable storage objects and uses on-demand GPU reconstruction on the read path to trade inexpensive compute for large persistent storage savings. Our design is guided by the first large-scale analysis of AI-generated image access we are aware of, based on a 35-month, 2-billion-request production trace from a major generative-content platform. Motivated by the trace analysis, LatentBox keeps frequently accessed images in decoded pixel format for fast hits, stores less-active objects as compressed latents to expand effective cache capacity, and continuously adjusts the splits between the image and latent cache to optimize user-perceived access latency.We build a LatentBox prototype and evaluate it with the production trace. LatentBox reduces persistent storage by 78.7% with competitive or even lower mean and tail latency over a pure image-based storage.

</details>


### [16] [Resilient Byzantine Agreement with Predictions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19452)
*Julien Dallot,Darya Melnyk,Tijana Milentijevic,Stefan Schmid,Patrik Welters*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究带有预测器的拜占庭协议问题，给出了预测器准确性与算法容错性之间的一致性-鲁棒性权衡的完整刻画，并证明了随着预测准确性下降，容错能力呈线性降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拜占庭协议是分布式系统中的核心问题，传统研究假设节点完全不可靠。本文引入预测器机制，能够提前标记可疑的拜占庭节点，研究在预测器辅助下算法容错能力如何提升以及预测准确性对容错性能的影响。

Method: 针对n个节点的系统，研究者设计了基于预测器的拜占庭协议算法，并通过理论分析建立了参数α∈[0,1]下的容错性界限。研究涵盖非认证和认证两种场景，分别推导一致性（预测器正确时）和鲁棒性（预测器完全错误时）的容错上界，并证明这些界限的紧致性。

Result: 主要结果包括：(1)一致性-鲁棒性权衡：预测器正确时可容忍α·n个故障节点；预测器完全错误时，非认证场景可容忍(1-α)/2·n-1个，认证场景提升至(1-α)·n-1个，这些界限是紧的；(2)平滑性刻画：容错能力随预测错误数线性下降，非认证场景每个错误预测损失1单位容错能力，认证场景则2个错误预测才损失1单位。

Conclusion: 本文首次完整刻画了预测器辅助下拜占庭协议的一致性与鲁棒性之间的最优权衡关系，证明了认证机制能显著改善预测器错误时的容错性能退化，为设计鲁棒的拜占庭容错算法提供了理论基础。

Abstract: This paper studies the Byzantine Agreement problem where the nodes have access to a predictor that flags nodes for suspicion of faulty (Byzantine) behavior. We focus on algorithmic resilience -- the maximum number of faulty nodes an algorithm can tolerate -- and present algorithms and impossibility results whose resilience depend on the accuracy of the predictor. As our first main result, we bring a complete characterization of the consistency--robustness trade-offs in both the non-authenticated and authenticated settings: for $n$ nodes and a parameter $α\in [0, 1]$, we present algorithms that tolerate up to $α\cdot n$ faulty nodes when the predictor is correct (consistency), and up to $\frac{1-α}{2} \cdot n - 1$ faulty nodes when the predictor is arbitrarily wrong (robustness); in the authenticated setting the robustness bound improves to $(1-α) \cdot n - 1$. These trade-offs are exactly tight as we show that one additional faulty node renders the problem impossible. Our second main result characterizes smoothness: the rate at which resilience degrades as the predictor becomes less accurate. We show that resilience linearly decreases in the number of wrong predictions as long as that number stays within a constant fraction of $n$. Concretely, in the non-authenticated setting each additional wrong prediction loses one unit of resilience, whereas in the authenticated setting the decline is halved since two wrong predictions are needed to lose one unit of resilience.

</details>

Main category: cs.DC

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统离线训练的驾驶员监控模型难以适应真实驾驶环境；标准联邦学习存在通信开销大、假设同构架构、难以处理个性化数据等问题。

Method: FedADAS通过在共享公共数据集上仅交换软标签（soft logits）实现完全模型异构性，允许每辆车运行定制模型；同时提出打哈欠识别流水线，提供性能高效型（99.7MB）和内存高效型（0.6MB）两种架构。

Result: 在最多115个边缘客户端的实验中，FedADAS在较高客户端参与度下显著优于传统联邦学习方法，通信成本降低高达9974倍，在极端数据异构性下保持个性化和泛化的优越平衡；打哈欠识别模型在Jetson NANO上达到98.3% F1分数、1.99ms推理时间。

Conclusion: FedADAS适合真实世界部署，有效解决了驾驶员疲劳检测中的模型异构性、数据个性化与通信效率问题，为车载边缘计算提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Driver fatigue is a critical safety concern in advanced driver assistance systems. Driver monitoring models trained off-site on static datasets adapt poorly to real-world conditions, while standard federated learning imposes high communication overhead, assumes homogeneous architectures, and struggles with personalized driver data. We present FedADAS, a federated distillation framework enabling collaborative on-device learning across heterogeneous vehicular networks. FedADAS enables full model heterogeneity by exchanging only soft logits on a shared public dataset, allowing each vehicle to run a customized model tailored to its computational constraints. Additionally, we introduce a yawn recognition pipeline supporting training and inference on edge devices that provides two robust architectures: Performance-Efficient (99.7 MB) achieving 98.3% F1-score with 1.99ms inference time on a Jetson NANO, and a Memory-Efficient (0.6 MB) that trains an epoch in 6.12 minutes on a Jetson AGX Orin. In experiments with up to 115 edge clients, FedADAS significantly outperforms traditional federated learning approaches at higher client participation, achieving up to 9974x reduction in communication cost while maintaining a superior tradeoff between personalization and generalization under extreme data heterogeneity, demonstrating its suitability for real-world deployment. Code is available at https://opensource.silicon-austria.com/mujtabaa/fedadas

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式计算中的唤醒问题要求设计对称协议，使处理器能利用共享寄存器确保所有（或足够多）其他处理器已完成操作。Kane和Kominers分析了该问题的一个更一般的非对称版本（多个并行且不可区分的房间），本文旨在解决他们留下的关于最小开关状态数的开放问题，并将研究扩展到对称唤醒版本。

Method: 首先针对非对称的囚徒问题变体，分析并确定了囚徒解决问题所需的最小开关状态数量；其次，考虑了对称的“唤醒”版本场景，建立了处理器数量与寄存器数量之间是否存在解的精确判定条件。

Result: 确定了在Kane和Kominers提出的多房间囚徒问题中，解决该问题所需的最小开关状态数；在对称唤醒版本中，精确刻画了对于哪些处理器数量和寄存器数量的组合，存在可行的解决方案。

Conclusion: 本文不仅回答了先前研究中的开放问题，确定了非对称设置下的最小资源需求，还为对称唤醒问题提供了完整的可能性刻画，为分布式计算中的资源约束问题提供了理论基础。

Abstract: The wakeup problem in distributed computing asks for a symmetric protocol that enables one of several processors to eventually guarantee that all (or, in a more general setting, enough) other processors have acted, using a shared register but no global clock. Dropping the symmetry requirement gives a well-known exercise often phrased in terms of prisoners entering, in an unknown sequence, a room equipped with a single binary switch, and using it to communicate. Kane and Kominers recently analysed a more general version of the latter with multiple parallel and indistinguishable rooms. We answer some open questions of Kane and Kominers regarding the minimum number of switch states needed for the prisoners to solve the problem. We also consider the symmetric ``wakeup'' version of this scenario, and establish exactly for which numbers of processors and registers a solution is possible.

</details>

Conclusion: 空间数据中心是一种极具前景的方法，能够通过在轨处理数据为客户提供可操作的见解，有效应对未来空间数据传输和处理面临的挑战。

Abstract: Dramatic cost reductions driven by private sector innovations have led to a rapid increase in the number of satellites in orbit and a corresponding surge in space-generated data. As this trend continues, transmitting large volumes of data to Earth for processing may become increasingly costly and challenging due to potential space-to-Earth link congestion and increased latency. Moreover, traditional ground station networks may face difficulties accommodating growing data flows and workloads because of capacity constraints, complex scheduling logistics, and restricted visibility windows, which can limit scalability. Space Data Centers (SDCs) -- software-driven, multi-tenant artificial intelligence-based service platforms capable of processing data in orbit to generate actionable insights for client satellites and ground users -- represent a promising approach to address these challenges.
  This article presents the architecture of a Low Earth Orbit SDC satellite constellation, considering orbital design, inter-satellite links and network topology, computational resource organization, and software service orchestration. We analyze the potential technical feasibility and economic viability of SDCs using forecasting models informed by technology roadmaps and illustrate the concept through Earth observation and lunar exploration use cases.

</details>


### [20] [Conflict-Freedom as a Progress Condition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19531)
*Petr Kuznetsov,Pierre Sutra,Guillermo Toyos-Marfurt*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的无锁进度条件——无冲突性（conflict-freedom），它是无阻碍性（obstruction-freedom）的自然推广，并基于一种新颖的commit-adopt对象泛化构造，证明了每个顺序对象都有读写无冲突的线性化实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无阻碍性实现保证操作在孤立执行时有进展，但作者发现并发操作的存在本身并不一定阻碍进展，只有未完成的冲突（非交换）操作才会。因此希望提出一种新的进度条件，以更好地利用对象的语义交换性来提高效率。

Method: 提出无冲突性作为无阻碍性的自然推广，证明每个顺序对象都有读写无冲突的线性化实现；设计了一种基于commit-adopt对象新颖泛化的通用构造方法。

Result: 证明了与无阻碍性类似，每个顺序对象都可以实现读写无冲突的线性化版本；提出了一种新颖的commit-adopt对象泛化，具有独立的研究价值。

Conclusion: 无冲突性是一种比无阻碍性更自然的进度条件，能够为具有语义交换性的对象提供高效的实现方案，且具有普遍适用性。

Abstract: An obstruction-free implementation guarantees progress to every operation that is given enough time to take steps in isolation. But, as we show in this paper, the mere presence of concurrent operations alone does not have to prevent progress; only incomplete conflicting (non-commuting) operations may do so. This progress condition, that we call conflict-freedom, is a natural generalization of obstruction-freedom that promises efficient implementations for objects exhibiting semantic commutativity. We show that, as with obstruction-freedom, every sequential object has a read-write conflict-free linearizable implementation. Our conflict-free universal construction is based on a novel generalization of the instrumental commit-adopt object, interesting in its own right.

</details>

*Thien Tran,Jonathan Kua,Thuong Hoang,Minh Tran,Yuemin Ding,Jiong Jin*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于有向无环图(DAG)的QoS感知动态任务放置(DTP)框架，用于解决网络化机器人中感知-规划-控制流水线的计算卸载问题，通过优化端到端延迟、截止时间违反率、硬件利用率和切换惩罚的权衡，实现在本地执行、静态卸载和混合模式之间的动态切换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前物理AI(Physical AI)严重依赖闭环视觉伺服流水线，其中的感知和规划阶段由于嵌入复杂模型而在机器人端产生巨大计算负担。在实际工业场景中，将感知任务静态卸载到边缘节点不适用于对延迟敏感的精密工业环境；而本地执行会饱和AI加速硬件，静态边缘卸载则暴露控制环路于网络抖动风险。现有的自适应任务放置(ATP)控制器仅能在二元阈值规则下重新定位单个流水线阶段，缺乏多阶段模型和明确的放置切换成本考量。

Method: 作者提出基于DAG的QoS感知动态任务放置框架：1) 将感知-规划-控制流水线形式化为DAG，包含任务级和节点级属性（计算成本、通信延迟、可行放置集合）；2) 定义小规模可解释候选集（完全本地、静态卸载、混合）；3) 设计基于窗口的成本函数，结合尾部端到端延迟、截止时间违反率、硬件利用率和汉明距离切换惩罚；4) 提出带有滞后机制和最小驻留时间约束的DTP算法，防止放置抖动。

Result: 作为Work-in-Progress论文，目前提出了理论框架、结构化定性分析，并规划了两阶段验证路线图：仿真验证和硬件在环(hardware-in-the-loop)验证。

Conclusion: 该框架为工业自动化中的3C协同设计提供了新思路，通过DAG形式化和多目标成本函数，在本地执行与边缘卸载之间实现动态平衡，为网络化机器人的任务放置问题提供了理论基础和系统化解决方案。

Abstract: Current Physical AI (PAI) relies heavily on closed-loop visual-servoing pipelines, whose perception and planning stages may become computationally intensive onboard due to complex models embedded on robots. In practice, offloading the perception task to on-site edges statically is inappropriate for latency-sensitive, precise industrial settings over a standardized industrial network. This emphasizes the importance of Control-Communication-Computing (3C) co-design in industrial automation: monolithic local execution saturates AI-accelerated machine and robot hardware, while static edge offloading exposes the control loop to network jitter. Existing adaptive task placement (ATP) controllers can partially address the gap by relocating a single pipeline stage on binary threshold rules, without a multi-stage model and an explicit cost on placement switching. In this Work-in-Progress (WiP) paper, we propose a directed acyclic graph (DAG) based quality-of-service (QoS)-aware dynamic task placement (DTP) framework for sensing-perception-planning-control pipelines in networked robotics. This pipeline is formalized as a DAG with task-level and node-level attributes for compute cost, communication delay, and feasible placement sets; over a small interpretable candidate set (fully local, static offload, hybrid), a window-based cost function combines tail end-to-end latency, deadline violation rate, hardware utilization, and a Hamming-distance switching penalty, and a DTP algorithm with hysteresis and a minimum dwell-time bounds placement chatter. Our WiP paper presents the theoretical framework, a structured qualitative analysis, and a two-phase simulation plus hardware-in-the-loop validation roadmap.

</details>


### [22] [Understanding Inference Scaling for LLMs: Bottlenecks, Trade-offs, and Performance Principles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19775)
*Moiz Arif,Avinash Maurya,Sudharshan Vazhkudai,Bogdan Nicolae*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文针对推理中心架构（如CoT模型）的新特性，分析了从8B到671B参数模型在GPU集群上的系统瓶颈，发现推理负载会使系统进入容量受限状态，不同模型规模和架构需要不同的并行策略组合来实现最优推理效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着生成式AI向推理中心架构（如长链式思维CoT处理）转变，推理负载特性发生根本变化，生成大量推理Token使系统从计算受限转为容量受限状态。现有标准扩展启发式方法无法有效应对这一范式转变，亟需系统性地分析推理负载下的瓶颈与最优并行策略。

Method: 在GPU集群上对8B至671B参数范围的模型进行全面系统特征分析，系统性地探索数据并行、张量并行和流水线并行三种策略之间的相互作用，识别推理负载下的关键性能瓶颈。

Result: 数据并行对小模型吞吐效率高，但在推理负载下会遇到容量陷阱，KV缓存碎片化导致提前限流和次优计算利用率；张量并行能释放闲置内存在32B交叉点附近带来次线性增益；前沿级密集模型（如Llama-405B）受互连和内存带宽限制适合高TP度，稀疏MoE模型（如DeepSeek-R1）受路由和同步延迟限制适合混合策略。

Conclusion: 研究为应对推理悬崖提供了严格的决策框架，为下一代推理基础设施确立了新的架构要求，不同规模和架构的模型需要定制化的并行策略组合才能实现最优性能。

Abstract: The transition from standard generative AI to \emph{reasoning-centric architectures}, exemplified by models capable of extensive Chain-of-Thought~(CoT) processing, marks a fundamental paradigm shift in system requirements. Unlike traditional workloads dominated by compute-bound prefill, reasoning workloads generate long chains of reasoning tokens that shift inference into a \emph{Capacity-Bound regime}. This paper presents a comprehensive system characterization, evaluating models ranging from 8B to 671B parameters on GPUs clusters. By systematically exploring the interplay between Data, Tensor, and Pipeline parallelism, we identify critical bottlenecks that defy standard scaling heuristics. Our analysis reveals that data parallelism is throughput efficient for small models but hits a capacity trap on reasoning workloads as KV-cache fragmentation forces early throttling resulting in sub-optimal compute utilization. Tensor parallelism unlocks stranded memory and delivers sublinear gains near the 32B crossover. At frontier scale, dense models (e.g., Llama-405B) are interconnect and memory-bandwidth bound and favor high-degree TP, while sparse Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models (e.g., DeepSeek-R1) are limited by routing and synchronization latency and benefit from hybrid strategies. These insights provide a rigorous decision framework for navigating the reasoning cliff, establishing new architectural imperatives for the next generation of inference infrastructure.

</details>


### [23] [GEM: GPU-Variability-Aware Expert to GPU Mapping for MoE Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19945)
*Sourish Wawdhane,Avinash Kumar,Poulami Das*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出GEM框架，通过感知GPU性能差异来优化MoE模型的专家映射，显著降低推理延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MoE服务引擎采用同步处理方式，性能受限于最慢的GPU（straggler）。现有专家放置方法虽能平衡token负载，但忽略了GPU性能差异，常将高频使用的专家分配给性能较弱的GPU，进一步加剧性能瓶颈。

Method: 提出GEM框架。核心洞察：(1) 应根据GPU性能差异分配非均匀的token负载，使所有GPU同步完成处理；(2) 将同时被使用的专家（consistent和temporal类型）分散到不同GPU，且避免分配给慢速GPU。GEM收集GPU性能差异配置文件和任务token负载分布，据此进行专家到GPU的映射优化。

Result: 实验结果表明，GEM相比基线方法平均降低端到端延迟7.9%，最高可达16.5%。

Conclusion: GEM证明了在MoE模型服务中考虑GPU性能差异进行专家映射的重要性，为解决同步处理中的straggler问题提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Expert (MoE) models enable efficient inference by employing smaller experts and activating only a subset of them per token. MoE serving engines distribute experts across multiple GPUs and route tokens to appropriate GPUs at inference time based on experts activated. They process tokens in lock-step fashion, where tokens within a batch must finish processing before proceeding to the next layer. This synchronization barrier acts as a critical bottleneck because the performance of MoE models is limited by the straggler GPU that finishes last. Stragglers emerge when too many heavily used experts are placed on the same GPU or the slowest GPU. While prior works place experts that balance token loads across GPUs, they all overlook GPU variability and often place highly used experts on the slowest GPUs.
  We propose GEM, GPU-variability-aware Expert Mapping, a framework for GPU variability-aware expert to GPU mapping for MoE models. GEM exploits two insights. First, we must place experts such that each GPU receives non-uniform token loads based on their variability and they all finish processing a layer at about the same time. Our studies show that there are two types of experts: consistent that are used most of the time and temporal that are often used together for the remaining time. Our second insight is that we must place simultaneously used consistent and temporal experts on different GPUs and avoid placing them on slower GPUs to reduce slowdown. GEM gathers the variability profile of GPUs for each model and task and uses the token load distributions per task to map experts to GPUs. Our experiments show that GEM improves end-to-end latency by 7.9% on average and by up to 16.5% compared to the baseline.

</details>
