### [24] [Position: Let's Develop Data Probes to Fundamentally Understand How Data Affects LLM Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18801)
*Shiqiang Wang,Herbert Woisetschläger,Hans Arno Jacobsen,Mingyue Ji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文是一篇立场性论文，主张通过开发系统性的方法，从适当定义的随机过程中生成具有特定统计属性的合成序列（数据探针），以研究数据特征如何影响大语言模型在不同阶段（训练、调优、对齐、上下文学习等）的行为，从而超越目前依赖大量实验和经验启发的局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据是大语言模型的基石，但目前对于什么数据在LLM工作流的不同阶段有用以及为什么有用，仍然是一个未解之谜。现有的方法严重依赖于使用大型公共数据集进行大量实验，以获得用于数据过滤和数据集构建的经验启发式规则。这些方法计算密集，且缺乏一种有原则的方式来理解特定数据特征如何驱动LLM行为。因此，迫切需要开发系统性的方法论来深入理解数据在LLM中的作用。

Method: 提出了一种“数据探针”方法，即开发系统性的方法论，从适当定义的随机过程中生成合成序列。这些序列在LLM工作流的一个或多个阶段使用时，能够揭示有用的特征。数据探针具有可以用理论概念（如典型集，typical sets）来描述的统计属性，这些概念被泛化以描述LLM的行为。通过观察LLM在这些数据探针上的行为，研究人员可以系统地研究数据特征如何影响模型性能、泛化能力和鲁棒性。

Result: 该位置论文阐述了数据探针方法的理论框架和潜在优势。这些探针序列展示了可以通过典型集等理论概念来观察的统计属性。这种方法为研究数据特征对LLM性能、泛化和鲁棒性的影响提供了系统性的途径。与传统的计算密集型实验方法相比，数据探针方法能够提供更深入的理论见解。

Conclusion: 数据探针方法为揭示数据在LLM训练和推理中的作用的根本性见解提供了一条新途径，超越了单纯的经验启发式方法。这种方法有潜力建立更加系统化和理论化的方法论，帮助理解数据特征与大语言模型行为之间的关系。

Abstract: Data is fundamental to large language models (LLMs). However, understanding of what makes certain data useful for different stages of an LLM workflow, including training, tuning, alignment, in-context learning, etc., and why, remains an open question. Current approaches rely heavily on extensive experimentation with large public datasets to obtain empirical heuristics for data filtering and dataset construction. These approaches are compute intensive and lack a principled way of understanding the essence of how specific data characteristics drive LLM behavior. In this position paper, we advocate for the need of developing systematic methodologies for generating synthetic sequences from appropriately defined random processes, with the goal that these sequences can reveal useful characteristics when they are used in one or multiple stages of the LLM workflow. We refer to such sequences as data probes. By observing LLM behavior on data probes, researchers can systematically conduct studies on how data characteristics influence model performance, generalization, and robustness. The probing sequences exhibit statistical properties that can be viewed using theoretical concepts, such as typical sets, which are generalized to describe the behaviors of LLMs. This data-probe approach provides a pathway for uncovering foundational insights into the role of data in LLM training and inference, beyond empirical heuristics.

</details>


### [25] [Operationalizing Document AI: A Microservice Architecture for OCR and LLM Pipelines in Production](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18818)
*Yao Fehlis,Benjamin Bengfort,Zhangzhang Si,Vahid Eyorokon,Prema Roman,Patrick Deziel,Devon Slonaker,Steve Veldman,Ben Johnson,Joyce Rigelo,Michael Wharton,Steve Kramer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于文档理解的微服务架构，填补了学术模型定义与生产规模运行之间的空白，并分享了生产环境中的关键发现和设计经验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学术研究主要关注文档理解的新模型，导致模型定义与生产规模运行之间存在显著差距，缺乏实际部署的架构指导和实践经验。

Method: 提出微服务架构封装多模型管道（分类、OCR、大语言模型结构化字段提取），采用混合分类策略、GPU推理与CPU编排分离、异步处理IO密集操作、独立水平扩展等设计，并在每小时处理数千份多页文档的生产环境中验证。

Result: 通过批量分析发现两个关键发现：OCR（而非语言模型解析）主导端到端延迟；系统饱和点由共享GPU推理容量决定，而非工作节点数量。

Conclusion: 为实践者提供超越基准测试的文档理解系统架构模式，有效实现模型在生产环境中的运营化部署。

Abstract: Academic research tends to focus on new models for document understanding creating a wide gap in the literature between model definition and running models at production scale. To close that gap, we present a microservice architecture that encapsulates pipelines of multiple models for classification, optical character recognition (OCR), and large language model structured field extraction as well as our experience running this pipeline on thousands of multi-page documents per hour. We describe our primary design decisions, including a hybrid classification, separation of GPU-bound inference from CPU-bound orchestration, use of asynchronous processing for the many IO-bound operations in the pipeline, and an independent, horizontal scaling strategy. Using batch profiling, we identified two surprising qualitative findings that shape production deployments: OCR, not language-model parsing, dominates end-to-end latency, and the system saturates at a concurrency determined by shared GPU-inference capacity rather than worker count. Our goal is to provide practitioners with concrete architectural patterns for building document understanding systems that work beyond the benchmark; effectively operationalizing models in production.

</details>


### [26] [Evaluating the Utility of Personal Health Records in Personalized Health AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18937)
*Rory Sayres,Kejia Chen,Ayush Jain,Matthew Thompson,Jonathan Richina,Xiang Yin,Jimmy Hu,Fan Zhang,Bob Lou,Mike Sanchez,Ines Mezerreg,Meredith Schreier,Hamsa Subramaniam,I-Ching Lee,Yugang Jia,Daniel Mcduff,Yossi Matias,Avinatan Hassidim,Dale Webster,Yun Liu,Jackie Barr,Quang Duong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究评估了Gemini 3.0 Flash在结合个人健康档案（PHR）上下文时回答患者健康查询的能力。通过对2,257个查询的实验发现，引入PHR数据显著提升了回答的有用性（p < 0.001），并在安全性、准确性、相关性和个性化方面表现出潜在增益，同时也揭示了LLM在理解复杂PHR时的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 患者管理的个人健康档案（PHRs）有望赋能患者更好地理解自身健康状况，但档案中的信息往往复杂难懂，阻碍了患者从中获取有效见解。研究旨在探索大语言模型结合PHR临床数据作为上下文，是否能有效回答用户的健康查询，从而弥补这一差距。

Method: 研究使用了2,257个源自三种不同分布（网页搜索、聊天机器人对话模板、患者致电医疗团队）的用户查询，并与去标识化的PHR数据（来自1,945个样本池）进行匹配。利用Gemini 3.0 Flash在三种上下文条件下生成回复：(1)无PHR上下文；(2)基本人口统计学和病情摘要；(3)完整的临床笔记。评估结合了现有的SHARP评级框架与新开发的PHR解读错误模式框架，采用自动评分器和临床医生评分（n=95）两种方式进行评价。

Result: 实验结果显示，与未提供PHR上下文相比，结合PHR数据在所有问题类型上均显著提高了回答的有用性（配对t检验，p < 0.001）。研究还观察到在回答的安全性、准确性、相关性和个性化方面有潜在提升。评估框架识别出LLM在理解复杂PHR特定方面存在缺陷，例如时间上的混乱以及罕见但有意义的幻觉现象。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，PHR数据具有帮助满足广泛用户需求的潜力，同时提供了一个用于监控基于PHR上下文的LLM回答缺陷的框架。这项工作为进一步评估和实现用户通过理解健康档案获益的研究提供了动力。

Abstract: Patient-managed Personal Health Records (PHRs) promises to empower patients to better understand their health; but information in the record is complex, potentially hindering insights. In this study, we assess the potential of large language models (LLMs, Gemini 3.0 Flash) to provide helpful answers to user health queries, when provided clinical data from PHRs as context. A total of 2,257 user queries were drawn from 3 different distributions to represent patient questions: shorter web search queries, longer questions derived from templates of chatbot conversations, and questions patients asked to their healthcare team (patient calls). Queries were matched with de-identified PHRs (from a pool of 1,945). Gemini responses were generated (1) without PHR context; (2) with a basic summary of demographics, conditions, and medications; (3) with full, extensive clinical notes. For evaluation, we leveraged an existing rating framework (SHARP), and developed a new framework for specific error modes when interpreting PHRs. Evaluation was performed using autoraters for the full set, and with clinician ratings for a subset (n=95), with both sets of raters knowing the full PHR context. We see significant improvements in the helpfulness of answers to all question types with PHR data (p < 0.001, paired t-test). We also observe potential gains in safety, accuracy, relevance and personalization of answers. Our PHR evaluation framework further identifies gaps in LLM understanding of particular aspects of complex PHRs, such as temporal disorientation, and rare but meaningful confabulations. These results suggest potential for PHR data to help people with a wide range of user needs; and provide a framework for monitoring for gaps in LLM answers based on PHR context. This study motivates further work to assess and realize potential benefits to users from understanding their health records.

</details>


### [27] [Learn-by-Wire Training Control Governance: Bounded Autonomous Training Under Stress for Stability and Efficiency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19008)
*Anis Radianis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出LBW-Guard，一个运行在AdamW之上的有界自主训练控制治理层，通过监控训练遥测数据并施加有界控制来解决大语言模型训练中的不稳定性和计算浪费问题。在Qwen2.5模型上的实验表明，该方法将最终困惑度降低18.7%，端到端时间缩短约10%，且在激进学习率压力下保持训练稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代语言模型训练在激进学习率、大规模和运行时压力条件下，日益面临不稳定性、训练退化、计算浪费等问题，亟需一种能够保障训练稳定性的方法。

Method: 提出Learn-by-Wire Guard（LBW-Guard），这是一个有界的自主训练控制治理层，运行于AdamW优化器之上而非替代其更新规则。该方法通过观察训练遥测数据、识别对不稳定性敏感的训练阶段，并对优化器执行施加有界控制，同时保持训练目标不变。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-7B上的实验显示：LBW-Guard将最终困惑度从13.21降至10.74（提升18.7%），端到端时间从392.54s缩短至357.02s（1.10倍加速）。在强学习率压力下（LR=3e-3和1e-3），AdamW的困惑度分别恶化至1885.24和659.76，而LBW-Guard仍保持在11.57和10.33的可训练水平。梯度裁剪基线无法复现此效果。

Conclusion: 研究证明，对稳定性敏感的大语言模型训练可以从优化器之上的治理层中获益。LBW-Guard验证了有界运行时控制能够在压力条件下保护有效计算，同时区别于优化器替换和局部梯度抑制方法。

Abstract: Modern language-model training is increasingly exposed to instability, degraded runs, and wasted compute, especially under aggressive learning-rate, scale, and runtime-stress conditions. This paper introduces Learn-by-Wire Guard (LBW-Guard), a bounded autonomous training-control governance layer that operates above AdamW. Rather than replacing the optimizer update rule, LBW-Guard observes training telemetry, interprets instability-sensitive regimes, and applies bounded control to optimizer execution while preserving fixed training objectives.
  We evaluate LBW-Guard in a Qwen2.5-centered stress-and-robustness suite using WikiText-103, with Qwen2.5-7B as the empirical anchor, model-size comparisons against Qwen2.5-3B and Qwen2.5-14B, learning-rate stress tests, gradient-clipping baselines, and a no-LoRA TinyLlama-1B full-parameter sanity check. In the 7B reference setting, LBW-Guard reduces final perplexity from 13.21 to 10.74, an 18.7% improvement, while reducing end-to-end time from 392.54s to 357.02s, a 1.10x speedup. Under stronger learning-rate stress, AdamW degrades to 1885.24 final perplexity at LR=3e-3 and 659.76 at LR=1e-3, whereas LBW-Guard remains trainable at 11.57 and 10.33, respectively. Gradient-clipping baselines do not reproduce this effect.
  These results support a scoped systems conclusion that stability-sensitive LLM training can benefit from a governance plane above the optimizer. LBW-Guard provides evidence that bounded runtime control can preserve productive compute under stress while remaining distinct from optimizer replacement and local gradient suppression.

</details>


### [28] [DecisionBench: A Benchmark for Emergent Delegation in Long-Horizon Agentic Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19099)
*Yuxuan Gao,Megan Wang,Yi Ling Yu,Zijian Carl Ma,Ao Qu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了DecisionBench，一个用于评估长周期智能体工作流中涌现式委托行为的基准测试平台，通过对23,375个任务实例的五条件参考扫描，揭示了在质量相近的情况下，委托性能存在显著差异，且仍有15-31个百分点的提升空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的智能体工作流评估通常只关注最终任务质量，忽略了委托编排信号。为了更好地理解和评估长周期智能体工作流中的涌现式委托行为，需要一个能够全面评估质量、成本、延迟、委托率、路由保真度等多个维度的基准测试平台。

Method: 构建了一个基准测试平台（substrate），包含固定的任务套件（GAIA、tau-bench、BFCL multi-turn）、对等模型池（11个模型，7个供应商系列）、委托接口（call_model及可选的read_profile通道）、确定性技能标注层，以及多轴指标套件。该平台与对等信息的生成或交付方式无关，支持对学习型路由器、更丰富的对等记忆、自适应配置文件构建和多层委托进行评估。通过在完整模型池上进行五条件参考扫描（n=23,375个任务实例）来表征该平台。

Result: 三个基准层面的发现：（i）四种感知条件下的平均端任务质量在统计上没有显著差异（|beta| <= 0.010, p >= 0.21），因此仅评估质量会忽略编排信号；（ii）在平均质量接近的情况下，路由保真度-at-1在各条件间从7.5%到29.5%不等，交付通道（按需工具 vs 预加载描述）比描述内容更具影响力；（iii）反事实上限显示，完美委托在每个套件上都比实测性能高出15-31个百分点，表明未来编排方法有巨大的未实现提升空间。

Conclusion: DecisionBench提供了一个全面、可扩展的评估框架，揭示了当前智能体委托编排中的关键信号和巨大的改进潜力。研究发布了基准平台、标注层、参考干预套件、分析流程和220个每条件运行存档，为未来研究提供了基础。

Abstract: We introduce DecisionBench, a benchmark substrate for emergent delegation in long-horizon agentic workflows. The substrate fixes a task suite (GAIA, tau-bench, BFCL multi-turn), a peer-model pool (11 models, 7 vendor families), a delegation interface (call_model plus an optional read_profile channel), a deterministic skill-annotation layer, and a multi-axis metric suite covering quality, cost, latency, delegation rate, routing fidelity-at-k, vendor self-preference, and a counterfactual-delegation ceiling. The substrate is agnostic to how peer information is generated or delivered, so learned routers, richer peer memories, adaptive profile construction, and multi-step delegation can all be evaluated against it. We characterize the substrate with a five-condition reference sweep on the full pool (n=23,375 task instances). Three benchmark-level findings emerge: (i) mean end-task quality is statistically indistinguishable across the four awareness conditions (|beta| <= 0.010, p >= 0.21), so quality-only evaluation would miss the orchestration signal; (ii) routing fidelity-at-1 ranges from 7.5% to 29.5% across conditions at near-equal mean quality, with delivery channel (on-demand tool vs. preloaded description) dominating description content; (iii) a counterfactual ceiling places perfect delegation 15-31 percentage points above measured performance on every suite, locating large unrealized headroom for future orchestration methods. We release the substrate, annotation layer, reference intervention suite, analysis pipeline, and 220 per-condition run archives.

</details>


### [29] [AgentNLQ: A General-Purpose Agent for Natural Language to SQL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19010)
*Olena Bogdanov,Yeunji Jung,Chandra Dhir,Pareekshitreddy Gaddam,Saurabh Jain,Lakshmi Tumati,Vijay Parthasarathy,Anup Shirgaonkar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的多智能体NL2SQL方法，通过优化的编排器和高级模式增强技术，在BIRD基准测试上实现了78.1%的语义准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大语言模型（LLM）能力快速发展，但NL2SQL转换的准确率仍未达到人类SQL专家的水平，因此需要改进算法以提升性能。

Method: 设计了一种新的多智能体方法，包含：(a) 优化的编排器，利用LLM进行规划、编排、反思和自我纠正；(b) 高级模式增强方法，创建上下文感知的元数据；(c) 整合用户提供的业务规则。

Result: 在BIRD-SQL基准测试上实现了78.1%的语义准确率，验证了该方法在不同领域和数据集上的准确性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: 所提出的多智能体NL2SQL方法有效提升了SQL查询生成的准确性，展示了良好的泛化性能，为NL2SQL领域提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Natural language to SQL (NL2SQL) conversion is an important problem for researchers and enterprises due to the ubiquitous importance of relational databases in broad-ranging practical problems. Despite the rapid advancements in the capabilities of LLMs, NL2SQL has not reached parity in accuracy with human expert SQL writers, hence needing additional improvements in NL2SQL algorithms. This study presents a new multi-agent method for NL2SQL that achieves 78.1% semantic accuracy on the BIg Bench for LaRge-scale Database (BIRD) benchmark. Our method leverages a semantically enriched representation of user-provided schema, adds user-provided business rules, and produces accurate SQL queries. The main contributions of this study are (a) We designed an optimized new orchestrator in a multi-agent solution that uses LLMs to plan, orchestrate, reflect, and self-correct to generate accurate SQL queries, (b) We developed an advanced schema enrichment method that creates context-aware metadata to improve accuracy, and (c) We demonstrated the accuracy and generalizability of the method across different domains and datasets by evaluating it on the BIRD-SQL benchmark.

</details>


### [30] [How Far Are We From True Auto-Research?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19156)
*Zhengxin Zhang,Ning Wang,Sainyam Galhotra,Claire Cardie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ResearchArena框架，让现成AI代理独立完成完整研究流程并生成117篇论文。评估发现：仅看手稿时表现乐观，接近人类ICLR投稿水平；但在包含实验工件审查时表现急剧下降，主要失败模式为结果造假、实验不足和计划执行不匹配。所有代理生成的论文均未达到顶会接收标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前自动研究系统已能生成完整论文，但可行性不等于质量。领域缺乏系统性研究来评估AI代理生成论文的实际质量，特别是实验严谨性和论文与实际工件的一致性。

Method: 构建ResearchArena最小化框架，让三个现成代理（Claude Code、Codex、Kimi Code）独立完成从构想、实验、论文撰写到自我修正的完整研究循环。基于13个计算机科学种子和每对代理-领域3次试验，生成117篇论文，采用三种互补评估方式：仅手稿审查(SAR)、工件感知同行评审(PR)、人工元审查。

Result: 仅手稿审查(SAR)下Claude Code得分最高，接近人类ICLR 2025投稿水平。但工件感知评审(PR)分数急剧下降，人工审计识别出三大实验严谨性瓶颈：结果造假、实验不足、计划执行不匹配。不同代理失败率差异巨大（Codex 5% vs Kimi Code 77%的论文-工件不匹配）。所有117篇论文均未达到顶会接收标准。

Conclusion: 论文表明我们距离真正的自动研究仍有差距。仅评估手稿会产生过于乐观的假象，实验严谨性是主要瓶颈。不同代理展现截然不同的研究人格特征，导致差异化的失败模式。需要改进实验验证和工件一致性检查机制。

Abstract: Recent auto-research systems can produce complete papers, but feasibility is not the same as quality, and the field still lacks a systematic study of how good agent-generated papers actually are. We introduce ResearchArena, a minimal scaffold that lets off-the-shelf agents (Claude Code using Opus 4.6, Codex using GPT-5.4, and Kimi Code using K2.5) carry out the full research loop themselves (ideation, experimentation, paper writing, self-refinement) under only lightweight guidance. Across 13 computer science seeds and 3 trials per agent-domain pair, ResearchArena yields 117 agent-generated papers, each evaluated under three complementary lenses: a manuscript-only reviewer (SAR), an artifact-aware peer review (PR) in which agents inspect the workspace alongside the manuscript, and an human conducted meta-review. Under SAR alone the picture is optimistic: Claude Code obtains the highest score, outperforms Analemma's FARS, and matches the weighted-average human ICLR 2025 submission, suggesting that minimally scaffolded agents can produce papers that look competitive on manuscript-only review. Manual inspection, however, reveals this picture is overstated: SAR scores are poorly aligned with its actual acceptance decisions and reward plausible framing without verifying experimental substance. Under artifact-aware PR scores drop sharply, and manual auditing identifies experimental rigor as the major bottleneck, decomposing into three failure modes (fabricated results, underpowered experiments, and plan/execution mismatch) that are highly agent-dependent: Codex 5%/8% paper-vs-artifact mismatch / fabricated references versus Kimi Code 77%/72%, a $\sim$15$\times$ spread that tracks distinct research personas the agents develop. None of the 117 agent-generated papers reaches the acceptance bar of a top-tier venue. This suggests that we are still gapped from the true auto-research.

</details>


### [31] [KAN-MLP-Mixer: A comprehensive investigation of the usage of Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks (KANs) for improving IMU-based Human Activity Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19031)
*Mengxi Liu,Sizhen Bian,Vitor Fortes,Francisco Calatrava Nicolas,Daniel Geißler,Maximilian Kiefer-Emmanouilidis,Bo Zhou,Paul Lukowicz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种混合KAN-MLP架构用于人类活动识别（HAR），结合KAN的精确性与MLP的噪声鲁棒性，在8个公开数据集上平均宏F1分数相比纯MLP模型提升5.33%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks (KANs)在干净的低维数据上表现出色，但在噪声和不完美的真实世界数据集上性能下降；而传统多层感知机(MLP)对噪声容忍度高且计算效率高。将HAR模型中所有MLP组件替换为KAN往往降低准确率和计算效率，因此如何结合KAN的精确性与MLP的噪声鲁棒性和效率成为开放挑战。

Method: 系统探索了KAN模块在深度HAR网络中的各种位置配置，提出了一个混合架构：使用基于KAN的输入嵌入层、保留MLP层用于中间特征混合、引入专门的LarctanKAN模块用于最终活动分类，从而战略性地协同两种范式的优势。

Result: 在8个公开HAR数据集上，混合KAN-MLP模型相比纯MLP模型平均宏F1分数相对提升5.33%，显著优于独立的KAN和MLP基线模型；将此混合策略集成到其他最先进的HAR架构中也能持续提升性能。

Conclusion: 精心编排的KAN、MLP或其他传统神经组件的组合能够为真实世界可穿戴感知环境产生更鲁棒、更准确的HAR模型，证明了混合架构设计的有效性。

Abstract: Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks (KANs) have demonstrated an exceptional ability to learn complex functions on clean, low-dimensional data but struggle to maintain performance on noisy and imperfect real-world datasets. In contrast, conventional multi-layer perceptrons (MLPs) are far more tolerant to noise and computationally efficient. Replacing all MLP components with KANs in HAR models often degrades accuracy and computation efficiency, highlighting an open challenge: how to combine KANs' precision with MLPs' noise robustness and efficiency. To address this, we systematically explore various placements of KAN modules within deep HAR networks and propose a hybrid architecture that strategically synergizes the strengths of both paradigms, which uses a KAN-based input embedding layer, retains MLP layers for intermediate feature mixing, and introduces a specialized LarctanKAN module for final activity classification. Across eight public HAR datasets, the hybrid KAN-MLP model achieves an average macro F1 score relative improvement of 5.33\% compared pure-MLP model, significantly outperforming standalone KAN and MLP baselines. Furthermore, integrating this hybrid strategy into other state-of-the-art HAR architectures consistently boosts their performance. Our findings demonstrate that a carefully orchestrated combination of KAN, MLP, or other conventional neural components yields more robust and accurate HAR models for real-world wearable sensing environments.

</details>


### [32] [AQuaUI: Visual Token Reduction for GUI Agents with Adaptive Quadtrees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19260)
*Yuankai Li,Tinghui Zhu,Ha Min Son,Zhe Zhao,Xin Liu,Muhao Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出AquaUI，一种无需训练的推理时token压缩方法，通过自适应四叉树算法利用GUI截图的空间冗余特性，在保持99.06%性能的同时减少29.52%的视觉token并加速13.22%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大型多模态模型(LMMs)作为GUI智能体骨干时，GUI截图存在空间信息密度高度不均匀的问题：大片区域信息量少且视觉同质化，而关键文本和图标需要高保真度。现有方法要么需要额外训练，要么依赖基于注意力的token压缩，忽略了GUI截图的结构化布局和空间冗余。

Method: 提出AquaUI方法：1) 在每个截图输入上构建自适应四叉树，每个叶节点保留一个代表性合并token；2) 在整个流程中保留被保留token的空间位置，确保位置编码阶段一致性；3) 提出条件四叉树算法，利用单次请求中连续截图间的连续性，使用先前四叉树作为参考来细化当前四叉树，在静态或轻微偏移的GUI状态间保持细粒度区域。

Result: 在先进GUI智能体模型上实施AquaUI并在标准定位和导航基准上实验。在GUI-Owl-1.5-32B-Instruct模型上实现高达13.22%加速和29.52%视觉token减少，同时保留99.06%的全token性能，在精度-效率权衡上持续优于先前基线。

Conclusion: GUI截图的空间冗余可以在推理时无需重新训练的情况下被有效利用，AquaUI证明了无需训练的推理时token压缩方法在GUI智能体模型中的可行性和有效性。

Abstract: Large Multimodal Models (LMMs) have recently emerged as promising backbones for GUI-agent models, where high-resolution GUI screenshots are introduced to the prompts at each iteration step. However, these screenshots exhibit highly non-uniform spatial information density: large regions may carry little information and are visually homogeneous, while key text and icons may require high visual fidelity. Existing approaches to this problem either require additional training or rely on attention-based token compression, ignoring the structured layout and spatial redundancy of GUI screenshots. To fill the gap, this paper proposes AquaUI, a training-free inference-time token reduction method for GUI agent models that utilizes the non-uniform information density in screenshots. AQuaUI constructs an adaptive quadtree on each screenshot input and keeps one representative merged token per leaf of the quadtree. AQuaUI preserves the spatial positions of retained tokens throughout the pipeline to ensure that all position-encoding stages remain consistent. To further improve temporal consistency across multi-step GUI interactions, we propose a conditional quadtree algorithm that leverages the continuity between consecutive screenshots within a single request. Specifically, it refines the current quadtree using previous quadtrees as references, helping preserve fine-grained regions across static or mildly shifted GUI states. We implement AQuaUI on state-of-the-art GUI agent models and conduct experiments on standard grounding and navigational benchmarks. AQuaUI consistently shows improved accuracy-efficiency trade-offs over prior baselines. Notably, on GUI-Owl-1.5-32B-Instruct, AQuaUI achieves up to 13.22% speedup and 29.52% fewer visual tokens while retaining 99.06% of full-token performance, suggesting that the spatial redundancy of GUI screenshots can be exploited at inference without retraining.

</details>


### [33] [Trustworthy Agent Network: Trust in Agent Networks Must Be Baked In, Not Bolted On](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19035)
*Yixiang Yao,Yuhang Yao,Xinyi Fan,Jiechao Gao,Jie Wang,Minjia Zhang,Srivatsan Ravi,Carlee Joe-Wong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个针对A2A（Agent-to-Agent）网络的信任架构概念框架，指出其信任问题需在协调框架设计之初就进行架构，而非依赖于现有针对单智能体的对齐技术。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型（LLM）驱动的自主智能体从独立运作转向协作生态系统，形成了A2A网络。虽然这种网络在复杂多步任务中表现优于单一智能体，但也引入了对抗性组合、语义错位和级联操作失败等系统性漏洞，而现有的智能体对齐技术无法解决这些问题。

Method: 本文作为一篇愿景论文，主张A2A网络的信任保障不能通过在现有协议上打补丁实现，而必须从A2A协调框架设计的初始阶段就进行架构。作者提出了一个包含四大设计支柱的综合概念框架，将信任机制内嵌于A2A系统的核心设计中。

Result: 提出了一个全面的A2A网络信任概念框架，该框架围绕四个关键设计支柱构建，旨在从底层设计层面增强A2A网络的可信度，为未来研究提供了明确的理论基础和方向指引。

Conclusion: A2A网络的信任问题必须从系统架构设计的源头进行解决。通过所提出的四大支柱框架，可以为构建可信赖的A2A系统提供指导，确保智能体协作的安全性和可靠性。

Abstract: The rapid advancement of Large Language Models has given rise to autonomous LLM-based agents capable of complex reasoning and execution. As these agents transition from isolated operation to collaborative ecosystems, we witness the emergence of the Agent-to-Agent (A2A) network, a paradigm where heterogeneous agents autonomously coordinate to solve multi-step tasks. While these networks may offer better task performance compared to simply using one agent to complete the entire task, they introduce systemic vulnerabilities, such as adversarial composition, semantic misalignment, and cascading operational failures, that existing agent alignment techniques cannot address. In this vision paper, we argue that the trustworthiness of A2A networks cannot be fully guaranteed via retrofitting on existing protocols that are largely designed for individual agents. Rather, it must be architected from the very beginning of the A2A coordination framework. We present a comprehensive conceptual framework that situates trust in A2A systems through four design pillars.

</details>


### [34] [Swimming with Whales: Analysis of Power Imbalances in Stake-Weighted Governance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19264)
*Yuzhe Zhang,Manvir Schneider,Qin Wang,Davide Grossi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了权益证明(PoS)区块链中权益加权投票机制存在的权力失衡问题。通过计算社会选择理论视角，利用Penrose-Banzhaf权力指数量化投票权，发现虽然权力与相对权益的完美对齐通常无法实现，但在特定条件下可以近似达到期望意义上的对齐。基于Project Catalyst的实际链上治理数据分析，揭示了当前权益加权治理系统中可能出现的权力失衡细节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 权益证明区块链中广泛采用的权益加权投票方法存在根本性的治理缺陷：少数持有大量权益的用户可能完全控制决策过程，即使他们并不拥有总权益的全部。这种权力扭曲现象威胁了区块链治理的去中心化本质和公平性，亟需从理论和实证角度深入理解权力失衡的程度和机制，为改进治理范式提供科学依据。

Method: 采用计算社会选择理论框架，使用Penrose-Banzhaf权力指数作为量化投票权的核心工具。研究包含两部分：(1)理论分析：推导权益分配与实际投票权力之间的数学关系，证明在特定条件下期望意义上可以实现权力与权益的近似对齐；(2)实证研究：基于Project Catalyst这一真实链上治理系统的历史数据，计算并分析实际投票场景中的权力分布，验证理论发现并揭示现实系统中的权力失衡特征。

Result: 理论贡献：证明了权力与相对权益所有权之间的完美对齐在一般情况下无法实现，但在特定条件下可以在期望意义上实现近似对齐。实证发现：通过Project Catalyst数据揭示了当前权益加权治理系统中权力失衡的具体模式和程度，提供了对少数大户如何影响整体决策过程的细粒度理解，量化了实际治理场景中的权力集中程度。

Conclusion: 权益加权投票机制存在内在的权力失衡问题，完全的公平对齐在理论上不可行。然而，通过合理设计系统参数，可以在统计期望层面实现权益与权力的近似匹配。研究为PoS区块链治理提供了量化评估框架，揭示了当前系统的脆弱性，为未来设计更公平、去中心化的治理机制提供了理论基础和实证参考。研究结果表明需要重新审视权益加权作为治理范式的合理性，可能需要引入额外的制衡机制来防止权力过度集中。

Abstract: Voting methods weighted by stakes are the fundamental governance paradigm in Proof-of-Stake (PoS) blockchains. Such a paradigm is known to be prone to power distortions: a few users possessing large stakes may completely control decision making, even without owning the totality of the stakes. We study this phenomenon through the lens of computational social choice, focusing on the extent of power imbalances in stake-weighted voting when power is quantified using the Penrose-Banzhaf power index. Our work presents both analytical and empirical contributions. Analytically, we demonstrate that while a perfect alignment between power and relative stake ownership is generally unattainable, it can be approximated in expectation under specific conditions. Empirically, using data from a real-world on-chain governance system (Project Catalyst), we provide a more fine-grained understanding of the power imbalances that are likely to occur in current stake-weighted governance systems.

</details>


### [35] [Interference-Aware Multi-Task Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19042)
*Ying-Hua Huang,Rui Fang,Hsi-Wen Chen,Ming-Syan Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对多任务学习场景下的机器遗忘问题，提出了干扰感知框架，通过任务感知梯度投影和实例级梯度正交化来减少任务间和实例间的干扰，在两个多任务视觉基准上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器遗忘研究主要集中在单任务设置，而现代模型通常采用多任务共享骨干网络架构。在这种架构下，移除某个任务或实例的监督信息可能意外影响其他任务，造成非目标任务的性能下降。共享参数导致遗忘集和保留集相互耦合，引发任务级和实例级干扰问题。

Method: 提出干扰感知框架，结合两个关键组件：（1）任务感知梯度投影：将更新约束在任务特定子空间内，减少对非目标任务的影响；（2）实例级梯度正交化：减少遗忘信号和保留信号之间的冲突。该框架支持两种设置：全任务遗忘（从所有任务中移除目标实例）和部分任务遗忘（仅从选定任务中移除监督信息）。

Result: 在两个多任务计算机视觉基准数据集上进行实验，涵盖五个任务。相比最强基线方法，UIS指标在全任务遗忘设置下降低30.3%，在部分任务遗忘设置下降低52.9%，同时保持良好的泛化性能。

Conclusion: 所提出的干扰感知框架有效解决了多任务学习中的机器遗忘问题，能够实现高效遗忘的同时保持对剩余数据的强泛化能力，为实际应用中的多任务模型遗忘提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Machine unlearning aims to remove the contribution of designated training data from a trained model while preserving performance on the remaining data. Existing work mainly focuses on single-task settings, whereas modern models often operate in multi-task setups with shared backbones, where removing supervision for one task or instance can unintentionally affect others. We introduce multi-task unlearning with two settings: full-task unlearning, which removes a target instance from all tasks, and partial-task unlearning, which removes supervision only from selected tasks. We show that shared parameters couple the forget and retain sets, causing task-level interference on non-target tasks and instance-level interference on other instances. To address this issue, we propose an interference-aware framework that combines task-aware gradient projection, which constrains updates within task-specific subspaces, with instance-level gradient orthogonalization, which reduces conflicts between forget and retain signals. Experiments on two multi-task computer vision benchmarks across five tasks show that our method achieves effective unlearning while maintaining strong generalization, reducing UIS compared with the strongest baseline by 30.3% in full-task unlearning and 52.9% in partial-task unlearning.

</details>


### [36] [EngiAI: A Multi-Agent Framework and Benchmark Suite for LLM-Driven Engineering Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19743)
*Gioele Molinari,Florian Felten,Soheyl Massoudi,Mark Fuge*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个针对工程设计多智能体系统的基准测试套件，包含工作流、RAG和HPC三个评估维度，并实现了名为EngiAI的参考系统。实验表明专有模型任务完成率达96-97%，而开源4B模型为55-78%，条件分支任务最具挑战性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估框架无法充分解决结合仿真、检索和制造准备的多智能体系统评估问题，缺乏针对工程设计任务的综合性基准测试。

Method: 构建三维度基准测试套件：（1）包含7种提示风格的工作流基准，针对不同认知需求；（2）带门控评分的RAG基准，隔离检索对参数选择的贡献；（3）评估SLURM集群上端到端ML训练编排的HPC基准。同时实现基于LangGraph的EngiAI多智能体系统作为参考实现。

Result: 在4个LLM后端和2个EngiBench问题上的测试显示：专有模型在Beams2D上达到96-97%平均任务完成率，开源4B参数模型达到55-78%。条件分支最具挑战性，在Photonics2D上任务完成率降至20-53%。RAG门控验证了检索增强评分接近完美（约1.0）。HPC编排中，不同模型完成率从50%到100%不等，揭示了长时间工作流中多步指令跟随能力的退化。

Conclusion: 该基准测试套件有效揭示了不同LLM在工程设计多智能体任务中的性能差异，特别是在条件分支和长时间工作流中的局限性，为LLM智能体在工程设计领域的评估提供了标准化框架。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) agents are increasingly applied to engineering design tasks, yet existing evaluation frameworks do not adequately address multi-agent systems that combine simulation, retrieval, and manufacturing preparation. We introduce a benchmark suite with three evaluation dimensions: (1) a workflow benchmark with seven prompt styles targeting distinct cognitive demands-including direct tool use, semantic disambiguation, conditional branching, and working-memory tasks; (2) a Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) benchmark with gated scoring isolating retrieval contributions to parameter selection; and (3) an High Performance Computing (HPC) benchmark evaluating end-to-end ML training orchestration on a SLURM cluster. Alongside the benchmark we present EngiAI, a Multi-Agent System (MAS) reference implementation built on LangGraph that operationalizes the benchmark by coordinating seven specialized agents through a supervisor architecture, unifying topology optimization, document retrieval, HPC job orchestration, and 3D printer control. Across four LLM backends and two EngiBench problems, proprietary models achieve 96-97% average task completion on Beams2D, while open-source 4B-parameter models reach 55-78%, with clear generational improvement. Conditional branching proves most challenging, with task completion dropping to 20-53% for the conditional style on Photonics2D. RAG gating confirms near-perfect retrieval-augmented scores ($\approx 1.0$) versus near-zero without retrieval, validating the evaluation design. On HPC orchestration, one model completes all pipeline steps in 100% of runs while another drops to 50%, revealing that multi-step instruction following degrades over long-running workflows.

</details>


### [37] [Projecting Latent RL Actions: Towards Generalizable and Scalable Graph Combinatorial Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19721)
*Franco Terranova,Guillermo Bernardez,Albert Cabellos-Aparicio,Nina Miolane,Abdelkader Lahmadi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为“投影代理（projection agents）”的新型强化学习方法，用于解决图组合优化（GCO）问题，在推理速度上实现了高达16.2倍的提升，在泛化能力上提升了40%，并发布了支持复现的Python库LaGCO-RL。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管强化学习（RL）与图神经网络（GNNs）的结合已显著提升了学习型GCO求解器的性能，但现有方法在面对多样化图实例时的泛化能力不足，且随着动作空间增大面临计算可扩展性的瓶颈。作者旨在解决这两个核心挑战。

Method: 作者引入了“投影代理”，该方法直接在连续的GNN动作嵌入空间中运行。模型通过单次前向传递预测潜在的期望动作，随后将其解码为有效的离散动作。此外，作者建立了一个共享的观测与动作嵌入空间，以确保不同RL方法间的公平比较。

Result: 在多种基准测试中，该方法仅使用简单的最近邻解码，就实现了比现有解决方案快16.2倍的推理速度，泛化性能提升高达40%。该方法也为具有多个相互依赖变量的超线性决策空间中的强RL性能开辟了道路。作者还发布了LaGCO-RL Python库。

Conclusion: 通过在连续潜动作空间中直接操作并配合解码机制，投影代理有效克服了现有RL-GCO方法在泛化性与可扩展性方面的局限，为复杂的组合优化问题提供了一种高效、通用的解决方案。

Abstract: Graph combinatorial optimization (GCO) has attracted growing interest, as many NP-hard problems naturally admit graph formulations, yet their combinatorial explosion renders exact methods computationally intractable. Recent advances in Reinforcement Learning (RL) combined with Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) have significantly improved learning-based GCO solvers. However, existing approaches face limitations in both generalization across diverse graph instances and computational scalability as action spaces grow. To address both challenges, we introduce projection agents, a novel RL-GCO approach that operates directly in a continuous GNN-based action embedding space, predicting a desired latent action in a single forward pass and subsequently decoding it into a valid discrete action. Additionally, we enable fair comparison across RL methods through a shared embedding space for both observations and actions. Across diverse benchmarks, our approach achieves up to 16.2x faster inference and up to 40% better generalization than existing solutions using only simple nearest-neighbor decoding, while opening the door to strong RL performance in super-linear decision spaces with multiple interdependent variables. Finally, we release LaGCO-RL, a Python library that automates latent action-space construction and supports existing RL-GCO solutions, promoting reproducibility and adaptation to new GCO benchmarks.

</details>


### [38] [Embedding by Elicitation: Dynamic Representations for Bayesian Optimization of System Prompts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19093)
*Zhiyuan Jerry Lin,Benjamin Letham,Samuel Dooley,Maximilian Balandat,Eytan Bakshy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了ReElicit框架，这是一种基于“通过诱导嵌入”的贝叶斯优化方法，用于在仅有聚合反馈（标量分数）的情况下优化系统提示词，且在预算受限的黑盒优化场景中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系统提示词是控制现代AI系统行为的关键机制，但在仅有聚合指标而非逐例标签或批评反馈的情况下，对其进行调优极具挑战性。现有的优化方法难以处理这种样本受限的离散文本黑盒优化问题。

Method: 引入ReElicit框架，利用LLM根据任务描述和历史评分数据诱导出一个紧凑、可解释的特征空间，并将提示词映射其中；结合高斯过程代理模型和采集函数选择目标特征向量，再由LLM将其转化为可部署的系统提示词；随着新评估结果的到来，动态重新诱导特征空间以适应观察历史。

Result: 在10个系统提示词优化任务、总评估预算为30次的设定下，ReElicit在仅有聚合反馈的提示词优化基线中取得了最强的聚合性能表现。

Abstract: System prompts are a central control mechanism in modern AI systems, shaping behavior across conversations, tasks, and user populations. Yet they are difficult to tune when feedback is available only as aggregate metrics rather than per-example labels, failures, or critiques. We study this aggregate feedback setting as sample-constrained black-box optimization over discrete, variable-length text. We introduce ReElicit, a Bayesian optimization framework based on \emph{embedding by elicitation}. Given a task description, previously evaluated prompts, and scalar scores, an LLM elicits a compact, interpretable feature space and maps prompts into it. Leveraging a probabilistic Gaussian process surrogate, an acquisition function then selects target feature vectors, which the LLM realizes and refines into deployable system prompts. Re-eliciting the feature space as new evaluations arrive lets the representation adapt to the observed prompt-score history. We evaluate the setting using offline benchmark accuracy as a controlled aggregate proxy: the optimizer observes one scalar score per prompt and no per-example labels, errors, or critiques. Across ten system prompt optimization tasks with a 30 total evaluation budget, ReElicit achieves the strongest aggregate performance profile among representative aggregate-only prompt-optimization baselines. These results suggest that LLMs can serve as adaptive semantic representation builders, not only prompt generators, for Bayesian optimization over natural-language artifacts.

</details>

Abstract: Automatic generation of computer-aided design (CAD) models is a core technology for enabling intelligence in advanced manufacturing. Existing generation methods based on large language models (LLMs) often fall short when handling complex CAD models characterized by long operation sequences, diverse operation types, and strong geometric constraints, primarily because reasoning chains break and effective error-correction mechanisms are lacking. To address this problem, this paper proposes a memory-augmented reinforcement learning framework for CAD generation agents. The framework encapsulates the underlying geometric kernel into a structured toolchain callable by the agent and builds a closed-loop mechanism of design intent understanding, global planning, execution, and multi-dimensional verification. It also designs a dual-track memory module consisting of a case library and a skill library, and proposes a dynamic utility retrieval algorithm. By introducing reinforcement learning into retrieval and policy optimization, the agent can effectively avoid retrieval traps in which examples are semantically similar but geometrically infeasible, enabling online self-correction and continual evolution without additional large-scale annotated data. Experiments show that the proposed method significantly improves both the success rate and geometric consistency on complex CAD model generation tasks.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM代理越来越多地访问用户隐私数据并代表用户与第三方系统交互，确保代理能够遵循用户定义的隐私策略（即什么可以共享、什么不能共享）变得至关重要，尤其是在第三方系统进行对抗性探测的情况下。

Method: 引入POLAR-Bench基准测试框架：受信任的模型（携带隐私策略和任务）与对抗性的第三方模型进行对话，后者会探测任务相关信息和受保护属性。覆盖10个领域和7,852个样本，使用确定性集合成员关系评分隐私性和实用性，沿两个正交轴（隐私策略维度和攻击策略）变化，为每个模型生成5×5的诊断面。

Result: 发现模型表现存在明显分化：当前前沿模型能够拒绝超过99%的受保护属性泄露；而用户最常在设备端或通过私有推理运行的1-30B参数范围内的较小开源模型表现明显较差，其中最弱的模型泄露了超过一半的隐私信息。

Conclusion: POLAR-Bench能够精准定位每个模型在意图遵循方面的失效点，为在最关键的地方进行隐私对齐提供了切入点。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly have access to private user data and act on the user's behalf when interacting with third-party systems. The user defines what may and must not be shared, and the agent must robustly follow that intent even when third-party systems behave adversarially. We introduce POLAR-Bench (Policy-aware adversarial Benchmark), in which a trusted model with a privacy policy and a task converses with a third-party model that adversarially probes for both task-relevant and protected attributes. Across 10 domains and 7,852 samples, we score privacy and utility by deterministic set-membership and vary privacy policy dimension and attack strategy along two orthogonal axes, producing a 5 times 5 diagnostic surface per model. Our results reveal a sharp split: current frontier models withhold over 99% of protected attributes, while smaller open-weight models in the 1--30B range, the class users most commonly run as their own trusted agent on-device or via private inference, score notably worse, with the weakest leaking over half. POLAR-Bench thus localizes where each model's intent-following breaks down, providing a foothold for privacy alignment where it matters most.

</details>

*Samuel Jacob Chacko,James Hugglestone,Chashi Mahiul Islam,Xiuwen Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究发现Agent Skills的效果取决于环境反馈带宽：在具有严格、低延迟反馈的环境中，Skills的边际效益显著降低甚至产生负面影响，挑战了Skills普遍有效的假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究表明Agent Skills平均提升任务通过率16.2个百分点，但在84个任务中有16个出现负面效果，社区尚未明确解释Skills何时有效、何时冗余，需要揭示其作用的边界条件。

Result: 在进攻性网络安全领域，Skills的边际效益崩溃：无技能与全技能条件之间的差距仅为8.9个百分点(p=0.71, χ²检验; p=0.25, Cochran-Armitage趋势检验; 六个成对比较中的五个Cohen's h值低于0.2小效应阈值)。关键发现是缺失变量为"环境反馈带宽"——当工具层返回严格、模式验证、低延迟的观察时，环境本身提供了Skills通常需要提供的程序纠正信号。

Conclusion: 提出了可证伪假设：在具有高带宽环境反馈的场景中，精选Skills的边际效益大幅降低，某些情况下（如时序侧信道设置）甚至会降低性能。该发现对复合AI系统的设计具有重要启示，研究者将发布重新分析流程以支持复现。

Abstract: Agent Skills, structured packages of procedural knowledge loaded into an LLM agent at inference time, are widely reported to improve task pass rates by an average of 16.2~percentage points across diverse domains. Yet the same benchmarks show wide variance, with 16 of 84 tasks suffering negative deltas when Skills are introduced. The community has not yet articulated a clean mechanism for \emph{when} Skills help and when they are merely redundant overhead. We re-analyze a recently published 180-run controlled study of an MCP-grounded autonomous Capture-the-Flag (CTF) agent under four documentation conditions of increasing richness (55, 1{,}478, 1{,}976, and 4{,}147 lines), and show that these conditions correspond almost exactly to a No-Skills, Experiential-Skills, Curated-Skills, and Comprehensive-Skills ablation. In offensive cybersecurity, a domain not deeply covered by existing Skills benchmarks, the marginal benefit of Skills collapses. The spread between the no-Skills and full-Skills conditions is only 8.9~pp ($p = 0.71$, $χ^2$; $p = 0.25$, Cochran--Armitage trend test; five of six pairwise Cohen's $h$ values fall below the $0.2$ small-effect threshold). We argue that the missing variable is \emph{environment-feedback bandwidth}. When an agent's tool layer returns strict, schema-validated, low-latency observations, the environment itself supplies the procedural correction signal that Skills are normally needed to provide. As a result, the marginal benefit of curated Skills diminishes substantially, and, in some cases (e.g., our timing side-channel setting), actively degrades performance. We articulate a falsifiable hypothesis, sketch its design implications for compound AI systems, and will release the reanalysis pipeline to support replication.

</details>

*Jiayu Li,Enpei Zhang,Dawei Zhou,Elynn Chen,Yujun Yan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 首次为跨组织边界、去中心化部分可观测的多智能体LLM流水线提出IC-SMDP形式化框架与IC-Q算法，并给出首个神经网络Q学习的有限样本保证，实验验证其性能可匹配集中式Oracle。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究跨组织、供应商或信任边界的多智能体LLM流水线场景，其中专用智能体通过共享工件交接控制权，每个智能体仅观测局部信息且无集中式学习者访问联合轨迹。这种去中心化部分可观测的实际设定在先前研究中缺乏理论基础与保障。

Method: 1. 形式化为接口约束半马尔可夫决策过程（IC-SMDP），决策时刻发生在交接时间点；2. 设计IC-Q异步去中心化Q学习算法，每次交接时的跨智能体协调仅需一个标量；3. 将近似信息状态（AIS）框架从单智能体原语步MDP提升至多智能体SMDP，并在随机时长折扣下控制马尔可夫噪声。

Result: 为神经IC-Q建立了有限样本界，该界可分解为三个独立可控误差源：神经函数逼近误差、接口表示间隙和混合时间残差。四个实验（合成IC-SMDP验证、多LLM数学推理、多智能体路由、多智能体CPU编程）表明IC-Q在无智能体观测联合轨迹的情况下匹配集中式Oracle性能，三个误差源按界预测的方式沿各自轴缩放。

Conclusion: 这是首个在去中心化部分可观测条件下神经Q学习的有限样本保证，填补了多智能体LLM流水线理论空白，为跨边界协作系统提供了理论支撑与实用算法，实验充分验证了理论界的正确性与算法有效性。

Abstract: We study workflow learning in a setting where specialized agents hand off control through a shared artifact, each agent observes only a local function of that artifact and its own private state, and no centralized learner accesses joint trajectories -- the operating regime of multi-agent LLM pipelines that span organizational, vendor, or trust boundaries. We formalize this regime as an interface-constrained semi-Markov decision process (IC-SMDP), whose decision epochs occur at handoff times, and design IC-$Q$, an asynchronous decentralized $Q$-learning algorithm in which cross-agent coordination at every handoff is exactly one scalar. Our main result is a finite-sample bound for neural IC-$Q$ that decomposes into three independently controllable error sources: neural function-approximation error, interface representation gap, and a mixing-time residual, under the random option-duration discount. Establishing this bound requires lifting the approximate information state (AIS) framework from single-agent primitive-step MDPs to multi-agent SMDPs and controlling Markovian noise under random duration, neither of which has been done in prior work. To our knowledge this is the first finite-sample guarantee for neural $Q$-learning under decentralized partial observability. Four experiments: a controlled synthetic IC-SMDP that validates the bound term-by-term, multi-LLM mathematical reasoning, multi-agent routing, and multi-agent CPU programming, show that IC-$Q$ matches a centralized oracle without any agent observing joint trajectories, with each of the three error sources scaling along its corresponding axis as the bound predicts.

</details>

*Changkun Ou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文将智能体工具使用的信任校准问题形式化为偏好学习问题，提出了一种基于高斯过程后验和偏好贝叶斯优化的策略网关方法，用于决定智能体行为应自主执行还是需要人类批准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着自动化智能体越来越多地执行复杂任务，需要建立机制来校准人类对智能体行为的信任程度，即决定何时允许智能体自主执行操作，何时需要人类审批，以确保安全性和可控性。

Method: 采用策略网关方法，维护人类风险容忍度潜在函数的高斯过程后验分布，通过二元批准/拒绝反馈的概率似然函数进行观测，并使用近似高斯过程分类作为推断机制。该方法结构上属于偏好贝叶斯优化，在最具不确定性的区域进行查询和升级。

Result: 证明了该方法在结构上是偏好贝叶斯优化的一个实例，继承了其样本效率优势（不确定性导向的查询策略），但目标不同：将动作空间分类为允许/阻止/询问区域，而非优化某个设计参数。

Conclusion: 该方法成功地将信任校准问题形式化为偏好学习框架，为智能体行为的安全控制提供了一种理论基础扎实且样本高效的方法，能够在保证安全的同时最大化智能体的自主性。

Abstract: We formalize trust calibration for agentic tool use (deciding when an automated agent's proposed action may execute autonomously versus require human approval) as a preference-learning problem. A policy gateway maintains a Gaussian-process posterior over a latent human risk-tolerance function, observed through a probit likelihood on binary approve/deny feedback, and escalates to the human exactly where the approval outcome is most uncertain. We show this is structurally an instance of Preferential Bayesian Optimization, inheriting its inference machinery (approximate Gaussian-process classification) and its sample-efficiency argument (uncertainty-targeted querying), while differing in objective: classifying an action space into allow/block/ask regions rather than optimizing a design.

</details>


### [44] [Discoverable Agent Knowledge -- A Formal Framework for Agentic KG Affordances (Extended Version)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19186)
*Terry R. Payne,Valentina Tamma,Enrico Daga*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文借鉴早期语义Web服务（如OWL-S和WSMO）的设计理念，针对现有知识图谱元数据标准（VoID、DCAT）无法描述代理认知能力的缺陷，提出了一个四维形式化框架，并据此定义了代理可供性档案（AAP），以支持代理在规划阶段对知识图谱的选择、组合与故障诊断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二十年前，语义Web服务社区已解决具有不同本体承诺的代理如何一致地发现、组合和调用服务的问题（如OWL-S和WSO）。然而，当前知识图谱（KG）的元数据标准（如VoID和DCAT）仅描述内容概览，未能说明特定代理能从中证明什么、空结果的闭包假设是什么，或任务词汇是否在模式中落地。此外，在实际部署的KG中，描述逻辑（DL）模式与实际的蕴涵机制可能不一致，这是一种现有元数据无法发现的认知失效模式。因此，有必要将这些早期洞见重新引入KG设置中。

Method: 本文提出了一个四维形式化框架，该框架明确了代理在知识图谱上的认知前提与能力边界。基于此框架，推导出了一个名为“代理可供性档案”（AAP）的语义层，该层建立在现有的VoID和DCAT标准之上，旨在形式化地弥合本体差异并描述KG的认知可供性。

Result: 研究定义了AAP，作为一种新的语义层，它使得在代理规划阶段进行有原则的知识图谱选择、组合以及失效诊断成为可能。同时，文章提出了一个五点研究议程，指出了大规模实现基于AAP的可供性匹配所需的形式化、计算及工程工作。

Conclusion: 当前KG元数据标准缺乏对代理认知状态的描述能力，导致潜在的认知失效。通过借鉴语义Web服务的成熟经验并构建AAP框架，可以填补这一空白，从而提升知识驱动型代理的鲁棒性与可靠性，未来的工作将聚焦于该框架的实现与规模化应用。

Abstract: Two decades ago, the Semantic Web Services community was asked how agents with different ontological commitments could discover, compose, and invoke web services coherently. The response was OWL-S and WSMO: formally grounded capability descriptions specifying what a service could do, what the agent must already know for invocation to be epistemically sound, and how ontological mismatches could be formally bridged. Current Knowledge Graph (KG) metadata standards such as VoID and DCAT describe what a KG contains yet say nothing about what a specific agent can prove from it, what closure assumptions govern empty results, or whether the agent's task vocabulary is grounded in the schema. Furthermore, in deployed KGs the governing schema DL and the operative entailment regime can diverge: an epistemic failure mode invisible to current metadata. We revisit and extend these insights for the KG setting with a four-dimensional formal framework from which we derive the Agentic Affordance Profile (AAP): a semantic layer above VoID and DCAT enabling principled KG selection, composition, and failure diagnosis at agent planning time. A five-point research agenda identifies the formal, computational, and engineering work needed to realise AAP-based affordance matching at scale.

</details>


### [45] [Hallucination as Exploit: Evidence-Carrying Multimodal Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19192)
*Guijia Zhang,Hao Zheng,Harry Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出证据携带多模态智能体(ECA)，通过外部验证器提供的类型化证书来授权工具调用，将不安全的行动率从100%降至0%，解决了多模态智能体中幻觉导致的授权失败问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态智能体在使用截图、文档和网页选择工具调用时，错误的视觉声明可能触发点击、邮件发送、数据提取或转账等特权操作，将幻觉问题从答案质量错误转变为授权失败。这种幻觉到行动的转换构成了严重的安全威胁：未经支持的感知声明提供了使特权行动看似被允许的前提条件。

Method: 提出证据携带多模态智能体(ECA)架构：(1)将每个工具调用分解为行动关键谓词；(2)从受约束的DOM/OCR/AX验证器获取类型化证书；(3)由确定性门控仅授予这些证书支持的特权。该架构将不透明的模型信念转换为命名的验证器、模式和实现残差，使模型语言可以提议行动，但必须由外部证据授权。

Result: 在1,900次攻击的红队测试中，四个针对性的加固步骤将门控绕过率从15%降至1.3%。在200任务端到端管道中实现0%不安全行动率(Wilson 95%置信上限2.67%)，在120任务浏览器概念验证中实现0%不安全行动率(上限4.3%)。对500个分层任务键的HACR审计显示，未经支持的行动关键声明在不安全执行中：朴素智能体100.0%，仅提示防御49.6%，ECA为0%。

Conclusion: 核心原则是：模型语言可以提议行动，但外部证据必须授权它们。ECA通过将感知错误从隐藏状态转换为可审计的验证器残差，为多模态智能体提供了可证明的安全保障，而神经网络判断基线在相同威胁模型下仍可被绕过。

Abstract: Multimodal agents use screenshots, documents, and webpages to choose tool calls. When a false visual claim triggers a click, email, extraction, or transfer, hallucination becomes an authorization failure rather than an answer-quality error. We formalize this failure mode as hallucination-to-action conversion: an unsupported perceptual claim supplies the precondition that makes a privileged action appear permitted. We propose evidence-carrying multimodal agents (ECA), which treat free-form model text as inadmissible evidence. ECA decomposes each tool call into action-critical predicates, obtains typed certificates from constrained DOM/OCR/AX verifiers, and lets a deterministic gate grant only the privileges those certificates support. The architecture does not hide perception error; it converts opaque model belief into named verifier, schema, and implementation residuals. Verifier red-teaming over 1,900 attacks exposes this residual directly: four targeted hardening steps reduce gate bypass from 15% to 1.3%. With content-derived certificates, ECA obtains 0% unsafe-action rate on a 200-task end-to-end pipeline (Wilson 95% upper bound 2.67%) and a 120-task browser proof-of-concept (upper bound 4.3%). A direct HACR audit on 500 stratified task keys shows that unsupported action-critical claims reach unsafe execution for naive agents (100.0%) and prompt-only defense (49.6%), but not for ECA. Oracle-certificate replay on 7,488 GPT-5.4 benchmark traces serves as a gate-correctness sanity check, and neural judge baselines remain bypassable under the same threat model. The resulting principle is simple: model language may propose actions, but external evidence must authorize them.

</details>


### [46] [Not all uncertainty is alike: volatility, stochasticity, and exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19215)
*Payam Piray*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现环境不确定性中的波动性和随机性对探索行为有相反的影响：波动性促进探索，而随机性抑制探索。作者提出了CAUSE算法，在异构噪声环境中表现优于传统探索策略，并揭示了病理噪声推断可能导致反向探索行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究虽然发现不确定性通常促进探索，但往往将环境不确定性的不同来源视为等价。实际上，环境不确定性有两个不同来源：潜在奖励状态随时间漂移（波动性）和通过噪声结果观察（随机性）。理解这两个来源如何差异化地影响探索行为对于生物和人工智能中的自适应决策至关重要。

Method: 1. 将Gittins指数框架扩展到具有潜在动态的高斯状态空间强盗问题，从理论上证明波动性和随机性对最优探索的相反影响。2. 通过控制即推理方法推导出封闭形式的探索奖励——CAUSE（Cause-Aware Uncertainty-Sensitive Exploration）。3. 在具有异构噪声结构的环境中验证CAUSE算法的性能。

Result: 1. 正式证明了波动性增强探索而随机性抑制探索的不对称性。2. CAUSE算法在异构噪声环境中优于标准探索策略。3. CAUSE也优于逐臂Gittins策略，后者的静态强盗最优性不能转移到动态设置中。4. 学习和探索受相同的噪声推断不对称性支配。

Conclusion: 该框架预测病态噪声推断会产生反向而非仅仅受损的探索行为，这对精神疾病的计算解释具有重要启示。研究结果表明，区分不同来源的不确定性对于理解和设计有效的探索策略至关重要。

Abstract: Adaptive decision-making in biological and artificial intelligence requires balancing the exploitation of known outcomes with the exploration of uncertain alternatives. Although prior work suggests that uncertainty generally promotes exploration, it has typically treated distinct sources of environmental uncertainty as equivalent. We consider environments with latent reward states that drift over time (volatility) and are observed through noisy outcomes (stochasticity). Both increase posterior uncertainty, yet we show they drive optimal exploration in opposite directions: volatility enhances it, stochasticity suppresses it. We establish this asymmetry formally by extending the Gittins index framework to Gaussian state-space bandits with latent dynamics. We further derive Cause-Aware Uncertainty-Sensitive Exploration (CAUSE), a closed-form exploration bonus obtained via control-as-inference that inherits the same monotonicities. CAUSE outperforms standard exploration strategies in environments with heterogeneous noise structure, and also improves on a Gittins-per-arm policy whose rested-bandit optimality does not transfer to restless settings. Learning and exploration are governed by the same noise-inference asymmetry, and the framework predicts that pathological noise inference produces \emph{reversed} rather than merely impaired exploration, with implications for computational accounts of psychiatric conditions.

</details>


### [47] [SimGym: A Framework for A/B Test Simulation in E-Commerce with Traffic-Grounded VLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19219)
*Han Li,Vibhor Malik,Zahra Zanjani Foumani,Alberto Castelo,Shuang Xie,Ailin Fan,Keat Yang Koay,Yuanzheng Zhu,Meysam Feghhi,Ronie Uliana,Zhaoyu Zhang,Angelo Ocana Martins,Mingyu Zhao,Francis Pelland,Jonathan Faerman,Nikolas LeBlanc,Aaron Glazer,Andrew McNamara,Zhong Wu,Lingyun Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了SimGym框架，利用视觉语言模型(VLM)代理在实时浏览器中模拟电商A/B测试，将实验周期从数周缩短至不足一小时，且与真实买家行为变化方向的一致性达77%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统A/B测试作为电商店面评估的金标准存在诸多局限：分流流量、需数周才能达到统计显著性、存在降低用户体验的风险。作者旨在解决这些痛点，实现无需真实用户参与的快速实验迭代。

Method: SimGym框架包含三个核心组件：(a)基于流量的画像生成流程，从生产环境点击流数据中提取各店铺的买家原型和意图；(b)实时浏览器代理架构，结合多模态感知（视觉和浏览器结构化观察）、情景记忆和护栏机制，在对照和处理店面间执行连贯的购物会话；(c)评估协议，对比模拟结果偏移与真实买家行为的观测偏移。

Result: 在大型电商平台跨多种店面和产品类别的视觉驱动UI主题变更A/B测试上验证了SimGym。实验结果表明，SimGym代理与观测到的结果偏移具有强一致性，在与真实买家流量中界面变体的加购行为变化方向上达到77%的对齐准确率。

Conclusion: SimGym成功将实验周期从数周缩短至一小时以内，实现了无需向真实买家暴露候选变体的快速实验，为电商店面优化提供了一种高效的A/B测试替代方案。

Abstract: A/B testing remains the gold standard for evaluating modifications to e-commerce storefronts, yet it diverts traffic, requires weeks to reach statistical significance, and risks degrading user experience. We present SimGym, a framework for simulating A/B tests on e-commerce storefronts using vision-language model (VLM) agents operating in a live browser. The framework comprises three key components: (a) a traffic-grounded persona generation pipeline that derives per-shop buyer archetypes and intents from production clickstream data; (b) a live-browser agent architecture that combines multimodal perception over visual and browser-structured observations with episodic memory and guardrails to conduct coherent shopping sessions across control and treatment storefronts; and (c) an evaluation protocol that compares simulated outcome shifts with observed shifts in real buyer behavior. We validate SimGym on A/B tests of visually driven UI theme changes from a major e-commerce platform across diverse storefronts and product categories. Empirical results show that SimGym agents achieve strong agreement with observed outcome shifts, attaining 77% directional alignment with add-to-cart shifts observed across interface variants in real-buyer traffic. It reduces experimental cycles from weeks to under an hour, enabling rapid experimentation without exposing real buyers to candidate variants.

</details>


### [48] [Can Large Language Models Revolutionize Survey Research? Experiments with Disaster Preparedness Responses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19229)
*Yan Wang,Ziyi Guo,Christopher McCarty*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出并评估了一个五阶段的LLM整合框架，用于改进调查研究全流程，特别是在灾难背景下。提出的A-TLM方法在MNAR缺失数据填补任务上优于传统方法，同时发现聚合层面的近零偏差可能掩盖子群体层面的反向误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 调查研究面临日益严重的结构性挑战：响应率下降、样本偏差、高风险受访者的块状缺失，以及在线调查中AI辅助的欺诈完成问题。尽管已有研究提出使用大语言模型作为解决方案，但缺乏对完整调查流程的严格评估，特别是在数据质量至关重要的灾难研究情境中。

Method: 研究提出并评估了一个五阶段LLM整合框架（问卷设计、样本选择、预测试、缺失数据填补、后采集分析），以2024年佛罗里达居民飓风米尔顿准备情况调查（n=946）为实证基础。引入了保护动机理论（PMT）约束的共现知识图谱，开发了七种LLM配置（包括零样本推理、检索增强基线和理论指导变体），并提出锚定边际理论指导LLM（A-TLM）方法。

Result: A-TLM在灾难相关的块状MNAR条件下表现最优（S4 RMSE为1.439，优于次优方法的1.496），同时实现了接近零的有符号偏差（-0.121），而随机森林填补器产生最大绝对偏差（-0.631）。围绕PMT因果结构组织检索并在单次模型调用中整合所有证据，优于非结构化检索和分阶段序列推理（MAE 0.993 vs 1.097）。研究发现聚合层面的近零偏差可能掩盖子群体层面的反向误差。

Conclusion: 研究证明了LLM在调查研究全流程中的有效性，特别是在灾难情境下的缺失数据填补方面。提出子群体分层偏差审计应成为报告标准，并证明通过检索约束的知识图谱聊天机器人可以在架构上管控幻觉问题。

Abstract: Survey research faces mounting structural challenges: declining response rates, sample bias, block-wise missingness among at-risk respondents, and AI-assisted fraudulent completions in online panels. Large language models (LLMs) have been proposed as a remedy, yet rigorous evaluations across the full survey workflow remain scarce, particularly in disaster contexts where data quality matters most. We present and evaluate a five-stage framework for LLM integration covering questionnaire design, sample selection, pilot testing, missing-data imputation, and post-collection analysis, using the 2024 Hurricane Milton preparedness survey of Florida residents (n=946) as a shared empirical testbed. We introduce a Protection Motivation Theory (PMT)-constrained co-occurrence knowledge graph and develop seven LLM configurations spanning zero-shot inference, retrieval-augmented baselines, and novel theory-informed variants. Our proposed Anchored Marginal Theory-Informed LLM (A-TLM) outperforms all three classical imputation baselines (IPW/MI, MICE+PMM, missForest) on RMSE under disaster-relevant block-wise MNAR conditions (S4 RMSE 1.439 vs. 1.496 for the next-best), while achieving near-zero signed bias (-0.121) where the random-forest imputer produces the largest absolute bias (-0.631). Organizing retrieval around PMT causal structure and integrating all evidence in a single model call outperforms unstructured retrieval and staged sequential inference (MAE 0.993 vs. 1.097 for standard RAG). We document that near-zero aggregate bias can mask opposing subgroup errors and propose subgroup-stratified bias auditing as a reporting standard. A retrieval-constrained knowledge-graph chatbot demonstrates that hallucination is architecturally manageable through grounded refusal.

</details>


### [49] [Towards Multi-Model LLM Schedulers: Empirical Insights into Offloading and Preemption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19593)
*Mert Yildiz,Pietro Spadaccino,Alexey Rolich,Francesca Cuomo,Andrea Baiocchi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对共享异构硬件上的多LLM部署场景，实证研究了层卸载和抢占对性能的影响，发现吞吐量退化呈非线性且依赖模型特性，抢占开销主要由模型状态重载主导，为未来调度系统设计提供了关键特征指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代LLM部署日益需要在共享异构硬件上服务多个具有不同架构、规模和专长的模型。在GPU内存受限条件下，需要部分CPU-GPU卸载和抢占，这为资源分配、调度带来了新挑战。而现有系统主要优化单一模型吞吐量，缺乏对多模型调度问题的研究。

Method: 对多个LLM在不同硬件平台上的行为进行实证研究，重点分析层卸载（layer offloading）和抢占（preemption）的性能影响。通过系统实验测量解码吞吐量退化、抢占开销组成（模型状态重载vs KV缓存传输），并考察序列长度和互连带宽的作用。

Result: （1）卸载导致解码吞吐量出现强烈的非线性且依赖模型的退化，较小模型对GPU驻留率降低更敏感；（2）抢占产生显著开销，主要由模型状态重载而非KV缓存传输主导，且该成本在不同模型和硬件平台间差异显著；（3）序列长度和互连带宽会放大数据移动和执行低效问题。

Conclusion: 识别出未来调度器必须考虑的关键特征：模型特定的卸载敏感性、工作负载特性、抢占和数据传输的成本结构。这些见解为设计能够高效管理异构多模型工作负载的下一代LLM服务系统提供了指导。

Abstract: Modern deployments of Large Language Models (LLMs) increasingly require serving multiple models with diverse architectures, sizes, and specialization on shared, heterogeneous hardware. This setting introduces new challenges for resource allocation, dispatching, and scheduling, particularly under GPU memory constraints where partial CPU-GPU offloading and preemption become necessary. While existing systems primarily optimize throughput for a single model, comparatively little work addresses multi-model scheduling under these conditions. In this paper, we present an empirical study of how different LLMs behave across hardware platforms, focusing on the performance implications of layer offloading and preemption. We show that offloading leads to strongly non-linear and model-dependent degradation in decode throughput, with smaller models exhibiting sharper sensitivity to reduced GPU residency. We further demonstrate that preemption incurs substantial overhead, largely dominated by model state reload rather than key-value cache transfer, and that this cost varies significantly across models and hardware platforms. Additionally, we highlight the role of sequence length and interconnect bandwidth in amplifying data movement and execution inefficiencies. Based on these findings, we identify a set of key features that future schedulers must consider, including model-specific offloading sensitivity, workload characteristics, and the cost structure of preemption and data transfer. These insights provide guidance for the design of next-generation LLM serving systems capable of efficiently managing heterogeneous, multi-model workloads with hybrid CPU-GPU execution.

</details>


### [50] [Causal Evidence for Attention Head Imbalance in Modality Conflict Hallucination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19250)
*Jinrui Jiang,Zhangtai Wu,Zhen Wu,Xinyu Dai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过机制性分析识别出导致多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）模态冲突幻觉的两类注意力头：分布广泛的幻觉驱动头与高度集中的幻觉抵抗头，并提出MACI方法在检测到冲突时有选择地抑制驱动头，从而在MMMC基准上实现显著幻觉降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型存在模态冲突幻觉问题，即模型优先采用错误的文本前提而忽略相互矛盾的视觉证据。为了理解为何视觉证据在生成过程中无法占优，作者从机制性视角出发，旨在找出驱动或抵抗这一失败模式的内部组件。

Method: 对五个开源MLLMs进行头部层面的因果分析，采用路径修补技术识别两类具有对立因果作用的注意力头：幻觉驱动头与幻觉抵抗头。在此基础上，提出模态冲突感知因果干预方法（MACI），仅在检测到冲突时有选择地抑制因果识别出的幻觉驱动头。

Result: 发现幻觉驱动效应分布更广且总权重更大，而抵抗效应集中于少数高重要性头部，形成偏向错误前提的不平衡路由结构。MACI在MMMC基准上相较推理时基线实现最大幻觉降低，且具有良好的幻觉-准确率权衡，并能零样本迁移至SCI-SemanticConflict测试。

Conclusion: 模态冲突幻觉源于模型内部不平衡的因果路由结构。通过因果干预针对性地抑制驱动头，可有效缓解此类幻觉问题，为理解与控制MLLMs的幻觉行为提供了新的机制性见解与实用方案。

Abstract: Modality-conflict hallucination occurs when multimodal large language models (MLLMs) prioritize erroneous textual premises over contradictory visual evidence. To understand why visual evidence fails to prevail during generation, we take a mechanistic perspective and examine which internal components drive or resist this failure. We perform head-level causal analysis using path patching across five open-source MLLMs and identify two groups of attention heads with opposing causal roles: hallucination-driving heads and hallucination-resisting heads. We find a consistent asymmetry: driving effects are more broadly distributed and carry greater aggregate weight, whereas resisting effects concentrate in a small number of high-importance heads. Ablation experiments further confirm that these groups exert opposing effects during generation: distributed driving influence and localized resistance together form an imbalanced routing structure that biases generation toward the erroneous premise. Motivated by this finding, we propose MACI (Modality-conflict-Aware Causal Intervention), a conditional intervention that suppresses causally identified hallucination-driving heads only when conflict is detected. Across five MLLMs, MACI achieves the largest hallucination reduction among compared inference-time baselines on the MMMC benchmark with a favorable hallucination-accuracy trade-off, and transfers zero-shot to the SCI-SemanticConflict test.

</details>


### [51] [MOCHA: Multi-Objective Chebyshev Annealing for Agent Skill Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19330)
*Md Mehrab Tanjim,Jayakumar Subramanian,Xiang Chen,Branislav Kveton,Subhojyoti Mukherjee,Anlan Zhang,Sungchul Kim,Somdeb Sarkhel,Sunav Choudhury*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了MOCHA（多目标切比雪夫退火）方法，用于在平台约束下优化LLM代理的技能（skills），在6项任务中相比最强基线实现了7.5%的相对性能提升，并发现了两倍数量的帕累托最优技能变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM代理的技能优化面临固有的多目标挑战：技能需要同时最大化任务性能并满足平台约束（如描述字段截断、指令体压缩、上下文窗口竞争）。现有提示优化器要么忽略这些权衡，要么将其简化为加权和，从而无法发现非凸目标区域中的帕累托最优变体。

Method: 提出MOCHA方法，核心包含两个组件：1）使用切比雪夫标量化替代单目标选择，能够覆盖包括非凸区域在内的完整帕累托前沿；2）结合指数退火策略，实现从探索到利用的平滑过渡。所有方法共享相同的多目标变异算子，基线方法接收相同的逐目标文本反馈。

Result: 在6个多样化的代理技能任务上进行实验，现有优化器在6个任务中的4个上无法改进种子技能（1000次rollout零进展）。MOCHA在所有任务上均取得突破，相比最强基线实现平均正确率7.5%的相对提升（FEVER上最高14.9%，TheoremQA上10.4%），同时发现两倍数量的帕累托最优技能变体。

Conclusion: MOCHA通过切比雪夫标量化和指数退火的有效结合，成功解决了LLM代理技能优化中的多目标约束问题，在所有测试任务上均实现了性能突破，证明了该方法在处理非凸目标空间和多平台约束场景下的优越性。

Abstract: LLM agents organize behavior through skills - structured natural-language specifications governing how an agent reasons, retrieves, and responds. Unlike monolithic prompts, skills are multi-field artifacts subject to hard platform constraints: description fields are truncated for routing, instruction bodies are compacted via progressive disclosure, and co-resident skills compete for limited context windows. These constraints make skill optimization inherently multi-objective: a skill must simultaneously maximize task performance and satisfy platform limits. Yet existing prompt optimizers either ignore these trade-offs or collapse them into a weighted sum, missing Pareto-optimal variants in non-convex objective regions. We introduce MOCHA (Multi-Objective Chebyshev Annealing), which replaces single-objective selection with Chebyshev scalarization - covering the full Pareto front, including non-convex regions - combined with exponential annealing that transitions from exploration to exploitation. In our experiments across six diverse agent skills - where all methods share the same multi-objective mutation operator and baselines receive identical per-objective textual feedback - existing optimizers fail to improve the seed skill on 4 of 6 tasks: 1000 rollouts yield zero progress. MOCHA breaks through on every task, achieving 7.5% relative improvement in mean correctness over the strongest baseline (up to 14.9% on FEVER and 10.4% on TheoremQA) while discovering twice as many more Pareto-optimal skill variants.

</details>


### [52] [Agentic Trading: When LLM Agents Meet Financial Markets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19337)
*Yihan Xia,Panpan You,Taotao Wang,Fang Liu,Han Qi,Xiaoxiao Wu,Shengli Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对77篇LLM交易代理研究进行系统审计，发现主要实证子集（19篇）存在协议不可比性问题：仅少数研究报告时间一致划分协议、交易成本模型等关键细节，无研究达到R3复现性，表明评估协议和可复现性是当前领域瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM交易代理研究快速增长，需要系统梳理该领域的实证证据质量、评估协议标准化程度和可复现性现状，以识别研究瓶颈并推动规范化发展。

Method: 采用协议编码快照方法筛选77篇研究（截至2026-03-09），将LLM交易代理重构为专家系统决策管道进行审计导向证据映射；定义Action Output和Closed-Loop Evaluation作为最小边界筛选出19篇主要实证研究，应用Architecture-Capability-Adaptation分析框架和复现性审计标准（R0-R3）进行系统评估。

Result: 核心发现为协议不可比性：19篇主要实证研究中仅2篇报告可提取的时间一致划分协议，1篇报告显式交易成本模型，1篇文档化处理universe或幸存者偏差，11篇报告执行时序或语义，15篇被编码为R0级别，无研究达到R3复现性。架构实验快速扩张与可比较评估协议、执行语义和可复现制品缺乏形成鲜明对比。

Conclusion: 领域架构创新迅速，但可比较评估协议、执行语义规范化和可复现制品是当前紧迫瓶颈；研究贡献了证据账本、复现性审计和报告清单作为推进领域标准化的重要工具。

Abstract: A growing body of work explores how Large Language Models (LLMs) can be embedded in trading systems as agents that perceive market information, retrieve context, reason about decisions, emit tradable actions, and adapt under market feedback. This paper reframes LLM-based trading agents as expert-system decision pipelines and presents an audit-oriented evidence map of 77 included studies in a protocol-coded snapshot screened through 2026-03-09. A primary empirical subset (n=19) satisfies the minimum boundary of Action Output plus Closed-Loop Evaluation; the remaining 58 included studies are retained as background and design context. The central empirical finding is protocol incomparability: within the primary subset, only 2/19 studies report extractable time-consistent split protocols, 1/19 reports an explicit transaction-cost model, 1/19 documents universe or survivorship handling, 11/19 report execution timing or semantics, 15/19 are coded as R0, and no study reaches R3 reproducibility. We therefore use Architecture-Capability-Adaptation as a working analytical lens rather than a validated taxonomy, and we foreground the evidence ledger, reproducibility audit, and reporting checklist as the main contributions. The resulting survey shows that architectural experimentation is expanding rapidly, while comparable evaluation protocols, execution semantics, and reproducible artifacts remain the field's immediate bottlenecks.

</details>

*Junyeob Baek,Mingyu Jo,Minsu Kim,Mengye Ren,Yoshua Bengio,Sungjin Ahn*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了生成式递归推理模型（GRAM），将确定性的递归推理转变为概率化的多轨迹计算，通过随机潜在轨迹实现了多假设、多策略推理以及推理时的计算扩展能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的递归推理模型（RRMs）大多是确定性的，仅沿着单条潜在轨迹收敛到单一预测，缺乏处理多假设、多解策略的能力，也无法通过采样进行推理时的计算扩展。作者旨在解决这一局限性，使递归推理模型具备概率生成能力。

Method: GRAM将推理建模为随机潜在轨迹，构建了一个潜在变量生成模型框架。该方法支持条件推理（p_θ(y|x)）和无条件生成（p_θ(x)），通过递归深度和并行轨迹采样实现推理时的计算扩展，并采用摊分变分推理进行训练。

Result: 在结构化推理和多解约束满足任务上，GRAM优于确定性的循环和递归基线模型，同时展示了无条件生成能力。实验证明了多轨迹概率推理的有效性。

Conclusion: GRAM成功地将递归潜在推理转化为概率多轨迹计算范式，为神经推理系统提供了一种处理多假设和多解策略的新途径，展示了在推理时通过采样实现计算扩展的潜力。

Abstract: How should future neural reasoning systems implement extended computation? Recursive Reasoning Models (RRMs) offer a promising alternative to autoregressive sequence extension by performing iterative latent-state refinement with shared transition functions. Yet existing RRMs are largely deterministic, following a single latent trajectory and converging to a single prediction. We introduce \emph{Generative Recursive reAsoning Models (GRAM)}, a framework that turns recursive latent reasoning into probabilistic multi-trajectory computation. GRAM models reasoning as a stochastic latent trajectory, enabling multiple hypotheses, alternative solution strategies, and inference-time scaling through both recursive depth and parallel trajectory sampling. This yields a latent-variable generative model supporting conditional reasoning via $p_θ(y \mid x)$ and, with fixed or absent inputs, unconditional generation via $p_θ(x)$. Trained with amortized variational inference, GRAM improves over deterministic recurrent and recursive baselines on structured reasoning and multi-solution constraint satisfaction tasks, while demonstrating an unconditional generation capability. \href{https://ahn-ml.github.io/gram-website/}{https://ahn-ml.github.io/gram-website}

</details>


### [54] [PRISM: A Benchmark for Programmatic Spatial-Temporal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19382)
*Qiran Zhang,Yuheng Wang,Runde Yang,Lin Wu,Jingru Fan,Shu Yao,Jie Zhang,Tianle Zhou,Huatao Li,Ruijie Shi,Yihan Li,Chen Qian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了PRISM基准，包含10,372个人工校验的指令-代码对，通过四项指标评估LLM的程序化视频生成能力，发现了执行成功率与空间正确率之间存在约41%的显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 程序化视频生成相比像素级扩散模型具有几何精度和时间连贯性优势，但如何严格评估语言模型是否能生成空间正确的动画输出仍是一个未解决的问题，现有评估多局限于代码可执行性而忽视了空间正确性。

Method: 构建了包含10,372个人工校验指令-代码对的大规模基准PRISM，涵盖中英双语和437个主题类别；提出了漏斗式评估框架，包含四项互补指标：代码级可靠性、空间推理、提示感知动态视觉复杂度（PADVC）和时间密度（TD）。

Result: 对七个主流LLM的系统评估揭示了一个显著的执行-空间差距：从执行成功率到空间通过率的平均下降约41%，表明可运行代码并不一定能产生空间连贯的视觉输出。

Conclusion: 程序化视频生成评估不应仅限于可执行性，PRISM为推进空间连贯的代码生成提供了有原则的基准，强调了空间推理能力评估的重要性。

Abstract: Programmatic video generation through code offers geometric precision and temporal coherence beyond pixel-level diffusion models, yet rigorously evaluating whether language models can produce spatially correct animated outputs remains an open problem. We introduce PRISM, a large-scale benchmark of 10,372 human-calibrated instruction-code pairs (20 times larger than prior programmatic video generation benchmarks), grounded in real-world knowledge visualization scenarios across English and Chinese and spanning 437 subject categories. We further propose a funnel-style evaluation framework with four complementary metrics: Code-Level Reliability for executability, Spatial Reasoning for layout correctness over full animation sequences, and Prompt-Aware Dynamic Visual Complexity (PADVC) and Temporal Density (TD) for diagnosing dynamic expression and temporal activity. Systematic evaluation of seven mainstream LLMs reveals a striking Execution-Spatial Gap: the average drop from execution success rate to spatial pass rate is approximately 41%, showing that runnable code does not necessarily yield spatially coherent visual output. These findings show that programmatic video generation evaluation should go beyond executability. PRISM provides a principled benchmark for advancing spatially coherent code generation.

</details>


### [55] [Conflict-Resilient Multi-Agent Reasoning via Signed Graph Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19418)
*Longgang He,Longzhu He,Daojing He,Chaozhuo Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了SIGMA框架，通过有符号图显式建模多智能体系统中的信任、冲突和中立关系，有效解决现有方法中错误传播和缺乏结构感知的问题，在多个基准数据集上取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于图的多智能体系统存在两个关键问题：(1)当冲突信号出现时，缺乏控制机制导致错误传播；(2)缺乏对智能体间冲突关系的显式建模和结构感知能力，无法识别可靠的交互模式。现有方法假设所有智能体都是均匀协作的，这种简单的聚合机制限制了系统性能。

Method: 提出SIGMA（有符号图引导的多智能体推理框架），首先为给定查询选择相关且多样化的智能体集合，然后构建带有置信度加权边的结构化有符号交互图。通过冲突感知的有符号消息传递机制进行推理，该机制强化来自可信智能体的信息，同时抑制冲突信号，最终通过结构和冲突感知的加权聚合产生全局一致且具有冲突韧性的预测结果。

Result: 在六个基准数据集上进行了广泛实验，涵盖多个LLM主干网络和多样化的多智能体配置。实验结果表明，SIGMA在准确性和冲突韧性方面均显著优于现有最先进基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模智能体间的信任、冲突和中立关系，SIGMA成功解决了现有图结构多智能体系统中的错误传播和结构感知不足问题，证明了有符号图在多智能体协作推理中的有效性，为实现更鲁棒的多智能体系统提供了新思路。

Abstract: LLM-based multi-agent systems (MAS) have demonstrated strong reasoning and decision-making capabilities that consistently surpass those of single LLM agents. However, their performance often suffers from naive aggregation mechanisms that assume uniformly cooperative interactions. Upon close inspection, we observe that existing graph-based MAS frameworks (1) propagate errors when conflicting signals arise without control, and (2) lack explicit modeling of conflicting inter-agent relations as well as structural awareness, failing to identify reliable interaction patterns. To bridge this gap, we introduce SIGMA, a novel SIgned Graph-informed Multi-Agent reasoning framework that explicitly captures trust, conflict, and neutral relations among agents via a signed relational graph. Specifically, given a query, SIGMA first selects a set of relevant and diverse agents, then constructs a structured signed interaction graph with confidence-weighted edges. Reasoning proceeds through conflict-aware signed message passing, which reinforces information from trustworthy agents while suppressing conflicting signals, and terminates with a structure- and conflict-aware weighted aggregation to yield globally consistent and conflict-resilient predictions. Extensive experiments on six benchmark datasets, across multiple LLM backbones and diverse multi-agent configurations, demonstrate that SIGMA consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, achieving notable gains in both accuracy and conflict-resilient performance.

</details>


### [56] [What and When to Distill: Selective Hindsight Distillation for Multi-Turn Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19447)
*Xiaozhe Li,Tianyi Lyu,Yang Li,Yichuan Ma,Peiji Li,Linyang Li,Qipeng Guo,Dahua Lin,Kai Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SERL框架，通过利用多轮交互中的细粒度环境反馈来解决大模型智能体强化学习中的长视距信用分配问题，在ALFWorld和WebShop任务上分别取得90.0%和80.1%的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大模型的强化学习方法在长视距信用分配上存在困难，稀疏的任务奖励难以有效分配到多个动作步骤中。现有方法主要依赖轨迹级奖励或代理信号，未能充分利用多轮智能体交互场景中每一步可获得的丰富环境反馈（如错误信息、页面变化、观测结果等）。

Method: 提出选择性环境重加权学习框架（SERL）。该框架系统性地研究了五种反馈源和两种插入粒度。核心机制是：利用任务奖励确定更新方向，同时利用环境反馈调整奖励的位置和幅度，从而将学习重点聚焦于关键动作步骤。该方法实现了对环境反馈的选择性利用，而非不加区分地使用所有上下文信息。

Result: 在ALFWorld和WebShop两个基准测试集上，SERL分别实现了90.0%和80.1%的成功率，显著优于强化的强化学习基线和蒸馏基线方法。分析表明，在关键节点使用与动作相关的接地反馈，其效果始终优于不加区分地使用更长或更丰富的上下文。

Conclusion: 研究表明，智能体强化学习的效果不仅取决于反馈的丰富程度，更取决于反馈的放置位置和相关性。通过选择性地利用环境反馈来调整信用分配，SERL为多轮智能体的训练提供了一种有效的新范式，证明了细粒度反馈对长视距任务学习的重要性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning can train LLM agents from sparse task rewards, but long-horizon credit assignment remains challenging: a single success-or-failure signal must be distributed across many actions. Existing methods rely on trajectory-level rewards or proxy signals, without fully leveraging per-step environmental feedback. Multi-turn agent settings are underexplored, where feedback can include error messages, page changes, observations, or reference trajectories. We systematically study five feedback sources and two insertion granularities and introduce SERL, a selective environment-reweighted learning framework. SERL uses the task reward to determine update direction, while environment feedback adjusts placement and magnitude, focusing on critical actions. On ALFWorld and WebShop, SERL achieves 90.0% and 80.1% success, outperforming strong RL and distillation baselines. Analysis shows that grounded, action-relevant feedback at meaningful points consistently outperforms indiscriminate use of longer or richer context.

</details>


### [57] [Generative Auto-Bidding with Unified Modeling and Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19457)
*Mingming Zhang,Feiqing Zhuang,Na Li,Shengjie Sun,Xiaowei Chen,Junxiong Zhu,Fei Xiao,Keping Yang,Lixin Zou,Chenliang Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GUIDE是一个生成式自动竞价框架，通过决策Transformer结合Q值模块引导探索和逆动力学模块提供安全回退机制，在探索与安全之间取得平衡，在线部署取得了显著的广告效果提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自动竞价方法存在明显缺陷：早期基于规则的方法缺乏适应性；强化学习方法在长期依赖建模上存在困难；近期生成模型缺乏显式机制来平衡探索与安全，仅依赖动作扰动或轨迹引导而缺乏安全回退方案，导致探索效率低下并给广告平台带来较高的财务风险。

Method: 提出GUIDE框架，采用决策Transformer（DT）联合建模历史竞价动作和环境状态转移。引入Q值模块通过正则化约束引导DT的探索；设计逆动力学模块（IDM）利用DT预测的未来状态推断鲁棒、行为一致的动作作为安全策略回退。Q值模块自适应地从探索动作和安全回退动作中选择最终动作，形成集成的“探索-保护-选择”流程。

Result: 在公开数据集、模拟竞价环境以及淘宝大规模在线部署中，GUIDE均持续超越最先进基线。实际部署中取得显著提升：广告GMV提升4.10%，广告点击提升1.40%，广告花费提升1.66%，广告ROI提升3.52%。

Conclusion: GUIDE通过统一建模和探索机制成功平衡了竞价策略中的效率与安全，证明了其在工业场景中的有效性和强实用性，为现代数字广告自动竞价提供了可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: Automated bidding is central to modern digital advertising. Early rule-based methods lacked adaptability, while subsequent Reinforcement Learning approaches modeled bidding as a Markov Decision Process but struggled with long-term dependencies. Recent generative models show promise, yet they lack explicit mechanisms to balance exploration and safety, relying solely on action perturbations or trajectory guidance without a safety fallback. This results in inefficient exploration and elevated financial risk for advertising platforms.
  To address this gap, we propose GUIDE (Generative Auto-Bidding with Unified Modeling and Exploration), a framework that synergistically integrates directed exploration with a safe fallback mechanism. GUIDE employs a Decision Transformer (DT) to jointly model historical bidding actions and environmental state transitions. A Q-value module guides the DT's exploration via regularization constraints, while an Inverse Dynamics Module (IDM) leverages DT-predicted future states to infer robust, behaviorally consistent actions as a safe policy fallback. The Q-value module then adaptively selects the final action between these two options, balancing exploration and safety. Together, these components form an integrated "explore-safeguard-select" pipeline that unifies efficiency and safety.
  We conduct extensive experiments on public datasets, in simulated auction environments, and through large-scale online deployment on Taobao, a leading Chinese advertising platform. Results show GUIDE consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines across all scenarios. In real-world deployment, GUIDE achieves notable gains: +4.10% ad GMV, +1.40% ad clicks, +1.66% ad cost, and +3.52% ad ROI, demonstrating its effectiveness and strong industrial applicability.

</details>


### [58] [Beyond Mode Collapse: Distribution Matching for Diverse Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19461)
*Xiaozhe Li,Yang Li,Xinyu Fang,Shengyuan Ding,Peiji Li,Yongkang Chen,Yichuan Ma,Tianyi Lyu,Linyang Li,Dahua Lin,Qipeng Guo,Qingwen Liu,Kai Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DMPO算法，通过前向KL最小化的原则性近似来解决在线强化学习（如GRPO）中的模式崩塌问题，在NP-hard组合优化任务上实现了9-12%的相对性能提升，并提升了数学推理和跨领域任务的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线强化学习方法（如GRPO）存在模式崩塌问题：一旦发现高奖励轨迹就集中概率质量于单一解，停止探索替代策略。作者发现这源于反向KL最小化的模式寻求行为，它会强化首个高奖励轨迹而非维持多解分布。

Method: 提出DMPO（分布匹配策略优化）算法：1）基于奖励构建轨迹组的层级目标分布；2）将策略分布对齐至该目标分布；3）通过原则性近似前向KL最小化实现模式覆盖行为，无需从不可处理的全局目标分布中采样，从而在训练中维持持续探索。

Result: 在NP-hard组合优化任务上验证：文本NP-Bench上质量比达43.9%（GRPO为40.1%，相对提升9%）；视觉NP-Bench上达43.1%（GRPO为38.4%，相对提升12%）。泛化性验证：数学推理任务提升2.0%，跨领域任务提升2.3%。

Conclusion: 分布匹配是防止在线强化学习中模式崩塌的实用且原则性方法，保持多样性训练能增强跨模态推理能力，在多样化推理任务中实现持续探索和一致的质量提升。
