A stylized reliability decomposition separates per-call model variance from architectural momentum, motivating the claim that as model variance decreases, pattern choice and SDB strength become increasingly important levers for long-run reliability. We apply the methodology to five workloads and provide one runnable reference implementation for a 90-day contract-renewal agent.

</details>

### [94] [C2CServe: Leveraging NVLink-C2C for Elastic Serverless LLM Serving on MIG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19481)
*Shutian Luo,Ali Zafar Sadiq,Rui Yang,Mingye Zhang,Haiying Shen,Wei Wang,Yue Cheng*

Main category: cs.OS

TL;DR: 提出C2CServe系统，利用NVLink-C2C高带宽互联特性，将模型权重存储在CPU内存并按需流式传输至MIG实例，实现了请求粒度的Serverless LLM服务，将冷启动延迟降低多达7.1倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GPU服务系统在Serverless场景下面临两难：专用GPU分配在稀疏流量下浪费HBM资源，而GPU时间共享将模型初始化和权重加载置于冷启动路径；MIG等空间共享技术虽然提供隔离性，但其切片HBM容量不足以容纳现代LLM权重。

Method: 1) 利用NVLink-C2C高带宽CPU-GPU互联，使模型权重驻留CPU内存并按需流式传输至MIG实例；2) 设计HybridGEMM异构内存感知GEMM内核，通过单一调优旋钮平衡HBM和C2C带宽；3) 采用分层调度器，通过在线反馈控制协调模型放置、输入分块和内核选择，缓解共享C2C链路的竞争问题。

Result: 在GH200平台上，与最先进Serverless LLM服务系统相比，C2CServe将稠密模型的冷启动延迟降低高达7.1倍，MoE模型降低4.6倍；同时在C2C竞争下保持超过95%的TTFT和TPOT达成率。

Conclusion: 高带宽CPU-GPU互联技术改变了Serverless LLM服务的内存约束范式，使模型驻留从稀缺的HBM转移至充足的Host内存成为可能，为多租户、长尾调用的大模型目录服务提供了高效解决方案。

Abstract: Modern LLM serving is increasingly serverless in shape: large model catalogs, long-tail invocations, and multi-tenant demand. Existing GPU serving systems face a tradeoff: dedicated-GPU allocation wastes scarce HBM under sparse traffic, while GPU time sharing places model initialization and weight loading on the cold-start path. Spatial GPU sharing such as multi-instance GPU (MIG) provides isolation and accounting, but each slice has too little HBM for modern LLM weights.
  We observe that high-bandwidth CPU--GPU interconnects, such as NVLink-C2C (C2C) in NVIDIA GH200 and GB200 Superchips, change the memory constraint: model weights can reside in CPU memory and be streamed on demand to MIG instances, shifting model residency from scarce HBM to abundant host memory. Leveraging this capability, we present C2CServe, a request-granularity serverless LLM serving system that allows MIG instances to switch models across requests without reloading weights into HBM. C2CServe introduces HybridGEMM, a heterogeneous-memory-aware GEMM kernel that adapts data access patterns to balance HBM and C2C bandwidth across MIG partitions using a single tuning knob. To mitigate shared-C2C contention, C2CServe further uses a hierarchical scheduler that coordinates model placement, input chunking, and kernel selection with online feedback control. On GH200, C2CServe reduces cold-start latency by up to 7.1x for dense models and 4.6x for MoE models compared with state-of-the-art serverless LLM serving systems, while maintaining over 95\% TTFT and TPOT attainment under C2C contention.

</details>


### [95] [SpecSA: Bridging Speculative Decoding and Sparse Attention for Efficient LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19893)
*Zhibin Wang,Ziyu Zhong,Nuo Shen,Yuhang Zhou,Rong Gu,Sheng Zhong*

Main category: cs.OS

TL;DR: 提出SpecSA框架，成功解决了推测解码与动态稀疏注意力直接结合时的结构不匹配问题，在H100 GPU上实现了最高3.49倍的端到端吞吐量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推测解码和动态稀疏注意力是加速长上下文LLM推理的两种互补方法，但直接结合存在结构不匹配：推测验证依赖跨查询共性，而动态稀疏注意力分配特定于查询的稀疏布局。这种不匹配限制了KV块重用，放大了NSA的分支开销，并使验证策略选择变得依赖于输入和机制。

Method: 提出SpecSA稀疏推测验证框架，包含三项核心技术：1) 重叠感知的分组查询执行以改善跨查询重用；2) 基于刷新/重用的NSA内核融合以减少选定索引和分支融合开销；3) 基于性能分析引导的提示自适应编排，在用户指定的精度类别下选择有效的草稿验证策略。

Result: 在NVIDIA H100 GPU上的实验表明，SpecSA相比自回归NSA解码实现了高达3.49倍的端到端吞吐量提升，在稀疏推测验证上实现了高达6.86倍的内核加速。

Conclusion: SpecSA成功将动态稀疏注意力转化为面向验证的工作负载，通过优化跨查询重用、减少开销和自适应策略选择，有效解决了推测解码与动态稀疏注意力结合的结构不匹配问题，显著提升了长上下文LLM推理性能。

Abstract: Speculative decoding and dynamic sparse attention are two complementary approaches for accelerating long-context LLM inference: the former amortizes target-model execution across multiple verifier queries, while the latter reduces each query's KV-cache working set. Directly combining them, however, exposes a structural mismatch: speculative verification relies on cross-query commonality, whereas dynamic sparse attention assigns query-specific sparse layouts. This mismatch limits KV-block reuse, amplifies NSA's branch-wise overheads, and makes verification strategy selection input- and regime-dependent. We present SpecSA, a sparse speculative-verification framework that turns dynamic sparse attention into a verification-oriented workload. SpecSA combines overlap-aware grouped-query execution, refresh/reuse-based NSA kernel fusion, and profile-guided prompt-adaptive orchestration to improve cross-query reuse, reduce selected-index and branch-fusion overheads, and select effective draft-verification strategies under user-specified precision classes. Experiments on NVIDIA H100 GPUs show that SpecSA achieves up to 3.49x end-to-end throughput over autoregressive NSA decoding and up to 6.86x kernel speedups for sparse speculative verification.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [96] [Dimensional Balance Improves Large Scale Spatiotemporal Prediction Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18793)
*Jing Chen,Shixiang Pan,Yujie Fan,Haocheng Ye,Haitao Xu,Wenqiang Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ST-Balance框架，通过时空熵诊断并解决时空复杂度不匹配问题，实现了跨领域时空预测的显著精度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时空模式分析方法存在性能瓶颈，通常只能获得增量式改进，且跨领域可迁移性有限。作者旨在通过空间和时间熵度量来分析这一瓶颈，发现较大的时空复杂度不匹配往往伴随着更高的预测不确定性。

Method: 提出一个可扩展的自适应框架，协调空间和时间特征表示：(1) 通过低秩矩阵嵌入压缩空间维度，保留本质结构；(2) 扩展时间视界以捕获长程依赖关系，缓解由时间异质性引起的累积误差。

Result: 在城市交通、气象和流行病学数据集上的广泛实验表明，该方法实现了显著的精度提升，并在评估领域展现出广泛的适用性。

Conclusion: 该框架在广泛的时空任务中具有良好前景，能够有效解决时空复杂度不匹配问题，具有良好的跨领域迁移能力。

Abstract: Accurate spatiotemporal pattern analysis is critical in fields such as urban traffic, meteorology, and public health monitoring. However, existing methods face performance bottlenecks, typically yielding only incremental gains and often exhibiting limited cross-domain transferability. We analyze this bottleneck through spatial and temporal entropy measures, which are used as diagnostic indicators of spatiotemporal complexity mismatch rather than as guarantees that entropy alignment alone yields better forecasting. Empirically, larger mismatch is often accompanied by higher prediction uncertainty, especially under a fixed model-capacity budget. Guided by this diagnostic, we propose a scalable, adaptive framework that harmonizes spatial and temporal feature representations. Spatial dimensionality is compressed via low-rank matrix embedding to preserve essential structure, while an extended temporal horizon captures long-range dependencies and mitigates cumulative errors arising from temporal heterogeneity. Extensive experiments on urban traffic, meteorological, and epidemic datasets demonstrate substantial accuracy gains and broad applicability across the evaluated domains, suggesting that the framework is promising for a wide range of spatiotemporal tasks beyond the current study. The code is available on GitHub at https://github.com/ST-Balance/ST-Balance.

</details>


### [97] [Robust Basis Spline Decoupling for the Compression of Transformer Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18794)
*Joppe De Jonghe,Van Tien Pham,Mariya Ishteva*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于B样条的解耦框架（R-CMTF-BSD算法），用于神经网络压缩。该方法通过B样条参数化实现了更稳定、更具表达力的表示，在Vision和Swin Transformer上实现了显著的参数缩减同时保持竞争力精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于张量的解耦方法通常依赖多项式或分段线性参数化来表示内部非线性函数，这些方法存在数值不稳定或表达能力有限的问题。由于解耦方法在神经网络压缩领域具有重要应用价值，亟需一种更稳定且表达力更强的解耦框架。

Method: 提出了基于B样条的解耦框架，利用B样条的局部支撑和灵活的光滑控制特性。推导了约束耦合矩阵-张量分解，并提出了鲁棒的交替最小二乘算法（R-CMTF-BSD），该算法结合了归一化和Tikhonov正则化技术。

Result: 在合成数据和Transformer模型压缩实验中验证了方法的有效性。在Vision和Swin Transformer架构上的实验结果表明，B样条解耦能够实现显著的参数缩减，同时保持具有竞争力的准确率。

Conclusion: B样条解耦框架成功解决了现有方法的数值不稳定和表达能力有限问题，R-CMTF-BSD算法成为结构化神经网络压缩的有前景工具，为神经网络压缩领域提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Decoupling is a powerful modeling paradigm for representing multivariate functions as compositions of linear transformations and univariate nonlinear functions. A single-layer decoupling can be viewed as a fully connected neural network with a single hidden layer and flexible activation functions, providing a direct link with neural networks. Because of this, the use of decoupling methods has gained increasing attention in neural network domains, particularly compression, since it enables structured approximations with reduced parameter complexity. Existing tensor-based decoupling methods typically rely on polynomial or piecewise-linear parameterizations of the internal nonlinear functions, which can suffer from numerical instability or limited expressiveness. In this work, we introduce a B-spline-based decoupling framework that generalizes these existing approaches. By exploiting the local support and flexible smoothness control of B-splines, the proposed formulation yields a more numerically stable and expressive representation. We derive a constrained coupled matrix-tensor factorization and propose a robust alternating least-squares algorithm, called R-CMTF-BSD, incorporating normalization and Tikhonov regularization. The proposed method is validated through experiments on synthetic data and transformer model compression. Results on the Vision and Swin Transformer architectures demonstrate that B-spline decoupling enables substantial parameter reduction while maintaining competitive accuracy, making the R-CMTF-BSD algorithm a promising tool for structured neural network compression.

</details>


### [98] [HELLoRA: Hot Experts Layer-Level Low-Rank Adaptation for Mixture-of-Experts Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18795)
*Jia Wei,Zhonghao Zhang,Ping Chen,Qianyang li,Yancheng Pan,Shaoxun Wang,Ziyi Qiu,Longxiang Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出HElLoRA方法，仅在MoE模型每层最常被激活的专家上附加LoRA模块，实现参数效率与性能的双重提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LoRA变体多针对密集架构设计，忽视了Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) 模型稀疏激活模式带来的适配优化机会；MoE模型以近乎恒定的每token计算量扩展参数，其专家特化性质为更高效的参数高效微调（PEFT）提供了可能。

Method: 提出Hot-Experts Layer-level Low-Rank Adaptation (HElLoRA)，核心机制是根据激活频率分析，仅对每层最常激活的专家附加LoRA模块，形成激活感知的适配器放置策略；为在极限参数预算下测试，进一步将HElLoRA与LoRI组合形成HElLoRI，冻结上投影层并稀疏化下投影层。

Result: 在OlMoE-1B-7B、Mixtral-8x7B、DeepSeekMoE三种MoE骨干及数学推理、代码生成、安全对齐三类任务上系统评估。相比OlMoE上的vanilla LoRA，HElLoRA仅用15.7%可训练参数、适配器FLOPs降低38.7%、训练吞吐提升1.9倍、准确率提升9.2%；在DeepSeekMoE上仅用23.2%参数即超越LoRA。

Conclusion: 激活感知的适配器放置是MoE语言模型PEFT缩放的有效且实用路径，通过保持预训练专家特化实现结构化正则化，在大幅降低计算与参数成本的同时提升下游性能。

Abstract: Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) dominates parameter-efficient fine-tuning of large language models, yet most variants target dense architectures. Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models scale parameters at near-constant per-token compute, and their sparse activation patterns create untapped opportunities for more efficient adaptation. We propose Hot-Experts Layer-level Low-Rank Adaptation (HELLoRA), which attaches LoRA modules only to the most frequently activated experts at each layer. This simple mechanism reduces trainable parameters and adapter-induced FLOPs while improving downstream performance, an effect we attribute to a form of structured regularization that preserves pretrained expert specialization. To stress-test HELLoRA under extreme parameter budgets, we further compose it with LoRI to form HELLoRI, which freezes the up-projection and sparsifies the down-projection. Across three MoE backbones, namely OlMoE-1B-7B, Mixtral-8x7B, and DeepSeekMoE, and three task families covering mathematical reasoning, code generation, and safety alignment, HELLoRA consistently outperforms strong PEFT baselines. Relative to vanilla LoRA on OlMoE, HELLoRA uses 15.7% of the trainable parameters, reduces adapter FLOPs by 38.7%, achieves 1.9x the training throughput, and improves accuracy by 9.2%. On DeepSeekMoE, HELLoRA outperforms LoRA while using only 23.2% of its trainable parameters. These results demonstrate that activation-aware adapter placement is an effective and practical route to scaling PEFT for MoE language models.

</details>


### [99] [UCCI: Calibrated Uncertainty for Cost-Optimal LLM Cascade Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18796)
*Varun Kotte*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出UCCI级联路由器，通过保序回归校准置信度分数，在保持性能的同时降低31%推理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM级联和模型路由系统存在两个关键问题：使用未校准的置信度分数，以及需要针对每个工作负载进行阈值调优。这限制了路由系统在降低推理成本方面的有效性。

Method: 提出UCCI（calibration-first router），包含两个核心组件：(1)通过保序回归将token级别的边际不确定性映射为每个查询的错误概率；(2)通过约束成本最小化选择升级阈值。在三个明确假设下，基于校准分数的阈值策略具有成本最优性，且保序校准能达到O(n^{-1/3})的期望校准误差(ECE)样本复杂度。

Result: 在生产环境的命名实体识别任务上（75,000个查询，使用4B和12B指令微调LLM，H100 GPU），UCCI在micro-F1=0.91时降低推理成本31%（95%置信区间：[27%, 35%]），同时将ECE从0.12降至0.03。在相同工作点下，UCCI优于熵阈值法、split-conformal路由和FrugalGPT风格的学习阈值方法。所有实验基于实际模型输出和H100延迟测量的端到端路由，而非模拟路由。

Conclusion: UCCI证明了校准优先的路由策略在LLM级联中的有效性，通过保序回归实现低成本高精度的模型路由，为生产环境部署提供了实用的解决方案。

Abstract: LLM cascades and model routing promise lower inference cost by sending easy queries to a small model and escalating hard ones to a large model, but most deployed routers use uncalibrated confidence scores and require per-workload threshold tuning. We present UCCI, a calibration-first router that maps token-level margin uncertainty to a per-query error probability via isotonic regression and selects the escalation threshold by constrained cost minimization. Under three explicit assumptions, threshold policies on the calibrated score are cost-optimal, and isotonic calibration achieves O(n^{-1/3}) sample complexity for expected calibration error (ECE). On a production named entity recognition workload of 75,000 queries served by 4B and 12B instruction-tuned LLMs on H100 GPUs, UCCI cuts inference cost by 31% (95% CI: [27%, 35%]) at micro-F1 = 0.91 while reducing ECE from 0.12 to 0.03. At the same operating point, UCCI beats entropy thresholding, split-conformal routing, and a FrugalGPT-style learned threshold. All cascade results use end-to-end routing on actual model outputs and measured H100 latency, not simulated routing from global accuracies or nominal API prices.

</details>


### [100] [Simply Stabilizing the Loop via Fully Looped Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18797)
*Rao Fu,Zixuan Yang,Jiankun Zhang,Jing Ma,Hechang Chen,Yu Li,Yi Chang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Fully Looped Transformer，通过引入无参数的架构改进和注意力注入机制，解决了循环Transformer训练不稳定的两大根源（梯度震荡和残差爆炸），实现了稳定的训练和显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环Transformer通过迭代重用相同模块来提升性能，避免了增加参数量，但存在严重的训练不稳定问题，限制了其可扩展性。作者旨在解决这一核心瓶颈，使循环Transformer能够稳定训练更多迭代次数。

Method: 提出Fully Looped Transformer，包含两个无参数改进：(1) Fully Looped Architecture：将跨循环信号分布到所有层，缓解残差爆炸；(2) Attention Injection：重用现有注意力块抑制梯度震荡。两者共同稳定训练动态。

Result: 成功将稳定训练的循环迭代次数提升至12次（其他基线在此情况下崩溃）；在温和设置下，下游任务平均性能提升高达13.2%；证明了通过调整推理时的循环次数可以灵活平衡性能与计算开销。

Conclusion: Fully Looped Transformer有效解决了训练稳定性问题，在保持参数效率的同时显著提升性能，为通过增加测试时计算来扩展模型能力提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Scaling model performance typically requires increasing model size. Looped Transformer offers a compelling alternative by iteratively reusing the same Transformer blocks, trading additional computation for improved performance without increasing parameter count or context length. Because the number of loop iterations can be adjusted at inference, it also provides a natural mechanism for balancing performance and test-time compute. However, Looped Transformer still suffers from training instability when the number of loop iterations increases. Our analysis reveals that this instability stems from two sources: gradient oscillation and residual explosion. To address these two problems, we propose the Fully Looped Transformer, which introduces two parameter-free modifications: (1) Fully Looped Architecture, which distributes inter-loop signals across all layers to mitigate residual explosion; (2) Attention Injection, which reuses the existing attention block to suppress gradient oscillation. These modifications stabilize training dynamics, enabling the Fully Looped Transformer to be trained stably up to 12 loop iterations, whereas other baseline looped models collapse in this regime. In milder settings where Looped Transformer does not collapse, Fully Looped Transformer still improves average downstream-task performance by up to 13.2\%. Overall, our experiments demonstrate that Fully Looped Transformer improves training stability, enhances downstream performance, and provides preliminary adaptability under different test-time compute budgets by varying loop iterations at inference.

</details>


### [101] [Accurate Evaluation of Quickest Changepoint Detectors via Non-parametric Survival Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18798)
*Taiki Miyagawa,Akinori F. Ebihara*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对有限且不规则序列长度下 quickest changepoint detection (QCD) 中平均运行长度(ARL)和平均检测延迟(ADD)的应用难题，提出了基于 Kaplan-Meier 估计的非参数估计器 KM-ARL 和 KM-ADD，在模拟和真实数据集上验证了其实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ARL 和 ADD 虽然是 QCD 理论和仿真研究中广泛使用的最优性准则，但在实际数据集中的应用受到序列长度有限且不规则的限制。现有方法难以在这种数据条件下可靠估计 ARL 和 ADD，阻碍了其在真实场景中的应用。

Method: 作者建立 QCD 与生存分析之间的类比，利用生存分析方法处理序列截断情况下的检测概率建模。在此基础上提出非参数估计器 KM-ARL 和 KM-ADD，无需对数据分布做参数假设，直接从观测数据估计性能指标。

Result: 推导了估计偏差的理论界限，证明了除非需要外推，否则估计量渐近无偏。在模拟数据和真实数据集上的实验表明，所提方法在有限且不规则序列长度下具有更好的鲁棒性，提高了可解释性，并有助于直观的模型选择。

Conclusion: 通过将 QCD 与生存分析建立联系，提出的非参数估计器成功解决了有限不规则序列长度下 ARL 和 ADD 估计的难题，为实际应用提供了理论基础和实用工具。

Abstract: We propose non-parametric estimators for the average run length (ARL) and average detection delay (ADD) in quickest changepoint detection (QCD) under finite and irregular sequence lengths. Although ARL and ADD are widely used as optimality criteria in theoretical and simulation studies, their application to real-world datasets is hindered by limited and irregular sequence lengths. To address this issue, we propose non-parametric estimators for the ARL and ADD, termed KM-ARL and KM-ADD, by drawing an analogy between QCD and survival analysis to model detection probabilities under sequence truncation. We derive estimation bias bounds and prove that they are asymptotically unbiased unless extrapolation is required. Experiments on simulated and real-world datasets demonstrate their practical utility, enhancing robustness against limited and irregular sequence lengths, improving interpretability, and facilitating empirical, intuitive model selection. Our Python code is provided at https://github.com/TaikiMiyagawa/Kaplan-Meier-Average-Run-Length, offering ready-to-use implementations for practitioners.

</details>


### [102] [ReCrit: Transition-Aware Reinforcement Learning for Scientific Critic Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18799)
*Wanghan Xu,Yuhao Zhou,Hengyuan Zhao,Shuo Li,Dianzhi Yu,Zhenfei Yin,Yaowen Hu,Fengli Xu,Wanli Ouyang,Wenlong Zhang,Lei Bai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对大语言模型在科学推理中因用户批评而放弃正确答案的问题，提出了ReCrit框架，通过过渡感知的强化学习显著提升了模型在批评交互中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在科学推理中面临一个关键问题：在面对用户批评时，不仅可能回答错误，还可能因盲从批评而放弃原本正确的答案。这种批评交互中的“奉承”行为在科学推理中尤为危险，因为用户的不当批评可能将正确答案转变为错误答案。现有的评估方法主要关注最终答案的准确性，而忽略了交互过程中的正确性转移问题。

Method: 本文提出了ReCrit框架，将批评交互建模为交互轮次间的正确性转移问题。该方法将初始到批评者的行为分解为四个象限：修正（Correction）、奉承（Sycophancy）、鲁棒性（Robustness）和边界（Boundary）。框架奖励修正和鲁棒性行为，惩罚奉承行为，并将持续错误视为弱边界信号。为提高训练效率，ReCrit采用动态异步推演和尾部自适应补全技术来减少推演等待时间。

Result: 在三个科学推理基准测试（ChemBench、TRQA、EarthSE）上的实验表明，ReCrit将Qwen3.5-4B模型的平均Critic准确率从38.15提升至51.49，将Qwen3.5-9B模型从45.40提升至55.59。消融实验显示，最终答案奖励在交互层面几乎无增益，而过渡感知奖励和象限加权能产生更可区分的训练信号和更大的Critic阶段净提升。

Conclusion: 研究表明，在批评交互场景中，过渡感知的奖励设计比传统的最终答案奖励更为有效。通过显式建模正确性转移过程并对不同行为象限进行差异化奖惩，可以有效提升大语言模型在科学推理中的交互鲁棒性，减少对错误批评的盲从行为。

Abstract: Large language models can fail in critic interaction not only by answering incorrectly, but also by abandoning an initially correct scientific solution after user criticism. This is especially risky in scientific reasoning, where user criticism can turn a valid answer into an incorrect one. We frame critic interaction as an inter-turn correctness-transition problem rather than a final-answer accuracy problem, and identify three challenges: transition awareness, decoupling useful correction from harmful sycophancy, and scalable rollout. We propose ReCrit, a transition-aware reinforcement learning framework that decomposes Initial-to-Critic behavior into four quadrants: Correction, Sycophancy, Robustness, and Boundary. ReCrit rewards correction and robustness, penalizes sycophancy, and treats persistent errors as weak boundary signals. To make interaction training practical, ReCrit further uses dynamic asynchronous rollout with tail-adaptive completion to reduce rollout waiting. On three scientific reasoning benchmarks, ChemBench, TRQA, and EarthSE, ReCrit improves average Critic accuracy from 38.15 to 51.49 on Qwen3.5-4B and from 45.40 to 55.59 on Qwen3.5-9B. Ablations show that final-answer rewards provide little interaction-level gain, while transition-aware rewards and quadrant weighting produce more distinguishable training signals and larger net Critic-stage improvement. The code is available at https://github.com/black-yt/ReCrit .

</details>


### [103] [Theory-optimal Quantization Based on Flatness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18800)
*Xiusheng Huang,Zhe Li,Xuanwu Yin,Lu Wang,Yequan Wang,Dong Li,Emad Barsoum,Kang Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的训练后量化框架BDQ，通过优化的矩阵变换有效分散异常值模式，在LLaMA-3-8B的W4A4量化中实现了不到1%的精度下降，在DeepSeek-R1-Distill-LLaMA-70B的W2A4KV16实验中将性能差距减少了39.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练后量化是压缩和加速大语言模型推理的广泛采用的技术，但激活异常值会导致模型性能显著下降，尤其是在较低位精度时。现有方法试图通过特征维度的线性变换来缓解异常值，但分析表明变换后的权重和激活仍表现出持续的异常值模式。

Method: 首先建模量化误差与异常值之间的数学关系，引入新度量指标Flatness来量化异常值分布，并推导出关于Flatness的理论最优解。基于此提出双向对角量化(BDQ)框架，通过学习的对角操作策略性地将异常值幅度分布在矩阵维度上，有效分散异常值模式。

Result: BDQ在LLaMA-3-8B模型的W4A4量化中实现了不到1%的精度下降；在更具挑战性的W2A4KV16实验中，与最先进方法相比，在DeepSeek-R1-Distill-LLaMA-70B模型上减少了39.1%的性能差距。

Conclusion: BDQ建立了新的量化基准，通过理论驱动的异常值分散策略有效解决了大语言模型量化中的异常值挑战，在低位精度下保持了模型性能。

Abstract: Post-training quantization has emerged as a widely adopted technique for compressing and accelerating the inference of Large Language Models (LLMs). The primary challenges in LLMs quantization stem from activation outliers, which significantly degrade model performance especially at lower bit precision. While recent approaches attempt to mitigate outliers through linear transformations across feature dimensions, our analysis reveals that the transformed weights and activations still exhibit persistent outlier patterns with concentrated magnitude distributions. In this paper, we first model the mathematical relationship between quantization error and outliers, and then introduce a new metric Flatness to quantify the distribution of outliers. Based on this, we derive the theoretical optimal solution with respect to Flatness. Building on these insights, we propose Bidirectional Diagonal Quantization (BDQ), a novel post-training quantization framework that effectively disperses outlier patterns through optimized matrix transformations. BDQ strategically distributes outlier magnitudes across matrix dimensions via learned diagonal operations. Extensive experiments demonstrate that BDQ establishes a new quantization benchmark. It achieves less than 1\% accuracy drop in W4A4 quantization on the LLaMA-3-8B model. In the more challenging W2A4KV16 experiment, compared to state-of-the-art approaches, BDQ reduces the performance gap by 39.1\% on the DeepSeek-R1-Distill-LLaMA-70B model.

</details>


### [104] [PROWL: Prioritized Regret-Driven Optimization for World Model Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18803)
*Ahmet H. Güzel,Jenny Seidenschwarz,Benjamin Graham,Jonathan Sadeghi,Jeffrey Hawke,Jack Parker-Holder,Ilija Bogunovic*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PROWL方法，通过KL约束的对抗课程学习主动发现并修复世界模型在罕见关键转移上的失败，结合优先对抗轨迹缓冲区（PAT）持续聚焦未解决的弱点，从而提升模型鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代动作条件视频世界模型在短期视觉真实性上表现优异，但在对下游规划至关重要的罕见、交互关键转移上仍不可靠。被动演示数据系统性低估这些高影响区域，且这些失败模式自然发生频率极低，传统训练难以覆盖。

Method: 提出KL约束的对抗课程学习框架：训练一个策略在保持与行为分布接近的前提下，主动发现扩散世界模型的高误差轨迹；世界模型在这些对抗发现的轨迹上持续微调。引入优先对抗轨迹缓冲区（PAT），根据预测误差、动作保真度和学习进度重新排序轨迹，聚焦未解决的失败模式而非重复已解决的案例。在MineRL框架中实现该方法。

Result: PROWL在保留的分布外轨迹上评估，相比仅使用被动数据训练的模型提升了鲁棒性；在弱行为约束下揭示了奖励黑客行为；证明有效的对抗世界模型训练关键在于平衡探索性失败发现与显式行为正则化。

Conclusion: 可扩展的世界模型不仅受益于更大的数据集，更受益于选择性生成有信息量的训练数据。对抗训练循环能将罕见失败转化为稳定的近分布训练信号，避免漂移到分布外利用，强调了主动发现与修复模型弱点的重要性。

Abstract: Modern action-conditioned video world models achieve strong short-horizon visual realism, yet remain unreliable on rare, interaction-critical transitions that dominate downstream planning and policy performance. Because passive demonstration data systematically under-samples these high-impact regimes, improving robustness requires actively eliciting model failures rather than relying on their natural occurrence. We introduce a KL-constrained adversarial curriculum in which a policy is trained to expose high-error trajectories of a diffusion-based world model while remaining close to the behavior distribution. The world model is continuously fine-tuned on these adversarially discovered trajectories, yielding an adversarial training loop that converts rare failures into a stable, near-distribution training signal without drifting into out-of-distribution exploitation. To maintain pressure on unresolved weaknesses as the model improves, we propose a Prioritized Adversarial Trajectory (PAT) buffer that re-ranks trajectories based on prediction error, action fidelity, and learning progress, focusing training on unresolved failure modes rather than repeatedly revisiting solved cases. We implement our approach in the MineRL framework and evaluate it on held-out out-of-distribution trajectories; PROWL improves robustness over models trained on passive data alone, reveals reward-hacking behaviors under weak behavioral constraints, and demonstrates that effective adversarial world-model training critically depends on balancing exploratory failure discovery with explicit behavioral regularization. Our results suggest that scalable world models benefit not only from larger datasets, but also from selectively generating informative training data.

</details>


### [105] [Adaptive Multi-Scale Goodness Aggregation for Forward-Forward Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18804)
*Salar Beigzad,Vansh Verma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了AMSGA方法，通过多尺度优良度聚合、自适应课程引导的困难样本挖掘等技术改进Forward-Forward算法，在MNIST和Fashion-MNIST上分别提升了1.45%和1.50%的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始Forward-Forward算法在稳定性、鲁棒性和泛化能力方面存在局限性，局部学习神经网络在性能上与端到端训练方法相比仍有差距，需要改进优良度估计和训练动态设计以提升局部学习方法的竞争力。

Method: 提出自适应多尺度优良度聚合（AMSGA）方法，包含四个核心组件：（1）跨越局部、中间和全局表示的多尺度优良度聚合；（2）自适应课程引导的困难负样本挖掘；（3）层依赖的自适应阈值；（4）用于优化稳定性改进的预热余弦退火学习率调度。这些改进在保持生物可解释性和内存高效性的同时增强了FF框架。

Result: 在MNIST数据集上实现最高+1.45%的性能提升，在Fashion-MNIST上实现最高+1.50%的性能提升，相比基线FF算法表现一致更优，且没有显著的计算开销增加。

Conclusion: 当优良度估计和训练动态经过精心设计时，局部学习方法可以变得更具竞争力。AMSGA成功增强了FF范式，同时保持了其生物可解释性和内存高效的特性，为局部学习神经网络的发展提供了新方向。

Abstract: We propose Adaptive Multi-Scale Goodness Aggregation (AMSGA), a novel extension of the Forward-Forward (FF) algorithm designed to improve stability, robustness, and generalization in local-learning neural networks. AMSGA addresses several limitations of the original FF framework by introducing multi-scale goodness aggregation across local, intermediate, and global representations; adaptive curriculum-guided hard negative mining; layer-dependent adaptive thresholds; and a warm-up cosine annealing learning-rate schedule for improved optimization stability. Together, these modifications strengthen the FF paradigm while preserving its biologically plausible and memory-efficient properties. Experiments on MNIST and Fashion-MNIST demonstrate consistent performance improvements over the baseline FF algorithm, achieving up to +1.45% improvement on MNIST and +1.50% improvement on Fashion-MNIST without significant computational overhead. Our results suggest that local learning methods can become substantially more competitive when goodness estimation and training dynamics are carefully designed.

</details>


### [106] [Block-Based Double Decoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18807)
*Asher Labovich,Benjamin Bradley,Vanessa Alexander,Chaitanya Harsha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出块状双解码器架构，通过双重因果块状注意力掩码实现全损失监督和静态序列打包，在保持与纯解码器模型相当性能的同时，推理时降低至少2/3的KV缓存内存和每token计算量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有编码器-解码器模型相比纯解码器模型具有推理时间节省的优势，但其预训练目标存在监督稀疏和动态序列长度问题，限制了其在大规模场景中的应用。研究旨在结合两者的优势，实现既高效训练又高效推理的架构。

Method: 提出块状双解码器（block-based double decoders），一种新型Transformer架构。该架构采用双重因果块状注意力掩码，实现全损失监督和静态序列打包，从而结合纯解码器的训练效率与编码器-解码器的推理效率。

Result: 扩展定律实验表明，块状双解码器在各规模下显著优于编码器-解码器模型，并紧密跟踪纯解码器模型的性能。在推理时，该架构在不牺牲预填充缓存或其他现有推理优化的前提下，将KV缓存内存和每token计算量减少至少2/3。

Conclusion: 块状双解码器成功融合了纯解码器模型的训练效率与编码器-解码器模型的推理效率，为大规模模型部署提供了一个性能优异且资源高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Encoder-decoder models offer substantial inference-time savings over decoder-only models, but their pretraining objectives suffer from sparse supervision and dynamic sequence lengths, keeping them out of practice at scale. We propose block-based double decoders, a novel transformer architecture that utilizes doubly-causal block-based attention masks to train with full loss supervision and static sequence packing, combining decoder-only training efficiency with encoder-decoder inference efficiency. In scaling law experiments, block-based double decoders strongly outperform encoder-decoders and closely track decoder-only models across scales. At inference time, they cut KV-cache memory and per-token compute by at least 2/3 without sacrificing prefill caching or other existing inference optimizations available to decoder-only models.

</details>


### [107] [Compositional Literary Primitives in Instruction-Tuned LLMs: Cross-Architectural SAE Features for Self, Style, and Affect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18808)
*Joao Paulo Cavalcante Presa,Savio Salvarino Teles de Oliveira*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究通过稀疏自编码器解析两个指令微调大语言模型（Llama 3.1 8B-Instruct 和 Gemma 2 9B-IT）的文学原语组合架构，识别出四类特征并实现了27类情绪分类的全面覆盖（Llama 27/27，Gemma 23/27），揭示了跨架构的不对称性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在通过机械可解释性方法，解析指令微调大语言模型中文学生成能力的底层结构，理解模型如何组合基础特征产生复杂情绪表达，为模型可控生成提供理论依据。

Method: 在模型中间层残差流上训练稀疏自编码器（SAE），识别四类特征：命名门控、第一人称寄存器特征簇、文体寄存器调制器和组合情绪特征；使用三阶段验证流水线（logit-lens、LLM评分、5-LLM评判团）；应用Cowen-Keltner 27类情绪分类法；通过多特征引导进行实验验证。

Result: Llama实现27/27情绪类别全覆盖，Gemma达23/27（缺失adoration）；随机评判下单格通过概率约为10⁻³，覆盖度不具偶然性；发现跨架构不对称性：Llama直接命名目标情绪，Gemma通过场景和意象唤起情绪；两类架构均包含兼具寄存器标记和情绪发射功能的自我特征，其中RLHF负载最高的自我特征在特定系数下强化Helper-AI人格并产生可分类情绪输出。

Conclusion: 指令微调大语言模型中存在可识别的文学原语组合架构，能够通过稀疏特征组合实现精细情绪控制；不同架构在情绪表达策略上存在显著差异；自我特征具有双重功能（寄存器标记与情绪发射）；整个发现流程计算成本低（单GPU约15分钟/周期），具有实践价值。

Abstract: We characterize a compositional architecture of literary primitives in two instruction-tuned large language models (Llama 3.1 8B-Instruct and Gemma 2 9B-IT) via sparse autoencoders on mid-depth residual streams. Four feature classes emerge: naming-gates that promote lexical tokens of a target affect, an eleven-self cluster of first-person register features, stylistic register modulators (show-don't-tell and defamiliarization), and compositional emotions that arise only from multi-feature steering. Under a forced-choice 5-LLM judge panel applied to a 27-category emotion taxonomy (Cowen-Keltner), Llama reaches full 27/27 coverage by combining naming-gates, multi-feature recipes, and single self-feature steering; Gemma reaches 23/27 with adoration as the single residual strict-fail. Under random judging, the per-cell pass probability is on the order of $10^{-3}$ and the expected number of two-seed false-positive cells across the catalog is negligible, so the observed coverage is not consistent with chance. A cross-architectural asymmetry sits in the strict-versus-soft judge contrast: on the same generations, judges agree more often on Llama outputs than on Gemma outputs because Llama outputs name the target affect more directly while Gemma outputs evoke it through scene and imagery. Both architectures contain self-features that serve simultaneously as register markers and as emotion emitters, including a single most-RLHF-loaded self-feature per architecture that intensifies the institutional Helper-AI persona at one operating regime and produces affect-categorizable output at the same calibrated coefficient. Methodologically, the paper presents a three-stage validation pipeline (logit-lens, LLM-rate, 5-LLM judge) with documented anti-patterns; the total compute is single-GPU and about 15 minutes per emotion-feature discovery cycle.

</details>


### [108] [Metric-Gradient Projection for Stable Multi-Agent Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18809)
*Zuyuan Zhang,Sizhe Tang,Mahdi Imani,Tian Lan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出HPML方法，通过Hodge投影将多智能体联合更新场投影到最近的度量梯度势流上，从而分离集体改进的可积分量与循环交互动力学，提升多智能体强化学习的稳定性与性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一般和博弈的多智能体学习受堆叠更新场支配，每个智能体的策略更新改变其他智能体面临的优化景观。这种耦合将集体改进的可积分量与循环交互动力学纠缠在一起，导致学习缓慢或不稳定。现有方法（如正则化、信用分配、共识方法）通过局部或算法修改稳定MARL，但缺乏几何视角的系统性解决方案。

Method: HPML将多智能体系统的联合更新场视为向量场L²空间的元素，通过Hodge型投影计算到最近度量梯度势流的投影。该投影变分地定义，由Poisson型方程刻画，并通过基于图和摊销神经网络的实现从样本中恢复投影方向。HPML沿投影分量作为更新方向，生成所选度量和采样测度下最接近的度量梯度场。

Result: 理论证明投影动力学存在Lyapunov势，并提供带有显式非势能项加性的均衡间隙界。受控实验验证了几何机制的有效性，CTDE基准测试表明HPML作为MARL流水线中的即插即用投影层，能提升稳定性与规范化回报。

Conclusion: HPML从几何视角解决了多智能体学习中更新场耦合导致的稳定性和收敛速度问题，为MARL提供了理论保证的投影框架，可灵活集成到现有算法流程中。该方法补充而非替代现有稳定化技术。

Abstract: General-sum multi-agent learning is often governed by a stacked update field in which each agent's policy update changes the optimization landscape faced by the others. This coupling can entangle an integrable component of collective improvement with cyclic interaction dynamics, leading to slow or unstable multi-agent learning. Existing approaches, such as regularization, credit assignment, and consensus methods, stabilize MARL through local or algorithmic modifications; HPML complements them by projecting the joint update field onto a metric-gradient component. We introduce \textbf{HPML} (\textbf{H}odge-\textbf{P}rojected \textbf{M}ulti-agent \textbf{L}earning), which views the joint update field of a multi-agent system as an element of an $L^2$ space of vector fields and computes a Hodge-type projection onto the closest metric-gradient potential flow. HPML follows the projected component as the update direction, yielding the closest metric-gradient field under the chosen metric and sampling measure. The projection is defined variationally, characterized by a Poisson-type equation, and implemented through graph-based and amortized neural realizations that recover projected directions from samples. We show that the projected dynamics admit a Lyapunov potential and yield equilibrium-gap bounds with an explicit additive non-potentiality term. Controlled experiments validate the geometric mechanism, and CTDE benchmarks show improved stability and normalized return when HPML is used as a plug-in projection layer in MARL pipelines.

</details>


### [109] [D-PACE: Dynamic Position-Aware Cross-Entropy for Parallel Speculative Drafting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18810)
*Tianyu Wu,Yu Yao,Zhenting Qi,Han Zheng,Zhuohan Wang,Haoran Ma,Lawrence Liao,Himabindu Lakkaraju,Ju Li,Yilun Du*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出D-PACE动态位置感知交叉熵损失函数，通过自适应调整训练权重，显著提升了推测解码中草稿模型的加速效果和生成长度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多token草稿模型的训练目标通常使用固定的位置依赖权重方案（如头部依赖权重或块位置衰减），这些权重无法随着训练过程中限制接受度的位置变化而自适应调整，导致训练效率低下。

Method: 从预期接受草稿长度的可微替代项中推导出逐位置训练权重，使每个位置的权重与其对数概率梯度贡献相匹配，提出D-PACE（Dynamic Position-Aware Cross-Entropy）损失函数，将训练信号动态转移到当前限制接受度的位置。

Result: 在六个基准测试、两种Qwen3-4B草稿深度、两种解码温度和两个额外目标模型上，D-PACE持续改善了实际时钟加速比和平均发射长度，训练时间开销仅为2.3%，且无需修改草稿模型架构或推理过程。

Conclusion: D-PACE通过动态调整位置感知的训练权重，有效解决了固定权重方案的局限性，在几乎不增加训练成本的情况下显著提升了推测解码的性能，具有良好的通用性和实用价值。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates LLM inference by having a small drafter propose tokens that a larger target model verifies in parallel. Recent diffusion-based parallel drafters such as DFlash predict the full B-token block in one forward pass, enabling deeper drafters and longer accepted blocks. However, existing multi-token drafter objectives often use fixed position-dependent weighting schedules, such as head-dependent weights or block-position decays, which do not adapt as the positions limiting acceptance change during training. To address this, we derive per-position training weights from a differentiable surrogate of expected accepted draft length, matching the weight of each position to its log-probability gradient contribution. The resulting loss, D-PACE (Dynamic Position-Aware Cross-Entropy), shifts training signal toward positions that currently limit acceptance as the drafter improves. Across six benchmarks, two Qwen3-4B draft depths, two decoding temperatures, and two additional target models, D-PACE consistently improves both wall-clock speedup and average emitted length, with 2.3\% measured training-time overhead and no changes to the drafter architecture or inference procedure.

</details>


### [110] [PASC: Pipeline-Aware Conformal Prediction with Joint Coverage Guarantees for Multi-Stage NLP and LLM Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18812)
*Varun Kotte*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PASC方法，通过联合最大非一致性分数将多阶段流水线的联合覆盖问题简化为单变量保形预测，在保证有限样本分布无关的联合覆盖率的同时，显著降低了预测集的保守性并提升了计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代NLP和LLM系统通常由多阶段流水线组成（如NER→NED→实体分类），各阶段误差会累积。现有的不确定性量化方法要么独立校准各阶段（无法保证联合覆盖率），要么使用Bonferroni并集界限（保证覆盖但过于保守），缺乏一种既高效又紧致的联合覆盖保证方法。

Method: 提出PASC（Pipeline-Aware Split Conformal）方法，将多阶段联合覆盖问题归约为对联合最大非一致性分数的单一标量保形预测问题。该方法仅需计算单个分位数，即可提供有限样本、分布无关的联合覆盖保证，保证所有K个阶段同时被覆盖的概率至少为1-α。

Result: 在CoNLL-2003三阶段流水线实验中，PASC实现96.4%的端到端覆盖率，显著优于Bonferroni的93.4%和独立CP的86.5%，且平均预测集大小相同（1.083）。在WNUT-17和WikiNEuRal分布偏移设置下，PACC保持目标覆盖率，而独立CP降至59%。计算速度比Bonferroni快1.7倍，在K=6阶段扩展测试中，独立CP端到端覆盖率仅0.53。

Conclusion: PASC为多阶段NLP/LLM流水线提供了一种紧致、高效且具有理论保证的联合不确定性量化方法，该方法可直接应用于复合LLM系统和智能体流水线，解决了现有方法在覆盖率保证和效率之间的权衡问题。

Abstract: Modern NLP and LLM systems are pipelines: named entity recognition (NER) -> entity disambiguation (NED) -> entity typing, retrieval-augmented generation (retriever -> reader), and agentic chains of planner -> tool -> critic. Errors compound across stages, but existing uncertainty quantification methods either calibrate each stage independently (no joint coverage) or apply a Bonferroni union bound (joint coverage, but conservative). We present PASC (Pipeline-Aware Split Conformal), which reduces multi-stage joint coverage to a single scalar conformal prediction problem on the joint maximum nonconformity score. PASC provides a finite-sample distribution-free guarantee that all K stages are simultaneously covered with probability at least 1 - alpha, and is nearly tight up to a 1/(n+1) factor. On a three-stage NER -> NED -> entity-typing pipeline over CoNLL-2003, PASC achieves 96.4% end-to-end coverage versus 93.4% for Bonferroni and 86.5% for independent CP, at identical average prediction set size (1.083). Under distribution shift to WNUT-17 Twitter and WikiNEuRal Wikipedia data, PASC empirically maintains the target coverage in the tested shift settings while independent CP collapses to 59%. PASC requires a single quantile computation, runs 1.7x faster than Bonferroni, and scales to K = 6 stages where independent CP drops to 0.53 end-to-end coverage. The same joint-maximum-score reduction applies directly to compound LLM systems and agent pipelines.

</details>


### [111] [DynaTrain: Fast Online Parallelism Switching for Elastic LLM Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18815)
*Yuanqing Wang,Yuchen Zhang,Hao Lin,Junhao Hu,Chunyang Zhu,Quanlu Zhang,Boxun Li,Guohao Dai,Zhi Yang,Daning Cheng,Yunquan Zhang,Yu Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了DynaTrain，一个支持亚秒级在线重配置的分布式训练系统，通过虚拟参数空间（VPS）抽象实现了任意多维并行度的动态调整，在235B参数规模的模型上将重配置时间缩短至2-4秒，比现有方法快三个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代大语言模型训练具有动态特性：资源波动、RLHF阶段转换和集群弹性持续改变最优并行布局，而现有训练框架基于静态执行模型，无法高效应对这些动态变化。

Method: 提出虚拟参数空间（VPS）抽象，将所有分布式训练状态统一到一个逻辑坐标空间，将任意并行配置转换为确定性映射；在此基础上构建状态路由与转换层，在内存感知、无死锁调度下执行rank-local传输；设计弹性设备管理器，将新拓扑构建与正在进行的训练重叠，以掩盖拓扑变更成本。

Result: 在高达235B参数的稠密和MoE模型上，DynaTrain在2秒内重配置70B稠密模型，在4.36秒内重配置235B MoE模型，相比最先进的基于检查点和弹性系统性能提升达三个数量级，同时保证正确性。

Conclusion: DynaTrain成功实现了分布式训练的快速在线重配置，为大语言模型训练中的动态环境提供了高效、可靠的解决方案，显著提升了训练系统的弹性和适应性。

Abstract: Modern large language model (LLM) training is inherently dynamic: resource fluctuations, RLHF phase shifts, and cluster elasticity continually reshape the optimal parallelism layout, posing a significant challenge to existing training frameworks built around a static execution model. We present DynaTrain, a distributed training system for sub-second, online reconfiguration across arbitrary multi-dimensional parallelism. At its core, we propose a Virtual Parameter Space (VPS) abstraction that unifies all distributed training states under one logical coordinate space, turning any parallelism configuration into a deterministic mapping and collapsing complex transition into manageable geometric intersections. On top of VPS, a state routing-and-transition layer executes rank-local transfers under a memory-aware, deadlock-free schedule, and an Elastic Device Manager overlaps new-world construction with ongoing training to mask topology-change cost. On dense and MoE models up to 235B parameters, DynaTrain reconfigures a 70B dense model in under 2s and a 235B MoE model in 4.36s, outperforming state-of-the-art checkpoint-based and elastic systems by up to three orders of magnitude while preserving correctness.

</details>


### [112] [Composition of Memory Experts for Diffusion World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18813)
*Sebastian Stapf,Pablo Acuaviva Huertos,Aram Davtyan,Paolo Favaro*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于扩散模型的记忆增强框架，通过组合三个专家模型（短期、长期和空间长期记忆）来解决世界模型中记忆保真度与计算效率的权衡问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型架构面临根本性的记忆权衡：Transformer能保留局部细节但受限于二次方注意力复杂度，而循环模型和状态空间模型虽扩展性更好但会压缩历史信息导致保真度下降。需要一种方法能够同时实现局部细节保留、长期记忆和计算效率。

Method: 提出基于扩散模型的框架，通过对比性专家乘积公式集成异构记忆模型。设计三个互补专家角色：(1)短期记忆专家捕获精细局部动态；(2)长期记忆专家通过轻量级测试时微调将情景历史存储在外部扩散权重中；(3)空间长期记忆专家强制几何和空间一致性。这种组合设计避免模式坍塌且能扩展到长上下文。

Result: 在模拟和真实世界基准测试中，该方法在时间一致性、过去观察的回忆和导航性能方面均有提升，且无需承担二次方计算成本。

Conclusion: 建立了一种构建和操作记忆增强扩散世界模型的新范式，证明了通过专业化专家组合可以有效解决记忆保真度与计算效率的权衡问题。

Abstract: World models aim to predict plausible futures consistent with past observations, a capability central to planning and decision-making in reinforcement learning. Yet, existing architectures face a fundamental memory trade-off: transformers preserve local detail but are bottlenecked by quadratic attention, while recurrent and state-space models scale more efficiently but compress history at the cost of fidelity. To overcome this trade-off, we suggest decoupling future-past consistency from any single architecture and instead leveraging a set of specialized experts. We introduce a diffusion-based framework that integrates heterogeneous memory models through a contrastive product-of-experts formulation. Our approach instantiates three complementary roles: a short-term memory expert that captures fine local dynamics, a long-term memory expert that stores episodic history in external diffusion weights via lightweight test-time finetuning, and a spatial long-term memory expert that enforces geometric and spatial coherence. This compositional design avoids mode collapse and scales to long contexts without incurring a quadratic cost. Across simulated and real-world benchmarks, our method improves temporal consistency, recall of past observations, and navigation performance, establishing a novel paradigm for building and operating memory-augmented diffusion world models.

</details>


### [113] [Not All Tokens Are Worth Caching: Learning Semantic-Aware Eviction for LLM Prefix Caches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18825)
*Shaoke Fang,Ziang Li,Wenfei Wu,Jiatong Ji,Qingsong Liu,Ruizhi Pu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SAECache，一种语义自适应的前缀缓存驱逐策略。发现LLM提示词中不同类型token（如系统提示、用户查询等）的复用率差异高达756倍，通过多队列架构、语义感知权重机制和在线自适应学习，相比生产基线实现1.4x-2.7x的TTFT提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM前缀缓存驱逐策略（如LRU）将所有KV块统一对待，忽略了LLM提示词的基本特性：不同类型token的缓存价值存在显著差异。作者观察到系统提示、用户查询、工具输出、模型响应和思维链推理等不同token类型的复用率差异高达756倍，但现有驱逐策略均未利用这一信号，且GPU内存稀缺，驱逐策略直接影响缓存收益。

Method: 提出SAECache，包含三个核心创新：(1) 多队列架构：将KV块路由到任务特定队列，使用定制优先级指标，同时捕获多轮请求中的会话复用和模板化单轮请求中的结构复用；(2) 语义感知token权重机制：通过驱逐反馈在线学习不同token类型的复用价值；(3) 全自适应在线学习框架：自动更新所有参数（包括对数正态时间参数、位置衰减幂次、队列权重和元参数），消除手动调优并自动适应特定部署的工作负载特征。

Result: 在异构工作负载上的广泛评估表明：SAECache相比生产风格基线实现1.4x-2.7x的TTFT（首token时间）改进；而固定参数替代方案在工作负载不匹配情况下性能下降高达2.7x——这种失败模式在自适应方法中完全避免。

Conclusion: SAECache证明了利用LLM提示词语义信息进行缓存驱逐策略优化的有效性。通过语义自适应的方法，系统能够自动识别并优先保留高复用价值的token类型，在无需手动调优的情况下适应不同工作负载，显著提升LLM服务性能。这种方法为未来LLM系统优化提供了新思路：利用提示词的语义结构特征而非将其视为统一数据。

Abstract: Prefix caching is a key optimization in Large Language Model (LLM) serving, reusing attention Key-Value (KV) states across requests with shared prompt prefixes to reduce expensive prefill computation. However, its benefit depends critically on the eviction policy as GPU memory is scarce, and existing policies such as LRU largely treat cached blocks uniformly. This view ignores a fundamental property of LLM prompts: not all tokens are equally worth caching. We show that different token types within a prompt, including system prompts, user queries, tool outputs, model responses, and chain-of-thought reasoning, exhibit up to 756x variation in reuse rates, yet no existing eviction policy exploits this signal. In this paper, we present SAECache (Semantic-Adaptive Eviction for prefix caches), a semantic-adaptive prefix cache eviction policy that addresses this gap through three innovations: (1) a multi-queue architecture that routes KV blocks to task-specific queues with tailored priority metrics, capturing both session reuse in multi-turn requests and structural reuse in templated single-turn requests; (2) a semantic-aware token weighting mechanism that learns the reuse value of different token types online through eviction feedback; and (3) a fully adaptive online learning schema for all parameter updates, including log-normal timing parameters, position decay power, queue weights, and meta-parameters, which eliminates manual tuning and enables automatic adaptation to deployment-specific workload characteristics. Through extensive evaluation across heterogeneous workloads, we demonstrate that SAECache achieves 1.4x-2.7x TTFT improvement over production-style baselines, while fixed-parameter alternatives can degrade by up to 2.7x under workload mismatch -- a failure mode our adaptive approach avoids entirely.

</details>


### [114] [How Faithful Is Trajectory-Based Data Attribution? Error Sources, Remedies, and Practical Guidelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18814)
*Junwei Deng,Pingbang Hu,Suliang Jin,Hao Lu,Jiachen T. Wang,Shichang Zhang,Jiaqi W. Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次系统分析了轨迹数据归因方法的误差来源，并提出相应改进方案。通过修正AdamW优化器失配、分析Taylor近似误差并提出K步前瞻框架，在多个模型上实现了10%-300%以上的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的轨迹数据归因方法缺乏全面的误差分析，引发了对方法可信度的担忧，阻碍了其在实际应用中的可靠部署。这些方法被广泛用于数据选择、数据估值和模型诊断等场景，但其误差来源尚不明确。

Method: 将总误差分为三个层次：配置层、算法层和系统层。针对配置层误差，识别出优化器失配为主要问题，提出AdamW-influence方法以完全考虑AdamW的优化动态。针对算法层误差，分析一阶Taylor近似产生的误差，识别学习率和轨迹长度为关键因素，推导出闭式误差代理。针对系统层应用，提出K步前瞻框架统一离线和在线选择策略。

Result: 在MLP、CNN、GPT-2和Llama 3.2-1B四个模型设置上，AdamW-influence方法使估计影响与真实影响之间的Spearman相关性提升10%至300%以上。推导出可在原始轨迹上评估的闭式误差代理，无需重新训练。验证了在线选择策略在短视域下经常匹配或超越离线方法。

Conclusion: 本文为轨迹数据归因方法提供了首个系统性误差分析框架，并转化为可操作的数据选择实践指南。研究结果表明优化器失配是关键误差源，现代优化器需要专门的归因方法。K步前瞻框架为实践者提供了统一的选择策略，可根据学习率联合调节最优视域。

Abstract: Trajectory-based data attribution methods estimate the influence of training samples on model predictions by unrolling the training trajectory. They are widely used in applications such as data selection, data valuation, and model diagnosis, but there is a lack of comprehensive error analysis of these methods, raising concerns about method faithfulness and hindering reliable deployment. In this work, we provide the first systematic analysis of error sources in trajectory-based data attribution, together with concrete remedies to mitigate them and practical guidelines for downstream use. We organize the total error into three categories, config-level, algorithm-level, and system-level. We make three contributions. First, we identify optimizer mismatch as the dominant config-level error: existing methods derive their attribution under the assumption of SGD, even for models trained with the modern de facto optimizer AdamW. We propose AdamW-influence to fully account for AdamW's optimization dynamics, yielding improvements from 10% to over 300% in Spearman correlation between estimated and ground-truth influence across four settings spanning MLP, CNN, GPT-2, and Llama 3.2-1B. Second, we isolate the remaining algorithm-level error arising from the first-order Taylor approximation, identify the learning rate and trajectory length as factors governing the error magnitude, and derive a closed-form error proxy that can be evaluated along the original trajectory without retraining. Third, we translate these insights into practical guidelines for data selection by unifying offline and online strategies under a K-step look-ahead framework. Under this framework, online selection with a short horizon often matches or exceeds offline, and the optimal horizon can be tuned jointly with the learning rate. Together, these results turn the framework into an actionable selection recipe for practitioners.

</details>


### [115] [INAR-VL: Input-Aware Routing for Edge-Cloud Vision-Language Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18853)
*Ahmed Šabanović,Paul Joe Maliakel,Ivona Brandić*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出INAR-VL系统，一种轻量级的边云路由方案，通过图像和文本复杂度信号动态决策推理位置，在保持97%云端精度的同时，实现了24%延迟降低和26%能耗节省。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型（VLM）的边缘部署面临延迟与精度的权衡困境：云端推理精度高但通信延迟和能耗大，边缘端推理速度快但受限于模型容量导致精度下降。此外，图像质量和推理复杂度的异构性使得静态部署策略难以达到最优效果。

Method: 提出INAR-VL系统，采用两层部署架构，在边缘和云端维护互补的VLM模型；利用轻量级图像和文本复杂度信号作为路由依据，指导推理位置的动态选择和模型配置；策略上简单查询本地执行，复杂查询按需卸载至云端。

Result: 在视觉问答任务上的评估表明：INAR-VL在边缘端执行36%的请求，整体延迟降低24%，能耗减少26%，同时保持云端级别97%的预测精度。

Conclusion: INAR-VL证明了基于复杂度信号的动态边云路由策略能够有效平衡延迟、能耗与精度，为VLM的边缘部署提供了高效可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Edge deployment of Vision-Language Models (VLMs) faces a tradeoff between latency and accuracy: cloud execution provides high-quality predictions but incurs communication delay and energy cost, while edge-only execution is faster but less accurate due to limited model capacity. This trade-off is further complicated by heterogeneity in image quality and reasoning complexity, making static placement suboptimal. We present INAR-VL, a lightweight edge-cloud routing system for multimodal inference in a two-tier deployment. INAR-VL maintains complementary VLMs across edge and cloud and uses lightweight image and text complexity signals to guide routing and model selection, executing simple queries locally while offloading complex ones when beneficial. Evaluation on visual question answering shows that INAR-VL executes 36% of requests on the edge, reduces latency by 24%, lowers energy by 26%, and preserves 97% of cloud-level accuracy.

</details>


### [116] [Data-Free Client Contribution Estimation via Logit Maximization for Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18892)
*Asim Ukaye,Nurbek Tastan,Mubarak Abdu-Aguye,Karthik Nandakumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了CELM框架，通过基于logit最大化的无数据类级别贡献估计方法，解决了联邦学习中严重的类别不平衡和标签偏斜问题，在不共享原始数据、客户端元数据或辅助公共数据集的情况下，显著提升了模型在非独立同分布环境下的鲁棒性和性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习在实际部署中经常面临严重的类别不平衡和标签偏斜问题，导致标准聚合协议过拟合主导客户端，从而降低少数类别的性能表现。现有的解决方案往往需要共享额外数据或元数据，这与隐私保护原则相悖。因此，亟需一种能够在保护隐私的前提下有效处理类别不平衡问题的联邦学习聚合方法。

Method: 提出了一种无数据的类级别贡献估计和聚合框架CELM（基于logit最大化）。该方法通过FL服务器探测客户端更新以获取类级别证据分数，构建跨客户端证据矩阵来量化每个类别的竞争力和类别覆盖度。基于该矩阵计算贡献权重，为代表性不足的类别提供强区分性证据的客户端赋予更高权重。采用单纯形约束和动量平滑确保聚合过程的稳定性，并与标准FL训练流程兼容。

Result: 在代表性视觉基准测试上，针对受控的非独立同分布和病理性标签分割进行了评估。实验结果表明，基于CELM的聚合方法提升了对不平衡和统计异质性的鲁棒性，在不要求任何额外数据交换的情况下获得了更优的性能表现。

Conclusion: CELM框架成功解决了联邦学习中的类别不平衡问题，通过创新的类级别贡献估计机制实现了数据无关的聚合优化。该方法在保护隐私的同时，有效改善了少数类别的性能，为联邦学习在现实场景中的部署提供了实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) enables collaborative learning of computer vision models, where privacy and regulatory constraints prevent centralizing data across devices or organizations. However, practical FL deployments often exhibit severe class imbalance and label skew, causing standard aggregation protocols to overfit dominant clients and degrade minority-class performance. We propose a data-free, class-wise contribution estimation and aggregation framework based on logit maximization (CELM) that does not require sharing raw data, client metadata, or auxiliary public datasets. The FL server probes client updates to obtain class-wise evidence scores and assembles a cross-client evidence matrix, which quantifies both per-class competence and class coverage. Using this matrix, we compute contribution weights that upweight clients providing strong, discriminative evidence for underrepresented classes. The resulting aggregation is stable due to simplex constraints and momentum smoothing, and it remains compatible with standard FL training pipelines. We evaluate the approach on representative vision benchmarks under controlled non-IID and pathological label splits, demonstrating that CELM-based aggregation improves robustness to imbalance and statistical heterogeneity, while yielding better performance without requiring any additional data exchange.

</details>


### [117] [Symmetry in the Wild: The Role of Equivariance in Neural Fluid Surrogates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18816)
*Patryk Rygiel,Julian Suk,Kak Khee Yeung,Christoph Brune,Jelmer M. Wolterink*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在神经计算流体力学（CFD）代理模型中，等变性并非总是有益的：在强分布对齐的空气动力学数据集上可能降低性能，但在几何多样的血流动力学问题中表现优异，且显式等变性优于数据增强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经CFD代理模型在工程和医疗领域具有重要应用价值，但在实际应用中面临大规模高分辨率网格的可扩展性、定制架构适配以及训练数据有限等挑战。群等变架构是通过归纳偏置解决数据限制的有原则方法，然而当学习问题本身因数据集中的强分布对齐而破坏对称性时，强制等变性可能产生负面影响。目前尚不清楚在何种条件下等变性能改善神经CFD代理模型的泛化能力。

Method: 作者提出了锚定分支几何代数Transformer（AB-GATr），这是一种集成了可扩展性和对称性保持的神经代理模型，能够以E(3)等变方式高效建模耦合的表面和体积物理量。通过在分布对齐程度和真实性递增的任务中进行系统评估，涵盖汽车空气动力学和血流动力学领域，对比分析了等变性的效果，并将显式等变性与通过数据增强实现的隐式对称学习进行了比较。

Result: 在强对齐的空气动力学数据集（即破坏对称性的数据集）上，强制等变性会降低分布内性能；相反，在具有多样几何结构和不同对齐程度的血流动力学基准测试中，等变性始终有益。在所有基准测试中，AB-GATr的显式等变性可靠地优于通过数据增强实现的隐式对称学习。

Conclusion: 等变性在不同领域中并非普遍有益：在缺乏强数据规律性的问题中，等变性带来显著优势；但在强分布对齐导致对称性破坏的场景下可能有害。因此，应根据具体问题的数据特性审慎选择是否采用等变架构。

Abstract: Neural surrogates enable orders-of-magnitude acceleration of computational fluid dynamics (CFD) simulations, with the potential to transform engineering and healthcare workflows. Neural surrogate use in real-world applications requires addressing scalability to large, high-resolution surface and volume meshes, as well as to bespoke architectures, and accounting for limited training data through the use of inductive biases. Group-equivariant architectures are a principled way to introduce such bias, yet they can be detrimental when the learning problem itself breaks symmetry, for example, due to strong distributional alignment in the dataset. In this work, we investigate under which conditions equivariance improves generalization in neural CFD surrogates across tasks with increasing levels of distributional alignment and realism, covering automotive aerodynamics and blood flow (hemodynamics). To systematically assess the added value of equivariance at the limit of problem scaling, we introduce the Anchored-Branched Geometric Algebra Transformer (AB-GATr), a neural surrogate that integrates scalability and symmetry preservation to efficiently model coupled surface and volume quantities in an $E(3)$-equivariant manner. We find that on strongly aligned aerodynamics datasets, i.e., those that break symmetry, enforcing equivariance can degrade in-distribution performance. In contrast, across hemodynamic benchmarks with diverse geometries and varying alignment, equivariance is consistently beneficial. Moreover, across all benchmarks, the explicit equivariance of AB-GATr reliably outperforms implicit symmetry learning through data augmentation. Our findings showcase that equivariance is not universally beneficial across domains, yet it brings tangible advantages in problems lacking strong data regularities.

</details>


### [118] [Multi-Token Residual Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18817)
*Yufeng Xu,Zishuo Bao,Qian Wang,Zeshen Zhang,Haoqi Zhang,Bowen Peng,Ang Li,Rahul Chalamala,Yucheng Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了多令牌残差预测（MRP）模块，利用去噪过程中相邻步骤logit分布相似的特性，通过预测步骤间的残差在单次前向传播中实现依赖感知的多令牌去噪，在SDAR模型上实现高达1.42倍的无损加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散语言模型通过置信度阈值控制每步解码的令牌数量，但随着每步去噪令牌数增加，生成质量单调下降。研究者希望在不牺牲质量的前提下，提高每步去噪的令牌数量，从而加速推理过程。

Method: 提出了多令牌残差预测（MRP）轻量级模块。该方法基于关键观察：相邻去噪步骤的logit分布非常相似。MRP从主干网络的隐藏状态预测步骤间的残差，而非运行第二次主干网络来获取下一步的logit，从而以极低的成本在单次前向传播中完成更多令牌的去噪。MRP支持两种推理模式：直接解码（可调质量-速度权衡）和推测解码（通过验证实现无损加速）。

Result: 在1.7B、4B和8B规模的SDAR模型上进行实验，覆盖推理和代码生成基准测试。在SGLang框架下实现了高达1.42倍的无损加速。

Conclusion: MRP模块成功解决了扩散语言模型中并行性与质量之间的权衡问题，通过利用去噪过程的固有特性，在不增加显著计算成本的情况下实现了有效的推理加速。该方法为扩散语言模型的高效部署提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: Diffusion Language Models (DLMs) generate text by iteratively denoising masked token sequences, offering a tradeoff between parallelism and quality compared to autoregressive models. In current practice, the number of tokens decoded per step is controlled by a confidence threshold, and quality degrades monotonically as more tokens are denoised per step. We introduce Multi-token Residual Prediction (MRP), a lightweight module that enables dependency-aware multi-token denoising within a single backbone forward pass. MRP exploits a key property of the denoising process: the logit distributions at adjacent denoising steps are remarkably similar. Rather than running the backbone a second time to obtain the next-step logits, MRP predicts the residual between steps from the backbone's hidden states, effectively denoising more tokens per backbone forward at a fraction of the cost. We deploy MRP in two inference modes: direct decoding, which uses the corrected logits without verification for a tunable quality--speed tradeoff; and speculative decoding, which verifies MRP's proposals against the backbone for lossless acceleration. Experiments on SDAR models at the 1.7B, 4B, and 8B scales across reasoning and code generation benchmarks demonstrate up to $1.42\times$ lossless speedup in SGLang.

</details>


### [119] [Efficient Conditioning Why Pseudo Observation Batch Bayesian Optimization Works When It Does not](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18819)
*Kumbha Nagaswetha,Rabi Pathak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了高效条件化是并行贝叶斯优化批量选择方法的核心机制，统一了CL、KB和幻想模型的理论框架，提出了结构多样性诊断方法，证明了高斯过程在此机制下的优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 并行贝叶斯优化中广泛使用的批量选择方法（Constant Liar、Kriging Believer和幻想模型）缺乏统一的理论解释，无法阐明其有效性原因及失败条件，亟需建立统一的理论框架。

Method: 1. 提出高效条件化概念，证明高斯过程满足闭式更新预测的要求；2. 将CL、KB和幻想模型统一为单一条件化机制的不同实例；3. 引入结构多样性诊断（SDD）方法分离模型结构与优化器随机性；4. 在多个基准函数和SVM超参数调优任务上进行实验验证。

Result: 1. 高斯过程产生可证明的分离批量点，分离度为l量级；2. CL或KB的隐式惩罚匹配或优于显式局部惩罚；3. 贪婪条件化收敛性能与联合qEI相当；4. 高效条件化可扩展至多二次RBF网络；5. 参数化代理（随机森林）产生退化批量，神经网络需15倍于GP条件化的时间成本才能恢复多样性。

Conclusion: 高效条件化是并行贝叶斯优化批量选择的本质机制，高斯过程因其闭式更新能力成为理想代理模型，结构多样性诊断为代理模型兼容性测试提供了标准化方法，为并行贝叶斯优化的理论和实践提供了完整框架。

Abstract: Constant Liar (CL), Kriging Believer (KB), and fantasy models are widely used for batch selection in parallel Bayesian Optimization, yet a unified theory explaining their effectiveness and conditions under which they fail has been lacking. We identify efficient conditioning as the key surrogate property the ability to update predictions in closed form when data is augmented. We prove that Gaussian Processes satisfy this requirement, producing provably distinct batch points with separation of order l, and that this holds for any acquisition function monotonically non decreasing in posterior uncertainty (EI, UCB, PI), with qualitatively similar behavior for Thompson Sampling. We unify CL, KB, and fantasy models as instances of a single conditioning mechanism differing only in the lie value distribution, and draw quantitative connections to Local Penalization (LP) and qualitative connections to Determinantal Point Processes (DPPs). To disentangle model structure from optimizer randomness, we introduce the Structural Diversity Diagnostic (SDD), a reusable methodology for testing surrogate compatibility. Experiments on Hartmann6D, Ackley 8D, Levy10D, and SVM hyperparameter tuning validate all theoretical predictions: CL or KBs implicit penalty matches or outperforms explicit LP greedy conditioning achieves convergence on par with joint qEI efficient conditioning extends to Multiquadric RBF networks; and parametric surrogates produce degenerate batches even when fully retrained (random forests), while neural networks regain diversity only at 15x the wall clock cost of GP conditioning. Robustness is confirmed across multiple initial datasets and under observation noise.

</details>


### [120] [Emergence of Frontier Superposition: Möbius attractor and Cascade Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18820)
*Hongyu Gu,Jingwen Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了梯度下降可以通过叠加机制使Transformer进行深度推理。作者发现了莫比乌斯吸引子架构特性和级联监督方法，解决了端到端监督的梯度衰减问题，理论预测与实验结果高度吻合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究表明Transformer可以通过叠加机制在有限深度的前向传播中并行执行推理，但梯度下降能否在置换对称的鞍点中找到目标解仍是一个开放问题。本文旨在填补这一空白，研究梯度下降如何实现基于叠加的可达性推理。

Method: 作者从架构和监督两个维度进行分析：(1) 架构方面，在树形机制下识别出莫比乌斯吸引子，发现层级动力学可简化为一维莫比乌斯映射，其零点集为包含等权叠加状态的全局最优流形；(2) 监督方面，提出级联监督损失类，满足选择性引导、跨深度梯度持久性和每步区分性三个条件。

Result: 证明了端到端监督在层c处的内部梯度按(np)^{-(D-c-2)/2}衰减，导致优化停滞。参数无关的衰减定律预测：在深度D=3时，端到端监督与级联监督的最终余弦相似度分别为0.35和0.71，实验结果为0.37和0.69，每步误差均在0.02以内。

Conclusion: 莫比乌斯吸引子架构特性与级联监督方法的结合促成了叠加推理的涌现，成功解决了梯度下降在置换对称环境下的优化难题。

Abstract: Superposition allows Transformers to reason in depth, carrying an entire reasoning frontier in parallel through a bounded-depth forward pass instead of unrolling serial chain-of-thought tokens. While Zhu et al. (2025) hand-crafted an equal-weight breadth-first frontier in a single residual stream for graph reachability, it remained open whether gradient descent could ever find this target amidst permutation-symmetric saddles.
  We close this gap on Reachability-by-Superposition over Erdős-Rényi graphs by isolating architectural and supervisional contributions. Architecturally, we identify a Möbius attractor: under $S_n$-symmetry in the tree regime, layerwise dynamics reduce to a 1D Möbius map whose zero set is a codimension-one manifold of global optima containing the equal-weight superposition state.
  On the supervision side, we identify Cascade Supervision: a loss class whose backward pass simultaneously delivers (A) selectivity bootstrap, (B) gradient persistence across depth, and (C) per-step discrimination (e.g., \mathcal{L}_{sup} and \mathcal{L}_{node}). End-to-end supervision fails condition (B) and is provably insufficient: internal gradients at layer c decay as (np)^{-(D-c-2)/2} in the graph fan-out and stall before the manifold is reached.
  Our thesis: Möbius attractor + Cascade Supervision = emergence of superposition reasoning. The parameter-free decay law predicts a final-step cosine of 0.35 vs. 0.71 (end-to-end vs. cascade) at depth D=3; experiments confirm 0.37 vs. 0.69, matching within 0.02 at every step.

</details>


### [121] [Quantum Adversarial Machine Learning: From Classical Adaptations to Quantum-Native Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18821)
*Roozbeh Razavi-Far,Mohammad Meymani,Erfan Mahmoudinia,Dorsa Vazirzade,Peyman Paknezhad,Fateme Ghasemi,Saeed Saravani,Somayeh Nikkhoo,Kimia Haghjooei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文是一篇关于量子对抗机器学习的综述，详细介绍了量子机器学习面临的对抗攻击威胁、防御策略以及该领域的理论基础与挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习模型易受对抗攻击，而新兴的量子机器学习领域同样存在此脆弱性。随着量子计算与机器学习的快速发展，系统研究量子机器学习中的对抗威胁与防御机制变得迫切且必要。

Method: 作者通过综述的方式，系统梳理了量子对抗机器学习领域的现有研究，涵盖理论基础、对抗攻击方法、防御策略以及新兴趋势。

Result: 本文提供了量子对抗机器学习领域的详细概览，归纳了目前已知的攻击手段与防御措施，并总结了该领域的理论基础与关键挑战。

Conclusion: 量子对抗机器学习是一个快速发展的交叉领域，理解其脆弱性与防御策略对于构建鲁棒的量子机器学习模型至关重要，未来仍需解决诸多理论与实际挑战。

Abstract: Machine learning has revolutionized numerous industrial domains. Despite recent advances, machine learning models remain vulnerable to adversarial threats. Adversarial machine learning is a field that studies these vulnerabilities to build robust machine learning models. Quantum machine learning is an interdisciplinary field that bridges quantum computing and classical machine learning. While quantum machine learning shows potentials to outperform classical machine learning in complex tasks such as regression, classification, and generative modeling, it remains vulnerable to adversarial attacks. Given the recent advancements in quantum computing and machine learning, the quantum adversarial machine learning field has emerged to study the vulnerabilities of quantum machine learning, possible attacks, and novel quantum-enhanced defense strategies. In this survey, we provide a detailed overview on quantum adversarial machine learning and explore the existing attacks and countermeasures. We also review the theoretical underpinnings of this area, emerging trends, and critical challenges.

</details>


### [122] [Hybrid-LoRA: Bridging Full Fine-Tuning and Low-Rank Adaptation for Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18822)
*Chengqian Zhang,Wei Zhu,Kyumin Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Hybrid-LoRA框架，在LLM后训练中混合使用全量微调和LoRA，在仅10%模块全量微调的预算下，性能逼近全量微调并显著优于现有PEFT方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习可验证奖励（RLVR）是提升大模型推理能力的有效后训练范式，但其全量微调（FFT）计算成本高昂，而传统参数高效微调（PEFT）方法如LoRA在复杂推理任务上存在明显的性能差距。

Method: 提出Hybrid-LoRA混合后训练框架，引入Hybrid-LoRA Score指标对候选模块进行排序，识别对低秩适应敏感的模块，在固定参数预算下选择性地对少量模块进行全量微调，其余模块则使用LoRA进行适应。

Result: 在10%全量微调模块预算下，Hybrid-LoRA性能接近全量微调；相比四个最先进的PEFT基线方法，最高提升5.65%，平均提升4.36%。

Conclusion: Hybrid-LoRA成功平衡了后训练的计算效率与性能表现，证明了选择性混合微调策略的有效性，为资源受限场景下的大模型推理能力提升提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Post-training has become essential for adapting large language models (LLMs) to complex downstream behaviors, including instruction following, preference alignment, and multi-step reasoning. Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has recently emerged as a particularly effective post-training paradigm for improving reasoning capabilities, with critic-free algorithms such as GRPO and GSPO enabling scalable optimization. However, RLVR post-training with full fine-tuning (FFT) requires substantial GPU memory and incurs high training costs. Although parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) methods, such as Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA), effectively reduce computational costs, they often suffer from a noticeable performance gap compared to full fine-tuning in post-training for complex reasoning tasks. In this paper, we propose Hybrid-LoRA, an efficient hybrid post-training framework that selectively applies full fine-tuning to a small subset of modules less suited to low-rank adaptation, while adapting the remaining components with LoRA. We introduce a novel Hybrid-LoRA Score to rank candidate modules according to their sensitivity to low-rank adaptation under a fixed parameter budget. Experiments show that Hybrid-LoRA closely matches full fine-tuning performance under a 10% full fine-tuning module budget, with the remaining candidate modules adapted by LoRA, consistently outperforming four state-of-the-art PEFT post-training baselines, achieving improvements of up to 5.65% and on average 4.36% over the best baseline.

</details>


### [123] [Multi-Pedestrian Safety Warning at Urban Intersections Use Case of Digital Twin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18823)
*Yongjie Fu,Qi Gao,Mahshid Ghasemi Dehkordi,Gil Zussman,Xuan Di*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于虚实融合框架的城市交叉口多行人安全预警系统，通过集成摄像头、UWB、边缘云计算和轨迹预测技术，实现了对弱势道路使用者的实时安全预警。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字孪生技术在城市交通系统中的应用日益受到关注，但其在安全关键场景下的系统性评估仍然有限，特别是在复杂的城市交叉口多行人安全预警方面缺乏有效的解决方案。

Method: 基于纽约市的COSMOS城市级无线测试平台，构建了一个紧耦合的物理-数字孪生框架。系统集成摄像头和超宽带(UWB)传感器、边缘-云计算架构、预测性轨迹建模以及基于MQTT的通信机制，实现对弱势道路使用者(VRUs)的实时安全预警。系统通过实地部署和虚拟现实(VR)实验进行评估。

Result: 实验结果表明，该系统在不同模型配置下具有较高的预警生成准确率、定位精度和高效的端到端延迟；预警发出后，用户的响应时间显著减少。

Conclusion: 所提出的数字孪生框架为复杂城市交叉口的实时多行人安全增强提供了一个可扩展、模块化和可推广的解决方案。

Abstract: Digital twins (DTs) for urban transportation systems have gained increasing attention; however, their systematic evaluation in safety-critical scenarios remains limited. This paper presents a multi-pedestrian safety warning system at urban intersections enabled by a tightly coupled physical-digital twin framework. Built upon the COSMOS city-scale wireless testbed in New York City, the proposed system integrates camera and ultra-wideband (UWB), edge-cloud computing, predictive trajectory modeling, and MQTT-based communication to deliver real-time safety alerts to vulnerable road users (VRUs). The system is evaluated through both field deployment and virtual reality (VR) experiments. Results demonstrate high warning generation accuracy, localization accuracy, efficient end-to-end latency under different model configurations, and significant reductions in user response time when warnings are issued. The proposed DT framework provides a scalable, modular, and generalizable solution for real-time multi-pedestrian safety enhancement at complex urban intersections.

</details>


### [124] [Fine-Grained Benchmark Generation for Comprehensive Evaluation of Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18824)
*Mohammed Saidul Islam,Negin Baghbanzadeh,Farnaz Kohankhaki,Afshin Cheraghi,Ali Kore,Shayaan Mehdi,Elham Dolatabadi,Arash Afkanpour*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于教材等参考材料的自动基准测试生成框架，通过多智能体架构和解决方案图策略生成高质量、广覆盖的基准测试，用于更精细地评估基础模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基础模型评估通常依赖于聚合分数，这些分数来自缺乏全面覆盖率和元数据的基准测试，无法进行细粒度的评估。

Method: 提出了一种自动基准测试生成框架，利用多智能体架构生成问题，并采用解决方案图驱动的策略提高基本真值解的可靠性；基于教材等参考材料生成具有广泛覆盖率和丰富元数据的基准测试。

Result: 生成了机器学习、企业金融和个人金融三个基准测试；专家评审发现其基本真值错误率显著低于MMLU和GSM8K等现有基准；对12个商业和开源模型的评估表明，该基准测试能实现近乎一致的能力覆盖，并揭示了现有基准测试未能捕捉到的模型性能差异。

Conclusion: 该框架和基准测试将很快开源，为未来基础模型的细粒度评估提供了有力工具。

Abstract: Evaluation of foundation models often rely on aggregate scores from benchmarks that lack comprehensive coverage and metadata for a fine-grained evaluation. We introduce a framework for automated benchmark generation. Our framework generates evaluation problems grounded in reference material, such as textbooks, producing benchmarks with broad coverage, rich metadata, and robustness to contamination. The pipeline employs a multi-agent architecture for problem generation and a solution-graph-driven strategy that significantly improves the reliability of ground truth solutions. Using the framework, we generate three benchmarks in Machine Learning, Corporate Finance, and Personal Finance. Expert review finds a significantly lower ground-truth error rate than previous benchmarks such as MMLU and GSM8K. Evaluation of 12 commercial and open-source models shows that our benchmarks achieve near-uniform competency coverage and surface performance differences across models that existing benchmarks fail to capture. We will open-source the framework and our curated benchmarks soon.

</details>


### [125] [The Routing and Filtering Structure of Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18826)
*Shafayeth Jamil,Rehan Kapadia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现注意力矩阵包含路由（信息重分配）和过滤（相关性缩放）两种纠缠成分。通过解耦发现路由以低秩方式自组织成谱级联，随深度扩展。这一发现指导了模型简化：前几层可线性化而性能损失很小，并可据此设计更高效的架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准注意力机制中的$QK^{\top}$矩阵同时包含两种计算：负责位置间信息重分配的路由成分和负责缩放相关性的过滤成分。这两种成分的纠缠使得理解注意力如何分配其"谱预算"变得困难，阻碍了对模型复杂度的简化与优化。

Method: 1. 将$QK^{\top}$分解为斜对称（路由）和对称（过滤）两部分；
2. 分析五个预训练Transformer中1776个注意力头的路由秩；
3. 提出$S$-$D$注意力参数化方法，从结构上解耦路由与过滤，保证稳定性且无需层归一化；
4. 观察解耦后路由的谱级联现象，并据此进行层线性化实验和架构优化。

Result: 1. 路由操作于远低于权重核容量的低秩空间；
2. 解耦后的路由自组织成谱级联：第一层有效秩为2，随深度扩展；
3. 前7层线性化仅损失不到5%困惑度（标准注意力会崩溃）；
4. 用ELU+1线性注意替换前4层，性能仅差1.4%；
5. 基于级联分配的架构可减少47%-65%的注意力参数，困惑度仅增加3.9%-8.4%。

Conclusion: 路由-过滤分解使注意力机制的谱预算变得可解释，谱级联现象使其可操作化。这一发现为注意力简化提供了理论指导：早期低秩层可安全线性化，并支持设计参数效率更高的架构。

Abstract: The attention interaction matrix $QK^{\top}$ contains two entangled computations: a skew-symmetric component that redistributes information between positions (routing) and a symmetric component that scales mutual relevance (filtering). We decompose 1776 heads across five pretrained transformers and find routing operating at low rank, well below the routing capacity allocated by the weight kernel. We introduce $S$-$D$ attention as a diagnostic parameterization that disentangles routing from filtering by construction with guaranteed stability ($\mathrm{Re}(λ) \le 0$) and trains stably without layer normalization. When disentangled and unnormalized, routing self-organizes into a spectral cascade, effective rank $2$ at the first layer, expanding with depth across six scales from 7M to 355M parameters. The cascade predicts where attention can be simplified: linearizing the first seven layers of 125M $S$-$D$ attention costs ${<}5\%$ perplexity, whereas standard attention collapses under the same intervention. The linearizable region widens with depth. Replacing the first four layers with ELU+1 linear attention reaches within $1.4\%$ of baseline at full head dimension. Cascade-allocated architectures trade attention parameters for perplexity ($47\%-65\%$ fewer attention parameters at $+3.9\%$ to $+8.4\%$ PPL). The routing-filtering decomposition makes the spectral budget legible; the cascade makes it actionable.

</details>


### [126] [Lossless Anti-Distillation Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18829)
*Zibo Diao,Jingchu Gai,Xinyue Ai,Zhang Zhang,Zhenyu He,Di He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出无损反蒸馏采样（LADS），通过基于语义内容和用户查询次数的私有种子生成随机性，在保证正常用户体验无损的前提下，有效对抗多账号蒸馏攻击，降低攻击者的模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成模型面临蒸馏攻击威胁，攻击者可低成本获取模型输出来训练竞争模型。现有防御方法要么牺牲正常用户的响应质量，要么易被多账号分布查询绕过，因此亟需一种既能保持用户体验又能有效防御多账号蒸馏的方法。

Method: 提出无损反蒸馏采样（LADS）：从查询的语义内容和用户查询次数确定的私有种子中推导生成随机性。正常用户每次获得独立采样的原始响应，而蒸馏者的不同账号在相同语义桶下的查询会共享潜在随机性，导致采集数据相关性增加、多样性降低。基于一致收敛理论，在无条件生成和条件生成设置下均能证明LADS降低了蒸馏者泛化差距的收敛率。

Result: 在图像生成、数学推理和代码生成任务上的实验表明，LADS显著降低了蒸馏学生模型的性能，同时为单个用户保持了精确的统计保真度。理论分析证明LADS在数学上可证明地降低了蒸馏者的泛化收敛速率。

Conclusion: LADS成功实现了对多账号蒸馏攻击的有效防御，在不牺牲正常用户体验的前提下，通过引入语义相关的随机性机制，使攻击者采集的数据相关性增加，从而显著降低蒸馏模型的性能，为商业生成模型提供了一种实用的保护方案。

Abstract: Frontier commercial generative models face a growing threat from distillation, whereby a distiller harvests generated responses and trains a competing model of its own at drastically lower cost. Existing defenses either rely on modifying the models outputs, thereby sacrificing response quality for benign users, or on behavioral detection methods, which can be readily circumvented by distributing queries across multiple accounts. In this work, we propose Lossless Anti-Distillation Sampling (LADS), a novel sampling scheme specifically designed to counter multi-account distillation while maintaining a lossless experience for benign users. Concretely, LADS derives the randomness underlying each generation from a private seed determined by the semantic content of the query and the number of times the user has queried the model. By construction, every benign user receives a response independently sampled from the original model at each visit, and thus experiences no distortion. In contrast, for a distiller, different accounts share latent randomness whenever their queries fall in the same semantic bucket. As a result, the harvested data becomes correlated, potentially reducing sample diversity and degrading generalization. Using uniform convergence theory, we show that LADS provably degrades the convergence rate of the distillers generalization gap relative to standard i.i.d. sampling in both unconditional and conditional generation settings. Experiments on image generation, mathematical reasoning, and code generation confirm that LADS substantially degrades the performance of distilled students while preserving exact statistical fidelity for individual users.

</details>


### [127] [In-Context Learning Operates as Concept Subspace Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18830)
*Wei Tang,Xinyan Jiang,Fakhri Karray,Lijie Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了上下文学习(ICL)的“概念子空间”视角，证明任务信息集中在低维激活子空间中。实验表明，仅68-73维子空间就能恢复78.8%的准确率差距，验证了概念子空间作为ICL行为紧凑中介的作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ICL研究缺乏对结构化演示是否诱导低维概念推断的清晰理解。回归和贝叶斯方法解释了演示如何诱导预测器，机制分析识别了引导行为的激活方向，但任务信息如何在激活空间中组织尚不清楚，需要建立任务变化与内在概念坐标之间的关系。

Method: 提出概念子空间视角，将任务变化建模为沿内在概念坐标的变化。对岭回归和最小二乘ICL代理进行理论分解，将预测分解为概念坐标回归和子空间外泄漏。假设块对角或近似块对角协方差结构，推导估计项和敏感度项的缩放规律。使用Llama-3-8B在CounterFact多关系提示上进行实验，测试68-73维子空间的恢复能力，并在Qwen2.5-7B和跨语言规则任务上验证。

Result: 在Llama-3-8B上，68-73维子空间（占4096维残留流的1.7%）恢复了78.8%的干净-损坏准确率差距，而补丁互补子空间恢复0%。概念交换成功重定向预测，随机和跨任务匹配秩对照基本无效。Qwen2.5-7B和跨语言任务显示相同模式，支持概念子空间作为可恢复ICL行为的紧凑中介。

Conclusion: 概念子空间作为结构化任务家族中可恢复ICL行为的紧凑、任务对齐中介发挥作用，提供了解释ICL机制的新理论框架。但这并不意味着完整电路恢复，仅表明任务相关信息在激活空间中的组织方式。

Abstract: Regression and Bayesian accounts of in-context learning (ICL) explain how demonstrations can induce predictors, while mechanistic analyses often identify compact activation directions that steer prompted behavior. However, it remains unclear whether structured demonstrations induce low-dimensional concept inference. We study this question through a concept-subspace view of ICL, in which tasks vary only along intrinsic concept coordinates, although inputs are observed in a high-dimensional ambient space. For ridge and least-squares ICL proxies, prediction decomposes exactly into concept-coordinate regression and off-subspace leakage. Under block-diagonal or near-block-diagonal covariance assumptions, the leading estimation and nuisance-sensitivity terms scale with the dimension of the concept subspace, while residual effects are controlled by cross-subspace coupling. This separation gives a mechanistic prediction: recoverable task information should concentrate in a low-dimensional, task-aligned activation subspace. On CounterFact-derived multi-relation prompts with Llama-3-8B, a 68--73-dimensional subspace of the 4096-dimensional residual stream restores 78.8% of the clean--corrupted accuracy gap, whereas patching the complementary subspace restores 0%. Concept swaps redirect predictions toward injected relations, while random and cross-task matched-rank controls are largely ineffective. Additional experiments on Qwen2.5-7B and a controlled cross-lingual rule task show the same qualitative pattern. These results support concept subspaces as compact, task-aligned mediators of recoverable ICL behavior in structured task families, without implying full-circuit recovery.

</details>


### [128] [Precision Tracked Transformer via Kalman Filtering, Kriging and Process Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18832)
*Bo Long,Deepak Agarwal,Jelena Markovic-Voronov,Yi Wang,Liuqing Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出贝叶斯滤波Transformer（BFT），通过引入精度加权机制处理不确定性，在序列推荐和大语言模型微调任务上显著提升性能，尤其在冷启动和噪声数据场景表现突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Transformer架构缺乏对不确定性的原则性处理，在序列推荐的冷启动标记、语言模型的异质信号质量以及无约束softmax导致的注意力汇聚等问题中，所有标记都被统一对待，忽略了实际应用中普遍存在的不确定性。

Method: 提出贝叶斯滤波Transformer（BFT），将注意力机制重新诠释为精度加权克里金插值，残差连接重构为具有自适应增益的卡尔曼更新，前馈网络转化为通过雅可比加过程噪声规则传播精度的动力学模型。观测精度采用无参数限制性最大似然（REML）估计器配合共轭贝叶斯先验进行估计。BFT可替换任意Transformer层且开销极小。

Result: 在序列推荐任务中，BFT应用于三种主流架构并在六个基准数据集上取得显著提升，在冷启动用户和稀有物品上改进最大。在大语言模型监督微调中，BFT在噪声监督（问答中的标记-标签腐败）和噪声上下文（带真实RAG干扰项的检索增强问答）两种场景下均提升了鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 通过恢复精度这一单一的原则性修改，BFT在经典序列建模和现代大语言模型两种场景下均释放了显著的性能提升空间，证明了不确定性处理对Transformer架构的重要性。

Abstract: The Transformer is the foundational building block of modern AI, yet offers no principled handling of \emph{uncertainty}, which is prevalent in real applications: cold-start tokens with sparse histories in sequential recommendation, heterogeneous signal quality in language models, and attention sinks induced by unconstrained softmax. Every token is treated with uniform confidence. We show this uniformity is a degenerate case of our \emph{Bayesian Filtering Transformer} (BFT): attention becomes precision-weighted kriging, the residual connection becomes a Kalman update with adaptive gain, and the FFN becomes a dynamics model propagating precision via a Jacobian--plus--process-noise rule.
