Observation precision comes from a parameter-free Restricted Maximum Likelihood (REML) estimator with a conjugate Bayesian prior. BFT replaces any Transformer layer with negligible overhead. On sequential recommendation, BFT applied to three major architectures yields significant gains on six benchmarks, with the largest improvements on cold-start users and rare items where uncertainty is highest. On supervised fine-tuning of large language models with noisy data, BFT improves robustness in two regimes: noisy supervision (token-label corruption in question answering) and noisy context (retrieval-augmented QA with real RAG distractors). A single principled modification -- restoring precision -- unlocks substantial headroom across both classical sequence-modeling and modern LLM regimes.

</details>


### [129] [Automated Big Data Quality Assessment using Knowledge Graph Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18833)
*Hadi Fadlallah,Rima Kilany,Mitri Haber,Ali Jaber*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于知识图谱嵌入的方法，用于自动化生成上下文相关的数据质量评估计划，解决了现有方法在上下文感知评估方面的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大数据自动化质量评估方法在实现准确的上下文感知评估方面面临挑战，传统方法仅依赖严格匹配而忽略了上下文特征，无法为特定上下文生成全面的质量评估计划。

Method: 提出一种基于知识的方法，利用知识图谱嵌入预测输入数据集上下文表示与相关质量规则和维度之间的缺失边。通过在知识图谱中整合多种表示形式（基于文献调查的上下文信息），并注入数值边属性来为每个预测的质量测量分配权重，从而生成针对特定上下文的综合数据质量评估计划。

Result: 使用AmpliGraph框架和黎巴嫩原子能委员会（LAEC-CNRS）提供的真实辐射传感器数据集进行评估，结果表明该方法能够为给定输入数据集生成全面的数据质量评估计划。

Conclusion: 通过利用知识图谱增强对输入数据集上下文的理解，该方法克服了传统方法的局限性，能够提供上下文特定且全面的数据质量评估方案。

Abstract: Automated data quality assessment is crucial for managing big data, but existing solutions face challenges in achieving accurate context-aware assessment. This paper presents a novel knowledge-based approach to enhance automated data quality assessment. Our approach utilizes knowledge graph embeddings to predict missing edges between the input dataset's context representation and the relevant quality rules and dimensions within a knowledge graph representing contextual data characteristics and the required quality assessment operations. We surpass conventional practices by integrating diverse representations within the knowledge graph, drawing insights from contextual information from a thorough literature investigation. This integration allows us to develop a comprehensive and context-specific data quality assessment plan tailored to each context. Leveraging the knowledge graph improves our understanding of the input dataset's context, overcoming the limitations of traditional methods that rely solely on strict matching and overlook contextual characteristics. By injecting numerical edge attributes, we assign corresponding weights to each predicted quality measurement, providing a comprehensive data quality assessment plan for the input dataset.
  To evaluate our approach, we leverage AmpliGraph, a framework developed and benchmarked by AccentureLabs. The evaluation involves employing a real-world radiation sensors dataset provided by the Lebanese Atomic Energy Commission (LAEC-CNRS). The results obtained from this evaluation demonstrate the capability of our solution to generate a comprehensive data quality assessment plan for the given input dataset.

</details>


### [130] [StampFormer: A Physics-Guided Material-Geometry-Coupled Multimodal Model for Rapid Prediction of Physical Fields in Sheet Metal Stamping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18835)
*Jiajie Luo,Mohamed Mohamed,Osama Hassan,Haosu Zhou,Yingxue Zhao,Haoran Li,Xinrun Li,Zhutao Shao,Yang Long,Nan Li,Jichun Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出StampFormer框架，融合几何与材料属性预测板料成形FEA结果，实现亚秒级高精度预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统板料成形的有限元分析（FEA）耗时且昂贵，显著延长设计周期。现有代理模型存在局限：基于标量的方法无法捕捉全面的场结果，而基于图像的模型常忽略材料属性的关键作用，仅关注几何信息。

Method: 开发物理引导的深度学习框架StampFormer，包含三个核心组件：材料增强几何网络（MAGN）融合几何与材料数据；分层材料嵌入注入单元（HMEIU）在多层级集成信息；以及采用适应性的Swin-UNet作为主骨干网络进行处理。

Result: 在横梁面板冲压的钢和铝数据集上验证，模型能在1秒内提供薄化、主/次应变、塑性应变和位移等关键物理场的高保真预测。相较FEA真值，四个二维场的平均相对误差低于8.5%，三维位移场的均方误差低于1.2 mm²。

Conclusion: StampFormer是一个实用且高效的框架，通过整合几何和材料属性的多模态信息，实现了快速准确的预测，使设计人员能够进行实时可制造性评估。

Abstract: Traditional sheet metal forming relies on time-consuming and expensive Finite Element Analysis (FEA) for design validation, a process that significantly prolongs design cycles. While surrogate models offer faster iteration, current approaches have limitations: scalar-based methods cannot capture comprehensive field-based FEA results, while existing image-based models often ignore the critical role of material properties by focusing solely on geometry. To address this gap, we develop a physics-guided deep learning framework, namely StampFormer, which simultaneously uses component geometry and material stress-strain responses to predict FEA outcomes. The StampFormer framework uses three core components to process data. A Material-Augmented Geometric Network (MAGN) first fuses geometric and material data. This information is then integrated at various levels by a Hierarchical Material Embedding Injection Unit (HMEIU) before being processed by the primary network backbone, an adapted Swin-UNet. We evaluated our model on the stamping of a crossmember panel with two simulation datasets for steel and aluminium panels, and results demonstrate that StampFormer provides high-fidelity predictions of critical physical fields - including thinning, major strain, minor strain, plastic strain, and displacement - in under a second. Compared with ground truth FEA, our model achieved an average relative error of less than 8.5% on the four 2D fields and a mean squared error of less than 1.2 mm2 for the 3D displacement field. In summary, we introduce a practical and efficient framework that integrates multimodal information, namely geometry and material properties, to provide fast and accurate predictions, enabling designers to perform real-time manufacturability assessments.

</details>


### [131] [Spectral Gradient Surgery for Domain-Generalizable Dataset Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18836)
*Minyoung Oh,Najeong Chae,Jae-Young Sim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了领域可泛化数据集蒸馏（DGDD）新范式，通过频谱梯度手术（SGS）方法解耦类别判别信息与领域特定信息，显著提升了蒸馏数据集在分布外（OOD）场景下的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据集蒸馏方法假设测试数据与训练数据分布一致，但实际场景中该假设常不成立。直接将领域泛化（DG）技术应用于蒸馏数据集效果不佳，因为现有DG方法依赖真实数据集的自然多样性，而紧凑的合成数据集缺乏这种多样性，且数据增强带来的计算开销与数据集蒸馏的效率目标相冲突。

Method: 本文以分布匹配（DM）这一经典数据集蒸馏方法为基线，提出频谱梯度手术（SGS）方法。核心思想是：在频谱域中，跨域梯度的一致性能够揭示哪些梯度成分是跨域共享的（即类别判别信息），哪些是领域特定的。基于此观察，SGS在标准DM更新过程中引入两个互补梯度：一个用于强化跨域共享成分，另一个用于显式促进蒸馏数据集内部的多样性。

Result: 在多个不同规模的基准数据集上进行了广泛实验，结果表明SGS方法显著提升了蒸馏数据集的分布外泛化性能，同时保持了与现有DM方法的即插即用兼容性。

Conclusion: 本文首次系统性地研究了数据集蒸馏的分布外泛化问题，提出的SGS方法通过频谱域梯度分析有效解耦了类别判别信息与领域特定信息，为构建更具鲁棒性的数据集蒸馏方法提供了新思路。该方法在不牺牲效率的前提下，成功弥合了数据集蒸馏与领域泛化之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: Dataset Distillation (DD) synthesizes a compact synthetic dataset that preserves the training utility of a full dataset. However, its standard formulation assumes that test data follow the same distribution as training data, an assumption that rarely holds in practice. A straightforward extension-applying post-hoc Domain Generalization (DG) techniques to distilled data-is ill-suited because existing DG methods rely on the natural diversity of real datasets, which compact synthetic sets inherently lack, while also incurring substantial augmentation overhead that conflicts with the efficiency objective of dataset distillation. To address this limitation, we introduce Domain Generalizable Dataset Distillation (DGDD), a new problem setting that explicitly targets out-of-distribution (OOD) generalization of distilled datasets. We study this problem through a widely adopted DD baseline of Distribution Matching (DM). We attribute the OOD vulnerability of DM to the entanglement of class-discriminative and domain-specific information within the compressed synthetic set, and propose Spectral Gradient Surgery (SGS) to disentangle the two. The key insight of SGS is that cross-domain agreement among domain-wise gradients in the spectral domain reveals which gradient components are shared across source domains-and are therefore class-discriminative-and which are domain-specific. Based on this observation, SGS augments the standard DM update with two complementary gradients: one that reinforces cross-domain shared components and another that explicitly promotes diversity within the distilled dataset. Extensive experiments on diverse-scale benchmarks demonstrate that SGS substantially improves OOD generalization while remaining plug-and-play compatible with existing DM methods.

</details>


### [132] [VCR: Learning Valid Contextual Representation for Incomplete Wearable Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18837)
*Yuxuan Weng,Wenhan Luo,Qijia Shao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了VCR框架，通过正交解耦分离共享语义与模态特有信息，仅重建可推断的共享成分，解决了可穿戴设备健康监测中传感器缺失导致的性能下降问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可穿戴设备的健康监测在实际部署中面临标注数据有限和传感器数据普遍缺失（模态缺失）的双重挑战。现有的自监督预训练方法大多假设模态完整，而当前的缺失处理方法倾向于重建整个缺失信号，这会导致模型产生无法从观测信号推断出的模态特有细节“幻觉”，从而降低模型的鲁棒性。

Method: 提出VCR自监督框架。核心包括：1）正交分词器，通过校正潜在流形和几何投影实现严格正交解耦，将各模态分离为共享语义和模态特有残差；2）缺失感知的混合专家骨干网络，以适应不同的模态可用模式；3）约束目标函数仅重建缺失模态的共享成分，避免对不可推断的模态特有细节产生幻觉。

Result: 在多种健康监测任务中，VCR在全模态、单模态缺失和多模态缺失场景下，相比强监督和自监督基线方法均表现出一致的性能提升和鲁棒性改善。

Conclusion: VCR框架通过信息完整性保护与针对性重建约束，有效缓解了模态缺失导致的幻觉问题，显著提升了多模态健康监测在现实不完美条件下的鲁棒性与性能。

Abstract: Wearable devices enable continuous health monitoring from multimodal signals, but real-world deployment is hindered by limited labeled data and pervasive sensor incompleteness. While large-scale self-supervised pretraining reduces label dependence, most existing methods assume full modality availability. Current approaches for handling modality missingness often reconstruct entire absent signals, which can encourage hallucinating modality-specific details that are not inferable from the observed sensor signals and degrade robustness. We propose VCR, a self-supervised framework that learns to extract valid representations robust to modality missingness. VCR employs an orthogonal tokenizer to enforce strict orthogonal disentanglement by rectifying latent manifolds and applying a geometric projection, separating each modality into shared semantics and modality-specific residuals. This design preserves complete information integrity while serving as a structural foundation for robust learning under modality missingness. The resulting tokens are processed by a missing-aware mixture-of-experts backbone that adapts to varying patterns of modality availability. By constraining the objective to reconstruct only the shared components of missing modalities, VCR effectively mitigates hallucinations of non-inferable modality-specific details. Across multiple health monitoring tasks, VCR consistently improves performance and robustness under full, single-missing, and multiple-missing modality settings compared with strong supervised and self-supervised baselines.

</details>


### [133] [Lying Is Just a Phase: The Hidden Alignment Transition in Language Model Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18838)
*Adil Amin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 模型在特定临界规模(Nc≈3.5B参数)下会发生“相变”：低于此规模，推理与真实性相互竞争；高于此规模，两者协同。通过架构设计、数据筛选和训练配方可独立调节Nc。研究提供了诊断工具和干预方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的缩放定律仅能通过计算量预测损失，但无法解释模型不同能力（特别是推理与真实性）之间的相互作用机制。研究者希望揭示模型能力间的耦合规律及其临界点。

Method: 跨越16个模型家族、63个基础模型进行大规模测量；使用宽度归一化方法分析输出投影瓶颈；通过注意力头分析内部机制；建立稀疏回归ODE模型进行跨预测验证。仅需公开基准测试分数即可完成诊断。

Result: 发现临界规模Nc≈3.5B参数(95%置信区间[2.9B, 13.4B])：低于Nc时推理与真实性负相关，高于Nc时正相关。高质量数据训练将Qwen的耦合值从0.025提升至0.830；Gemma-4在4B参数即达到通常13B+模型的耦合水平；宽度归一化可消除所有测试家族的负相关；40个模型中38个未显示竞争性注意力头。ODE模型对Llama-2的预测误差仅5.6%。前沿模型仍保持协同机制(r=+0.72)。

Conclusion: 模型能力间存在可预测的相变机制，临界规模Nc可由架构、数据筛选和训练配方独立调控。该发现为模型设计提供了理论指导和实用工具，研究者开源了诊断工具、激活引导工具及交互式仪表盘。

Abstract: Scaling laws predict loss from compute but not how capabilities interact. We measure the coupling between reasoning and truthfulness across 63 base models from 16 families and find a regime change invisible to loss curves: below a family-dependent critical scale $N_c$, capabilities anticorrelate; above it, they cooperate. $N_c \approx 3.5$B parameters [2.9B, 13.4B] (bootstrap 95% CI), but model size is not the only variable that determines phase. Architecture, data curation, and training recipe each shift $N_c$ independently: curated training eliminated the coupling dip between Qwen generations ($0.025 \to 0.830$ at matched scale), Gemma-4 at 4B achieves coupling 0.871, characteristic of 13B+ standard-trained models, through distillation and architectural innovation, and Phi at 1B matches web-trained coupling at 10B through data curation alone. Width normalization eliminates the anticorrelation across all tested families, supporting an output-projection bottleneck. Internally, 38 of 40 models show zero competing attention heads. A sparse-regression ODE cross-predicts held-out Llama-2 at 5.6% error. The diagnostic requires no model internals -- only public benchmark scores across a model family. The cooperative regime extends to the frontier ($r = +0.72$, 34 models, 10 labs). Code, data, and an open-source activation-steering tool for any open-weight model are released alongside an interactive dashboard that diagnoses any model's coupling phase, suggests concrete interventions (data curation, width, benchmark rotation), and provides ODE scaling predictions, frontier diagnostics, and eigenstructure analysis: https://zehenlabs.com/cape/.

</details>


### [134] [An Integrated Forecasting Prototype for Emergency Department Boarding Time to Support Proactive Operational Decision Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18839)
*Orhun Vural,Abdulaziz Ahmed,Ferhat Zengul,James Booth,Bunyamin Ozaydin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究开发了多时间视野预测框架，利用深度学习模型（DLinear和NLinear）预测急诊科（ED）滞留时间，整合了天气、节假日等外部数据，并开发了MLOps网络应用原型以支持实际应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 急诊科（ED）过度拥挤是全球持续存在的运营挑战，导致护理交付延误和下游拥堵。ED滞留时间是衡量拥堵的关键指标，提前预测滞留时间可以在拥堵升级前进行主动运营决策。

Method: 开发了多时间视野时间序列预测框架，在6、8、10、12和24小时视野预测ED滞留时间。利用美国一所大学附属医院的真实数据，整合天气、节假日和重大本地事件等外部上下文数据源。使用基于分解的线性（DLinear）和基于归一化的线性（NLinear）时间序列预测深度学习模型。

Result: DLinear和NLinear模型在多个视野上表现优越，在以高滞留时间为特征的极端拥堵场景下也进行了评估。模型在预测准确性方面优于其他方法。

Conclusion: 该预测框架能够有效预测ED滞留时间，支持主动运营决策。开发的MLOps网络应用原型通过集成数据摄取、预测可视化、实验和重训练，支持将预测框架转化为实际应用。

Abstract: Overcrowding in emergency departments (ED) remains a persistent operational challenge worldwide, causing delays in care delivery and downstream congestion. ED boarding time, defined as the duration admitted patients remain in the ED while awaiting inpatient bed placement, is a key indicator of this congestion. Predicting ED boarding time in advance enables proactive operational decision making before congestion escalates. We developed and evaluated a multi-horizon time series forecasting framework to predict ED boarding time at 6, 8, 10, 12, and 24-hour horizons. Real-world data from a university-affiliated urban hospital in the United States were utilized and integrated with external contextual data sources, including weather, holidays, and major local events. Decomposition-based Linear (DLinear) and Normalization-based Linear (NLinear) time series forecasting deep learning models showed superior performance across multiple horizons. Models were also evaluated under extreme congestion scenarios characterized by elevated boarding times. In addition, a Machine Learning Operations (MLOps) web application prototype was developed to support translation of the forecasting framework into practice through integrated data ingestion, forecast visualization, experimentation, and retraining.

</details>


### [135] [The Growing Pains of Frontier Models: When Leaderboards Stop Separating and What to Measure Next](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18840)
*Adil Amin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种诊断前沿AI模型能力协同与权衡的新方法，发现不同实验室在代码与推理能力上呈现显著差异的合作模式，并预测了下一阶段基准测试的转型方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的排行榜仅对前沿模型进行独立维度的排名，无法揭示不同能力之间是相互促进还是此消彼长。在前沿模型领域，这种能力间的相互作用才是更具信息量的信号，需要新的分析框架来诊断和预测能力发展趋势。

Method: 提出了一种分解方法，将SWE-bench和GPQA Diamond的配对分数分解为群体耦合趋势和逐版本残差（h-field）。该方法能够诊断能力侧重点，并识别下一个最具信息量的测量或压力测试。研究覆盖了来自10个实验室的34个模型（2024-2026年）。

Result: 能力间呈现显著合作关系（r = +0.72, p < 10^-6），但不同实验室差异巨大：DeepSeek从推理优先转向代码优先（h值从+11.2变为-4.7，波动15.9个百分点）；Google保持一致的推理侧重点；Anthropic在代码突破与恢复间震荡。各实验室的耦合斜率差异达5倍（Google 1.15 vs DeepSeek 0.23）。在30-72B参数规模确认了第二次能力转型，SWE-bench正在饱和，而HLE和指令跟随仍具区分度。

Conclusion: 能力合作不是静态的而是级联演进的。研究提供了三级策略手册（定位、诊断、轮换）、实验室测量优先级表，以及未来12个月前沿发布的七个可证伪预测。2026年4月的五个发布样本外验证了诊断的有效性（r从+0.72升至+0.75）。交互式仪表板提供了相位分类、h-field诊断、实验室耦合轨迹、ODE扩展预测和基准轮换指导。

Abstract: Leaderboards rank frontier models on independent axes but do not reveal whether capabilities reinforce or trade off across releases -- and at the frontier, this interaction is the more informative signal. We decompose paired SWE-bench and GPQA Diamond scores into a population coupling trend and per-release residual ($h$-field) that diagnoses capability emphasis and identifies which measurement or stress test is most informative next. Across 34 models from 10 labs (2024--2026), capabilities cooperate ($r = +0.72$, $p < 10^{-6}$), but cooperation varies by lab and over time: DeepSeek reversed from reasoning-rich to coding-first ($h$: $+11.2 \to -4.7$, 15.9-pp swing); Google maintains consistent reasoning emphasis; Anthropic oscillates between coding excursions and recovery. Cooperation is not static -- it cascades. Six open-weight architectures confirm a second capability transition at 30--72B, and SWE-bench is now saturating while HLE and instruction-following retain discriminatory spread -- signaling the next axis rotation. We provide a three-level playbook (locate, diagnose, rotate), a per-lab measurement-priority table, and seven falsifiable predictions with timestamped criteria for the next 12 months of frontier releases. Per-lab coupling slopes vary $5\times$ (Google $1.15$ vs. DeepSeek $0.23$), quantifying how efficiently each recipe converts coding gains into reasoning. Five April 2026 releases confirm the diagnostic out of sample ($r$ rises from $+0.72$ to $+0.75$). An interactive dashboard provides phase classification with actionable recommendations, $h$-field diagnostics, per-lab coupling trajectories, ODE-based scaling predictions, benchmark rotation guidance, self-steering demo, and live tracking of all seven predictions: https://zehenlabs.com/cape/.

</details>


### [136] [From Cumulative Constraints to Adaptive Runtime Safety Control for Nonstationary Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18841)
*Timofey Tomashevskiy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了约束投影安全护盾（CPSS），一种运行时机制，通过将累积安全预算转换为自适应的状态级控制约束，解决了非平稳环境下强化学习中的安全问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的强化学习安全方法通常通过累积代价约束来规定安全性，但这种轨迹级别的保证无法直接防止不安全的个体决策。在持续和非平稳环境中，由于相同动作的风险会随上下文变化，固定的状态级阈值可能过于保守或过于宽松，导致安全约束难以有效执行。

Method: 提出约束投影安全护盾（CPSS），这是一种运行时机制：追踪剩余安全预算，将其投影为时变的可允许风险阈值，并过滤掉预测安全代价超过当前阈值的策略动作。该阈值根据上下文信号在线调整，在要求更高或变化更快的场景中更严格，在安全预算充足时更宽松。

Result: 理论分析表明CPSS保证执行动作满足每步状态阈值，产生有限时域累积代价界限，并在干预频率和每步奖励失真方面提供性能退化界限。在非平稳高速公路合流场景的实验中，CPSS显著减少了基于距离的安全违规，增加了分离间距，且仅选择性干预而非主导学习策略。

Conclusion: 自适应预算到阈值投影是将累积安全规范转化为持续强化学习系统有效局部安全控制的实用方法，为非平稳环境下的安全强化学习提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Safety in reinforcement learning is often specified through cumulative cost constraints, but these trajectory-level guarantees do not directly prevent unsafe individual decisions, especially under nonstationarity. In continual and nonstationary settings, the difficulty is amplified because the risk associated with the same action can vary across contexts, while a fixed state-level threshold may be either too conservative or too weak. We propose Constraint Projection Safety Shield (CPSS), a runtime mechanism that converts a cumulative safety budget into adaptive state-level control constraints during execution. CPSS tracks the remaining safety budget, projects it into a time-varying admissible risk threshold, and filters policy actions whose predicted safety cost exceeds the active threshold. The threshold is adjusted online using contextual signals so that enforcement becomes stricter in more demanding or rapidly changing regimes and less restrictive when the available safety budget is sufficient. We analyze the resulting shielded policy and show that the mechanism guarantees per-state threshold satisfaction for executed actions, induces finite-horizon cumulative cost bounds, and yields a performance degradation bound in terms of intervention frequency and per-step reward distortion. We evaluate CPSS in nonstationary highway merging scenarios using highway-env. Across multiple seeds, CPSS substantially reduces proximity-based safety violations and increases separation margins while intervening selectively rather than dominating the learned policy. These results support adaptive budget-to-threshold projection as a practical way to transform cumulative safety specifications into effective local safety control for continual reinforcement learning systems.

</details>


### [137] [Safe Continual Reinforcement Learning under Nonstationarity via Adaptive Safety Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18842)
*Timofey Tomashevskiy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了LILAC+框架，通过三种自适应安全机制（基于上下文的安全约束、自适应速度约束和预算到状态安全执行）解决非平稳环境下的安全持续强化学习问题，在保持任务性能的同时显著减少分布偏移下的安全违规。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的安全强化学习方法通常假设固定约束或稳定的环境条件，这在分布偏移情况下会变得不充分。非平稳环境下的安全强化学习需要能够随环境条件变化而适应的安全机制。

Method: 提出LILAC+框架，结合三种自适应安全机制：（1）基于上下文的安全约束：利用推断和预测的环境上下文调整安全要求；（2）自适应速度约束：当环境变化速率超过智能体安全适应能力时收紧安全要求；（3）预算到状态安全执行：将累积安全要求转换为可在决策时执行的局部状态级控制约束。这些机制共同提供了持续强化学习中主动和反应式安全适应的统一方法。

Result: 在模拟驾驶环境中进行了评估，包括平稳、已见非平稳和未见非平稳条件。结果表明，自适应安全约束在分布偏移下显著减少了安全违规，同时与无约束和固定约束基线相比保持了竞争性的任务性能。

Conclusion: 安全持续强化学习需要自适应约束机制，这些机制不仅要响应当前状态信息，还要响应预测的环境上下文、适应需求和剩余安全预算。研究强调了在非平稳环境中主动和反应式安全适应的重要性。

Abstract: Safe reinforcement learning in nonstationary environments requires safety mechanisms that adapt as environmental conditions change. Standard safe reinforcement learning methods often assume fixed constraints or stable environmental conditions, which can become inadequate under distribution shift. We propose LILAC+, a framework for safe continual reinforcement learning under nonstationarity that combines three adaptive safety mechanisms: context-based safety constraints, adaptation-speed constraints, and budget-to-state safety enforcement. Context-based constraints adjust safety requirements using inferred and predicted environmental context. Adaptation-speed constraints tighten safety requirements when the rate of environmental change exceeds the agent's ability to adapt safely. Budget-to-state enforcement converts cumulative safety requirements into local state-level control constraints that can be enforced at decision time. Together, these mechanisms provide a unified approach for proactive and reactive safety adaptation in continual reinforcement learning. We evaluate the framework in simulated driving environments under stationary, seen nonstationary, and unseen nonstationary conditions. The results show that adaptive safety constraints substantially reduce safety violations under distribution shift while maintaining competitive task performance compared with unconstrained and fixed-constraint baselines. These findings suggest that safe continual reinforcement learning requires adaptive constraint mechanisms that respond not only to current state information but also to predicted environmental context, adaptation demand, and remaining safety budget.

</details>


### [138] [TEMPO: Temporal Enforcement via Mode-Separated Policy Optimization for Trustworthy LLM Backtesting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18843)
*Zeyu Zhang,Bradly C. Stadie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TEMPO方法，通过两阶段奖励机制和GRPO训练管线，使大模型学会基于截止日期的条件性证据选择，有效减少时间泄露并提升任务表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型在回测历史事件时，常泄露预训练中获得的截止日期后知识，导致评估准确性虚高。现有方法如提示约束在因果相关时失效，知识遗忘无法解决因实例特定的时间合规问题——同一事实对不同截止日期的合法性不同。核心挑战在于模型需要学习“时间纪律”，而非简单删除知识。

Method: 提出TEMPO（Temporal Enforcement via Mode-separated Policy Optimization）方法，包含两个核心贡献：(1) 双模式奖励机制，其中泄露模式首先将截止后声明驱动至零作为硬性先决条件，随后性能模式优化任务表现；(2) 基于GRPO的训练管线，使模型能够发现时间有效的推理策略。该方法训练模型学习基于实例特定截止日期的条件性证据选择。

Result: 在三个预测任务和两个模型上的实验表明，TEMPO将泄露率从2~13%降低至0.6~3.7%（所有条件）。在存在强截止前信号时，任务性能提升6~13%；在仅凭有效信息难以预测的任务中性能得以保持。理论上证明了训练单调减少泄露、收敛至无泄露最优解，并在合规后提升任务表现。

Conclusion: TEMPO成功实现了大语言模型的时间纪律学习，在不删除知识的前提下，通过条件性证据选择有效解决了时间泄露问题。该方法在减少泄露的同时，在可行情况下提升任务性能，为历史事件回测提供了可靠的评估框架。

Abstract: Backtesting large language models on historical events requires reasoning exclusively from information available before a specified cutoff date. Yet models routinely leak post-cutoff knowledge from pre-training into their reasoning, inflating apparent accuracy and undermining evaluation validity. Prompt-based constraints fail when suppressed content is causally related to the prediction, and knowledge unlearning cannot address this problem because temporal compliance is instance-specific: the same fact may be legitimate evidence for one cutoff date and a violation for another. Rather than erasing knowledge, the model must learn temporal discipline: selecting evidence conditioned on each instance's cutoff date. We propose TEMPO (Temporal Enforcement via Mode-separated Policy Optimization), which trains this discipline via two contributions: (1) a two-mode reward where a leakage mode drives post-cutoff claims to zero as a hard prerequisite before a performance mode optimizes task performance; and (2) a GRPO-based training pipeline that enables the model to discover temporally valid reasoning strategies. We prove that training monotonically decreases leakage, converges to the leak-free optimum, and improves task performance once compliance is achieved. On three prediction tasks and two models, TEMPO reduces leakage from 2~13% to 0.6~3.7% across all conditions, with task performance improving 6~13% where strong pre-cutoff signals exist and maintained where the prediction task is inherently difficult from valid information alone.

</details>


### [139] [Graph-Driven Cross-Industry Real-Time Monitoring Framework for Anti-Money Laundering Detection in Converged Mobility-Energy Supply Chain Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18844)
*Rong Liu,Xiaojun Xiao,Zhanqing Su*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个图驱动的跨行业实时反洗钱监控框架（GCRMF），通过构建跨行业异构图并利用时序双图注意力网络编码资金流特征，结合元路径子图推理和自监督在线学习机制，有效识别跨行业供应链金融中的合谋洗钱行为，F1分数相比现有方法提升超过17.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着旅游与能源行业深度融合，跨行业供应链金融逐渐成为洗钱事件频发的高风险领域，迫切需要一种能够实时监控并识别复杂跨行业洗钱行为的有效框架。

Method: 首先构建涵盖新能源汽车租赁平台、能源供应商、金融科技机构等的跨行业异构图（CIHG），通过时序双图注意力网络（Temporal Dual-GAT）集成行业语义并动态编码资金流路径与时序演化特征；其次，提出基于对比学习和层次图采样的元路径子图推理模块以识别合谋主体的结构化欺诈行为；同时采用自监督在线学习机制实现对新型洗钱策略的实时适应与持续优化。

Result: 实验结果表明，在跨行业场景下，GCRMF框架相比现有图神经网络方法，F1分数提升超过17.8%，并大幅降低了误报率。

Conclusion: 所提出的GCRMF框架能够有效应对跨行业供应链金融中的复杂洗钱风险，在提升检测性能的同时实现对新洗钱策略的实时适应，具有重要的实际应用价值。

Abstract: With the deep integration of the travel and energy industries, cross-industry supply chain finance has gradually become a high-risk field of hidden money laundering incidents. For this reason, this work proposes a graph-driven cross-industry real-time anti-money laundering monitoring framework (GCRMF) for integrated travel - energy supply chain networks. First, a cross-industry heterogeneous graph (CIHG) covering new energy vehicle rental platforms, energy suppliers, fintech institutions, etc., is constructed, and industry semantics are integrated through temporarily Dual-GAT (Temporal Dual-Graph Attention Network), dynamically encoding capital flow paths and evolution features over time. Subsequently, in order to identify the structural fraud behavior together produced by colluding subjects, a meta-path subgraph reasoning module based on contrastive learning and hierarchical graph sampling is proposed to enhance the discrimination capability of cross-industry recurring money laundering behavior. Meanwhile, a self-supervised online learning mechanism is adopted for real-time adaptation and continuous optimization to new money laundering strategies. The experimental results show that compared with existing graph neural network methods in cross-industry scenarios, GCRMF improves the performance by more than 17.8% of F1 score and greatly reduces the false positive rate.

</details>


### [140] [First-Passage Prediction of Grokking Delay: ACalibrated Law under AdamW with Causal Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18845)
*Truong Xuan Khanh,Truong Quynh Hoa,Luu Duc Trung,Phan Thanh Duc*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次提出了AdamW优化器下grokking延迟的定量预测定律，通过将延迟视为首达时间问题，推导出闭式公式，并在多种架构和任务上验证了其预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Grokking现象（模型在长时间过拟合后才突然泛化）的延迟时间预测一直缺乏定量理论。理解并预测这一延迟对于掌控模型训练动态和泛化机制具有重要意义。

Method: 将grokking延迟建模为参数范数V_t的首达时间问题，推导闭式定律T_grok - T_mem = (1 / 2 kappa_LL eta lambda) log(V_mem / V_star)。引入分位数边际定理建立充要条件：正延迟需要范数分离V_mem > V_post和角度可达性阈值alpha_star。通过因果干预（冻结范数或移除权重衰减）验证机制。

Result: 在单一超参数单元校准后，预测26个留出运行的grokking延迟，MAPE为17.7%（覆盖41倍延迟范围）；推广至MLP时MAPE为18.0%（N=34）；跨任务扩展时MAPE为23.3%（N=46，43.5倍范围）。角度阈值预测误差仅1.3%（p=97时预测47.2度，观测47.8度）。因果干预实验完全消除grokking现象（0/6 vs 3/3基线）。

Conclusion: 范数首达是grokking延迟的必要非充分条件，角度可达性是另一关键因素。定律在架构内具有良好泛化性，但跨架构变体时kappa_LL差异约2倍。该理论目前限于算法任务，向自然语言规模模型的迁移仍是开放问题。

Abstract: We give the first quantitative prediction of grokking delay under AdamW. Treating the delay as a first-passage time, we derive a closed-form law T_grok - T_mem = (1 / 2 kappa_LL eta lambda) log(V_mem / V_star), where V_t = ||theta_t||^2 is the squared parameter norm, V_star is an architecture-dependent threshold, and kappa_LL absorbs the AdamW correction to the clean-SGD contraction rate 2 eta lambda. Calibrating (kappa_LL, V_star) on a single hyperparameter cell predicts grokking delays on 26 held-out runs with MAPE 17.7% over a 41x delay range; the law generalises to MLPs (MAPE 18.0%, N=34) and degrades to 23.3% on cross-task extension (N=46, 43.5x range), with a structured residual in which V_star / V_mem stays comparatively stable within architecture (CV about 14% on the 1L transformer).
  First-passage of V_t is necessary but not sufficient. A quantile-margin theorem establishes that positive delay requires both norm separation V_mem > V_post and angular reachability of a threshold alpha_star = arcsin(C / V_T_mem^(1/2)), where C is computable from the empirical NTK feature map and the validation-margin quantile. Calibrating C on modulus p=89 predicts alpha_star = 47.2 degrees at p=97 (observed 47.8 degrees, error 1.3%) as a prior cross-cell prediction. Causal interventions that freeze the norm or remove weight decay at memorisation eliminate grokking (0/6 vs. 3/3 baseline), trapping the angular displacement near 12 degrees.
  kappa_LL is empirically measured per architecture rather than derived from (beta_1, beta_2, epsilon); within-architecture CV stays at most 15% across four architectures, but values differ by about 2x between architectural variants beyond depth alone. Empirical scope is algorithmic tasks (modular arithmetic, sparse parity) under AdamW; whether the law transfers to natural-language scale models is open.

</details>


### [141] [Lost and Found in Translation: Variational Diagnostics for Neural Codebook Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18846)
*Yusuke Hayashi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了神经码本信道$K_{e\to d}$及其架构无关的Bernoulli-KL证书，用于诊断VAE编码器-解码器之间的码本失配问题，填补了标准诊断仅能验证潜在空间是否被使用而无法验证编解码是否一致的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统通信系统会在发射器与接收器使用不兼容的操作码本时失败。类似地，VAE的编码器$q_φ$与解码器$p_θ$联合训练后，从业者将其潜在空间视为离散码，用于聚类、条件生成和机制可解释性。然而，标准VAE诊断工具（ELBO、活跃单元、互信息和码直方图）仅能证明该码是否被使用，无法验证解码器是否以编码器预设的方式读取每个潜在变量，存在诊断盲区。

Method: 提出神经码本信道$K_{e\to d}(j|i)$作为耦合的编码器-解码器诊断工具，其非对角质量受架构无关的Bernoulli-KL证书$d_{\mathrm{bin}}(1-\mathcal{A} \| \barη_p) \le \barΔ$约束，该证书由变分间隙控制。该证书是经典KL链式法则在分解条件下对编码器-解码器不一致事件的操作特化，并辅以构造性边际不可能结果：边际直方图、熵、活跃码计数或互信息的任何组合都无法确定$K_{e\to d}$。

Result: 在四个sklearn数据集上验证证书（有限网格精确方法，5/5种子，20/20对满足边界）；在二维模型上边界非空（为观测不一致度的2.71倍），四项恒等式闭合误差在$10^{-4}$内；MNIST重要性采样控制下验证；VQ-VAE达到预测极限$\hat{\mathcal{A}}=1.000$。审计报告单元$(K_{e\to d}, \mathcal{A}, R_{\mathrm{eff}}, R, \mathrm{AU})$已可用。

Conclusion: 本框架使"失配解码"——这一经典通信理论数十年前命名的故障模式——在单一深度生成模型内部可见。神经码本信道填补了VAE诊断领域的空白，为评估潜在空间的编解码一致性提供了可操作的理论工具。

Abstract: Classical communication systems fail not only through random noise but also when transmitter and receiver use incompatible operational codebooks. Variational autoencoders (VAEs) train an encoder $q_φ$ and decoder $p_θ$ jointly, and practitioners treat the resulting latent space as a discrete code -- for clustering, conditional generation, and mechanistic interpretability. Yet standard VAE diagnostics -- ELBO, active units, mutual information, and code histograms -- certify only whether this code is used, never whether the decoder reads each latent under the encoder's code.
  We close this gap with the neural codebook channel $K_{e\to d}(j\mid i)$, a coupled encoder-decoder diagnostic whose off-diagonal mass is bounded by an architecture-free Bernoulli-KL certificate $d_{\mathrm{bin}}(1-\mathcal{A} \,\|\, \barη_p) \le \barΔ$ controlled by the variational gap. The certificate is the operational specialization of the classical KL chain rule under disintegration to the encoder-decoder disagreement event, complemented by a constructive marginal-impossibility result: no combination of marginal histograms, entropies, active-code counts, or mutual information determines $K_{e\to d}$.
  We audit the certificate on four sklearn datasets (finite-grid exact, 5/5 seeds, 20/20 pairs satisfy the bound), a 2D model where the bound is non-vacuous at $2.71\times$ the observed disagreement and the four-term identity closes within $10^{-4}$, MNIST under importance-sampling control, and a VQ-VAE attaining the predicted limit $\hat{\mathcal{A}}=1.000$. The package $(K_{e\to d}, \mathcal{A}, R_{\mathrm{eff}}, R, \mathrm{AU})$ is an audit-ready reporting unit. More broadly, the framework makes mismatched decoding -- a failure mode classical communication theory named decades ago -- visible inside a single deep generative model.

</details>


### [142] [Transformers Linearly Represent Highly Structured World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18847)
*Roman Kniazev,Nathanaël Fijalkow*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过机制可解释性分析发现，在数独求解任务上训练的Transformer会构建反映领域约束代数结构的内部世界模型，并形成稀疏、可解释的“唯一候选数电路”。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究Transformer在序列推理轨迹上训练时是否构建底层任务的内部模型，以及这些内部表示的结构是否反映领域结构。

Method: 在一个8层Transformer上训练数独求解轨迹，并对其内部计算进行机制分析。

Result: 发现模型构建了子结构世界模型：信息围绕数独约束作用的行、列和宫组织，而非逐格表示；识别出一个“唯一候选数电路”，即最后一层MLP中的一小组专用神经元，能够检测特定单元格何时仅剩一个可能数字并可靠地推广该数字。

Conclusion: 模型内部世界模型的几何形状由领域的约束代数而非其表面表示所塑造，由此产生的决策电路是稀疏、单义且完全可解释的，证明了机制可解释性工具可以还原Transformer解决组合推理任务的端到端算法过程。

Abstract: Do transformers, when trained on sequential reasoning traces, build internal models of the underlying task? And if so, does the structure of those internal representations mirror the structure of the domain? We train an 8-layer transformer on Sudoku solving traces and perform a mechanistic analysis of its internal computation. We establish two results. First, the model builds a substructure world model: it does not represent the board state cell by cell, as a human analyst would expect, but organizes information around the rows, columns, and boxes that Sudoku's constraints act on. Second, we identify a naked-single circuit: a small set of dedicated neurons in the final MLP layer, each individually detecting when exactly one digit remains possible for a specific cell, and reliably promoting that digit. These findings show that the geometry of an emergent world model is shaped by the constraint algebra of the domain, not its surface presentation, and that the resulting decision circuit is sparse, monosemantic, and fully interpretable. More broadly, they demonstrate that mechanistic interpretability tools can recover an end-to-end algorithmic account of how a transformer solves a combinatorial reasoning task.

</details>


### [143] [Exact Linear Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18848)
*Weinuo Ou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了精确线性注意力（ELA），利用核函数的精确分解特性实现了Transformer注意力的线性计算复杂度且无近似误差，同时引入了超链接结构、记忆叶模块和基于路由分数的偏置机制等工程创新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的线性注意力方法存在梯度爆炸和token注意力稀释的问题，且通常引入近似误差。本文旨在通过施加保证非负性、可辨别性和几何可解释性的核函数约束来解决这些问题，实现精确且高效的线性注意力。

Method: 提出Exact Linear Attention (ELA)，利用核函数的精确分解属性，设计了满足非负性、可辨别性和几何可解释性的核函数（如Hadamard Exp Kernel等）。此外，引入三项工程创新：替代残差连接的Hyper Link结构以缓解梯度退化；基于双向线性注意力的Memory Lobe模块以捕获层间变换流；Mixture of Experts中基于路由分数的偏置机制以提升可解释性。

Result: ELA实现了线性计算复杂度的注意力机制，且无近似误差。通过核函数约束有效解决了梯度爆炸和token注意力稀释问题。所提工程创新模块进一步提升了模型的训练稳定性和表达能力。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了可以通过核函数的精确分解构建无近似误差的线性注意力机制，并展示了配套的网络结构设计（Hyper Link、Memory Lobe等）能够进一步提升模型性能，为高效Transformer架构提供了新的设计思路。

Abstract: This paper introduces Exact Linear Attention (ELA), a mechanism that achieves linear computational complexity for Transformer attention by leveraging the exact decomposition property of kernel functions, without any approximation error. It identifies and addresses gradient explosion and token attention dilution in prior linear attention methods by imposing kernel constraints that ensure non-negativity, discriminability, and geometric interpretability. Several kernel functions are proposed, including the Hadamard Exp Kernel, Summation Squared Euclidean Distance Kernel, and Subtraction Squared Euclidean Distance Kernel. Beyond the core attention formulation, the paper presents three engineering innovations: a Hyper Link structure that replaces traditional residual connections to mitigate gradient degradation, a Memory Lobe module based on bidirectional linear attention that captures transformation flow across layers to implement qualitative memory and an implicit reinforcement learning paradigm, and a routing score based bias mechanism for Mixture of Experts to improve interpretability and semantic alignment.

</details>


### [144] [INSIGHTS: Demonstration-Based Summaries of Time Series Predictors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18849)
*Bar Eini Porat,Rom Gutman,Uri Shalit,Ofra Amir*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了INSIGHTS，一种模型无关、以用户为中心的时间序列模型全局解释方法，通过生成平衡重要性和多样性的样本摘要来概述模型行为，实验和用户研究表明该方法能有效构建样本子集并提升用户对模型的理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的可解释性方法大多关注局部、实例级的归因，而时间序列模型的全局解释方法仍然发展不足，缺乏能够提供模型整体行为概览的工具。

Method: 提出了INSIGHTS方法，这是一种模型无关的方法，通过平衡时间序列样本的重要性和多样性来生成样本摘要，使用捕获领域特定行为（如超出领域规范）的效用函数创建信息丰富的样本子集，设计上优先考虑简单性、效率和透明度。

Result: 实验表明INSIGHTS能有效构建全面、多样的时间序列子集，生成的摘要适合单独评估；领域专家更偏好该方法，因其能提供稳定的模型行为理解和高质量的样本识别；用户研究显示，使用INSIGHTS摘要的参与者对模型整体行为的理解得到增强。

Conclusion: INSIGHTS成功填补了时间序列模型全局解释的空白，为利益相关者提供了一种简单、高效、透明的解决方案，能够有效提升用户对模型行为的理解，具有良好的实用价值和推广前景。

Abstract: Explainability methods have progressed rapidly, but global explanations for time-series models remain underdeveloped, with most approaches focusing on local, instance-level attributions. We introduce INSIGHTS, a model-agnostic, user-centric approach for providing global explanations of time series models. Our approach prioritizes simplicity, efficiency, and transparency in its design, ensuring that stakeholders can readily adopt its outputs. While current methods focus on local explanations, INSIGHTS generates sample summaries that offer a comprehensive overview of model behavior. It balances the importance and diversity of time series samples to create informative subsets using utility functions that capture domain-specific aspects of time series behavior, such as exceeding domain norms. We evaluate INSIGHTS through experiments, interviews, and a user study. Our results indicate INSIGHTS effectively constructs comprehensive, diverse time series subsets, producing summaries manageable for individual evaluation. It is preferred by domain experts for its ability to provide a stable understanding of model behavior and the quality of the samples identified. Moreover, user study participants presented with INSIGHTS-based summaries exhibit an enhanced understanding of the model's overall behavior.

</details>


### [145] [STRIDE: Learnable Stepwise Language Feedback for LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18851)
*Junjie Zhang,Guozheng Ma,Shunyu Liu,Zetian Hu,Yongcheng Jing,Ting-En Lin,Yongbin Li,Dacheng Tao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出STRIDE框架，利用可学习的逐步语言反馈替代标量奖励，在无需人工标注的情况下显著提升大语言模型的推理能力，尤其在标量方法失效的零通过率问题上取得突破。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的强化学习方法存在显著局限：步骤级方法依赖昂贵的人工标注且领域覆盖有限；标量奖励存在信息瓶颈，无法为中间决策提供充分的语义指导；而基于冻结或外部评论家的语言反馈方法虽能提供丰富反馈，但缺乏持续策略改进所需的可扩展性。

Method: 提出STRIDE（Language-driven Stepwise Trajectory Redirection）框架，仅使用基于结果的奖励协同训练生成器和生成式验证器，消除对外部标注的依赖。验证器生成逐步语言评论，显式定位并解释失败原因，指导生成器在中间步骤重定向推理轨迹。轨迹重定向设计保证了即使在噪声或次优验证器反馈下也能实现无害的策略改进。

Result: 在多个推理基准测试上，STRIDE显著优于最先进的基线方法。特别地，在消融实验中，针对标量方法因无学习信号而失效的零通过率问题，STRIDE实现了突破性性能提升。

Conclusion: 可学习的逐步语言反馈相比传统标量奖励能够为中间决策提供更丰富的语义信息，有效增强大语言模型的推理能力，为过程监督提供了一种可扩展且无需人工标注的新范式。

Abstract: Recent advances in Reinforcement Learning (RL) have underscored its potential for incentivizing reasoning capabilities of Large Language Models (LLMs). However, existing step-level efforts suffer from costly annotations that limit domain coverage, while scalar scores further impose an information bottleneck, offering insufficient semantic bandwidth to improve intermediate decisions. Alternative language-critique approaches, which rely on frozen or external critics, provide richer textual feedback but lack the scalability needed for sustained policy improvement. In this work, we propose language-driven stepwise trajectory redirection, termed as STRIDE, a novel training framework that shifts process supervision from scalar rewards to learnable stepwise language feedback. Specifically, we co-train a generator and a generative verifier using only outcome-based rewards, eliminating external annotations, while delivering sustained policy improvement through jointly aligned verifier training. The verifier's stepwise language critiques explicitly localize and explain failures, enabling the generator to redirect reasoning trajectories at intermediate steps toward alternative decisions. The trajectory redirection design guarantees harmless policy improvement, even under noisy or suboptimal verifier feedback. Experiments on diverse reasoning benchmarks show that STRIDE significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, as well as achieving breakthroughs on zero-pass-rate problems where scalar methods yield no learning signal in our ablation studies, demonstrating the effectiveness of learnable stepwise language feedback for enhancing LLM reasoning.

</details>


### [146] [Robust Checkpoint Selection for Multimodal LLMs via Agentic Evaluation and Stability-Aware Ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18852)
*Qinwu Xu,Zhuoheng Li,Jessie Salas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对多模态大语言模型（MLLM）在性能差异微小且评估信号易受噪声干扰时的检查点选择难题，提出了一个结合真实数据、LLM判断和多阶段排序的鲁棒决策框架，并通过置信度估计和惩罚尾部失败提升可靠性，同时强调了数据质量（特别是OCR可读性）的关键作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的检查点选择方法过度依赖静态基准或点对点评分，与实际应用场景不一致，且缺乏鲁棒的不确定性估计，尤其在OCR密集场景中表现不佳。当模型性能差异微弱且评估信号存在噪声时，如何进行可靠的检查点选择成为亟待解决的问题。

Method: 提出了一个多阶段框架：(1)整合精选的真实世界数据；(2)采用结构化LLM判断；(3)设计多阶段排序协议，包括点对点过滤、列表排序和成对比较的渐进式优化；(4)引入基于子采样的置信度估计；(5)采用基于百分位数的评分公式，捕捉分布特征并惩罚尾部失败。

Result: 验证了该框架能够有效处理评估不确定性下的检查点选择问题；证明了数据质量（特别是OCR可读性）是评估有效性的关键决定因素；所提方法能够更好地捕捉分布特征并对尾部失败进行惩罚。

Conclusion: 将检查点选择问题形式化为评估不确定性下的鲁棒决策问题，通过多阶段评估系统和置信度估计机制提升了选择的可靠性，为MLLM的实际部署提供了更可靠的模型选择方案，强调了高质量评估数据的重要性。

Abstract: Checkpoint selection for multimodal large language models (MLLMs) presents significant challenges when performance differentials are marginal and evaluation signals are prone to noise. Existing methodologies rely heavily on static benchmarks or pointwise scoring, which frequently misalign with in-the-wild usage and lack robust uncertainty estimation, particularly in OCR-heavy scenarios. In this work, we formulate checkpoint selection as a robust decision problem under evaluation uncertainty. We propose a multi-stage framework that integrates curated real-world data, structured LLM-based judgment, and multi-stage ranking protocols. The evaluation system orchestrates progressive refinement via pointwise filtering, listwise ranking, and pairwise comparison. To enhance reliability, we introduce subsampling-based confidence estimation and a percentile-based scoring formulation that captures distributional characteristics while penalizing tail failures. Furthermore, we demonstrate that data quality, specifically OCR readability, is a critical determinant of evaluation validity.

</details>


### [147] [Evaluating Memory Condensation Strategies for Coding Agents in Data-Driven Scientific Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18854)
*Renuka Chintalapati,Sid Raskar,Anurag Acharya,Jared Willard,Patrick Emami,Sameera Horawalavithana*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统性评估了8种记忆压缩策略，发现压缩策略对假设质量无显著影响，LLM压缩显著增加成本，掩码工具调用可节省成本，最优策略因领域和任务长度而异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码智能体在长期任务中积累大量上下文，固定上下文窗口迫使在截断与任务失败间选择。虽有多种记忆压缩策略被提出，但缺乏系统性比较，特别是在科学发现任务中。

Method: 使用GPT-4o在60个DiscoveryBench任务上进行评估，跨越6个科学领域，共480次评估，系统性比较8种记忆压缩策略。

Result: 没有压缩器显著改变假设质量；LLM压缩器增加24-94%的token成本；掩码工具调用输出实现8.6%的净节省；最优压缩器因科学领域和任务长度而异。

Conclusion: 在数据驱动的科学发现中，记忆压缩策略的选择需要根据具体领域和任务长度进行权衡，简单的掩码策略在成本效益上可能优于复杂的LLM压缩方法。

Abstract: Coding agents accumulate extensive context during long-running tasks, yet fixed context windows force practitioners to choose between truncation and task failure. While numerous memory condensation strategies have been proposed, from simple sliding windows to LLM-generated summaries, no systematic comparison exists to guide strategy selection, especially in scientific discovery tasks. We evaluate eight memory condensation strategies using GPT-4o on sixty DiscoveryBench tasks spanning six scientific domains (480 total evaluations). We find that no condenser significantly alters hypothesis quality, while LLM-based condensers increase token costs by 24-94 percent, and masking tool-call outputs achieves an 8.6 percent net savings. We also observe that the optimal condenser for data-driven scientific discovery varies by scientific domain and task length.

</details>


### [148] [Delta Attention Residuals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18855)
*Cheng Luo,Zefan Cai,Junjie Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Delta Attention Residuals，通过关注层间变化量（delta）而非累积状态，解决了标准Attention Residuals中的路由崩塌问题，在多种模型规模上实现1.7-8.2%的验证困惑度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Attention Residuals在先前层的累积隐藏状态上执行注意力机制，这些状态存在高度冗余性。这种冗余导致深层网络中出现路由崩塌现象：注意力权重变得低对比度且趋向均匀分布（最大权重≈0.2），限制了模型选择信息丰富状态的能力。这引发了关于Attention Residuals应该路由什么层表示的关键设计问题。

Method: 提出Delta Attention Residuals，在层间变化量（delta）上执行注意力机制，即每个子层引入的变化（vi = hi+1 - hi），而非累积状态。Delta表示具有结构多样性，能产生更高对比度的注意力分布（最大权重≈0.6），从而实现更具选择性和有效性的跨层路由。该原理可应用于子层级和块级两种粒度。

Result: 在所有测试规模（220M-7.6B参数）上，Delta Attention Residuals持续优于标准残差连接和Attention Residuals，实现1.7-8.2%的验证困惑度提升。注意力权重从低对比度（最大≈0.2）提升至高对比度（最大≈0.6），有效缓解了路由崩塌问题。此外，该方法支持通过标准微调将预训练检查点转换为Delta Attention Residuals。

Conclusion: Delta Attention Residuals通过路由层间变化量而非累积状态，成功解决了Attention Residuals中的冗余性问题，实现了更有效的跨层信息选择。该方法在多种模型规模上展现出一致的性能提升，具有广泛的适用性和实用价值。

Abstract: Attention Residuals replace standard additive residual connections with learned softmax attention over previous layer outputs, enabling selective cross-layer routing. However, standard Attention Residuals still attend over cumulative hidden states in previous layers, which are highly redundant. We show that this redundancy leads to routing collapse in deeper layers: attention weights become low-contrast and closer to uniform (max weight ${\approx}$0.2), limiting the model's ability to select informative states in previous layers. This raises a key but underexplored design question: what layer-wise representations should be routed in Attention Residuals? To answer this question, we propose Delta Attention Residuals, which attend over deltas -- the change introduced by each sublayer ($\mathbf{v}_i = \mathbf{h}_{i+1} - \mathbf{h}_i$) -- instead of cumulative states. Delta representations are structurally diverse and yield higher-contrast attention distributions (max weight ${\approx}$0.6), enabling more selective and effective routing across layers. This principle applies at both per-sublayer and block granularity. Across all tested scales (220M--7.6B), Delta Attention Residuals consistently outperform both standard residuals and Attention Residuals, with 1.7--8.2\% validation perplexity gains. Delta Attention Residuals also enables converting pretrained checkpoints into Delta Attention Residuals via standard fine-tuning. Code is available at https://github.com/wdlctc/delta-attention-residuals-code.

</details>


### [149] [SPHERICAL KV: Angle-Domain Attention and Rate-Distortion Retention for Efficient Long-Context Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18856)
*Anay Chauhan,Gurucharan Marthi Krishna Kumar,Arion Das,Amit Dhanda,Vinija Jain,Aman Chadha,Amitava Das*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Spherical KV方法，通过角度域注意力(ADA)和率失真保留(RDR)两个组件解决长上下文推理中的KV缓存瓶颈，减少内存占用并提升解码效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文推理中，KV缓存导致驻留内存随上下文长度增长，解码过程受限于高带宽内存(HBM)重复流传输而非算术运算。现有方法（驱逐、窗口化、量化、卸载）虽能减少内存占用，但往往未能完全解决关键路径瓶颈，特别是在解码时压缩状态仍需重建为稠密向量的情况下。

Method: 提出Spherical KV方法，包含两个核心组件：(1)角度域注意力(ADA)：将键存储为球面参数化形式（标量半径和紧凑角度编码），直接从这些编码计算注意力对数而无需重建稠密键，保持分页、块局部、融合友好的解码路径；(2)率失真保留(RDR)：在固定预算下联合选择每个token和head的保留/丢弃决策及精度层级，生成层级同构页面和轻量级元数据以实现合并读取。

Result: 该方法在保持解码效率的同时减少了KV驻留内存，通过直接在角度域计算注意力避免HBM流量，并在实际服务场景中实现了部署导向的内存优化机制。

Conclusion: Spherical KV成功将KV分配建模为基于注意力几何的率失真问题，在固定预算约束下实现了KV缓存的高效压缩与精准分配，为长上下文推理提供了一种实用的部署解决方案。

Abstract: Long-context inference is increasingly constrained by the KV cache: resident memory grows with context length, and decoding becomes limited by repeated High Bandwidth Memory (HBM) streaming rather than arithmetic. Existing methods such as eviction, windowing, quantization, and offloading reduce footprint, but often leave the critical-path bottleneck only partially addressed, especially when compressed states must still be reconstructed into dense vectors during decoding.
  We present Spherical KV, a long-context inference method that treats KV allocation as a rate-distortion problem grounded in attention geometry for efficient decoding. The method is built on two ideas: (i) represent directional information cheaply in the decode hot loop, and (ii) allocate retention and precision according to estimated future utility. Its first component, Angle-Domain Attention (ADA), stores keys in a spherical parameterization consisting of a scalar radius and compact angle codes, and computes attention logits directly from these codes without reconstructing dense keys. This preserves a paged, block-local, fusion-friendly decode path and directly targets HBM traffic in realistic serving settings. Its second component, Rate-Distortion Retention (RDR), jointly chooses keep/drop decisions and precision tiers per token and head under a fixed budget, producing tier-homogeneous pages with lightweight metadata and coalesced reads. Together, ADA and RDR provide a deployment-oriented mechanism for reducing KV residency while preserving decode efficiency.

</details>


### [150] [When Individually Calibrated Models Become Collectively Miscalibrated](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18858)
*Zhaohui Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在多智能体概率预测中，单独校准的模型在策略性交互下会导致集体校准失败。研究证明基于Brier分数的聚合存在系统性偏差（PoA可达7.25倍），而VCG机制聚合可实现激励相容和近优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 概率预测系统常假设单独校准的模型聚合后仍能保持校准。作者旨在揭示该假设在多智能体策略交互场景下失效的问题，并寻找有效的聚合机制来解决集体校准失败。

Method: 理论证明：在正相关的Brier分数聚合下，个体最优报告会系统性低估正类概率。提出使用VCG（Vickrey-Clarke-Groves）机制聚合，通过奖励边际贡献实现激励相容。在三个真实数据集（NSL-KDD、UNSW-NB15、信用卡欺诈）上验证。

Result: 在典型设置（5智能体、相关系数0.5、基准率0.3）下，假阴性率的PoA达7.25倍。VCG聚合实现占优策略激励相容，在数据稀疏和对抗场景表现优异，在分布漂移下自适应加权进一步提升性能。

Conclusion: 单独校准不足以保证集体校准，Brier分数聚合在策略交互下存在固有缺陷。VCG机制通过边际贡献激励，有效解决多智能体预测中的校准问题，为聚合预测提供了稳健的机制设计解决方案。

Abstract: Probabilistic prediction systems often aggregate probability estimates from multiple models into a single decision. A common assumption is that if each model is individually calibrated, the aggregate prediction will also be well calibrated. We show that this assumption fails in multi-agent settings: individually calibrated predictors can become collectively miscalibrated when their predictions interact strategically, in the game-theoretic sense of Brier-optimal local response, even without deliberate coordination.
  This phenomenon arises naturally when agents are independently trained on overlapping data. We prove that under Brier-score-based aggregation with positively correlated beliefs, each agent's individually optimal report systematically underestimates the positive-class probability, yielding a Price of Anarchy greater than one whenever Cov(b_i, b_j) > 0.
  In a canonical setting (n = 5 agents, pairwise correlation = 0.5, base rate = 0.3), the empirically measured PoA in false-negative rate reaches 7.25x. In contrast, VCG-based aggregation aligns incentives by rewarding marginal contribution, achieving dominant-strategy incentive compatibility and near-optimal performance.
  Experiments on three real-world datasets (NSL-KDD, UNSW-NB15, Credit Card Fraud) show that VCG provides strong robustness while maintaining comparable accuracy. It performs particularly well in data-sparse and adversarial settings, and adaptive weighting further improves performance under distribution shift.

</details>


### [151] [TwinRouterBench: Fast Static and Live Dynamic Evaluation for Realistic Agentic LLM Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18859)
*Pei Yang,Wanyi Chen,Tongyun Yang,Pengbin Feng,Jiarong Xing,Wentao Guo,Yuhang Yao,Yuhang Han,Hanchen Li,Xu Wang,Zeyu Wang,Jie Xiao,Anjie Yang,Liang Tian,Lynn Ai,Eric Yang,Tianyu Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了TwinRouterBench，首个针对长周期应用（如编码智能体）的步骤级LLM路由基准，包含静态和动态两条评测轨道，解决了现有基准仅评估单次提示、缺乏中间步骤可见性和下游任务成功验证的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM路由基准仅评估单次提示，无法模拟长周期应用中单个用户请求触发多次模型调用的场景。现有方法存在三个关键缺陷：从未暴露中间智能体步骤的路由可见前缀、未测试更便宜的替代模型是否保留下游任务成功率、以及在评估时依赖在线LLM评判器。

Method: 提出TwinRouterBench基准，包含两条互补轨道：静态轨道提供来自SWE-bench、BFCL、mtRAG、QMSum和PinchBench的970个路由可见前缀（520个实例），每个前缀配有在发布降级级联协议下估计的执行验证目标层级，评分基于层级标签、轨迹成员关系和token成本的确定性算术运算，评估端无在线LLM评判器；动态轨道提供在完整500例SWE-bench Verified套件上运行路由器的测试工具，路由器从锁定池中选择具体模型，通过官方任务解决率和实际API支出来衡量成功。

Result: 静态轨道提供了跨5个数据集的大规模步骤级路由评测数据；动态轨道在论文中报告了100例留出评估（与静态SWE监督分割不相交）。两条轨道支持快速离线迭代，随后在实时智能体执行下进行端到端验证。

Conclusion: TwinRouterBench填补了长周期应用中步骤级LLM路由评测的空白，通过静态和动态双轨道设计实现了快速迭代与端到端验证的平衡，为路由器在复杂智能体系统中的实际部署提供了可靠的评测框架。

Abstract: LLM routing matters most in long-horizon applications such as coding agents, deep research systems, and computer-use agents, where a single user request triggers many model calls. Routing each call to the cheapest sufficient model can cut costs without sacrificing quality, yet existing router benchmarks evaluate routers only on one-shot prompts. They never expose the router-visible prefix at an intermediate agent step, never test whether a cheaper replacement preserves downstream task success, and often rely on online LLM judges at evaluation time. We introduce TwinRouterBench, a step-level routing benchmark with two tracks. The static track provides 970 router-visible prefixes from 520 instances across SWE-bench, BFCL, mtRAG, QMSum, and PinchBench, each paired with an execution-verified target tier estimated under a released downgrade-and-cascade protocol; scoring is deterministic arithmetic over tier labels, trajectory membership, and token costs, with no online evaluator-side LLM judge. The dynamic track supplies a harness that runs routers on the full 500-case SWE-bench Verified suite; in this paper we report a 100-case held-out evaluation disjoint from the static SWE supervision split. At each LLM call the router selects a concrete model from a locked pool, and success is measured by official task resolution and realized API spend. The two tracks support fast offline iteration followed by end-to-end validation under live agent execution. Code and data are available at https://github.com/CommonstackAI/TwinRouterBench.

</details>


### [152] [From Llama to Cria: Scaling Down Neural Networks via Neuron-Level Spectral Structural Importance Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18860)
*Yongyu Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于谱结构重要性评估的神经元剪枝框架，通过图信号处理方法分析神经元的结构关系，实现神经网络的有效压缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的剪枝方法缺乏对神经元在内部表示转换中结构重要性的理论性评估。本文旨在通过图信号处理和谱图理论，提供一种更有原则的神经元重要性度量方法，从而在保持性能的同时实现网络压缩。

Method: 该方法将神经元建模为图节点，隐藏状态视为图信号。利用图信号处理技术推断每层的输入和输出图，以表征层变换前后神经元间的结构关系。通过谱图理论分析图之间的变换来评估神经元的谱结构重要性。高重要性神经元被保留，低重要性神经元作为剪枝候选。采用迭代剪枝策略，先移除低重要性神经元得到紧凑架构，最后进行一次恢复性微调。

Result: 在CIFAR-10图像分类和SST-2情感分类任务上的实验表明，该方法能有效移除低重要性神经元，在恢复微调后获得具有竞争力性能的紧凑网络。

Conclusion: 通过将神经元剪枝与图信号处理及谱结构分析相结合，本文提出的框架为在保持解质量的同时减少神经网络规模提供了一种有原则的方法，证明了谱结构重要性评估在神经网络剪枝中的有效性。

Abstract: This paper proposes a neuron pruning framework based on neuron-level spectral structural importance evaluation. Given a trained neural network, we record the hidden states of each hidden layer during inference and model neurons as graph nodes, with hidden states treated as graph signals. Using ideas from graph signal processing, we infer layer-wise input and output graphs that characterize the structural relationships among neurons before and after each layer transformation. We then evaluate the spectral structural importance of neurons by analyzing the transformation between these graphs based on spectral graph theory. Neurons with high spectral structural importance are regarded as strongly involved in the internal representation transformation and are therefore preserved, while neurons with low importance scores are selected as pruning candidates. The pruning process is conducted iteratively until a predefined effective parameter reduction target is reached. Instead of fine-tuning after every pruning step, the proposed strategy first removes low-importance neurons to obtain a compact architecture and then applies a final recovery fine-tuning stage to restore task performance. By connecting neuron pruning with graph signal processing and spectral structural analysis, the proposed framework offers a principled way to reduce neural network size while maintaining solution quality. Experimental results on CIFAR-10 image classification and SST-2 sentiment classification show that our method can effectively remove low-importance neurons and achieve compact networks with competitive performance after recovery fine-tuning.

</details>


### [153] [Towards Family-Grouped Hierarchical Federated Learning on Sub-5KB Models: A Feasibility Study of Privacy-Preserving ECG Monitoring for Ultra-Resource-Constrained Wearables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18862)
*Hangyu Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Family-FL框架（家庭分组层级联邦学习）配合Tiny CNN-LSTM模型，在超低功耗MCU上实现隐私保护的ECG心律失常检测，通信量减少76.7%（最低降至FedAvg的0.31%），模型仅4.65KB Flash/2.95KB RAM，准确率91.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心血管疾病是全球首要死因，可穿戴设备持续ECG监测可早期发现心律失常，但存在两大挑战：1) 标准联邦学习通信开销过大；2) 标准深度学习模型无法运行于超低功耗微控制器。

Method: 提出Family-FL三层架构（设备-家庭-全局），以家庭为隐私边界进行组内聚合后再全局同步；设计硬件约束的Tiny CNN-LSTM模型，仅669参数，INT8量化后占用4.65KB Flash和2.95KB RAM，满足STC32G12K128级MCU约束。

Result: 在MIT-BIH心律失常数据库上，Family-FL通信量相比FedAvg减少76.7%且准确率相当；Family-FL-Tiny准确率91.9±1.2%，宏F1为0.483±0.031，总通信量仅为FedAvg的0.31%；室性心律失常检测F1达0.80。

Conclusion: 证明了在超资源受限微控制器上实现隐私保护联邦学习的技术可行性，适用于家庭初筛场景。作者诚实指出局限性：无硬件实际部署、单数据集验证、稀有类别敏感性降低、缺乏形式化差分隐私保证。

Abstract: Cardiovascular disease remains the leading cause of death worldwide, and early detection of arrhythmias through continuous ECG monitoring on wearable devices can prevent life-threatening events. Federated Learning (FL) enables privacy-preserving collaborative training by keeping raw ECG data on device, yet standard FL incurs prohibitive communication overhead and standard deep learning models cannot fit on ultra-low-power microcontrollers. We propose Family-Grouped Hierarchical Federated Learning (Family-FL), a three-tier architecture that uses the family as a natural privacy boundary for intra-family aggregation before global synchronization. We further design a hardware-constrained Tiny CNN-LSTM architecture with only 669 parameters, INT8-quantized to occupy merely 4.65KB Flash and 2.95KB RAM, meeting the constraints of STC32G12K128-class microcontrollers. Experiments on the MIT-BIH Arrhythmia Database (mean of 5 independent runs with different seeds) demonstrate that Family-FL reduces communication volume by 76.7% compared to FedAvg while maintaining comparable accuracy. Family-FL-Tiny achieves 91.9 +/- 1.2% accuracy with macro-F1 of 0.483 +/- 0.031, reducing total communication to 0.31% of FedAvg. The model achieves reliable ventricular arrhythmia detection (per-class F1 = 0.80), the most clinically critical abnormality for home-based preliminary screening. These results demonstrate the technical feasibility of privacy-preserving federated learning on ultra-resource-constrained microcontrollers through simulation-based evaluation. We honestly discuss limitations: no hardware deployment, single-dataset validation (MIT-BIH, 47 subjects), reduced rare-class sensitivity, and absence of formal differential privacy guarantees.

</details>


### [154] [SAGE: Shaping Anchors for Guided Exploration in RLVR of LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18864)
*Chanuk Lee,Minki Kang,Sung Ju Hwang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对RLVR训练中反向KL正则化抑制新推理模式产生的问题，提出SAGE框架，通过引导函数重塑锚定分布，在数学推理基准上同时提升了pass@1和pass@k性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究表明强化学习与可验证奖励（RLVR）虽能提升pass@1，但在pass@k上提升有限。先前分析认为这只是提高了基础模型中已有推理模式的采样效率，而非真正获得新的推理能力，主要归因于标准RLVR目标函数的结构特性导致探索压力不足。本文认为核心结构约束来自反向KL正则化，它在稳定训练的同时将策略锚定到参考分布，从而抑制了替代推理模式的出现。

Method: 提出SAGE框架，通过引导函数q(x,y)重塑反向KL锚定分布本身，实现可控的经验支持扩展。该方法既避免了移除KL项或替换为前向KL所带来的奖励黑客或概率质量分配到非目标区域的问题，又解决了效率-覆盖率权衡的矛盾。

Result: 在具有挑战性的数学推理基准测试中，SAGE框架在pass@1和pass@k指标上均取得了一致的改进。

Conclusion: SAGE成功解决了RLVR训练中效率与覆盖率之间的权衡问题，通过可控的经验支持扩展，使模型能够真正获得新的推理能力，而非仅仅增强已有推理模式的采样效率。

Abstract: Recent studies observe that reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) reliably improves pass@1 on reasoning tasks, yet often fails to yield comparable gains in pass@k, raising the question of whether RLVR genuinely enables large language models to acquire novel reasoning abilities or merely enhances the efficiency of sampling reasoning modes already present in the base model. Prior analyses largely support the latter view, attributing this limitation to structural properties of standard RLVR objectives that result in insufficient exploration pressure. In this work, we argue that a central structural constraint arises from reverse-KL regularization, which stabilizes training but inherently anchors the policy to the reference distribution, thereby suppressing the emergence of alternative reasoning modes. However, we show that neither removing the KL term nor replacing it with forward-KL provides a satisfactory solution, as both disrupt the efficiency-coverage trade-off by either inducing reward hacking or allocating probability mass to off-target regions. To resolve this tension, we propose SAGE, a principled framework that enables controllable empirical support expansion by reshaping the reverse-KL anchor distribution itself through a guide function q(x,y), achieving consistent improvements in both pass@1 and pass@k across challenging mathematical reasoning benchmarks. Our code is available at https://github.com/tally0818/SAGE.

</details>


### [155] [From Sparsity to Simplicity: Enabling Simpler Sequential Replacements via Sparse Attention Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18865)
*Yuxin Ren,Maxwell D Collins,Miao Hu,Huanrui Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出利用注意力稀疏性指导Transformer中注意力层的替换，发现替换稀疏注意力层比替换稠密层导致的精度损失更小，从而实现更高效的模型压缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自注意力机制是大规模Transformer预训练的核心，但其二次方的token交互成本导致推理开销巨大。虽然用简单的序列模块替换注意力很有吸引力，但简单的替换往往会导致性能损失，尤其是在大规模模型上。

Method: 基于观察到Transformer各层存在多样化的稀疏性模式，作者提出了一个即插即用的逐层蒸馏框架来近似和替换预训练视觉Transformer中的注意力功能。具体方法包括：1) 在固定训练预算下进行受控的分组替换实验；2) 通过AViT风格的token保留机制对预训练ViT施加显式注意力稀疏性；3) 进行稀疏性引导的蒸馏以训练序列替换模型。

Result: 实验结果揭示了一个清晰的模式：用稀疏注意力层进行替换比替换稠密注意力层导致的精度下降要小得多。增加教师模型的稀疏性可以持续减小学生-教师模型之间的差距。所提出的方法成功实现了高效的注意力替换，减少了参数量和延迟。

Conclusion: 预训练Transformer将复杂的token依赖关系分解为不同复杂度的序列到序列映射，其中一些层功能可以用更简单的序列模块近似替换而不造成损失。注意力稀疏性可以作为指导注意力层高效替换的有效指标。

Abstract: Self-attention serves as the core foundation of large-scale transformer pretraining, but its quadratic token interaction cost makes inference expensive. Replacing attention with simpler sequential modules is appealing, yet naive substitution is often lossy, especially at larger scales. This paper revisits attention replacement through the lens of sparsity. Based on the observation of diverse sparsity patterns across transformer layers, we posit that pretrained transformers decompose the complex token dependency across tokens into various sequence-to-sequence mappings of diverse complexities, where some layer functionalities can be approximated and replaced with much simpler sequential modules without loss. We evaluate this premise using a plug-and-play layer-wise distillation framework to approximate and replace attention functionalities in pretrained vision transformer models. Controlled group-wise replacements under a fixed training budget reveal a clear pattern: substituting layers with sparser attention incurs substantially smaller accuracy drops than replacing denser ones. We further impose explicit attention sparsity on the pretrained ViT via AViT-style token retention and perform sparsity-guided distillation for sequential replacing models, where we see increasing teacher sparsity consistently reduces the student-teacher gap. The proposed method achieves efficient attention replacement for reduced parameter size and latency through the guidance of attention sparsity.

</details>


### [156] [FLUIDSPLAT: Reconstructing Physical Fields from Sparse Sensors via Gaussian Primitives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18866)
*Huaxi Huang,Meng Li,Zhengqing Gao,Xi Zhou,Xiaoshui Huang,Xiao Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FLUIDSPLAT方法，利用各向异性高斯基元构建显式流场表示，在圆柱流和AirfRANS基准测试中，相比最强基线误差降低11-23%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经方法将传感器数据编码为隐式潜在表示，缺乏空间可解释性，且缺乏关于表示容量如何随观测数量扩展的形式化指导。

Method: 受3D高斯泼溅启发，提出传感器条件模型FLUIDSPLAT，预测K个各向异性高斯基元形成单位划分支架，作为流场的显式可解释中间表示。理论分析揭示稀疏感知下的方差瓶颈，据此设计了结合状态条件残差解码器的完整架构。

Result: 在圆柱流基准测试中，FLUIDSPLAT在所有表面传感器布局上均取得最佳平均误差；在AirfRANS数据集上使用8个表面压力传感器时，在三个标准划分上相比最强基线误差降低11-23%。理论证明了O(K^{-s/d})的近似率和最优基元数量K*~(N/σ²)^{d/(2s+d)}。

Conclusion: 高斯基元支架提供了空间显式且可解释的流场表示，通过理论分析指导的架构设计有效解决了稀疏感知下的方差瓶颈问题，在流场重建任务上取得了优越性能。

Abstract: Reconstructing continuous flow fields from sparse surface-mounted sensors is central to aerodynamic design, flow control, and digital-twin instrumentation. Existing neural methods for this task typically encode sensor readings into implicit latent codes with little spatial interpretability and limited formal guidance on how representational capacity should scale with observation count. Inspired by 3D Gaussian Splatting, we introduce FLUIDSPLAT, a sensor-conditioned model that predicts K anisotropic Gaussian primitives forming a partition-of-unity scaffold, a spatially explicit and interpretable intermediate representation of the flow. For an idealized Gaussian primitive estimator, we prove an $O(K^{-s/d})$ approximation rate for fields with Sobolev smoothness $s$; incorporating $N$ noisy observations yields a squared-risk decomposition with bias $O(K^{-2s/d})$ and variance $O(σ^{2}K/N)$.Balancing the two yields $K^{*}\!\sim\!(N/σ^{2})^{d/(2s+d)}$: primitive count cannot grow freely under sparse sensing, revealing a variance bottleneck that motivates complementing the scaffold with a state-conditioned residual decoder. On a standard cylinder-flow benchmark, FLUIDSPLAT achieves the best mean error across all surface-sensor layouts; on AirfRANS with 8 surface-pressure sensors, it reduces error by 11-23% over the strongest baseline across three standard splits.

</details>


### [157] [EVA-0: Test-Time Model Evolution with Only Two Forward Passes per Sample](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18867)
*Guohao Chen,Shuaicheng Niu,Geng Li,Yunbei Zhang,Shilin Shan,Chunyan Miao,Jianfei Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了EVA-0，一种无需反向传播的零阶测试时模型演化框架，仅需两次前向传播即可完成推理与自适应，在ImageNet-C与ViT-Base上性能优于基于BP的DeYO和BP-free的FOA，且速度相比FOA提升14倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有测试时模型演化方法大多依赖反向传播（BP），这带来了显著的内存开销，导致难以在边缘设备、量化模型、专用加速器或黑盒模型上部署。为了推动自适应方法在实际高效部署场景中的应用，本文研究在严格“两次前向传播”预算下的测试时模型演化。

Method: 提出EVA-0，一种最小化零阶自适应框架：1) 保持损失尺度不变性以避免捷径解；2) 设计锚点引导优化策略缓解权重漂移；3) 采用样本级对称双侧扰动进行更新方向估计与推理。该方法无需BP，每样本仅需两次前向传播即可同时完成推理与自适应。

Result: 在ImageNet-C与ViT-Base上的实验表明，EVA-0在性能上优于基于BP的DeYO和BP-free的FOA方法，同时实现相比FOA的14倍加速。

Conclusion: EVA-0成功解决了零阶测试时优化中的关键障碍，在严格计算预算下实现了高效且有效的测试时模型演化，为实际部署提供了可行的解决方案。代码将会开源。

Abstract: Test-time model evolution offers a promising way for deployed models to improve from unlabeled test-time experience, yet most existing methods depend on backpropagation (BP), which incurs substantial memory overhead and makes them difficult to deploy on edge devices, quantized models, specialized accelerators, or black-box models. In this work, we study test-time model evolution under a strict two-forward budget, a setting that pushes adaptation toward highly efficient real-world deployment. We reveal three key obstacles in zeroth-order test-time optimization: susceptibility to shortcut solutions, uncontrolled weight drift, and ineffective update direction estimation. To overcome them, we propose EVA-0, a minimal zeroth-order adaptation framework that: 1) keeps the loss scale-invariant to prevent shortcut solutions; 2) devises an anchor-guided optimization strategy to alleviate weight drift; 3) uses sample-wise symmetric two-sided perturbation for update direction estimation and inference. EVA-0 requires no BP and performs both inference and adaptation within only two forward passes per sample. Results on ImageNet-C & ViT-Base show that EVA-0 outperforms both BP-based DeYO and BP-free FOA, while achieving a 14x speed-up over FOA. Code will be released.

</details>


### [158] [MO-CAPO: Multi-Objective Cost-Aware Prompt Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18869)
*Jan Büssing,Moritz Schlager,Timo Heiß,Tom Zehle,Matthias Feurer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了MO-CAPO算法，一种面向大语言模型的多目标提示优化方法，在优化性能的同时考虑推理成本，并通过预算分配机制提高优化效率，使实践者能够从多个性能-成本权衡的提示方案中进行选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有提示优化方法主要关注单一性能目标，忽视了推理成本、延迟等竞争目标；而现有的多目标优化方法直接使用通用NSGA-II算法，未考虑优化效率问题，缺乏针对LLM推理特点的专门设计。

Method: 提出MO-CAPO（Multi-Objective Cost-Aware Prompt Optimization）算法，通过预算分配机制实现成本高效的多目标提示优化；设计面向部署的成本目标，全面捕捉LLLM推理的计算特性；引入噪声R2指标和近似差距评估方法，首次考虑多目标机器学习实验中的泛化性和鲁棒性。

Result: 在四个任务和三个LLM上的实验表明：MO-CAPO在12个案例中的8个上，在噪声R2指标方面优于NSGA-II基线；在较低预算下即可达到具有竞争力的性能；发现的解集覆盖了单目标优化器忽略的多样化性能-成本权衡方案，且top-performance候选方案与单目标优化解具有竞争力。

Conclusion: MO-CAPO能够在保持成本效率的同时，一致地识别出强健、鲁棒且多样化的帕累托前沿近似，为实践者提供了一系列具有不同性能-成本权衡的提示方案选择，弥补了现有方法在多目标优化效率和成本感知方面的不足。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) achieve strong performance across a wide range of tasks but are highly sensitive to prompt design, motivating the need for automatic prompt optimization. Existing methods predominantly focus on performance alone, ignoring competing objectives such as inference cost or latency. At the same time, existing work on multi-objective prompt optimization relies on off-the-shelf NSGA-II, ignoring optimization efficiency. As a remedy, we introduce MO-CAPO, a novel multi-objective prompt optimization algorithm that jointly optimizes performance and inference cost while leveraging budget allocation for cost-efficient optimization. We further propose a deployment-oriented cost objective that captures the full computational profile of LLM inference. We evaluate our approach across four tasks and three LLMs and compare it to an NSGA-II-based multi-objective method and state-of-the-art single-objective prompt optimizers. Results show that MO-CAPO consistently identifies strong, robust, and diverse Pareto front approximations while maintaining cost-efficiency. It outperforms the NSGA-II baseline on 8 out of 12 cases in terms of the noisy R2 metric and achieves competitive performances often already at a considerably lower budget. The discovered solution sets span diverse performance-cost trade-offs that are omitted by single-objective optimizers, yet the top-performance candidates remain competitive with single-objective solutions. Additionally, we conduct the first evaluation of multi-objective machine learning experiments that considers generalization and robustness through noisy R2 and approximation gap, enabling a more realistic assessment of solution quality. MO-CAPO enables practitioners to select from an efficiently discovered set of multiple prompts offering different trade-offs between performance and cost.

</details>


### [159] [Multi-Headed Transformer Architectures as Time-dependent Wasserstein Gradient Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18870)
*Alex Massucco,Leonardo Del Grande,Marcello Carioni,Christoph Brune,Carola-Bibiane Schönlieb*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将多头Transformer架构的数据流建模为时间相关的梯度流，并证明了其稳定性与收敛性，为Transformer提供了严格的理论基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年来Transformer架构在语言处理领域取得了革命性进展，但现有文献中的数学模型往往与实际架构缺乏直接联系，且依赖于强简化假设。本文旨在通过建立多头Transformer数据流与梯度流之间的联系来缩小这一理论与实践的差距。

Method: 将多头Transformer架构中的数据流建模为交互能量的时间相关梯度流，该能量函数刻画了注意力机制的设计。模型中显式的时间依赖允许为每个头和每层分配不同权重，且对初始化方法无约束。通过分析ω-极限集、研究初始数据和权重的扰动、以及证明Γ-收敛性来研究梯度流的稳定性。

Result: 证明了在适当的权重演化可积性假设下，梯度流的ω-极限集的每个元素都是极限权重分布下交互能量的驻点；建立了梯度流对初始数据的连续依赖性和流的唯一性；证明了扰动交互能量到未扰动交互能量的Γ-收敛性；数值实验验证了能量耗散恒等式，并阐明了自洽类（Ornstein-Uhlenbeck）和非自治（振荡权重）两种情况下的渐近行为。

Conclusion: 本文成功地为多头Transformer架构建立了严格的数学理论框架，通过梯度流理论刻画了数据流动力学，证明了系统的稳定性和收敛性，为Transformer架构的理论理解提供了重要基础，实验结果与理论预测一致。

Abstract: In recent years, transformer architectures have revolutionized the field of language processing, opening the door to previously unforeseen possibilities. However, from a theoretical point of view, the mathematical models proposed in the literature often lack direct contact with the actual architectures and depend on strong simplifying assumptions. In this paper, we reduce this gap by modelling the data flow in multi-headed transformer architectures as time-dependent gradient flows for a suitable interaction energy capturing the design of the attention mechanism. The explicit dependence on time allows us to consider different weights for each head and for each layer, without imposing constraints on the initialization method. Moreover, we prove that, under a suitable integrability assumption on the evolution of the weights, each element of the $ω$-limit set of the gradient flows is a stationary point of the interaction energy at a limiting weight distribution. Finally, we analyse the stability of the gradient flows considering perturbations of both the initial data and the weights. Specifically, on the one hand, we study the robustness of the proposed models with respect to noisy inputs, establishing a continuous dependence of the gradient flows on the initial data and uniqueness of the flows. On the other hand, we prove the $Γ$-convergence of the perturbed interaction energy to the unperturbed one, leading to the convergence of the corresponding gradient flows. We complement these theoretical results with numerical experiments that confirm the predicted energy-dissipation identity and clarify the asymptotic behavior of the dynamics in both the autonomous-like (Ornstein--Uhlenbeck) and the genuinely non-autonomous (oscillating-weights) regimes.

</details>


### [160] [Distributional Energy-Based Models for Uncertainty-Aware Structured LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18871)
*Shireen Kudukkil Manchingal,Abhey Kalia,Fernanda Gonçalves,Shebin Rawther*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合学习质量评分器与确定性约束惩罚的分解能量函数，用于验证大语言模型的结构化输出。该149M参数的验证器在五个基准测试中协调多个开源生成器，表现优于Qwen-72B，在MuSR上匹配Claude Sonnet 4.6，在TravelPlanner上减少53%的约束违规。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在生成结构化输出（如旅行计划、代码解决方案、多步证明）时，单个推理步骤可能看似正确，但整体输出却可能违反预算、未通过测试用例或与先前的推论相矛盾。需要一种方法来有效验证和改进这些结构化输出。

Method: 提出分解能量函数验证器，结合异构集成的低秩适配器质量评分器（仅3%可训练参数）与确定性分析约束惩罚。集成均值对候选进行排序，标准差量化认知不确定性，驱动两阶段推理循环触发定向重新生成或弃权。使用149M参数的验证器协调7-26B规模的开源生成器池。

Result: 在五个基准测试（GSM8K、MuSR、TravelPlanner、TACO、Knights & Knaves）上，验证器在所有基准上均优于单次Qwen-72B；在MuSR上匹配Claude Sonnet 4.6（67.7% vs 68.0%）；在TravelPlanner上相对于Opus 4.6减少53%的约束违规（oracle 0.028，random 0.231）。在MuSR上训练的评分器在GSM8K上实现93.9%准确率，实现了零样本迁移。

Conclusion: 结构化验证与预训练规模先验具有互补性：当约束可检查时结构化验证获胜（验证器捕获前沿模型无法自检测的信号），当约束不可检查时预训练规模先验获胜（叙事推理、代码语义）。跨数据集混淆分析确认在四个推理任务上具有真实质量判别能力，并识别出代码任务中存在模型身份捷径，可通过最后一层重新训练缓解。

Abstract: When Large Language Models produce structured outputs such as travel plans, code solutions, or multi-step proofs, individual reasoning steps may appear correct while the output as a whole violates budgets, fails test cases, or contradicts earlier deductions. We propose a decomposed energy function that combines a learned quality scorer with deterministic analytical constraint penalties for verifying structured LLM outputs. The quality scorer is a heterogeneous ensemble of low-rank adapters on a single frozen encoder (3% trainable parameters); the ensemble mean ranks candidates while the standard deviation quantifies epistemic uncertainty, driving a two-pass inference loop that triggers targeted regeneration or abstention. Across five benchmarks (GSM8K, MuSR, TravelPlanner, TACO, Knights & Knaves), our 149M-parameter verifier orchestrating a pool of 7-26B open generators outperforms single-shot Qwen-72B on every benchmark, matches Claude Sonnet 4.6 on MuSR (67.7% vs. 68.0%), and reduces constraint violations by 53% relative to Opus 4.6 on TravelPlanner (oracle 0.028, random 0.231). The two routes are complementary: structural verification wins when constraints are checkable (the verifier captures signal frontier models cannot self-detect), while pretraining-scale priors win where they are not (narrative inference, code semantics). A cross-dataset confounding analysis confirms genuine quality discrimination on four reasoning tasks and identifies a model-identity shortcut on code, mitigated via last-layer retraining. Scorers trained on difficult data transfer zero-shot: a MuSR-trained scorer achieves 93.9% on GSM8K without seeing a math problem.

</details>


### [161] [EUPHORIA: Efficient Universal Planning via Hybrid Optimization for Robust Industrial Robotic Assembly](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18872)
*Shih-Yu Lai,Chia-Ching Yen,Yang-Ting Shen,Peter Yichen Chen,Yu-Lun Liu,Bing-Yu Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了EUPHORIA框架，解决了建筑机器人装配中规划器通用性差和效率低的问题，实现了对未见复杂几何结构的少样本适应与高效装配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有建筑装配机器人规划器存在两大瓶颈：一是高度专业化，对新几何设计需昂贵的重新训练；二是将结构序列规划与运动学处理解耦，导致运行效率低下。

Method: 提出统一框架EUPHORIA，包含四个核心组件：1) 基于图超网络的元几何编码器，从少量样本动态生成策略参数，实现无需梯度重训练的参数级适应；2) 基于软演员-评论家（SAC）训练的物理信息图Transformer，引入物理偏置注意力机制，利用离散元模型接触力引导规划器关注结构关键连接；3) 运动学感知序列规划，在SAC目标函数中惩罚高能量状态转换；4) 残差稳定性校正，通过可微优化层联合最小化能量-稳定性代价以弥合仿真到现实（Sim2Real）鸿沟。

Result: 实验表明，EUPHORIA显著降低了相比解耦基线的能耗，并在仅需极少样本的情况下，对未见过的非标准几何结构实现了最先进的装配成功率。

Conclusion: 该研究成功将元学习、物理信息注意力机制与残差优化融合为一个统一的规划器，为建筑机器人装配提供了一个兼具通用少样本适应能力与动态运行效率的通用解决方案。

Abstract: Robotic assembly in architectural construction faces a persistent bottleneck: existing planners are either highly specialized, requiring prohibitive retraining for every new geometric design, or operationally inefficient, treating structural sequencing and kinematic motion as disjoint processes. We present EUPHORIA, a unified framework that achieves universal few-shot adaptability and dynamic efficiency through a hybrid optimization strategy. To overcome the retraining bottleneck, we propose a Meta-Geometric Encoder based on Graph Hypernetworks: unlike standard contrastive learning, which performs only feature-level recognition, our hypernetwork dynamically generates policy parameters from a minimal support set, enabling parameter-level adaptation to complex topologies (e.g., domes, arches) without gradient-based retraining.
