For structural reasoning, we introduce a Physics-Informed Graph Transformer trained via Soft Actor-Critic (SAC), with a Physics-Bias Attention mechanism that modulates attention scores using contact forces from Discrete Element Model (DEM) simulations, guiding the planner toward structurally critical connections. We further ensure operational efficiency through Kinematics-Aware Sequencing, where the SAC objective penalizes high-energy transitions. Finally, we bridge the Sim2Real gap via Residual Stability Correction, a differentiable optimization layer that fine-tunes coarse assembly actions by minimizing a joint energy-stability cost prior to execution. Experiments show that EUPHORIA significantly reduces energy consumption over decoupled baselines and achieves state-of-the-art success rates on unseen, non-standard geometries with minimal few-shot examples, fusing meta-learning, physics-informed attention, and residual optimization into a cohesive, generalized planner.

</details>


### [162] [ZeroUnlearn: Few-Shot Knowledge Unlearning in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18879)
*Yujie Lin,Chengyi Yang,Zhishang Xiang,Yiping Song,Jinsong Su*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ZeroUnlearn，一种基于模型编辑的高效机器遗忘框架，通过将敏感输入重映射到中性目标状态并施加表示正交性约束，实现了无需昂贵重训练的精准遗忘，同时保持了模型的整体性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在海量网络语料库上训练时不可避免地保留了敏感信息，这些信息可能导致有害生成，引发隐私和安全问题。现有的机器遗忘方法主要依赖重训练或激进的微调，前者计算成本高昂，后者容易降低相关知识及模型的整体效用。

Method: 将机器遗忘重新定义为通过模型编辑实现的精确知识重映射问题。提出ZeroUnlearn框架，通过将敏感输入映射到中性目标状态并移除其原始表示来覆盖敏感输入；利用具有闭式解的乘法参数更新来强制表示正交性，实现高效且有针对性的遗忘。此外，还将该方法扩展为基于梯度的变体以支持多样本遗忘。

Result: 实验结果表明，该方法在遗忘效果上优于现有基线方法，同时能够有效保持模型的通用性能。

Conclusion: ZeroUnlearn成功地将机器遗忘问题转化为精确的知识重映射任务，提供了一种计算高效、目标精准且能保持模型效用的遗忘解决方案，并通过实验验证了其优越性。

Abstract: Large language models inevitably retain sensitive information, defined as inputs that may induce harmful generations, due to training on massive web corpora, raising concerns for privacy and safety. Existing machine unlearning methods primarily rely on retraining or aggressive fine-tuning, which are either computationally expensive or prone to degrading related knowledge and overall model utility. In this work, we reformulate machine unlearning as a precise knowledge re-mapping problem via model editing. We propose ZeroUnlearn, a few-shot unlearning framework. It overwrites sensitive inputs by mapping them to a neutral target state and removing their original representations. ZeroUnlearn enforces representational orthogonality through a multiplicative parameter update with a closed-form solution, enabling efficient and targeted unlearning. We further extend ZeroUnlearn to a gradient-based variant for multi-sample unlearning. Experiments demonstrate that our approach outperforms existing baselines while preserving general model utility. Our code is available at the github: https://github.com/XMUDeepLIT/ZeroUnlearn.

</details>


### [163] [A Multi-Dimensional Clustering Approach for Identifying Inborn Errors of Immunity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18880)
*Nishad Kulkarni,Alexandra K. Martinson,Nicholas L. Rider,Michael Keller,Syed Muhammad Anwar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种针对罕见病（特别是免疫出生错误IEI）的机器学习管道，能够从国家电子健康记录（EHR）数据中识别新的罕见病模式并提取IEI相关特征，为早期诊断提供支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 罕见病如免疫出生错误（IEI）需要早期诊断以预防终末器官损伤并改善生活质量。然而，访问和管理大规模电子健康记录（EHR）数据的障碍限制了常规数据驱动分析在IEI和其他罕见病趋势研究中的应用。此外，目前针对IEI的机器学习算法开发以及系统性处理和整合复杂医疗数据的方法论研究均十分有限。

Method: 研究提出了一个完整的数据处理管道，包括数据管理和机器学习聚类算法。该方法首先将原始免疫学实验室数据转换为向量格式，然后结合超参数调优技术，通过聚类算法进行疾病模式识别，从而系统地处理和整合复杂的医疗数据。

Result: 该方法成功实现了从国家数据注册库中识别新的罕见病模式，并能够提取与IEI相关的特征。研究开发了适用于罕见病人群分析的数据工具包，成功将复杂的医疗记录转换为可被无监督机器学习算法解释的数据结构。

Conclusion: 本研究提高了对IEI特征的认知水平，开发了专门用于罕见病人群分析的数据工具包，并拓展了将复杂医疗记录转换为可供无监督机器学习解释的数据结构的方法，为罕见病的早期诊断和数据驱动研究提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Rare diseases such as inborn errors of immunity (IEI) require early diagnosis to prevent end organ damage and improve quality of life. Hurdles in accessing and curating large scale electronic health record (EHR) data limit routine data driven analyses to remain on the forefront of IEI and other rare disease trends. Development of machine learning (ML) algorithms in IEI for pattern recognition as well as published methodology examining how to systematically process and integrate complex medical data is limited. Our proposed pipeline, including data curation and ML clustering algorithms, is designed to recognize novel rare disease patterns and extract IEI- associated features from a national data registry. Our methodology for EHR data formatting and processing presents the pipeline that transforms raw immunologic lab data into vectors. This is further combined with hyperparameter tuning for diseases pattern recognition via clustering. This study refines IEI feature awareness, develops data tool kits for rare disease populations analysis, and expands on transforming complex medical records in data structures interpretable by unsupervised ML.

</details>


### [164] [Emergence of a Flow-Assisted Casting Strategy for Olfactory Navigation via Memory-Augmented Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18881)
*Changxu Zhao,Dongxiao Zhao,Xin Bian,Gaojin Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究利用强化学习智能体揭示了动态流场中气味搜索的最优记忆窗口机制：智能体自发习得流场辅助的casting策略，通过调节搜索轨迹几何形态和浓度阈值来最大化搜索成功率，并发现平均趋源速度与记忆长度呈非单调关系，可用"扇形搜索"模型解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管动态流场中气味检测具有随机性，多种动物仍展现出卓越的气味搜索能力，且存在一个最优时间窗口用于整合检测结果以最大化搜索效率。为理解这一现象背后的机制，作者旨在研究强化学习智能体在非定常流场中的导航表现。

Method: 采用强化学习方法，在无预定义模型的情况下，让智能体在不同记忆长度和流动条件的非定常流场中进行气味源导航任务。研究者观察并分析了智能体在不同参数设置下的行为策略和性能表现。

Result: 智能体自主发展出流场辅助的casting（投掷/摆动搜索）策略，并能够自适应地调整：1）搜索轨迹的几何形态；2）启动casting行为的浓度阈值。研究发现智能体向气味源移动的平均速度与记忆长度呈现非单调依赖关系，此现象可通过"扇形搜索"(sector-search)模型进行解释。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了动态流场中气味搜索的一个关键机制：存在最优记忆长度用于整合随机检测结果。强化学习智能体能够自发涌现出类似于生物的适应性搜索策略，证明了在没有预定义模型的情况下，智能体可以习得有效的环境感知与决策策略。

Abstract: In dynamic flow fields, various animals exhibit remarkable odor search capabilities despite relying on stochastic detections. Interestingly, there exists an optimal time window for integrating these detections that maximizes search efficiency. To understand the underlying mechanism, we investigate the navigation performance of Reinforcement Learning (RL) agents in unsteady flows under varying memory lengths and flow conditions. Without any predefined models, the agents develop a flow-assisted casting strategy and adaptively adjust both the geometry of their search trajectories and the concentration threshold for initiating casting to maximize the success rate. The agent's average speed toward the odor source exhibits a non-monotonic dependence on memory length, which can be explained by the "sector-search" model.

</details>


### [165] [To Call or Not to Call: Diagnosing Intrinsic Over-Calling Bias in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18882)
*Wei Shi,Ziheng Peng,Sihang Li,Xiting Wang,Xiang Wang,Mengnan Du,Na Zou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LLM智能体普遍存在“过度调用工具”的问题。研究发现这是由内在偏差引起的，并利用稀疏自编码器（SAE）量化了该偏差，提出AMCS方法进行因果干预校正，有效缓解了过度调用并提升了整体准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体在决策时表现出明显的过度调用倾向，即在无需调用工具的场景下仍错误地选择调用。现有模型在“调用”任务上准确率高，但在“不调用”任务上准确率低，导致整体性能受限。本文旨在揭示这一现象背后的内在机制。

Method: 本文提出内在偏差假说（IBH），认为决策映射中存在独立于激活的调用偏移。方法上：1) 使用稀疏自编码器（SAE）提取调用/不调用决策的行为对齐特征基；2) 将其简化为有符号激活边界并估计偏移量；3) 提出自适应边界校正导向（AMCS）方法，沿SAE解码器方向进行闭式反偏移干预。

Result: 在三个模型家族的六个模型上验证了IBH：模型仅在“不调用”激活强于“调用”激活时才保持决策中立。通过AMCS抵消诊断出的偏移后，有效缓解了过度调用现象，整体准确率得到提升，且调用准确率仅有微乎其微的下降。

Conclusion: 本研究将智能体“过度调用工具”这一经验现象转化为可进行因果校正的机制对象，证明了通过机制性解释和干预手段可以有效纠正模型行为偏差。

Abstract: LLM agents exhibit a consistent tendency to over-call, invoking tools even in situations where none is needed. On the When2Call benchmark, six models from three families show high call accuracy but much lower no-call accuracy, leaving overall accuracy in the 55%-70% range. We trace this to an Intrinsic Bias Hypothesis (IBH): the call/no-call decision mapping carries an activation-independent call offset, so the model favors call even at activation parity. Using Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs), we recover behavior-aligned feature bases for the call/no_call decision, reduce them to a signed activation margin, and estimate the offset directly. Across all six models, the model is decision-neutral only when no_call activation outweighs call activation, consistent with IBH. We then causally test IBH with Adaptive Margin-Calibrated Steering (AMCS), a closed-form counter-bias shift along SAE decoder directions. Cancelling the diagnosed offset mitigates over-calling and improves overall accuracy with a negligible drop in call accuracy. Our work recasts over-calling from an empirical phenomenon into a mechanistic object amenable to causal correction. Code is available at https://github.com/SKURA502/agent-sae/.

</details>


### [166] [Prediction Is Not Physics: Learning and Evaluating Conserved Quantities in Neural Simulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18883)
*Andrew Bukowski,Aditya Kothari,Simba Shi,Ishir Rao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了神经网络能否从物理轨迹中发现全局守恒量。结构化能量模型在清洁数据上表现优异（R²≥0.9999），黑盒守恒发现网络（CDN）在有对齐损失时也能达到R²≥0.996，但在噪声条件下CDN在某些系统中表现更鲁棒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准扩散模型虽然能达到接近$10^{-3}$的预测误差，但其能量标准差比真实值大7500-36000倍，无法保持守恒定律。这引发了核心问题：神经网络能否从物理轨迹中学习或选择全局守恒量？

Method: 在三个哈密顿系统（抛体运动、摆、弹簧-质量）上进行实验，比较四种方法：结构化$T(v)+V(q)$能量模型、黑盒守恒发现网络（CDN）、多项式CDN、条件扩散基线。通过调节对齐损失系数$λ_{align}$和时间一致性约束来训练模型。

Result: 结构化网络在清洁数据上R²≥0.9999；黑盒CDN在$λ_{align}=0.2$时R²≥0.996，但$λ_{align}=0$时R²崩溃至< $10^{-3}$，表明仅靠时间一致性不足以识别真实能量。在1%高斯噪声下，CDN在抛体和弹簧-质量系统上优于结构化模型。多项式CDN对训练配置敏感：短训练R²=0.78，充足训练可达R²=0.9998。

Conclusion: 神经网络可以从物理轨迹中发现守恒量，但需要额外的对齐损失来确保识别真实的能量函数。结构化模型在清洁数据上最准确，而黑盒CDN在噪声条件下可能更鲁棒。守恒量的发现需要时间一致性与其他约束条件的结合，单独的时间一致性不足以为准。

Abstract: A diffusion model trained on Hamiltonian trajectories can achieve rollout MSE near $10^{-3}$, but the standard deviation of its energy over time is between 7500 and 36000 times larger than the ground-truth energy standard deviation, indicating a failure to preserve conservation laws. This gap motivates our central question of whether neural networks can learn or select globally conserved quantities from physical trajectories. We investigate this across three Hamiltonian systems: projectile motion, pendulum, and spring-mass. We use a structured $T(v)+V(q)$ energy model, a black-box Conservation Discovery Network (CDN), a polynomial CDN, and a conditional diffusion baseline. The structured network reaches $R^2 \geq 0.9999$ against analytical energy on clean data, while the black-box CDN reaches $R^2 \geq 0.996$ when trained with temporal consistency plus a small alignment loss to analytical energy at $t=0$ ($λ_{\mathrm{align}}=0.2$). With $λ_{\mathrm{align}}=0$, CDN Pearson $R^2$ collapses on pendulum and spring-mass ($< 10^{-3}$), showing that temporal consistency alone is not enough to reliably identify the true energy. Under $1\%$ additive Gaussian noise, the CDN outperforms the structured model on the projectile and spring-mass systems, suggesting that the CDN may be more robust to noisy inputs in this setting. However, the polynomial CDN is sensitive to training configuration: it achieves $R^2=0.78$ under a short training schedule on the pendulum system, but reaches $R^2=0.9998$ with more training time and data, regardless of whether noise is added.

</details>


### [167] [Navigating the Emotion Tree: Hierarchical Hyperbolic RAG for Multimodal Emotion Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18884)
*Zeheng Wang,Bo Zhao,Yijie Zhu,Zhishu Liu,Hui Ma,Ruixin Zhang,Shouhong Ding,Qianyu Xie,Zitong Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出HyperEmo-RAG框架，通过双曲空间层次化检索和结构化证据注入，利用情感知识库增强多模态大语言模型的情感识别能力，显著提升细粒度情感分类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态大语言模型在情感识别中存在两大局限：一是将情感类别视为独立标签，忽略了心理学中丰富的层次化情感分类体系；二是缺乏外部语境知识，导致模型容易过度解读噪声线索，影响细粒度情感分类的准确性。

Method: 提出检索增强生成框架HyperEmo-RAG，包含两项核心创新：1）层次化双曲空间嵌入，将层次化情感标签和多模态样本联合嵌入庞加莱球双曲空间，设计层次化束搜索推理过程，实现从粗粒度到细粒度的渐进式样本检索；2）结构化证据注入，基于检索证据构建证据图谱，通过Tree-Aware Attention机制和EmotionGraphFormer将结构化知识作为显式认知上下文注入大语言模型，保持图结构信息完整性。

Result: 在多个数据集上的实验表明，HyperEmo-RAG显著优于现有方法，在细粒度情感分类任务上取得了性能提升。

Conclusion: 通过引入情感知识库的结构化知识，并利用双曲空间的层次化建模能力，可以有效增强多模态大语言模型的情感理解能力，为情感识别任务提供了新的解决思路。

Abstract: Multimodal emotion recognition aims to integrate text, audio, and video sources to understand human affective states. Although multimodal large language models excel at multimodal reasoning, they typically treat emotion categories as independent labels, ignoring the rich hierarchical taxonomy of human psychology. Moreover, lacking external contextual knowledge makes them highly susceptible to over-interpreting noisy cues, further complicating fine-grained emotion classification. To address these issues, we propose \textbf{HyperEmo-RAG}, a retrieval-augmented generation framework that leverages a structured emotional knowledge base. Our framework introduces two key innovations. 1) Hierarchical hyperbolic grounding. Recognizing the inherent hierarchical tree structure of emotion taxonomies, we jointly embed hierarchical emotion labels and multimodal samples into a continuous hyperbolic space (Poincaré ball) and design a hierarchical beam-search deliberation process that progressively retrieves samples from coarse to fine-grained levels. 2) Structured evidence injection. Based on the retrieved evidence, we construct an evidence graph and inject the structured knowledge as explicit cognitive context into the LLM through a Tree-Aware Attention mechanism and an EmotionGraphFormer, preserving the integrity of graph-structured information. Experiments on multiple datasets demonstrate that HyperEmo-RAG significantly outperforms existing methods.

</details>


### [168] [Soft Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18889)
*Mohammed Aledhari,Ali Aledhari,Fatimah Aledhari,Mohamed Rahouti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Soft Learning是一个集成学习框架，通过交叉验证的非负最小二乘法自动组合异构专家模型，在CPU上比深度网络快72-435倍，在37个数据集中70%的任务上排名第一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代机器学习迫使实践者在强大但昂贵的深度网络和快速但有限的经典算法之间做出选择。为了弥合这一差距，作者提出了一个能够自动发现最优模型组合的框架，避免手动选择单一算法的困境。

Method: 引入Soft Learning框架，维护包含线性模型、树集成、核方法和神经网络等异构专家的模型库，通过交叉验证的非负最小二乘法发现理论上有保证的最优组合权重。该框架保证性能不劣于其专家的最佳加权组合，且添加新专家不会降低性能。

Result: 在37个数据集（25个分类，12个回归）上与包括CatBoost和调优深度网络在内的9种方法对比，Soft Learning在70%的任务上排名第一，取得最佳平均排名（Friedman检验，p=1.12×10^-12），是唯一同时在分类和回归任务上表现卓越的方法。训练速度比深度网络快72-435倍，无需GPU或超参数调优。

Conclusion: 研究范式从"哪种算法最好"转变为"最优组合是什么"。Soft Learning为任意数据模态提供了形式化保证，学习到的权重提供了固有的可解释性，揭示了哪种算法范式最适合数据。

Abstract: Modern machine learning forces practitioners to choose between powerful but expensive deep networks and fast but limited classical algorithms. Here we introduce Soft Learning, a framework that maintains a library of heterogeneous specialists -- spanning linear models, tree ensembles, kernel machines, and neural networks -- and discovers provably optimal combination weights through cross-validated non-negative least squares. Soft Learning is guaranteed to match or exceed the best weighted combination of its specialists, trains over two orders of magnitude faster than deep networks on CPU alone (72-435x faster across tested configurations), provides inherent interpretability through learned weights that reveal which algorithmic paradigm best fits the data, and is future-proof: adding specialists is mathematically guaranteed to maintain or improve performance. Across 37 datasets (25 classification, 12 regression) against nine methods including CatBoost and tuned deep networks, Soft Learning ranks first on 70% of tasks, achieves the best mean rank (Friedman test, p = 1.12 x 10^-12), and is the only method to simultaneously excel at both classification and regression -- all without GPU hardware or hyperparameter tuning. These results suggest a paradigm shift from "which algorithm is best?" to "what is the provably optimal combination?" -- a question Soft Learning answers with formal guarantees for any data modality.

</details>


### [169] [Auditing Reasoning-Trace Memorization Claims after Unlearning with Head-Conditioned Canaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18891)
*Yanhang Li,Zhichao Fan,Zexin Zhuang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现，推理模型中观察到的“bypass gap”（回答侧遗忘但思维链保留信息）并不能可靠证明模型权重层面仍保留记忆。通过对DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-7B的实验，这种差距可能仅源于解码时的模式不匹配，而非隐藏的记忆残留。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前对推理模型进行机器遗忘（unlearning）评估时，常观察到一种bypass模式：模型在最终回答中似乎已遗忘目标内容，但其内部思维链（thinking trace）仍持续输出该内容。研究者通常将这种差距视为模型权重仍保留记忆的证据。本文旨在审查这一解读的可靠性。

Method: 作者使用DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-7B模型，通过LoRA注入虚构作者信息，并采用NPO（Negative Preference Optimization）方法进行遗忘训练。实验以一个六词元的金丝雀标记头（canary head）为条件，在不同随机种子下，将思维链替换为简短的非金丝雀预填充文本，观察回答率的变化。

Result: 在一个随机种子下，将思维链替换为非金丝雀预填充文本后，回答率下降幅度与bypass gap本身相当，无论预填充是否模仿训练模板。而在另一个种子下，bypass gap缩小而非消失，预填充替换反而使回答率升至天花板水平。在不同蒸馏模型上，该指标甚至因解析器无法找到闭合标签而翻转符号。

Conclusion: 正向的解析器分离bypass gap既不能单独证明存在隐藏的权重级记忆，也不能排除其存在。作者建议在标准审计之外，采用解码时模板替换作为一种低成本的合理性检查方法。

Abstract: Evaluations of unlearning on reasoning models sometimes show a bypass pattern. The answer side looks unlearned, but the model's own thinking trace keeps emitting the forgotten content, and the gap is taken as evidence that the weights still remember. We audit this reading on DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-7B with LoRA-memorized fictional authors and NPO unlearning, conditioned on a six-token canary head. On one seed, swapping the thinking trace for a short non-canary prefill on the same weights drops the answer rate by as much as the bypass gap itself, whether the prefill mimics the training template or not. On a second seed the bypass gap shrinks rather than vanishing, and the prefill swap reverses direction and brings the answer rate to ceiling. A positive parser-split bypass gap thus does not by itself identify hidden weight-level memorization, and does not rule it out either. On a different distillate the same metric flips sign because the parser cannot find the closing tag. We recommend a decode-time template swap as a cheap sanity check alongside the canonical audit.

</details>


### [170] [Position: Graph Condensation Needs a Reset -- Move Beyond Full-dataset Training and Model-Dependence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18893)
*Mridul Gupta,Samyak Jain,Vansh Ramani,Hariprasad Kodamana,Sayan Ranu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文是一篇立场性论文，指出现有图蒸馏方法存在依赖全量数据训练、计算开销大、泛化能力差等系统性缺陷，且评估协议存在误导性，呼吁领域重新审视研究范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图神经网络在处理大规模图数据时面临可扩展性挑战，图蒸馏被提出作为解决方案，但现有主流方法（如梯度匹配）存在根本性矛盾——需要基于全量数据进行压缩，违背了效率初衷；同时面临计算开销大、跨架构泛化差、过度依赖特定模型配置等问题，且社区采用的评估协议（如节点压缩比）无法反映真实资源节省和压缩开销，这些问题已阻碍该领域的实质性进展。

Method: 本文作为立场性论文，未提出具体技术方法，而是系统性地识别并批判了现有图蒸馏研究的核心方法论缺陷，包括：对全量数据训练的依赖、模型依赖性设计、误导性评估协议等，并指出了关键的研究方向。

Result: 揭示了图蒸馏领域的系统性问题：1）基于梯度匹配的方法在压缩过程中仍需全量数据训练，违背效率目标；2）跨GNN架构泛化能力差；3）评估协议无法真实反映资源节省和实际部署价值；4）社区对神经架构搜索等应用场景的预期是虚幻的。

Conclusion: 呼吁图蒸馏领域需要重新定位，主张摆脱全量数据训练和模型依赖设计，转向轻量级、架构无关、可实际部署的方法，以真正实现大规模GNN训练的高效、泛化和可用性。

Abstract: Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) are powerful tools for learning from graph-structured data, but their scalability is increasingly strained by the size of real-world graphs in domains like recommender systems, fraud detection, and molecular biology. Graph condensation -- the task of generating a smaller synthetic graph that retains the performance of models trained on the original -- has emerged as a promising solution. However, the dominant approach of gradient matching introduces a fundamental contradiction: it requires training on the full dataset to create the compressed version, thereby undermining the goal of efficiency. Worse still, these methods suffer from high computational overhead, poor generalization across GNN architectures, and brittle reliance on specific model configurations. Equally concerning is the community's reliance on misleading evaluation protocols such as node compression ratios, which fail to reflect true resource savings, condensation overhead, and illusory application to neural architecture search. These shortcomings are not incidental -- they are systemic, and they obstruct meaningful progress.
  In this position paper, we argue that graph condensation, in its current form, needs a reset. We call for moving beyond full-dataset training and model-dependent design, and instead advocate for methods that are lightweight, architecture-agnostic, and practically deployable. By identifying key methodological flaws and outlining concrete research directions, we aim to reorient the field toward approaches that deliver on the true promise of condensation: efficient, generalizable, and usable GNN training at scale.

</details>


### [171] [A Two-Parameter Weibull Framework for Diagnosing Transformer Weight Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18898)
*Tiexin Ding*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于威布尔分布的权重幅度诊断框架，发现Transformer不同功能模块具有独特的参数k特征：传输类（FFN、W_o）k值窄带聚集，选择类（W_q、W_k）偏离威布尔族且受存储方式影响，参数λ标记训练进度，为训练动态提供细粒度诊断工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有训练动态诊断方法依赖聚合统计量，无法捕捉各组件、各层级、各训练步的细粒度信息。缺乏一个原理性、架构无关的测量标尺来量化训练过程中权重分布的演化规律。

Method: 采用威布尔分布（两参数极值理论分布族）作为诊断框架，拟合Transformer中每个权重矩阵的元素级幅度分布。以初始化时的独立同分布高斯权重作为锚点（k≈1.20），对12个模型（覆盖7个架构家族）的每个权重矩阵在各层各检查点独立拟合，获得k和λ两个参数的演化轨迹。

Result: 发现三大规律：（1）传输类模块（FFN、W_o）k值在[1.186,1.204]窄带内，跨架构族、激活函数、归一化位置和模型规模一致；（2）选择类模块（W_q、W_k）偏离威布尔族，偏离程度受存储方式影响：独立存储的Q/K偏离深（k∈[0.76,0.99]），GQA模型偏离轻（k∈[1.10,1.16]），合并存储的W_qkv处过渡区；（3）λ随训练大幅增长且与sqrt(η/λ_wd)强相关（Pearson r=0.94）。

Conclusion: 威布尔分布的两个参数携带独立信息：k标记功能类别（传输类vs选择类），λ标记训练进度。该框架为Transformer训练动态提供了原理性的细粒度诊断工具，揭示了不同模块在训练过程中的系统性差异规律。

Abstract: We apply the Weibull distribution -- a two-parameter family from extreme-value theory -- as a diagnostic framework for element-wise weight magnitude distributions in transformers. At initialization, i.i.d. Gaussian weights give |w| ~ HalfNormal, yielding k ~ 1.20 via middle-80% probability-plot fit (the protocol used throughout this work). This anchor makes k a principled, architecture-independent measuring stick for training dynamics; fitting each weight matrix independently at every layer at every checkpoint enables per-component, per-layer, and per-step diagnostics that aggregate statistics cannot resolve.
  Applying this framework to 12 model entries spanning 7 architectural families (Pythia, OLMo-1/2, LLaMA-3, Mistral, Qwen2.5/3) reveals three findings. First, FFN modules and the attention output projection W_o -- the Transmission Class -- fall in a narrow k band: median terminal k in [1.186, 1.204] across 12 entries (cross-family CV = 0.51%), shared across SwiGLU/GeLU activations, Pre-LN/QK-Norm placements, and 70M-14B sizes. Second, the attention input projections W_q, W_k -- the Selection Class -- depart from the Weibull family, with severity shaped by storage: separately-stored Q/K (OLMo-1, OLMo-2) yields k in [0.76, 0.99] (deep); GQA models yield k in [1.10, 1.16] (mild); Pythia's merged W_qkv occupies a transitional zone tracking training budget T/tau monotonically. Third, lambda grows substantially during training and scales with sqrt(eta/lambda_wd) within the Pythia family (Pearson r = 0.94, three Transmission kinds), directionally consistent with Fan et al. (2025). The two parameters carry independent information: k labels the functional class, lambda labels training progress.
  We release npm-weibull-py v0.4 (Python library) and DATABASE_v9_1 at https://github.com/tiexinding/NPM-Weibull-public .

</details>


### [172] [Don't Let Bandit Feedback Pull Continual LLM-Recommender Updates Off Target](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18899)
*Taesan Kim,Hyeongjun Yun,Jaegul Choo,Chung Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了ABPO框架，通过在GRPO中插入暴露项作为锚点并使用逆倾向评分校正暴露偏差，同时利用模型置信度缓解模糊负反馈的过度惩罚，实现了生成式LLM推荐系统的持续更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式LLM推荐器在部署后需要持续更新，但部署日志仅提供由先前策略塑造的上下文赌博机反馈：仅在先前策略暴露的物品上观察到结果，导致暴露偏差，且反馈信号不对称——正响应相对可靠，而无响应则模糊不清（可能代表真实不感兴趣或未观察到的外部因素）。

Method: 提出锚定赌博机策略优化（ABPO）框架：(1) 将暴露的推荐项作为日志锚点插入到每个GRPO展开组中，使组相对归一化相对于先前策略实际暴露的动作进行校准；(2) 对正响应和无响应两种反馈类型应用自归一化逆倾向评分（IPS）以校正策略不匹配；(3) 对两种反馈类型进行非对称处理：使用模型输出token的置信度（自确定性）作为无验证器的可靠性信号，缓解模糊无响应的过度惩罚。

Result: 在来自Amazon Reviews和MovieLens的五个领域上进行实验，ABPO方法在推荐准确性方面实现了持续的后更新提升，同时比先前基线方法更有效地缓解了由先前策略引起的暴露偏差。

Conclusion: ABPO框架成功解决了生成式LLM推荐器持续更新中的暴露偏差和反馈模糊性问题，通过锚定机制、IPS校正和置信度调节的非对称处理，实现了更准确、更稳健的策略更新。

Abstract: Generative LLM-based recommenders (LLM-Rec) require continual post-deployment updates, yet deployment logs provide only policy-shaped contextual bandit feedback: outcomes are observed solely for items exposed by a prior serving policy, inducing exposure bias and yielding partial, asymmetric signals consisting of relatively reliable positive responses and ambiguous no-responses. We propose an Anchored Bandit Policy Optimization (ABPO) framework for continual LLM-Rec updates that combines group-relative policy optimization (GRPO) with explicit treatment of exposure bias and feedback ambiguity. Specifically, we insert the exposed recommendation as a logged anchor into each GRPO rollout group, so that group-relative normalization is calibrated against the action actually exposed by the prior policy rather than against newly sampled rollouts alone. Because both positive- and no-responses are observed only through prior-policy exposure, we apply self-normalized inverse propensity scoring to the fixed anchor for both feedback types to correct for policy mismatch. At the same time, we treat the two feedback types asymmetrically in reliability: positive responses provide relatively direct endorsement signals, whereas no-responses remain ambiguous because they may reflect either true disinterest or unobserved external factors. To avoid overly aggressive updates from ambiguous no-responses, we temper their penalties with self-certainty, using the model's output-token confidence as a verifier-free reliability signal. Across five domains from Amazon Reviews and MovieLens, our method yields consistent post-update gains in recommendation accuracy while mitigating prior-policy-induced exposure bias more effectively than prior baselines.

</details>


### [173] [Reasoning Portability: Guiding Continual Learning for MLLMs in the RLVR Era](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18903)
*Qiuhe Hong,Yuyang Liu,Shuo Yang,Tiantian Peng,Fei Zhu,Yonghong Tian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RDB-CL方法，通过推理级可移植性指导多模态大语言模型的持续学习，相比基线提升Last准确率12.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型的持续学习（VLM-CL）需要在适应新多模态任务时保持已有知识。当前将多模态大语言模型与可验证奖励强化学习（RLVR）相结合的新范式需要一种新的持续适应模式。研究发现推理级别的约束在分布外样本上仍然可靠，而答案级别的信号则不然，这为在推理层面施加约束提供了动机。

Method: 提出推理可移植性（RP）作为样本级度量，衡量先前策略在新任务上的可重用性。基于此提出RDB-CL方法：根据RP动态调节RLVR中每个样本的KL正则化强度——对高RP样本使用紧密锚点以保持可重用推理，对低RP样本使用宽松锚点以允许探索新的推理路径。

Result: RDB-CL在实验中持续优于基线方法，相比普通RLVR基线，Last准确率提升+12.0%。同时验证了推理级信号在分布外样本上保持可靠，而答案级信号则不可靠。

Conclusion: 推理级别的可移植性为多模态大语言模型的持续学习提供了有效指导，通过动态平衡保留可重用推理与探索新推理路径，实现了更好的持续学习性能。这为MLLMs与RLVR结合的持续学习范式提供了新的研究方向。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models in Continual Learning (VLM-CL) aim to continuously adapt to new multimodal tasks while retaining prior knowledge. The emerging paradigm that couples Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) with Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) calls for a new pattern to guide continual adaptation. Advances in reasoning capability now make it feasible to impose constraints at the reasoning level. We formalize portability, a sample-level measure of how reusable the previous policy's behavior is on a new task, and empirically show that reasoning-level signals remain reliable on out-of-distribution samples while answer-level signals do not. We instantiate this as Reasoning Portability (RP) and propose Reasoning-based Dynamic Balance Continual Learning (RDB-CL), which modulates the per-sample Kullback-Leibler regularization in RLVR according to RP: a tight anchor preserves reusable reasoning on high-RP samples, while a relaxed anchor on low-RP samples permits exploration of new reasoning pathways. Experiments show that RDB-CL consistently outperforms baselines, improving Last accuracy by +12.0% over the vanilla RLVR baseline.

</details>


### [174] [Dynamic Model Merging Made Slim](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18904)
*Guodong Du,Wanyu Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了DiDi-Merging，一种轻量级动态模型合并框架，通过可微秩分配平衡共享参数与专家参数，在仅1.24倍单模型参数量下达到现有基线性能，在1.4倍时超越基线，显著优于需>2倍存储的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动态模型合并方法在精度-效率权衡上存在不足：要么维护全量共享模型加微小专家，要么为专家分配过多容量，导致存储开销大或性能次优。

Method: 提出DiDi-Merging框架，将参数预算建模为低秩模块中的可微秩优化问题，动态平衡共享参数与专家参数；引入无数据精炼步骤以恢复任务保真度，实现跨视觉、语言和多模态任务的通用性。

Result: 在仅1.24倍单模型参数量下匹配现有动态基线性能，在1.4倍参数量下超越基线，相比需要超过2倍存储的方法显著降低了存储开销。

Conclusion: DiDi-Merging通过可微秩分配实现了紧凑高效的动态模型合并，在精度-效率权衡上取得显著提升，适用于多模态任务场景。

Abstract: Model merging enables the reuse of fine-tuned models without joint training or access to original data. Dynamic merging further improves flexibility by selectively activating task-relevant parameters and efficiently composing experts across multiple tasks. However, existing dynamic methods either maintain a full shared model with tiny experts or allocate excessive capacity to experts, leading to suboptimal accuracy--efficiency trade-offs. To address this, we propose DiDi-Merging, a slim dynamic merging framework that leverages differentiable rank allocation to balance shared and expert parameters. By formulating parameter budgeting as differentiable rank optimization in low-rank modules and introducing a data-free refinement step to recover task fidelity, DiDi-Merging matches prior dynamic baselines at only 1.24x the parameters of a single fine-tuned model and surpasses them at 1.4x, substantially more compact than methods requiring > 2x storage. DiDi-Merging applies across vision, language, and multimodal tasks.

</details>


### [175] [Stability and Discretization Error of State Space Model Neural Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18905)
*Abderrahim Bendahi,Adrien Fradin,Johan Peralez,Julie Digne,Madiha Nadri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文建立了神经算子离散化误差与稳定性的理论保证，证明了求解正则性与输入离散化的解析界限，并以状态空间模型神经算子(SS-NOs)和傅里叶神经算子(FNOs)为例推导了离散化误差定理，通过ISS分析评估了离散化对稳定性的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经算子已成为求解偏微分方程的强大框架，虽然DeepONet和FNOs等方法已实现算子的通用近似并展现出代数收敛率，但连续理论与其离散数值实现之间的精确理论联系仍是一个挑战，特别是连续形式与离散数值稳定性之间的关系尚未被充分探索。

Method: 建立神经算子近似方案的离散化误差和稳定性的理论保证；证明求解正则性与输入离散化之间的解析界限，提供实际数值约束下神经算子精度的形式化量化；将界限推导至状态空间模型神经算子(SS-NOs)和傅里叶神经算子(FNOs)；通过输入到状态稳定性(ISS)分析，形式化评估离散化对连续域SS-NOs结果稳定性的影响。

Result: 推导出了SS-NOs和FNOs的离散化误差定理；建立了求解正则性与输入离散化的解析界限；通过输入到状态稳定性(ISS)分析，形式化评估了离散化对稳定性的影响；在1D和2D基准测试上的实验验证了理论界限的正确性，并展示了SS-NOs在不同分辨率下的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 本文成功填补了神经算子连续理论与离散数值实现之间的理论空白，为离散化误差和稳定性提供了严格的理论保证，所提出的解析界限能够有效量化实际数值约束下的神经算子精度，实验结果表明SS-NOs在变化分辨率下具有良好鲁棒性，为神经算子的实际应用提供了可靠的理论基础。

Abstract: Neural operators have emerged as a powerful, discretization-invariant framework for solving partial differential equations (PDEs). Although established approaches like the Deep Operator Network (DeepONet) have successfully achieved universal approximation for operators, and architectures such as Fourier Neural Operators (FNOs) have shown algebraic convergence rates, a precise theoretical connection between the continuous theory and its discrete numerical implementation remains a challenge. Specifically, the relationship between the continuous formulation and the discrete numerical stability has yet to be fully explored. In this paper, we address this gap by establishing theoretical guarantees for the discretization error and stability of neural operator approximation schemes. We prove analytical bounds that link solution regularity to input discretization, providing a formal quantification of neural operator accuracy under real-world numerical constraints. We derive these bounds to the specific cases of State Space Model-based Neural Operators (SS-NOs) and FNOs, thus providing a new discretization error theorem for these models. Additionally, through an input-to-state stability (ISS) analysis, we formally assess the impact of discretization on the stability of SS-NOs results obtained in the continuous domain. Our empirical experiments on 1D and 2D benchmarks validate our theoretical bounds and show the robustness of SS-NOs under varying resolutions.

</details>


### [176] [Descriptive versus Regulatory Uncertainty in Bounded Predictive Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18909)
*Ahmed Gamal Eldin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文形式化证明当前Transformer架构在推理时仅存在“描述性不确定性”，无法通过不确定性驱动自适应行为调整；熵与任务表现正交，且该缺陷无法通过增加参数或数据解决。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 任何有限表征能力的系统都必须压缩，压缩意味着先验即偏差。关键问题在于：不确定性是否参与调控系统未来行为（即具有因果作用），还是仅仅作为输出分布的无后果描述？作者旨在厘清不确定性在系统动力学中的功能角色。

Method: 提出“描述性不确定性”与“调节性不确定性”的结构性区分，并形式化证明Transformer推理时受限于前者。结合兰道尔原理从热力学视角论证：若不确定性为调节性，认知错误需付出真实能量代价，而在解耦系统中幻觉与正确推导耗散相同能量。在本地部署的三个语言模型（3B、8B、70B参数）上，测量跨任务（模式检索、因果算子应用、分布外因果泛化）的词元级香农熵与任务准确率，并进行统计比较。

Result: 所有三个模型中，词元级香农熵在跨任务条件下统计不变（所有成对p≥0.568；模型内范围0.011-0.028 nats），而相同条件下的任务准确率变化显著（0%-100%）。熵与准确率正交。该解耦具有尺度不变性：更大模型准确率更高但熵平坦度相同。此结构性缺陷无法通过额外参数或训练数据解决。

Conclusion: 当前Transformer架构的结构性限制使其无法实现调节性不确定性。真正的认知根基需要热力学基底状态与信息处理代价之间的物理耦合。仅增加模型规模或数据不能赋予系统将不确定性转化为行为调控信号的能力。

Abstract: Any system that models the world under finite representational capacity must compress; any compression entails a prior; and the prior is the system's bias. What has not been established is whether uncertainty participates in the dynamics governing future behavior, or merely describes the output distribution without consequence. We introduce a structural distinction between descriptive uncertainty, which does not recursively modulate the system's policy, and regulatory uncertainty, which directly enters the optimization landscape and drives persistent adaptive restructuring. We prove formally that current transformer architectures are confined to descriptive uncertainty at inference. We ground this in thermodynamics via Landauer's principle: for uncertainty to be regulatory, epistemic error must cost real energy; in a decoupled system, hallucinations and correct derivations dissipate identical energy. We test this empirically across three locally-deployed language models (3B, 8B, 70B parameters). Token-level Shannon entropy is statistically invariant across tasks spanning pattern retrieval, causal operator application, and out-of-distribution causal generalization in all three models (all pairwise p >= 0.568; within-model ranges 0.011-0.028 nats), while task accuracy varies substantially across the same conditions (0%-100%). Entropy and accuracy are orthogonal. The decoupling is scale-invariant: larger models achieve higher accuracy but identical entropy flatness. This structural incapacity is not resolvable by additional parameters or training data. Genuine epistemic grounding requires physical coupling between thermodynamic substrate state and information processing cost.

</details>


### [177] [Does Your Wildfire Prediction Model Actually Work, or Just Score Well?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18911)
*Yangshuang Xu,Yuyang Dai,Liling Chang,Qi Wang,Yushun Dong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个专门用于野火预测的基础模型WILDFIRE-FM，并引入了一个固定契约评估框架，解决了因野火事件稀疏导致的评估敏感性问题，发现迁移学习结论高度依赖于评估设计和任务制定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的地球基础模型主要针对通用大气和地球物理目标进行预训练，缺乏针对野火预测的专门优化；同时，野火事件在时空上极为稀疏，导致迁移学习结论对匹配规则和评估设置高度敏感，评估结果不可靠。

Method: 1. 提出WILDFIRE-FM，利用天气、活跃火点观测、地形、植被和静态环境数据进行预训练；2. 引入固定契约评估框架，包含两项控制检查：固定输出检查（消除匹配规则影响）和固定特征检查（消除头部选择影响）；3. 在匹配契约下与十个地球基础模型基线进行对比实验。

Result: 在占用率、蔓延、检索和回归任务上对WILDFIRE-FM进行了全面评估。实验结果表明，野火迁移学习的结论强烈依赖于评估设计和任务制定方式，揭示了当前评估方法存在的问题。

Conclusion: WILDFIRE-FM和提出的评估框架为未来野火专用地球基础模型的研究和基准测试奠定了基础，强调了在稀疏事件预测任务中采用严格评估协议的重要性。

Abstract: Wildfire prediction is important for early warning and resource allocation, yet existing Earth foundation models (Earth FMs) are pretrained for general atmospheric and geophysical objectives rather than wildfire forecasting. To address this gap, we introduce WILDFIRE-FM, the first foundation model pretrained specifically for wildfire prediction using weather, active-fire observations, topography, vegetation, and static environmental data. However, introducing a domain-specific backbone alone does not solve the evaluation problem: wildfire events are sparse in space and time, making transfer conclusions highly sensitive to matching rules and evaluation settings. To address this problem, we introduce a fixed-contract evaluation framework with two controlled checks: a fixed-output check for matching-rule effects and a fixed-feature check for head-selection effects. Under matched contracts, we compare WILDFIRE-FM with ten Earth-FM baselines across occupancy, spread, retrieval, and regression tasks. Our results show that wildfire transfer conclusions depend strongly on evaluation design and task formulation. We hope this framework and WILDFIRE-FM provide a foundation for future wildfire-specific Earth-FM research and benchmarking. Our code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/Wildfire-fm-evaluation-contracts-5AE9/.

</details>


### [178] [HypergraphFormer: Learning Hypergraphs from LLMs for Editable Floor Plan Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18932)
*Nikita Klimenko,Hesam Salehipour,Parham Eftekhar,Amir Khasahmadi,Ramon Elias Weber*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了HypergraphFormer，一种基于大语言模型（LLM）学习超图表示的楼层平面图生成方法，在多种指标上优于现有技术，并具有良好的泛化性、数据效率和可编辑性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的楼层平面图生成方法主要基于栅格化或向量化表示，在处理任意不规则边界、分布偏移以及设计工作流中的可编辑性方面存在局限性。作者旨在通过引入超图表示与大语言模型，解决这些限制，实现更灵活、高效且可编辑的平面图生成。

Method: 提出HypergraphFormer模型，利用大语言模型通过监督微调（Supervised Fine-Tuning）学习超图表示。该表示将楼层平面图的空间关系和连接信息编码为文本形式，从而将公寓轮廓与其功能和几何 subdivisions 解耦。研究在RPLAN数据集上进行训练和评估，并发布了一个新的分布外数据集以验证泛化能力。

Result: 该方法在RPLAN数据集及新的分布外数据集上，在多种评价指标上均优于基于栅格化或向量化表示的最先进技术。模型展现出更高的数据效率，特别是在分布偏移的情况下。此外，该方法能够处理任意不规则的用户指定边界，并支持高度可编辑的设计工作流。

Conclusion: HypergraphFormer成功地将大语言模型与超图表示相结合，为楼层平面图生成提供了一种高效、灵活且可编辑的新范式，显著优于现有方法并具备良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: In this work, we propose HypergraphFormer, a novel and efficient approach to floor plan generation based on learning hypergraph representations with a large language model (LLM). The model is trained via supervised fine-tuning to generate a hypergraph-based textual representation that encodes spatial relationships and connectivity information within floor plans. We train and evaluate our approach on the RPLAN dataset, and further demonstrate its generalizability on a separate out-of-distribution dataset, which we release in this paper. Our method outperforms state-of-the-art techniques based on rasterized or vectorized representations across a diverse set of metrics. We also show improved data efficiency, particularly under distribution shift. The hypergraph formulation enables the generation of floor plans for arbitrary, irregular, user-specified boundaries by decoupling apartment footprints from their functional and geometric subdivisions. Furthermore, we show that the proposed methodology offers a high degree of editability, making it particularly well suited to design-oriented workflows supported by LLMs.

</details>


### [179] [A Geometric Analysis of Sign-Magnitude Asymmetry in a ReLU + RMSNorm Block under Ternary Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18933)
*Lei Dong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从几何角度解释了Pre-norm Transformer对三值量化{-1,0,+1}的低敏感度：ReLU产生的方向不对称性使得符号翻转比幅值扰动多产生约2.75倍的输出能量，而异常特征则是实际模型中符号敏感性的主要来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期研究表明Pre-norm Transformer配合RMSNorm在三值权重量化下损失 surprisingly 小，但缺乏理论解释。本文旨在从几何视角揭示其内在机制，解释为何符号量化误差对模型影响有限。

Method: 通过符号-幅值分解分析权重扰动，建立两层ReLU+RMSNorm理论模型，证明符号翻转比等Frobenius范数的符号保持幅值扰动产生约2.75倍的横向输出能量。利用Bussgang常数(2/π)、RMSNorm几何特性和ReLU半空间结构，推导符号量化误差的角对齐特性，并在TinyLlama-1.1B上验证线性响应阶段的异常特征影响。

Result: 理论发现：(1)符号翻转产生的横向输出能量是幅值扰动的π/(π-2)≈2.75倍(Theorem 3)；(2)符号量化误差的角对齐cos²→2/π(Theorem 4)；(3)ReLU对三值误差近似透明，其后径向分数(0.365)与预ReLU值1-2/π误差仅0.4%。实验验证：在TinyLlama-1.1B上，线性响应阶段计数匹配NLL杠杆稳定在~10×≈nE[α²]，异常特征α在layer 12的中位值为0.024、最大值0.26，证实重尾分布集中。

Conclusion: Pre-norm Transformer对三值量化的容忍性源于RMSNorm几何、ReLU半空间结构和Bussgang常数的协同作用，造成符号-幅值不对称性。实际模型偏离多层复合2.75×因子的原因在于异常特征违反离域假设，其影响由R∝nα²主导。该理论框架量化了符号翻转的影响，为理解Transformer量化鲁棒性提供了几何洞见。

Abstract: Pre-norm Transformers with RMSNorm tolerate ternary {-1,0,+1} weight quantization with surprisingly small loss (Ma et al., 2024). We give a geometric explanation via sign-magnitude decomposition of weight perturbations. In a two-layer ReLU + RMSNorm model with i.i.d. Gaussian weights, sign-flips produce $π/(π-2) \approx 2.75$ times more transverse output energy than sign-preserving magnitude perturbations of equal Frobenius norm, as the flip rate $p \to 0$ (Theorem 3). The mechanism: ReLU creates a hidden-space directional asymmetry between the two perturbation types, which RMSNorm's transverse-projection Fréchet derivative selectively exposes. Sign-quantization error is itself a sign-preserving perturbation with angular alignment $\cos^2 \to 2/π$ (Theorem 4); its post-ReLU radial fraction ($0.365$) matches the pre-ReLU value $1-2/π$ within $0.4\%$, so ReLU is approximately transparent to ternary error. Multi-layer compounding of the $2.75\times$ factor is not experimentally supported; the gap to real-model sign sensitivity arises from outlier features violating delocalization. For an input dimension with amplitude $α$, a single sign-flip produces post-ReLU energy amplified by $R \approx nα^2$ relative to a delocalized entry. On TinyLlama-1.1B, at linear response ($p \leq 0.5\%$), count-matched NLL leverage stabilizes at $\sim 10\times \approx n\mathbb{E}[α^2]$, matching the per-entry theory; the all-column NLL ratio of $5.0\times$ falls within $R_{\mathrm{col}} \leq 19$ ($67\times$ PPL gap reflects metric nonlinearity). Measured outlier $α$ at layer 12 (median $0.024$, max $0.26$) confirms heavy-tailed concentration. The Bussgang constant $2/π$, RMSNorm geometry, and ReLU half-space structure together explain sign-magnitude asymmetry in pre-norm models, with $R \propto nα^2$ accounting for real-model deviations.

</details>


### [180] [FedMental: Evaluating Federated Learning for Mental Health Detection from Social Media Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18936)
*Nuredin Ali Abdelkadir,Anjali Ratnam,Zeerak Talat,Stevie Chancellor*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文评估了联邦学习（FL）和差分隐私联邦学习（DP-FL）在心理健康预测任务中的表现。实验表明，普通FL在抑郁症检测上能达到与集中式训练相当的效果，但DP-FL会因隐私保护引入的噪声破坏关键语义特征，导致严重的性能下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交媒体文本数据是训练机器学习模型识别高风险心理健康行为的重要资源，但直接共享此类敏感数据存在严重的隐私风险，并限制了基准数据集的增长。因此，研究旨在探索隐私保护技术是否能在保护隐私的前提下实现数据共享并维持模型性能。

Method: 研究选取了两个经典任务——Twitter上的抑郁症检测和Reddit上的自杀危机检测。方法上采用联邦学习（FL）和差分隐私联邦学习（DP-FL），通过将每个用户视为一个客户端来模拟非独立同分布（non-IID）的真实数据共享场景，并评估了不同客户端参与比例、聚合策略及隐私预算（epsilon）对模型的影响。

Result: 实验结果显示，普通联邦学习在抑郁症识别任务上表现优异（集中式F1=85.63，最佳FL模型F1=83.16）。然而，差分隐私联邦学习存在巨大的性能-隐私权衡：即使隐私预算较低（epsilon=50），F1分数也下降了多达27.01。分析发现，这是因为隐私噪声扭曲了与心理健康高度相关且稀疏的语言标记（如健康话题和情绪词汇）。

Conclusion: 本研究通过实证分析揭示了当前隐私保护技术在心理健康推断任务中的潜力与局限。虽然联邦学习可以有效替代集中式训练以保护数据隐私，但引入差分隐私会显著破坏心理健康相关的关键特征，导致模型性能大幅下降，需要在隐私保护与模型效用之间寻找更好的平衡。

Abstract: Social media text data are often used to train Machine Learning (ML) models to identify users exhibiting high-risk mental health behaviors. However, sharing this sensitive data poses privacy risks and limits the growth of benchmark datasets. We comprehensively evaluate whether privacy-preserving ML techniques can enable safer data sharing while preserving performance. Specifically, we apply federated learning (FL) and Differentially Private FL for two widely-studied mental health prediction tasks: depression detection on X (Twitter) and suicide crisis detection on Reddit. We simulate realistic data-sharing scenarios by treating each user as a client in a non-IID setting, evaluating across different client fractions, aggregation strategies, and privacy budgets. While FL achieves comparable performance to centralized training (centralized F1 = 85.63; best FL model F1 = 83.16) on depression identification, we find that Differentially Private FL has a large performance-privacy trade-off (up to F1 = 27.01 drop) even with low levels of noise (epsilon = 50). This is due to the distortion of highly informative yet sparse mental health linguistic markers related to mental health, like health topics and emotion words. This research empirically demonstrates the potential and limitations of current privacy preservation techniques for mental health inference tasks.

</details>


### [181] [Shaping the Prior: How Synthetic Task Distributions Determine Tabular Foundation Model Quality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18971)
*Mohamed Bouadi,Nassim Bouarour,Varun Kulkarni,Shivam Dubey,Aditya Tanna,Vinay Kumar Sankarapu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出O'Prior，一种面向表格基础模型的组合式现实先验，通过改进合成预训练分布的设计，在保持架构和计算预算不变的情况下，显著提升了下游任务的准确性与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表格基础模型的合成先验分布设计过于理想化，缺乏现实数据中的不规则性和失效模式，导致模型部署时的鲁棒性不足，而这一关键因素此前未被充分理解。

Method: 提出O'Prior框架，包含四个耦合组件：层次化结构因果模型(SCM)元生成器、覆盖异质边缘分布与缺失值的现实性引擎、注入混淆与分布偏移的显式压力模块，以及课程控制与防泄漏的生成协议；在固定架构、优化器和计算预算下，仅改变合成任务分布以进行对照实验。

Result: 在真实表格基准测试中，O'Prior在下游准确性和鲁棒性上取得持续且显著的提升，尤其在分布不规则的场景下效果明显；消融实验证实机制多样性、现实性组合和偏移感知压力各自独立贡献，不可相互替代。

Conclusion: 合成先验的构建是决定表格基础模型质量的一阶关键因素，此前被严重忽视；精心设计的合成分布能从根本上提升模型性能。

Abstract: What determines the quality of a tabular foundation model? Unlike language or vision, tabular foundation models acquire their inductive biases almost entirely from synthetic pretraining distributions, yet the design of these distributions remains poorly understood. Standard synthetic priors are too well-behaved: they omit the irregularities and failure modes that determine deployment robustness. We introduce O'Prior, a compositional realism prior built around four coupled components: a hierarchical SCM meta-generator spanning diverse functional families; a modular realism engine covering heterogeneous marginals, missingness, and target transforms; an explicit stress module injecting confounding and support-query mismatch; and a curriculum-governed, leakage-safe generation protocol. To isolate prior design as the scientific variable, we hold architecture, optimizer, and compute budget fixed and vary only the synthetic task distribution. O'Prior yields consistent and substantial improvements in downstream accuracy and robustness across real tabular benchmarks, with gains concentrated in regimes characterized by distributional irregularities. Ablations confirm that mechanism diversity, realism composition, and shift-aware stress each contribute independently, their effects are not interchangeable. These results establish synthetic prior construction as a first-order and largely overlooked determinant of tabular foundation model quality

</details>


### [182] [TabQL: In-Context Q-Learning with Tabular Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18979)
*Qisai Liu,Zhanhong Jiang,Timilehin Ayanlade,Ashutosh Kumar Nirala,Yang Li,Aditya Balu,Soumik Sarkar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了TabQL，一种用具备上下文学习能力的表格化基础模型替代DQN中参数化Q网络的强化学习框架，通过序列到序列建模实现快速适应，在理论和实验上均显示出比DQN更好的样本效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的深度Q网络（DQN）依赖参数化的Q网络，通常需要大量的在线交互数据进行梯度更新，样本效率较低。为了利用基础模型强大的上下文学习能力并提升强化学习的适应效率，作者动机在于构建一种能够利用少量在线交互快速适应的表格化Q学习方法。

Method: 提出Tabular Q-Learning（TabQL）框架：核心是用一个基于序列到序列的表格化基础模型表示Q值，输入为状态-动作-Q值元组的表格化表示。该方法利用上下文学习能力实现Q值的零样本或少样本推断，并设计了预热的启动阶段：先运行标准DQN生成高质量上下文，再用DQN预测的Q值指导TabQL生成新转移数据以增强上下文质量。

Result: 在温和假设下对TabQL的收敛性和样本复杂度进行了形式化分析，证明TabQL在理论上是介于原始Q-learning和具有上下文学习能力的DQN之间的插值方法；分析表明，TabQL通过上下文学习分摊了Bellman更新，相比DQN实现了更高的样本效率。大量基准数值实验进一步验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: TabQL成功地将表格化基础模型的上下文学习能力引入强化学习，替代了传统的参数化Q网络，在保持理论收敛性的同时显著提升了样本效率，为结合基础模型与强化学习提供了一种有效的新范式。

Abstract: We propose Tabular Q-Learning (TabQL), a reinforcement learning framework that replaces the conventional parametric Q-network in Deep Q-Learning (DQN) with a tabular foundation model endowed with in-context learning capabilities. The key idea is to represent Q-values through a sequence-to-sequence foundation model operating over a tabularized representation of state-action-Q-value tuples, enabling rapid adaptation from limited online interaction by conditioning on recent experience. TabQL departs from classical DQN by leveraging (i) zero- or few-shot Q-value inference via in-context updates, and (ii) a warm-up phase using standard DQN to bootstrap high-quality context. Particularly, to enhance the context quality, new transitions are generated by executing actions output by TabQL with predicted Q values from DQN. We formalize TabQL, analyze its convergence and sample complexity under mild assumptions, and show that TabQL interpolates between vanilla Q-learning and DQN with in-context learning. Our analysis demonstrates that TabQL achieves improved efficiency compared to DQN by amortizing Bellman updates through in-context learning. Extensive numerical experiments with several benchmarks showcase the effectiveness and efficacy of the proposed TabQL.

</details>


### [183] [Distilling Linearized Behavior for Effective Task Arithmetic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18993)
*Thomas Sommariva,Francesca Morandi,Simone Calderara,Angelo Porrello*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种知识蒸馏方法，将曲率正则化的线性化模型作为教师模型，指导标准非线性微调的学生模型训练，使非线性模型继承了线性化模型在任务向量算术上的优势，同时避免了推理时的额外计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 任务向量组合（如模型合并与遗忘）依赖于任务向量的解耦特性。线性微调能产生天然解耦的任务向量，但存在表达能力受限和推理成本高的问题；而标准非线性微调虽无上述缺点，但其任务向量易受干扰。因此，研究动机在于弥合两者差距，寻求一种兼具线性化优势与非线性效率的方法。

Method: 本文提出在激活空间施加约束以实现参数空间的线性化属性。具体而言，首先训练一个经过曲率正则化的线性化模型作为教师模型，随后将其隐藏层表示蒸馏到通过常规方式微调的非线性学生模型中，从而使学生模型在保持标准架构的同时获得线性化的有益特性。

Result: 该方法生成的模型成功继承了线性化模型在任务算术方面的关键特性，能够有效进行任务向量的组合操作。在视觉和语言的多个基准测试中均取得了强劲的性能表现，且在推理阶段不引入任何额外计算开销。

Conclusion: 通过对激活空间施加约束进行知识蒸馏，可以在不牺牲模型推理效率的前提下，将线性微调的优良特性迁移至标准非线性模型，为模型编辑与任务向量组合提供了一种实用且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Task vector composition has emerged as a promising paradigm for editing pre-trained models, enabling model merging through addition and unlearning through subtraction. Fine-tuning in the tangent space of a pre-trained model (linear fine-tuning) has proven effective, as it produces task vectors that are naturally disentangled and resistant to interference. However, linearized models suffer from limited expressivity during training and incur higher computational costs at inference time, which restrict their practical applicability. In this work, we bridge the gap between linear and standard non-linear fine-tuning. We show that linearity with respect to weight perturbations, a property defined in parameter space, can be enforced through constraints in activation space during training. Concretely, we distill hidden representations from a curvature-regularized linearized teacher into a non-linear student trained via conventional fine-tuning. We find that the resulting model inherits key properties of linearized models for task arithmetic, enabling effective composition of task vectors and achieving strong performance across vision and language benchmarks without incurring any inference-time overhead.

</details>


### [184] [Distance-Aware Muon: Adaptive Step Scaling for Normalized Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18999)
*Yury Demidovich,Abhishek Chakraborty,Grigory Malinovsky,Angelia Nedić,Peter Richtárik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对Muon优化器对归一化步长尺度敏感的问题，提出了三种自适应缩放算法（Distance-Adaptive Muon、Scale-Calibrated Muon和Distance-Free Muon），在减少人工调参敏感性的同时，理论证明了收敛性保证，实验表明其在语言建模和图像分类任务上匹配或优于调参后的Muon基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon及其相关归一化优化器虽然解耦了更新方向与步长尺度的选择，但其实际性能仍对归一化步长的尺度高度敏感，需要大量人工调参。现有方法缺乏对一般范数几何下自适应缩放规则的理论理解与实用算法。

Method: 针对不同目标函数性质提出三种互补算法：(1)对于光滑非凸目标，提出Distance-Adaptive Muon，基于轨迹探索半径动态设置信赖域半径；(2)对于星凸目标，提出Scale-Calibrated Muon，保留指数移动平均但基于当前梯度与动量计算局部下降证书来设置步长；(3)提出Distance-Free Muon，一种重新中心的信赖域方法，通过标量距离证书和一维优化的信赖域半径选择，无需已知初始化到全局极小值的距离。

Result: 理论方面：Distance-Adaptive Muon在轨迹有界假设下证明了平稳性保证；Scale-Calibrated Muon在有界初始子水平集假设下证明了O(1/T)的目标函数间隙界。实验方面：在GPT-124M/WikiText-103语言建模和ViT-Tiny/CIFAR-100图像分类任务上，所提自适应缩放规则减少了对人工尺度调参的敏感性，并在测试预算下匹配或优于调参后的固定尺度Muon基线。

Conclusion: 本文系统研究了Muon在一般范数几何下的自适应缩放规则，提出了三种具有理论保证的算法，成功解决了归一化优化器对步长尺度敏感的实践难题，为深度学习优化提供了更鲁棒的自适应方法选择。

Abstract: Muon and related normalized optimizers decouple the choice of update direction from the choice of step scale, but their practical performance remains sensitive to the scale of the normalized step. We study adaptive scaling rules for Muon in general norm geometries and develop three complementary algorithms. For smooth non-convex objectives, we introduce Distance-Adaptive Muon, whose trust-region radius is set from the radius explored by the trajectory, and prove a stationarity guarantee under a bounded-trajectory assumption. We then turn to star-convex objectives, a tractable model of the favorable global geometry often used to reason about the empirical loss landscapes of deep neural networks, where objective-gap guarantees are possible. In this setting, we first introduce Scale-Calibrated Muon, which keeps Muon's exponential moving average but sets the step length from a local descent certificate computed from the current gradient and momentum. For this method, we prove a last-iterate O(1/T) objective-gap bound under a bounded initial sublevel-set assumption, where the corresponding radius parameter appears only in the analysis and not in the algorithm. Finally, we develop Distance-Free Muon, a recentered trust-region method that uses a scalar distance certificate and a majorized one-dimensional search to select the trust-region radius without requiring the unknown distance from the initialization to a global minimizer. Experiments on Transformer language modeling (GPT-124M/WikiText-103) and image classification (ViT-Tiny/CIFAR-100) show that the proposed adaptive scaling rules reduce sensitivity to manual scale tuning and match or improve tuned fixed-scale Muon baselines under the tested budgets.

</details>


### [185] [SAGA: A Sequence-Adaptive Generative Architecture for Multi-Horizon Probabilistic Forecasting with Adaptive Temporal Conformal Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19014)
*Gustav Olaf Yunus Laitinen-Fredriksson Lundström-Imanov,Hafize Gonca Cömert*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SAGA模型（基于Transformer的不规则表格面板序列解码器），结合split conformal校准方法预测个人终身收入分布，在1-30年视野下预测精度显著优于传统参数模型，并提供了有限样本覆盖率保证的预测区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 财政部和央行使用的微观模拟模型依赖于参数化终身收入过程，仅捕捉条件分布的一阶和二阶矩，忽略了长程非线性结构，导致预测精度不足。

Method: 提出SAGA——一种针对不规则表格面板序列的decoder-only transformer模型，配合split conformal校准包装器提供具有有限样本边缘覆盖率保证的个体级预测区间。模型在瑞典LISA纵向登记数据（1990-2022年，214万个体，6128万人年）上训练，通过蒙特卡洛方法将年度劳动收入预测聚合为贴现终身收入分布。

Result: 在10年视野下连续排序概率得分降低31.9%，20年视野下平均绝对误差降低37.7%；conformal区间边缘覆盖率偏差在0.4个百分点内，最差人口亚组偏差在2.4个百分点内；重建的终身收入基尼系数为0.327（部分观测真值为0.341，GKOS估计为0.378）。

Conclusion: SAGA模型成功克服了传统参数模型的局限性，能够更准确地预测长期收入动态和终身收入分布，为政策分析提供了更可靠的微观模拟工具，并公开了模型权重和合成数据集以促进复现研究。

Abstract: Microsimulation models used by ministries of finance and central banks rely on parametric processes for lifetime earnings that capture only first and second moments of the conditional distribution and miss long-range nonlinear structure. We propose SAGA, a decoder-only transformer for irregular tabular panel sequences, paired with a split conformal calibration wrapper that delivers individual-level prediction intervals with finite-sample marginal coverage guarantees. Trained on the longitudinal Swedish LISA register over 1990 to 2022, comprising 2,143,817 individuals and 61,284,903 person-years, the model forecasts annual labor earnings at horizons of one to thirty years and aggregates them by Monte Carlo into present-discounted lifetime earnings distributions. Against the canonical Guvenen, Karahan, Ozkan, and Song parametric process and tabular and recurrent baselines, SAGA reduces continuous ranked probability score by 31.9 percent at the ten-year horizon and mean absolute error by 37.7 percent at the twenty-year horizon. Conformal intervals achieve nominal coverage to within 0.4 percentage points marginally and within 2.4 percentage points on the worst-case demographic subgroup. The reconstructed lifetime earnings Gini coefficient is 0.327 against the partially observed truth of 0.341 and the GKOS estimate of 0.378. Model weights, calibration tables, and a synthetic equivalent dataset are released for replication outside the protected SCB MONA environment.

</details>


### [186] [LoRA vs. Full Fine-Tuning: A Theoretical Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19018)
*Ali Zindari,Rotem Mulayoff,Sebastian U. Stich*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在线性回归设定下理论分析了LoRA，证明了当预训练与下游任务差异为低秩时，LoRA在特定条件下可优于全量微调，并解释了小秩反而能提升泛化的现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LoRA作为一种高效的参数高效微调方法被广泛使用，但其理论特性尚不明确。现有研究缺乏对LoRA与全量微调在泛化性能上的理论比较，特别是在不同参数设定下LoRA何时更优尚不清楚。本文旨在通过理论分析揭示LoRA的行为机制，为实际应用提供指导。

Method: 1. 在线性回归设定下建立理论框架，分析LoRA与全量微调的超额风险（excess risk）。
2. 在过定（overdetermined）和欠定（underdetermined）两种设定下进行比较分析。
3. 理论推导LoRA能够优于全量微调的条件。
4. 分析LoRA秩的选择对泛化性能的影响。
5. 在实际任务上进行实验验证理论结论。

Result: 1. 识别出LoRA在过定和欠定设定下均能取得更低超额风险的机制。
2. 理论证明：当预训练任务与下游任务之间的差异为有效低秩时，LoRA可超越全量微调。
3. 解释了使用极小秩在某些情况下反而提升测试精度的现象——尽管小秩限制了表达能力，但带来了更好的泛化。
4. 实验验证了理论发现可推广到线性回归之外的实际任务。

Conclusion: 本文首次从理论角度系统分析了LoRA的泛化特性，揭示了低秩约束在特定条件下可成为正则化优势而非劣势。研究为LoRA的实际应用提供了理论指导：当任务差异具有低秩结构时，选择合适的秩可同时获得计算效率和更好的泛化性能。该理论框架为理解更复杂模型中的参数高效微调奠定了基础。

Abstract: Fine-tuning adapts a pre-trained model to downstream tasks using a small amount of labeled data. Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) is an efficient fine-tuning method that reduces memory and computation costs while often achieving performance close to full fine-tuning. Despite its widespread use, the theoretical behavior of LoRA is not yet well understood. In this paper, we study LoRA in a simple linear regression setting and compare its excess risk with that of full fine-tuning. Our analysis identifies regimes in which LoRA achieves lower excess risk than full fine-tuning in both overdetermined and underdetermined settings. Specifically, our theory predicts that LoRA can outperform full fine-tuning when the difference between the pretraining and the downstream tasks is effectively low-rank. We further show how the choice of LoRA rank affects generalization performance, explaining why using a very small rank can improve test accuracy in certain settings, even though it limits model expressivity. Finally, we support our theoretical results with experiments on practical tasks, suggesting that the identified tradeoffs and insights extend beyond linear regression.

</details>


### [187] [Deep Neural Sheaf Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19021)
*Remi Bourgerie,Sarunas Girdzijauskas,Viktoria Fodor*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出深度神经层扩散（DNSD）方法，通过用层邻接算子替代层拉普拉斯算子，解决了神经层扩散（NSD）在深度增加时信号消失的问题，在合成长距离数据集上准确率提升高达30个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度图神经网络面临表示崩溃和因重复聚合导致的敏感性降低问题。虽然神经层扩散（NSD）在理论上能避免崩溃，但在实践中，随着深度增加，层拉普拉斯算子的分歧信号会消失，限制了深层层的贡献。

Method: 提出深度神经层扩散（DNSD），用层邻接算子替代层拉普拉斯算子以保持跨层信息信号，并辅以归一化、奇非线性激活和门控机制。该方法将标量注意力分数替换为矩阵值边函数，并对节点表示而非注意力分数进行归一化。

Result: 在合成长距离数据集上，DNSD准确率相比GNN和NSD基线提升高达30个百分点；在真实世界基准测试中持续优于基线方法，有效利用了深度聚合。

Conclusion: 基于层的架构通过支持有效的深度结构，有望成为图基础模型的重要构建模块。

Abstract: Deep Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) are essential for capturing complex dependencies in graph-structured data. However, scaling GNNs to depth remains challenging, as stacking layers leads to representation collapse and diminishing sensitivity due to repeated aggregation. While Neural Sheaf Diffusion (NSD) provides strong theoretical guarantees against such collapse, these guarantees do not translate to practice: as depth increases, the disagreement signal of the sheaf Laplacian vanishes, limiting the contribution of deeper layers. We identify mechanisms that hinder NSD effectiveness at depth and propose \emph{Deep Neural Sheaf Diffusion} (DNSD), which replaces the sheaf Laplacian with a sheaf adjacency operator to maintain informative signals across layers. This is complemented by normalization, odd nonlinearities, and gating. To provide a principled explanation of the expected performance improvement, we contrast sheaf diffusion to graph attention mechanisms, highlighting that DNSD replaces scalar attention scores with matrix-valued edge functions and normalizes node representations rather than attention scores. We demonstrate empirically that DNSD effectively utilizes deep aggregation in graph tasks, outperforming GNN and NSD baselines with up to 30pp accuracy on synthetic long-range datasets, and consistently outperforming them on real-world benchmarks. These results position sheaf-based architectures as a promising building block for graph foundation models by supporting effective deep architectures.

</details>


### [188] [Learning When to Adapt](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19028)
*Ali Zindari,Xiaowen Jiang,Rotem Mulayoff,Sebastian U. Stich*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DISeL通过在LoRA的秩一组件上引入轻量级输入依赖门控机制，实现了动态适应与遗忘缓解的平衡，在保持精调性能的同时显著降低了灾难性遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统LoRA的更新参数对所有输入静态不变，这种输入无关的特性导致模型在适应精调分布与保留预训练知识之间存在不可避免的权衡，从而引发灾难性遗忘问题。

Method: 提出DISeL方法，在LoRA模块的单个秩一组件上添加轻量级的输入依赖门控机制。该机制默认保持预训练模型行为，训练时学习激活特定组件以降低精调损失，同时仅增加少量参数并保持低秩结构。

Result: 在RoBERTa (GLUE)、Llama和Mistral模型（数学推理和代码生成任务）上的实验表明，DISeL相比LoRA及其相关变体显著降低了遗忘，同时保持了竞争性的精调准确率。此外，门控激活提供了可解释的诊断视图，揭示了任务特定适应集中活跃的层和秩组件。

Conclusion: DISeL成功解决了LoRA静态更新导致的灾难性遗忘问题，通过输入敏感的动态门控机制实现了性能与可解释性的双重提升，为参数高效精调提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Low-rank adaptation (LoRA) is a widely used parameter-efficient fine-tuning method, yet its learned correction is static: the same low-rank update is applied to every input. This input-agnostic approach creates an inevitable compromise between adapting to the fine-tuning distribution and preserving pre-trained behavior on inputs outside that distribution, contributing to catastrophic forgetting. We introduce DISeL (Dynamic Input-Sensitive LoRA), which augments LoRA modules with lightweight input-dependent gates over individual rank-one components. The gating mechanism is designed to preserve the pre-trained model's behavior by default, while training learns to activate selected components that reduce the fine-tuning loss. DISeL adds only a small number of parameters and preserves the low-rank structure. Across RoBERTa on GLUE, and Llama and Mistral models fine-tuned for mathematical reasoning and code generation, DISeL reduces forgetting relative to LoRA and related variants while maintaining competitive fine-tuning accuracy. In addition, the learned gate activations provide an interpretable diagnostic view of which layers and rank components are most activated during fine-tuning, giving insight into where task-specific adaptation is concentrated. Code available at https://github.com/alizindari/DISeL .

</details>


### [189] [KVBuffer: IO-aware Serving for Linear Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19049)
*Longwei Zou,Lin Zhong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了KVBuffer，一种面向线性注意力的IO感知服务机制，通过缓冲键值对并采用分块计算等策略，显著降低了解码延迟并提升了服务吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的线性注意力服务系统在每一步解码时都需要循环更新巨大的线性注意力状态，导致严重的内存访问瓶颈，抵消了线性注意力在长上下文推理中理论上恒定计算成本的优势。

Method: 提出KVBuffer机制，通过缓冲最近的键和值，实现了更灵活高效的计算策略：1) 在解码时采用分块计算，延迟状态更新并批量应用以减少内存访问；2) 在推测解码中并行验证草稿token，避免存储临时状态；3) 针对短上下文直接利用缓冲数据计算，无需创建或更新状态。该机制已在SGLang中为Qwen3-Next实现。

Result: 实验评估表明，KVBuffer将线性注意力解码延迟降低了最高45.17%，在验证四个草稿token的推测解码场景下，将最大服务请求数量提升了5倍。

Conclusion: KVBuffer有效解决了线性注意力服务中的内存访问瓶颈问题，通过IO感知的优化策略显著提升了服务效率，证明了通过优化键值管理可以大幅改善线性注意力模型的实际部署性能。

Abstract: Linear attention has recently gained significant attention for long-context inference due to its constant decoding cost with respect to context length. However, existing serving systems typically serve linear attention by recurrently computing and updating a large linear attention state in every decoding step. Since the state is much larger than the per-token key and value, recurrent decoding incurs substantial memory access and becomes inefficient for serving linear attention. In this paper, we propose KVBuffer, an IO-aware serving mechanism for linear attention. By buffering recent keys and values, KVBuffer enables serving systems to compute linear attention outputs in more flexible and memory-efficient ways. For decoding, KVBuffer enables chunkwise computation, which reduces average memory access and decoding latency by deferring state updates and applying them in batch. For speculative decoding, KVBuffer verifies draft tokens in parallel and avoids storing temporary states. For short contexts, KVBuffer computes attention outputs directly from buffered keys and values, without creating or updating the linear attention state. We implement KVBuffer in SGLang for Qwen3-Next. Our evaluations show that KVBuffer can reduce linear attention decoding latency by up to 45.17% and increase the maximum number of serving requests by 5x for speculative decoding when verifying four draft tokens.

</details>


### [190] [Generative Pseudo-Force Fields for Molecular Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19050)
*Stefaan Simon Pierre Hessmann,Khaled Kahouli,Stefan Gugler,Michael Plainer,Frank Noé,Klaus-Robert Müller,Niklas Wolf Andreas Gebauer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出生成式伪力场(GPFF)，结合机器学习力场与扩散模型优势，无需从头算数据即可高效生成稳定分子构象，在QM9上100%有效性(256 NFE)且在6次神经函数评估下仍保持50%以上有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分子构象生成方法存在权衡：机器学习力场(MLFFs)虽能基于物理力采样稳定构象，但需要昂贵的从头算训练数据；扩散模型(DMs)仅需平衡态数据学习，但依赖噪声调度和时间步条件。需要一种方法结合两者优势。

Method: 提出生成式伪力场(GPFF)，通过训练MLFF学习相对于参考平衡结构的二次伪势能面。无需对扰动几何进行从头算计算，通过对平衡态施加高斯噪声在线生成非平衡训练数据。GPFF构成时间步无关的方差爆炸扩散模型变体：分数来自预测伪力，但力幅度隐含编码噪声水平，因此无需时间步条件。支持任意结构先验和几何约束。

Result: GPFF可作为标准扩散采样（祖先采样、Heun）的直接替代，并支持更高效的自适应变体和MLFF启发的直接去噪方案。在QM9数据集上，GPFF在256次神经函数评估(NFE)时达到100%有效性，在仅6次NFE时仍保持超过50%有效性，在所有采样器上均优于扩散基线。结合自定义先验，在药物设计分子编辑器中实现实时分子生成。

Conclusion: GPFF成功桥接了物理力场和生成模型的范式，消除了对昂贵从头算数据的依赖，同时避免了扩散模型的时间步条件限制，实现了高效、准确的分子构象生成，在药物设计等实际应用中展现出快速实时生成的潜力。

Abstract: Generating stable molecular conformations typically forces a tradeoff between the physical realism of energy-based relaxation and the sampling efficiency of data-driven generative models. While machine learning force fields (MLFFs) can sample stable conformations by relaxing molecular geometries according to physical forces, they require costly ab-initio training data. Conversely, diffusion models (DMs) learn from equilibrium data alone but are dependent on noise schedules and time-step conditioning. In this work, we propose generative pseudo-force fields (GPFFs) to bridge these paradigms by training an MLFF on a quadratic pseudo-potential energy surface relative to reference equilibrium structures. Because no ab-initio calculations are required for the perturbed geometries, non-equilibrium training data can be generated on the fly by perturbing the equilibria with Gaussian noise. We show that GPFFs constitute a time-step-agnostic variant of variance exploding DMs: the score comes from the predicted pseudo-forces but because force magnitudes implicitly encode the noise level, no time-step conditioning is needed. Our GPFF can hence be used as a drop-in replacement in standard diffusion sampling (ancestral, Heun) but also facilitates more efficient, adaptive variants and an MLFF inspired direct denoising scheme. Our proposed sampling algorithms support arbitrary structural priors and geometric constraints. On QM9, GPFF has 100 % validity at 256 neural function evaluations (NFE) and over 50 % at just 6 NFE, outperforming diffusion baselines across all samplers. Combined with custom priors, we showcase the fast and accurate generation process of our method in a molecular editor for a drug design setting, where a molecule is generated in real time.

</details>


### [191] [Mapping Uncharted Symmetries: Machine Discovery in Combinatorics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19063)
*Eugenio Cainelli,Lorenzo Luccioli,Alessandro Iraci,Michele D'Adderio,Giovanni Paolini*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将机器学习应用于代数组合数学的开放难题，提出了SLURP框架和两种方法（MapSeek-Functional和MapSeek-Symbolic），发现了q,t-Narayana多项式的新组合解释，并给出了此前未解决情形下的对称性证明，同时提供了Lean 4的形式化验证代码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受代数组合数学中长期未解决的开放问题启发，探索现代机器学习是否能对可验证的数学发现做出实质性贡献，特别是在精确分布约束下构建简单数学函数的问题。

Method: 提出Simple Learning Under Rigid Proportions (SLURP)框架，并引入两种方法：1) MapSeek-Functional，通过交替伪标记和监督训练步骤建模目标函数；2) MapSeek-Symbolic，直接生成符号公式。

Result: 成功将方法应用于代数组合数学的研究问题，发现了源于表示论的q,t-Narayana多项式的新组合解释（基于非交叉分区，据知为首例），并利用发现的统计量在先前未解决的情况下证明了这些多项式的对称性。

Conclusion: 证明了机器学习可以有意义地促进可验证的数学发现，并通过发布所有代码（包括在Lean 4中对所有数学发现的形式化）来确保验证和复现性。

Abstract: Inspired by long-standing open problems in algebraic combinatorics, we show that modern machine learning can meaningfully contribute to verifiable mathematical discoveries. In particular, we focus on the construction of simple mathematical functions under exact distributional constraints, a setting we formalize as Simple Learning Under Rigid Proportions (SLURP). We tackle this problem by introducing two methods: MapSeek-Functional, which models the desired function alternating pseudo-labeling and supervised training steps; and MapSeek-Symbolic, designed to directly produce symbolic formulas. We successfully apply both methods to a research problem in algebraic combinatorics, discovering a new combinatorial interpretation of the $q,t$-Narayana polynomials arising from representation theory. To our knowledge, this is the first such interpretation based on noncrossing partitions. Using one discovered statistic, we find a combinatorial proof of the symmetry of these polynomials in a previously unsolved case. To streamline verification and reproducibility, we release all code, including a formalization of all the mathematical discoveries of this paper in Lean 4.

</details>


### [192] [Riemannian Networks over Full-Rank Correlation Matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19073)
*Ziheng Chen,Xiaojun Wu,Bernhard Schölkopf,Nicu Sebe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次在满秩相关矩阵流形上引入黎曼神经网络，系统扩展了基本层并提出了针对两种相关几何的反向传播方法，验证了其优于SPD和Grassmann流形网络的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管对称正定（SPD）流形上的表示学习已受到广泛关注，但作为SPD矩阵规范化替代方案的满秩相关矩阵流形却鲜有探索。为了填补这一空白，本文致力于在相关流形上开发黎曼神经网络。

Method: 利用五种最新发展的相关几何，系统地将多层逻辑回归（MLR）、全连接（FC）和卷积层等基本层扩展到这些几何上；同时针对两种相关几何提出了精确的反向传播方法，构建了完整的黎曼网络框架。

Result: 实验表明，所提出的相关流形黎曼网络在性能上优于现有的SPD流形网络和Grassmann流形网络，验证了该方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 本研究成功将黎曼神经网络拓展至相关流形，为相关矩阵的深度学习提供了新的理论基础和实现方案，证明了相关流形在实际应用中的潜力和价值。

Abstract: Representations on the Symmetric Positive Definite (SPD) manifold have garnered significant attention across different applications. In contrast, the manifold of full-rank correlation matrices, a normalized alternative to SPD matrices, remains largely underexplored. This paper introduces Riemannian networks over the correlation manifold, leveraging five recently developed correlation geometries. We systematically extend basic layers, including Multinomial Logistic Regression (MLR), Fully Connected (FC), and convolutional layers, to these geometries. Besides, we present methods for accurate backpropagation for two correlation geometries. Experiments comparing our approach against existing SPD and Grassmannian networks demonstrate its effectiveness.

</details>


### [193] [The impact of observation density on Bayesian inversion of latent dynamics in shock-dominated flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19076)
*Bipin Tiwari,Muhammad Abid,Omer San*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于卷积自编码器降阶模型（AE-ROM）的高效贝叶斯初始状态反演框架，成功从稀疏噪声观测中推断激波主导可压缩流的未知初始状态，并实现了不确定性量化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在激波主导的可压缩流中，由于非线性波相互作用和有限的传感器测量，从未知初始状态推断稀疏噪声测量是一个具有挑战性的不适定逆问题。传统的反演方法计算成本高且难以进行不确定性量化，需要开发高效的计算框架来解决这一问题。

Method: 提出了一种非侵入式降阶建模框架，结合了卷积自编码器（CAE）与学习的潜在空间前向算子。自编码器将高维流场压缩为紧凑的非线性潜在表示，前向算子从编码的初始条件预测最终时刻的潜在状态。该AE-ROM代理模型实现了快速前向评估，并嵌入No-U-Turn采样器（NUTS）中进行后验探索。使用拉丁超立方采样生成500个高保真Sod激波管仿真，并采用五阶WENO格式求解。

Result: AE-ROM能够准确重建关键的激波管结构，包括稀疏波、接触间断和激波锋面。潜在维度为32时在重建精度和降维紧凑性之间取得了有效平衡，250个训练仿真即可实现准确重建。增加观测密度显著缩小了后验不确定性，密度的平均后验标准差降低约78%，压力降低约76%。

Conclusion: 该框架为激波主导流动的逆分析提供了一种计算高效且具有不确定性感知的方法，具有扩展到多维可压缩流和数字孪生应用的潜力。

Abstract: Inferring unknown initial states in shock-dominated compressible flows from sparse and noisy measurements is a challenging ill-posed inverse problem due to nonlinear wave interactions and limited sensing. In this work, we develop a non-intrusive reduced-order modeling framework for efficient Bayesian initial-state inversion with uncertainty quantification. The framework combines a convolutional autoencoder with a learned latent-space forward operator. The autoencoder compresses high-dimensional flow fields into a compact nonlinear latent representation, while the forward operator predicts final-time latent states from encoded initial conditions. This AE-ROM surrogate enables rapid forward evaluations and is embedded within a No-U-Turn Sampler (NUTS) for posterior exploration. The framework is demonstrated using 500 high-fidelity Sod shock tube simulations generated through Latin hypercube sampling and solved using a fifth-order WENO scheme. The inverse problem seeks to recover unknown left and right density and pressure states from sparse noisy observations of final-time density and pressure fields. Results show that the AE-ROM accurately reconstructs key shock-tube structures, including the rarefaction wave, contact discontinuity, and shock front. A latent dimension of 32 provides an effective balance between reconstruction accuracy and reduced-space compactness, while 250 training simulations are sufficient for accurate reconstruction. Increasing observation density significantly contracts posterior uncertainty, reducing the mean posterior standard deviation by approximately 78% for density and 76% for pressure. Overall, the proposed framework provides a computationally efficient and uncertainty-aware approach for inverse analysis of shock-dominated flows, with potential extensions to multidimensional compressible-flow and digital-twin applications.

</details>


### [194] [MANGO: Meta-Adaptive Network Gradient Optimization for Online Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19080)
*Ankita Awasthi,Marco Apolinario,Kaushik Roy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了MANGO框架，通过梯度门控和元学习正则化来解决在线持续学习中的灾难性遗忘问题，在三个基准数据集上达到了最先进的性能，并在CLEAR-10上实现了正向的后向迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线持续学习（OCL）需要在单次遍历非平稳数据流的情况下学习，且仅能访问有限的记忆重放缓冲区，面临灾难性遗忘（稳定性）与高效学习新任务（可塑性）之间的平衡挑战。现有方法（如重排练、输出蒸馏、固定正则化或元学习）存在局限性：重排练引入存储样本偏差；蒸馏仅在输出分布上操作而不调节参数更新；固定正则化不顾参数敏感性进行惩罚；仅流式元学习缺乏反馈控制的参数更新。

Method: 提出Meta-Adaptive Network Gradient Optimization (MANGO)框架，结合梯度门控（gradient-gating）和元学习正则化来平衡稳定性与可塑性。梯度门控根据参数敏感性缩放参数更新，防止破坏性更新；元学习正则化自适应调整稳定性系数，评估参数更新对重放的影响。在MANGO中，重放既作为训练信号，也作为遗忘评估器。

Result: 在三个标准OCL基准数据集上进行了评估。MANGO优于强基线方法，在不同重放缓冲区大小下均取得一致的性能表现。在CLEAR-10的域增量学习和CIFAR-100及Tiny-ImageNet的类增量学习任务中，MANGO在所有基线中达到了最高准确率，并在CLEAR-10上实现了正向的后向迁移，克服了遗忘问题。

Conclusion: MANGO框架通过梯度门控和元学习正则化有效解决了OCL中的稳定性-可塑性权衡问题，实现了最先进的性能表现，证明了其在不同重放大小和不同增量学习场景下的有效性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: In Online Continual Learning (OCL), a neural network sequentially learns from a non-stationary data stream in a single-pass with access only to a limited memory replay buffer. This contrasts sharply with off-line continual learning where training is multiple epoch dependent on large datasets. The main challenge faced by OCL is to overcome catastrophic forgetting of past tasks (stability) while learning new ones efficiently (plasticity). Existing methods counter forgetting via replay-based rehearsal, output level distillation, fixed regularization, or meta-learning on the current data. However, these methods have limitations: rehearsal introduces a stored sample bias; distillation operates on output-distributions without modulating parameter updates; fixed-regularization penalizes parameters irrespective of sensitivity; stream-only meta-learning lacks a feedback controlled parameter update. We propose Meta-Adaptive Network Gradient Optimization (MANGO), an OCL framework that balances stability-plasticity via gradient-gating and meta-learned regularization. Gradient-gating scales parameter updates based on sensitivity, preventing destructive updates. Meta-learned regularization adapts stability coefficients, evaluating the effect of parameter update on replay. In MANGO, replay acts as both a training signal and a forgetting evaluator. We evaluated our method on three standard OCL benchmark datasets. MANGO outperforms strong baselines, achieving state-of-the-art results with consistent performance across replay sizes. In domain incremental learning on CLEAR-10 and class incremental learning on CIFAR-100 and Tiny-ImageNet, it achieves highest accuracy among all baselines and achieves positive Backward Transfer, overcoming forgetting on CLEAR-10.

</details>


### [195] [Chessformer: A Unified Architecture for Chess Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19091)
*Daniel Monroe,George Eilender,Philip Chalmers,Zhenwei Tang,Ashton Anderson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Chessformer，一种统一的编码器-only Transformer架构，通过将棋盘格子视为token并引入几何注意力偏置(GAB)，在棋类AI的三个核心目标上同时取得突破：人类走子预测准确率达57.1%，为Leela Chess Zero提升超过100 Elo并在大赛中击败Stockfish，同时实现了细粒度的可解释性分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 国际象棋AI领域长期存在建模方法分化的问题：最大化博弈强度、预测人类走子和实现可解释性通常采用截然不同的架构，且这些设计往往与领域的几何结构不匹配。这引发了核心问题：这些目标是否需要独立的建模范式，还是存在单一架构能同时支持所有目标？

Method: 提出Chessformer统一架构：(1) 编码器-only Transformer设计，将棋盘格子表示为token；(2) 引入新颖的动态位置编码机制——几何注意力偏置(GAB)，自适应领域特定几何结构；(3) 采用基于注意力的源-目标策略头预测动作。该设计将模型的token化、位置编码和输出结构与棋盘领域的底层结构相对齐。

Result: 三个核心任务的突破性成果：(1) 开发Maia-3模型家族，人类走子预测准确率达57.1%，超越先前最优结果且参数量不足四分之一；(2) 集成至Leela Chess Zero引擎，博弈强度提升超过100 Elo，在重大计算机国际象棋竞赛中战胜Stockfish；(3) 方格token设计使注意力模式和激活可直接归因于棋盘格子，支持细粒度可解释性分析，这是先前架构无法自然实现的。

Conclusion: 研究表明，将模型的token化、位置编码和输出设计与领域的底层结构相对齐，可以在性能、人类兼容性和可解释性三个维度同时获得提升，证明了统一架构在棋类AI建模中的可行性和优越性。

Abstract: Chess has long served as a canonical testbed for artificial intelligence, but modeling approaches for its central tasks have diverged. Maximizing playing strength, predicting human play, and enabling interpretability are typically solved with disparate architectures, and these designs are often misaligned with the geometry of the domain. This raises the natural question of whether these objectives require separate modeling paradigms, or if there exists a single architecture that supports them simultaneously. We introduce Chessformer, a unified architecture that advances the state of the art on all three central goals in chess modeling. Chessformer is an encoder-only transformer that represents board squares as tokens, augments self-attention with a novel dynamic positional encoding called Geometric Attention Bias (GAB) that adapts to domain-specific geometry, and predicts actions with an attention-based source-destination policy head. We evaluate Chessformer on each front. First, we develop \maiathree, a family of models for human move prediction that reaches 57.1\% move-matching accuracy, significantly surpassing the previous state of the art with fewer than a quarter of the parameters. Second, we integrate Chessformer into Leela Chess Zero, a leading open-source engine, adding over 100 Elo of playing strength and resulting in tournament victories over Stockfish in major computer chess competitions. Third, we show that Chessformer's square-token design makes attention patterns and activations directly attributable to board squares, enabling granular interpretability analyses that prior architectures do not naturally support. More broadly, our results demonstrate that aligning a model's tokenization, positional encoding, and output design with the underlying structure of a domain can yield simultaneous gains in performance, human compatibility, and interpretability.

</details>


### [196] [Counterfactual Likelihood Tests for Indirect Influence in Private Reasoning Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19092)
*Alexander Boesgaard Lorup*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于反事实似然的测试方法，用于测量推理系统中私有通道间的影响，发现私有通道评估应分别报告直接和间接影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理系统越来越多地将中间计算分离到私有和公共通道中，这导致评估场景变得复杂：独立共推导、直接访问私有内容以及通过公共通信的间接影响在记录中看起来相似。现有的文本探测方法（如n-gram重叠）不可靠，存在位置混淆等问题，因此需要一种更准确的方法来测量私有推理通道之间的影响边界。

Method: 提出反事实似然测试方法：用长度匹配的供体块替换上游私有块，保持公共token序列和下游目标固定，测量下游目标的负对数似然偏移。在7B角色通道推理模型上进行验证，使用长度匹配控制RoPE位置混淆，并通过图分离控制实验验证公共通道路径作为测量信号的完整载体。

Result: 文本探测方法不可靠：原始n-gram重叠夸大泄漏，校正重叠仍有噪声，金丝雀重现无区分能力。反事实似然能有效分离未掩码和掩码条件。在强化的掩码验证中，反向B到A影响接近零，而A到B影响通过公共语音隐藏状态持续存在。跨三个检查点、五个种子和13,734个有效方向对比的多检查点验证重现了这种不对称性。图分离控制实验在所有13,734个控制评估中产生位相同的自然和反事实分数。

Conclusion: 私有通道评估应分别报告直接和间接影响，反事实似然探针为测量这些边界提供了实用的默认方法。研究结果证明了所识别的公共通道路径在实施的角色可见性掩码下是测量反事实信号的完整载体。
