Abstract: Reasoning systems increasingly separate intermediate computation into private and public channels, creating evaluation cases that look similar in transcripts: independent co-derivation, direct access to private content, and indirect influence through public communication. This paper presents a counterfactual likelihood test for measuring influence between private reasoning channels. The method replaces an upstream private block with a length-matched donor block, holds the public token sequence and downstream target fixed, and measures the downstream target's negative-log-likelihood shift. On a 7B role-channel reasoning model used for validation, textual probes are unreliable: raw n-gram overlap overstates leakage, corrected overlap remains noisy, and canary reproduction reports no discrimination. Counterfactual likelihood separates unmasked and masked conditions, while length matching controls a RoPE positional confound. In the hardened masked validation, reverse B-to-A influence is near zero, while A-to-B influence persists through public-speech hidden states. A multi-checkpoint validation across three checkpoints, five seeds, and 13,734 valid directional contrasts replicates this asymmetry. A graph-separation control that blocks private-to-public carrier edges produces bit-identical natural and counterfactual scores across all 13,734 control evaluations, identifying the tested public-channel pathway as the complete carrier of the measured counterfactual signal under the implemented role-visibility mask. The results show that private-channel evaluation should report direct and indirect influence separately, and that counterfactual likelihood probes provide a practical default for measuring these boundaries.

</details>


### [197] [ScheduleFree+: Scaling Learning-Rate-Free & Schedule-Free Learning to Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19095)
*Aaron Defazio*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了ScheduleFree+方法，通过多项改进成功将Schedule-Free Learning扩展至大语言模型训练规模，在长训练时长下性能显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然Schedule-Free Learning在标准基准测试中表现良好，但在大语言模型训练中仅在较小规模上得到验证。为了将其扩展到更大的批次和模型规模，需要解决一系列技术问题。

Method: 识别并实施了多项必要的改进措施，将Schedule-Free Learning扩展到更大批次和模型规模，提出了无需学习率调整和无需调度器的方法ScheduleFree+。

Result: ScheduleFree+显著优于Warmup-Stable-Decay调度策略；在长训练时长（每参数1000个token）下，性能比SOTA调度策略提高31%；为预训练中的模型平均和检查点合并提供了理论基础。

Conclusion: Schedule-Free Learning能够成功扩展至大语言模型训练规模，特别适合长训练时长场景，为模型平均和检查点合并等技术提供了理论支撑。

Abstract: Schedule-Free Learning has shown promise as a practical anytime training method for machine learning, showing success across dozens of standard benchmark problems. However, strong performance for LLM training has only been demonstrated at small scales. We identify a number of fixes necessary to scale up Schedule-Free Learning to larger batch sizes and model sizes, and present a learning-rate-free and schedule-free method (ScheduleFree+) for training large language models which greatly outperforms Warmup-Stable-Decay (WSD) schedules. We also demonstrate that Schedule-Free Learning is most effective for long duration training, and at 1000 tokens per parameter, it outperforms SOTA schedules by 31%. Schedule-Free Learning provides a theoretical foundation for the use of model averaging and checkpoint merging during pretraining.

</details>


### [198] [Performance Monitoring of Proton Exchange Membrane Water Electrolyzer by Transformers-Based Machine Learning Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19107)
*Bingqing Chen,Ivan Batalov,Qiu Chen,Weiqi Ji,Lei Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于编码器-解码器Transformer的机器学习框架，能够在PEM电解槽正常运行期间虚拟重建电化学表征（极化曲线），实现了无需中断操作的实时健康状态监测，MSE相比普通Transformer降低了10倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 绿色氢能在脱碳过程中至关重要，PEM电解槽是极具前景的生产技术。然而，传统的实验室健康监测方法需要周期性暂停正常运行来进行电化学测试，这在全规模部署中并不实用，限制了系统操作员对设备健康状态（SoH）的实时评估能力。因此，亟需一种能够在正常运行中实现连续性能监测的方法。

Method: 提出了一种机器学习框架，利用基于操作数据条件的编码器-解码器Transformer，在正常运行期间虚拟执行电化学表征，重点重建极化曲线。受基于分块的序列标记化启发，将输入数据分割成补丁并编码为有意义的标记，从而显著提高了学习效率。

Result: 在四个纵向运行（持续时间长达478小时，涉及不同测试电池和负载循环）中，该模型准确重建了极化曲线，与普通Transformer相比，均方误差（MSE）降低了10倍。编码器成功捕获了有意义的SoH潜在表示。

Conclusion: 这项概念验证研究表明，机器学习模型能够实现PEM电解槽的连续性能监测，无需中断正常运行。该方法为未来推导可解释的健康状态指标开辟了机会，有助于推动绿色氢能技术的规模化部署。

Abstract: Green hydrogen plays an essential role in decarbonization, with capacity projected to scale to 560 GW by 2030 (vs. 1.39 GW in 2023) in net-zero settings. Proton exchange membrane (PEM) electrolysis is one of the most promising technology routes to green hydrogen production, and real-time system health monitoring of PEM electrolyzers is essential for their scalable deployment. In lab settings, performance degradation can be characterized through electrochemical testing protocols by periodic pauses of normal operation. Such interruption is not practical for full-scale stack deployments, limiting system operators' ability to make real-time assessments of state-of-health (SoH). We present a machine learning (ML) framework that performs virtual electrochemical characterization during normal operation. The method uses an encoder-decoder transformer, conditioned on operational data, to reconstruct characterization outputs, focusing here on polarization curves. Inspired by patch-based sequence tokenization, we segment the inputs into patches and encode them to form meaningful tokens, which substantially improves learning efficiency. Across four longitudinal runs, lasting up to 478 hours on different test cells and loading cycles, the model accurately reconstructed polarization curves and achieved 10x reduction in mean squared error (MSE) compared to a vanilla transformer. This proof-of-concept demonstrates that ML models can enable continuous performance monitoring for PEM electrolyzers and that the encoder captures meaningful latent representations of SoH, opening up opportunities to derive interpretable indicators in future work.

</details>


### [199] [EgoBabyVLM: Benchmarking Cross-Modal Learning from Naturalistic Egocentric Video Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19130)
*Dongyan Lin,Phillip Rust,Angel Villar Corrales,Alvin W. M. Tan,Mahi Luthra,Charles-Éric Saint-James,Rashel Moritz,Sheila Krogh-Jespersen,Vanessa Stark,Surya Parimi,Jiayi Shen,Youssef Benchekroun,Yosuke Higuchi,Martin Gleize,Tom Fizycki,Nicolas Hamilakis,Manel Khentout,Sho Tsuji,Balázs Kégl,Juan Pino,Michael C. Frank,Emmanuel Dupoux*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出EgoBabyVLM挑战，发现现有视觉语言模型依赖高度语义对齐的训练数据，无法像人类婴儿那样从自然弱对齐的自我中心视角数据中学习语言接地。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类儿童能从有限的视觉-语言输入中稳健地习得语言接地能力，而当前最先进的大型多模态模型在处理可穿戴设备、具身智能体和婴儿头戴摄像头产生的稀疏、弱对齐自我中心数据流时泛化能力差，且缺乏统一评估流程来衡量该领域的进展。

Method: 在具有不同程度视觉-语言语义对齐的数据集上训练视觉语言模型，包括自然主义的婴儿和成人自我中心视频；使用综合评估套件进行评估，核心为Machine-DevBench——一个基于语料库的词汇和语法能力基准，从模型训练词汇中按对数频率区间自动生成，消除了先前发展基准的训练/评估不匹配和低统计功效问题。

Result: 当前视觉语言模型范式严重依赖精选数据的紧密语义对齐，无法利用主导自然主义自我中心输入的弱对齐信号——而这正是人类擅长学习的场景。

Conclusion: 当前VLM范式与人类学习机制存在根本差距，引入EgoBabyVLM挑战以推动开发能够从类婴儿自然数据中进行接地语言学习的模型。

Abstract: Children acquire language grounding with remarkable robustness from limited visuo-linguistic input in ways that surpass today's best large multimodal models. Recent research suggests current vision-language models (VLMs) trained on curated web data fail to generalize to the sparse, weakly-aligned egocentric streams produced by wearable devices, embodied agents, and infant head-cams -- and no fixed evaluation pipeline exists for measuring progress on this regime. We train VLMs on datasets with varying degrees of semantic alignment between visual and linguistic inputs, including naturalistic infant and adult egocentric videos, and evaluate them with a comprehensive suite spanning multimodal language grounding and unimodal vision and language tasks. At the core of this suite is Machine-DevBench, a corpus-grounded benchmark of lexical and grammatical competence, automatically generated from the model's training vocabulary across logarithmic frequency bins to eliminate the train/eval mismatch and low statistical power of prior developmental benchmarks. Our results show that current VLM paradigms hinge on the tight semantic alignment of curated data and fail to exploit the weakly-aligned signal that dominates naturalistic egocentric input -- the very regime in which humans thrive. To motivate progress, we introduce the EgoBabyVLM Challenge to drive the development of models capable of grounded language learning from the kind of naturalistic data that human infants experience.

</details>


### [200] [CLIC: Contextual Language-Informed Cardiac Pathology Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19132)
*Giovani D. Lucafo,Rafael da Costa Silva,João Lucas Luz Lima Sarcinelli,Andre Guarnier De Mitri,Diego Furtado Silva*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了CLIC框架，通过将患者人口统计学特征和技术元数据转换为自然语言描述，与原始ECG信号结合进行多模态学习，显著提升了心脏病理分类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床实践中，心内科医生诊断ECG时会结合患者特征和数据采集背景信息，但现有深度学习算法大多仅分析信号本身，忽略了这些重要的上下文信息，导致诊断精度受限。

Method: 提出了上下文语言增强的心脏病理分类框架（CLIC），这是一个多模态方法：将患者层面的上下文数据（人口统计学特征、技术元数据）转换为描述性文本作为信息锚点，帮助模型消除复杂生理模式的歧义。同时探索了使用大语言模型生成更丰富的临床描述，以及基于模板的受控临床文本生成方法。

Result: 实验证明，将患者级上下文数据转换为描述性文本能有效帮助模型消除复杂生理模式的歧义。对比研究发现，虽然LLM生成的文本具有一定竞争力，但基于受控模板的上下文临床文本在下游分类任务中能取得更稳定的性能提升。

Conclusion: CLIC框架成功实现了通过自然语言编码患者特征和技术元数据来增强ECG诊断精度，为多模态医学信号分析提供了新思路。基于模板的文本生成方法在临床应用中更具优势，为将上下文信息整合到自动化ECG分析中提供了可行方案。

Abstract: The electrocardiogram (ECG) is the gold standard for non-invasive diagnosis of cardiac pathologies and is a fundamental pillar of cardiovascular medicine. Recent progress in deep learning has led to the development of robust automated classifiers that achieve high performance by processing raw physiological signals. However, in clinical practice, diagnosis is rarely based solely on the signal. Cardiologists commonly support their interpretation with the patient's characteristics and the specific data-acquisition context. Despite this, most current algorithms remain restricted to signal-only analysis, failing to integrate technical metadata and demographic variables. This paper proposes Contextual Language-Informed Cardiac pathology classification (CLIC), a multimodal framework that significantly enhances diagnostic precision by encoding these variables through natural language. We demonstrate that translating patient-level contextual data into descriptive text provides an informative anchor that helps the model disambiguate complex physiological patterns. We further investigate the use of Large Language Models to synthesize richer clinical descriptions and observe that, while these generated texts remain competitive, controlled template-based contextual clinical text leads to consistent improvements in downstream classification performance.

</details>


### [201] [Identifiable Multimodal Causal Representation Learning under Partial Latent Sharing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19135)
*Manal Benhamza,Marianne Clausel,Myriam Tami*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对多模态数据场景，在非参数假设和欠完备设置下，证明了因果潜在表示的分量级可识别性，并提出了一种基于Wasserstein距离的可微分模块来恢复部分共享的潜在结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因果表示学习（CRL）旨在从高维观测数据中发现潜在的因果变量和结构，其中可识别性是保证模型可解释性和鲁棒性的关键。然而，证明可识别性极具挑战性，且现有研究尚未解决多模态数据这一更为复杂的场景。本文旨在填补这一空白，解决多模态观测数据下潜在因果结构的可识别性问题。

Method: 作者考虑了具有部分共享潜在结构的多模态观测数据，假设每个模态由特定子集的因果潜在变量通过非线性混合函数生成。在灵活假设且不限制潜在变量参数分布的条件下，理论证明了因果潜在表示的分量级可识别性（适用于欠完备场景）。为验证理论，提出了一个基于Wasserstein距离的模块来恢复部分共享的潜在结构，该模块可微分且易于集成到各类架构中。

Result: 理论分析证明了在多模态和非线性混合函数设置下，因果潜在表示的分量级可识别性得到保证。实验结果表明，该方法在合成数据集和真实数据集上均显著优于现有的最先进（SOTA）方法。

Conclusion: 本文成功将因果表示学习的可识别性理论扩展至多模态场景，为从多模态观测数据中恢复真实的因果机制提供了理论保障和有效的实践方法，提升了表示的可解释性与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Causal representation learning (CRL) seeks to uncover meaningful latent variables and their corresponding causal structure from high-dimensional observational data. Although its significance, CRL identifiability remains a crucial property, as it ensures the recovery of the mechanisms behind the data generation process, and hence the interpretability and robustness of the representation. Proving identifiability in CRL is intrinsically difficult, and we address in this work an even more challenging setting: multimodality. We consider multimodal observed data with a latent partially shared structure. Each modality is generated, through non linear mixing functions, from a specific subset of causal latent variables. Under flexible assumptions and without imposing any parametric distribution on the latent variables, we establish component-wise identifiability guarantees for the causal latent representation. Our identifiability results, furthermore, apply to the undercomplete scenario where we have, for each modality, more observed than latent variables. To instantiate our theoretical analysis, we introduce a Wasserstein-based module to recover the partially shared latent structure. Due to its differentiability, the latter can be easily integrated into all types of architecture, only requiring minimal changes. Extensive experiments on synthetic and realistic datasets validate the superiority of our approach over SOTA methods.

</details>


### [202] [GRASP: Deterministic argument ranking in interaction graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19141)
*Diganta Misra,Antonio Orvieto,Rediet Abebe,Volkan Cevher*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了GRASP框架，通过聚合稳定的局部交互判断来生成全局排名，解决了LLM作为评判者在整体评估中存在的不一致性问题，提供了一个透明、可审计的替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型越来越多地被部署为自动化评判者来评估论证强度，但常见的整体评判实践（holistic judging）存在严重的模型间不一致性，这种不稳定性源于将辩论的复杂交互结构简化为单一的不透明分数，缺乏一致性、透明度和区分论证结构与修辞吸引力的能力。

Method: 提出GRASP（Gradual Ranking with Attacks and Support Propagation）框架，这是一个确定性框架，通过收敛的攻击-防御传播操作符，将稳定的局部交互判断聚合为全局排名。该方法利用局部交互判断比整体排名更具可复现性的特点。

Result: 实验证明：1）局部交互判断在LLM-as-a-Judge评估中比整体排名更具可复现性；2）GRASP能够产生更一致的全局排名；3）GRASP分数与人类的"说服力"标签不相关，表明其测量的是结构充分性而非说服力、事实性或修辞吸引力。

Conclusion: GRASP提供了一个透明且可审计的整体LLM评判替代方案，它测量的是防御感知的论证稳健性概念——即基于显式交互图的论证结构充分性，而非说服力或修辞吸引力，这揭示了重要的社会技术区分。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly deployed as automated judges to evaluate the strength of arguments. As this role expands, their legitimacy depends on consistency, transparency, and the ability to separate argumentative structure from rhetorical appeal. However, we show that holistic judging - a common LLM-as-a-Judge practice where a model provides a global verdict on a debate - suffers from substantial inter-model disagreement. We argue that this instability arises from collapsing a debate's complex interaction structure into a single opaque score. To address this, we propose GRASP (Gradual Ranking with Attacks and Support Propagation), a deterministic framework that aggregates stable local interaction judgments into a global ranking via a convergent attack--defense propagation operator. We show that local interaction judgments are more reproducible than holistic rankings in LLM-as-a-Judge evaluations, allowing GRASP to produce more consistent global rankings. We further show that GRASP scores do not correlate with human "convincingness" labels, highlighting a vital sociotechnical distinction: GRASP does not measure persuasion, factuality, or rhetorical appeal, but structural sufficiency - a defense-aware notion of argument robustness over the explicit interaction graph. Overall, GRASP offers a transparent and auditable alternative to holistic LLM judging.

</details>


### [203] [PMF-CL: Pareto-Minimal-Forgetting Continual Learner for Conflicting Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19145)
*Srijith Nair,Atilla Eryilmaz,Jia,Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于多任务学习视角的持续学习框架（PMF-CL），通过寻找帕累托最优解来最小化灾难性遗忘，并针对二次问题实现了O(d²)的静态内存占用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有持续学习方法缺乏对“应存储何种信息以最小化灾难性遗忘”的理论理解；虽然理论上存在共同全局最小化器时可完全避免遗忘，但实际任务往往不存在该最小化器，必然存在一定程度的遗忘。

Method: 从多任务学习（MTL）视角出发，提出基于帕累托最优解的持续学习框架。该方法通过定义帕累托意义上的最小遗忘解，推导出适用于线性回归、基函数回归及具有二次上界的一般损失函数（如逻辑回归）的Pareto-minimal-forgetting CL算法。

Result: 对于二次问题，该方法使用内存高效的迭代更新，仅需O(d²)的静态内存占用（d为模型参数量），实现了理论上有保障的最小遗忘。

Conclusion: 该框架为冲突任务间的持续学习提供了理论基础和系统化方法，通过帕累托最优性原则在理论与实践之间建立了桥梁，为持续学习算法设计提供了新视角。

Abstract: In the literature, many continual learning (CL) algorithms have been proposed to address the issue of catastrophic forgetting in ML models (i.e., learning new tasks leads to the loss of performance on previously learned tasks). Although all CL approaches use some form of memory to retain information about past tasks, a grounded understanding of what information needs to be stored to minimize catastrophic forgetting remains elusive. Recently, it has been recognized that under the strong assumption of the existence of a common global minimizer over all tasks, catastrophic forgetting can be completely avoided. However, in practice, tasks rarely have a common global minimizer, and a certain amount of forgetting is inevitable. In this paper, we propose a foundational framework for principled and systematic CL of conflicting tasks using a multi-task learning (MTL) perspective. The approach is based on finding Pareto-optimal solutions, i.e., the solutions which, by definition, minimally forget the previous tasks in the Pareto sense. We derive Pareto-minimal-forgetting CL algorithms for linear and basis-function regression, and general loss functions which have a quadratic upper bound, e.g., logistic regression. For quadratic problems, PMF-CL uses memory-efficient iterative updates with a static memory footage of $\mathcal{O}(d^2)$ for models with $d$ parameters.

</details>


### [204] [Flash PD-SSM: Memory-Optimized Structured Sparse State-Space Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19150)
*Aleksandar Terzić,Francesco Carzaniga,Nicolas Menet,Yannick Biehl,Michael Hersche,Thomas Hofmann,Abbas Rahimi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Flash PD-SSM，通过在每时间步离散选择结构化稀疏矩阵，打破状态空间模型中效率与表达能力的权衡，实现了与非结构化矩阵相当的FSA表达能力，同时保持了高效率，并在多项任务中取得SOTA结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 状态空间模型（SSMs）面临一个核心权衡：非结构化转移矩阵表达能力极强（可建模有限状态自动机FSA），但计算和内存成本过高；而现有的结构化转移矩阵虽然高效，但表达能力受限。作者旨在解决这一效率与表达能力之间的矛盾。

Method: 提出Flash PD-SSM模型。该方法维护一组可训练的结构化稀疏矩阵，在每个时间步离散地选择其中一个矩阵作为转移矩阵。这种设计使得模型能够模拟FSA（达到非结构化矩阵的表达水平），同时保持结构化矩阵的高效性（高吞吐量、低内存消耗），适合大规模训练。

Result: 1. 在合成机制和状态跟踪任务上验证了其理论表达能力在实践中得以实现；2. 在长度超过17,000的多变量时间序列任务上，在竞争性SSM方法中达到了新的SOTA精度；3. 作为混合LLM的即插即用替代品，在自然语言状态跟踪和通用语言建模中均有所提升，相比当前前沿语言模型中使用的SSM，吞吐量更高且内存消耗更低。

Conclusion: Flash PD-SSM成功打破了SSM中效率与表达能力的根本性权衡，证明了可以通过结构化稀疏矩阵的离散选择机制，在保持高计算效率的同时实现强大的表达能力。该方法为构建下一代高效且强大的序列模型提供了有价值的解决方案。

Abstract: State-space models (SSMs) face a fundamental trade-off between efficiency and expressivity that is mainly dictated by the structure of the model's transition matrix. Unstructured transition matrices enable maximal expressivity, as measured by their ability to model finite-state automaton (FSA) transitions, but come at a prohibitively high compute and memory cost. In contrast, most structured transition matrix forms are highly efficient both in runtime and memory consumption, but suffer from limited expressivity. Building on recent work on structured sparse SSMs, we propose Flash PD-SSM, a novel SSM that achieves comparable throughput to widely-used structured SSMs with significantly better expressivity guarantees. Flash PD-SSM maintains a trainable set of structured sparse matrices, a single one of which is discretely selected at each time-step, enabling FSA expressiveness at the level of unstructured matrices while maintaining the efficiency required for training models at scale. First, we validate Flash PD-SSM against a suite of alternative models on synthetic mechanistic and state-tracking tasks, finding that its theoretical expressivity is achieved in practice. Second, on multivariate time-series tasks involving sequences of length over 17,000, we find that Flash PD-SSM defines a new state-of-the-art (SoTA) accuracy among competing SSM methods. Finally, we demonstrate that Flash PD-SSM is an effective drop-in replacement for hybrid LLMs, yielding improvements both in natural language state-tracking and in common language modeling scenarios. The model exhibits increased throughput and decreased memory consumption compared to SSMs widely used in frontier language models.

</details>


### [205] [Bridge: Retrieval-Augmented Spatiotemporal Modeling for Urban Delivery Demand](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19172)
*Yihong Tang,Tong Nie,Junlin He,Qianjun Huang,Dingyi Zhuang,Lijun Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Bridge，一种检索增强的时空图框架，通过结合归纳式上下文图主干和区域-时间窗口的记忆机制，有效解决了新服务区域缺乏历史数据时的城市配送需求冷启动预测问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时空预测模型高度依赖参数化方法，当面对缺乏历史记录的新服务区域（冷启动区域）时，难以捕捉短期运营动态。地理空间嵌入虽能提供位置和功能信息，但无法直接揭示相似区域在可比时间背景下的行为模式。

Method: 提出Bridge框架，包含归纳式上下文图主干和区域-时间窗口记忆库。对于目标区域，利用区域上下文和近期动态从记忆库中检索未来需求模式，并通过门控融合机制修正主干预测结果。引入未来感知目标函数训练检索器，以对齐检索结果与预测效用。

Result: 在四个真实配送数据集上的实验表明，Bridge在城市内冷启动和跨城市部分观测迁移两种场景下，均持续优于具有竞争力的时空基线模型。

Conclusion: 当单纯的参数化图泛化能力不足时，检索增强机制为冷启动城市需求预测提供了有效的操作性记忆，显著提升了预测性能。

Abstract: Forecasting urban delivery demand becomes substantially more challenging when newly added service regions lack historical records. Existing spatiotemporal forecasters effectively model spatial dependence once sufficient node histories are available. Still, they remain parametric and therefore struggle to recover short-term operational dynamics in cold-start regions. Geospatial embeddings help identify where a region is and what function it serves, yet they do not directly reveal how a similar region behaves under a comparable temporal context. We propose Bridge, a retrieval-augmented spatiotemporal graph framework that combines an inductive contextual graph backbone with a time-aware memory of region-time windows. For each target region, Bridge retrieves future demand patterns from the memory using both regional context and recent dynamics, and refines the backbone forecast through a gated fusion mechanism. To align retrieval with forecasting utility, we further train the retriever with a future-aware objective that favors entries whose future trajectories best match the target. Experiments on four real-world delivery datasets show that Bridge consistently improves over competitive spatiotemporal baselines in both within-city cold-start and cross-city transfer with partial observations. The results show that retrieval augmentation provides a useful operational memory for cold-start urban demand forecasting when parametric graph generalization alone is insufficient.

</details>


### [206] [Planner-Admissible Graph-PDE Value Extensions for Sparse Goal-Conditioned Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19185)
*Shiheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将稀疏目标条件规划建模为图PDE Dirichlet扩展问题，提出基于动作间隙的局部证书，证明AMLE（p=∞图p-拉普拉斯）优于调和扩展，在AntMaze实验中成功率从0.584提升至0.970以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在稀疏目标条件规划中，如何将目标边界上的稀疏代价标签扩展到未标记图节点，使得贪婪rollout能到达目标，是一个核心问题。现有方法缺乏对规划器可采纳性的理论保证，作者旨在研究何种图值扩展方法在argmin-Q规划器下是规划器可采纳的。

Method: 1) 将稀疏目标条件规划形式化为图PDE Dirichlet扩展问题；2) 提出局部动作间隙证书定理：若代理值误差沿rollout保持低于真实动作间隙的一半，则贪婪rollout到达目标；3) 证明AMLE（p=∞图p-拉普拉斯端点）通过比较原理填充距离界实例化该证书；4) 分析调和扩展因反映边界命中概率而非最短路径贪婪顺序导致的局部动作误排序问题；5) 在120个AntMaze布局派生图配置上进行实证验证。

Result: 理论贡献：建立了局部动作间隙证书定理，证明AMLE满足该证书条件，而调和扩展不满足。实验结果：调和扩展聚合rollout成功率为0.584，AMLE达到0.970；有限高p方法进入高成功区域，p=4成功率为0.903，p=8为0.973，固定预算p=16求解器为0.982。机制审计显示AMLE纠正了大多数调和扩展在rollout加权决策范围内的动作逆序。

Conclusion: AMLE通过满足局部动作间隙证书，在稀疏目标条件规划中提供了理论可保证的规划器可采纳性，显著优于调和扩展。高p图p-拉普拉斯方法也展现出良好的实证表现，为稀疏标签扩展提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Sparse goal-conditioned planning with few cost-to-go labels can be viewed as a graph-PDE Dirichlet extension problem: extend sparse labels on a goal-dependent boundary to unlabelled graph vertices so that greedy rollouts reach the goal. We study which graph value extensions are planner-admissible under the operational argmin-Q planner. Our main result is a local action-gap certificate: if the surrogate value error along the rollout stays below half the true action gap, then the greedy rollout reaches the goal. Absolutely Minimal Lipschitz Extension (AMLE), the p=infinity endpoint of the graph p-Laplacian family, instantiates this certificate through a comparison-principle fill-distance bound. Harmonic extension, by contrast, can mis-rank local actions because its values reflect boundary hitting probabilities rather than shortest-path greedy order.
  On 120 AntMaze layout-derived graph configurations, harmonic extension achieves 0.584 aggregate rollout success, while AMLE reaches 0.970. Finite high-p methods also enter a high-success regime, with success 0.903 for p=4, 0.973 for p=8, and 0.982 for a fixed-budget p=16 solver, though the p=16 row is not used as a converged endpoint ranking due to incomplete solver certification. Mechanism audits show that many rollout decisions occur in AMLE-compatible but harmonic-incompatible local geometry, and that AMLE corrects most harmonic inversions on the rollout-weighted decision scope.

</details>


### [207] [Sequential Consensus for Multi-Agent LLM Debates: A Wald-SPRT compute governor with calibration-based failure detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19193)
*Andrea Morandi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将经典统计学的序贯概率比检验（SPRT）引入多智能体LLM辩论，作为计算资源的动态调控器，在GSM8K上以仅2%的准确率代价实现了3.7倍的API调用减少，为多智能体系统提供了低成本的计算控制与失效检测层。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多智能体LLM辩论方法普遍采用固定辩论轮数，导致对简单问题计算过度（浪费资源）而对困难问题计算不足（影响效果）。研究旨在引入一种自适应机制，根据辩论收敛情况动态决定何时停止，以优化计算效率与准确率的权衡。

Method: 将Wald序贯概率比检验（SPRT）作为即插即用的计算调控器。核心流程为：每轮辩论后，LLM裁判输出[0,1]区间的共识分数；Wald监视器在Beta似然族下累积"有效收敛"与"尚未有效"的对数似然比；当触及预设边界时停止辩论，或在达到最大轮数R_max时返回当前最优结果。同时使用独立校准子集对裁判评分进行领域校准。

Result: 在GSM8K上，该方法平均1.01轮（4.06次LLM调用）即停止，准确率达97.0%；相比固定5轮辩论（15次调用，99.0%准确率），以2个百分点的准确率损失换取了3.7倍的调用缩减。在MMLU上，校准后的KL散度趋近于0，99.5%的项目触及上限，成本为基准的2.1倍，表明在该场景下辩论收敛判别困难。理论层面，在i.i.d.假设下继承了SPRT的第一、二类错误保证。

Conclusion: SPRT的核心价值并非提升辩论准确率，而是作为一种低成本的计算控制与失效检测层，能根据问题难度自适应分配计算资源。其在不同领域的有效性强烈依赖于裁判评分能否有效区分"有效收敛"与"无效收敛"，因此领域校准是实际部署的关键。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM debate improves factuality and reasoning, but most recipes pick a fixed round count, over-spending on easy items and under-spending on hard ones. We adapt Wald's Sequential Probability Ratio Test (SPRT) as a plug-in compute governor for LLM debates. After each round, an LLM judge emits a [0,1] consensus score on the latest agent positions; a Wald monitor accumulates the log-likelihood ratio of "useful convergence" vs "not yet useful" under a Beta likelihood family, and stops when either boundary is crossed or returns a capped best-effort outcome at R_max. Under i.i.d. assumptions the rule inherits SPRT type-I/type-II error guarantees; in deployment the calibration itself is the more important object, since it estimates whether the judge score actually separates useful from unhelpful convergence in a given domain. We evaluate two tracks: (i) a Monte-Carlo study under calibrated Beta models characterising working curves, error rates, capping behaviour, and sensitivity; and (ii) a real-LLM evaluation on 200 attempted MMLU and 200 attempted GSM8K items with three heterogeneous agents (gpt-5, claude-opus-4-6, gemini-2.5-pro) and a claude-opus-4-6 judge, using disjoint 40-item calibration subsets. On GSM8K the rule stops in 1.01 average rounds (4.06 LLM calls) at 97.0% accuracy vs 99.0% for fixed-5 debate at 15 calls: a 3.7x call reduction at -2pp accuracy. On MMLU the calibrated KL collapses to about 0 and the rule caps on 99.5% of items at 2.1x cost. The takeaway is not that SPRT makes debate more accurate, but that a classical sequential test serves as a cheap compute-control and failure-detection layer for multi-agent LLM systems.

</details>


### [208] [On-Device Continual Learning with Dual-Stage Buffer and Dynamic Loss for Point-of-Care Pneumonia Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19201)
*Danu Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PneumoNet方法，通过轻量级CNN、双阶段平衡缓冲区和动态类加权损失实现域增量学习，在域偏移场景下达到86.6%准确率和1.4%遗忘率，适用于资源受限环境的肺炎诊断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型在胸部X光片肺炎检测中表现优异，但在设备、患者或机构差异导致的域偏移下性能显著下降。资源受限环境中的即时诊断需要能够适应域变化且可在设备端运行的轻量级解决方案。

Method: 提出PneumoNet域增量学习方法，包含三个核心组件：(1)轻量级CNN用于设备端预测；(2)双阶段平衡缓冲区用于类平衡回放；(3)动态类加权损失函数以纠正训练批次不平衡。在模拟五种现实域变化场景的域偏移PneumoniaMNIST数据集上进行评估。

Result: PneumoNet达到86.6%准确率，遗忘率仅1.4%，模型尺寸更小、推理速度更快，优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: PneumoNet展示了在即时医疗设备上实现自适应、隐私保护诊断AI的潜力，适用于现实世界和疫情应对的医疗场景，尤其适合资源受限环境。

Abstract: Deep learning models detect pneumonia from chest X-rays with high accuracy, but the performance declines under domain shifts caused by differences in devices, patients, or institutions. We present PneumoNet, a domain-incremental learning method for point-of-care pneumonia diagnosis in resource-limited settings. PneumoNet combines a lightweight CNN for on-device prediction, a dual-stage balanced buffer for class-balanced replay, and a dynamic class-weighted loss to correct training-batch imbalances. Evaluated on a domain-shifted PneumoniaMNIST dataset simulating five realistic domain change scenarios, PneumoNet achieves 86.6% accuracy with 1.4% forgetting while being smaller and faster than existing baselines. These results highlight PneumoNet's potential to enable adaptive, privacy-preserving diagnostic AI directly on point-of-care medical devices in real-world and pandemic-ready healthcare.

</details>


### [209] [Worst-Group Equalized Odds Regularization for Multi-Attribute Fair Medical Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19214)
*Nikhil Cherian Kurian,Victor Caquilpan Parra,Abin Shoby,Luke Whitbread,Lauren Oakden-Rayner,Robert Vandersluis,Jessica Schrouff,Lyle J. Palmer,Mark Jenkinson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种最差群体均衡赔率边际正则化方法，用于解决医疗AI在不同人口群体间诊断性能差异的问题，能够在保持诊断性能的同时有效减少公平性差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗AI在不同人口群体间的诊断性能存在系统性差异，但群体AUC指标可能掩盖临床上重要的差异。在固定推理时操作点上，某些群体可能表现出过度诊断行为（真阳性率和假阳性率升高），而其他群体则表现出诊断不足模式（真阳性率和假阳性率降低）。这些相反趋势在聚合AUC中可能相互抵消，但会在临床决策中产生有意义的不公平现象。需要评估和缓解在操作点及跨多人口属性情况下的此类差异。

Method: 提出了一种最差群体均衡赔率边际正则化器，该方法明确针对推理时真阳性和假阳性侧的群体层面偏差。在每次更新时，该方法识别出由显式人口属性（如年龄、性别、种族）定义的、表现出最极端边际偏差的群体，并应用统一的惩罚项，从而实现在无需显式交集约束的情况下跨多人口轴进行公平性优化。

Result: 在两个真实多标签场景的医疗影像数据集上，该方法能够持续减少均衡赔率和均衡机会方面的差异，同时对AUC的影响极小，在改善公平性的同时保持了诊断性能。

Conclusion: 所提出的正则化方法能够有效减少医疗AI模型在多人口属性维度的公平性差异，无需显式交集约束即可优化跨多人口轴的公平性，在保持诊断性能的同时改善公平性，为医疗AI的公平性优化提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Diagnostic performance in medical AI varies systematically across demographic groups, yet subgroup AUC can mask clinically important disparities. At a fixed inference-time operating point, some groups may exhibit over-diagnostic behaviour, characterized by elevated true and false positive rates, while others show under-diagnostic patterns with reduced true and false positive rates. These opposing tendencies can cancel in aggregate AUCs while producing meaningful inequities in clinical decision-making. Motivated by the need to assess and mitigate such disparities at the operating point and across multiple demographic attributes simultaneously, we propose a worst-group equalized-odds margin regularizer. The proposed regularizer explicitly targets subgroup-level deviations on both the true positive and false positive sides at inference. At each update, the method identifies subgroups defined by explicit demographic attributes (e.g., age, sex, and race) that exhibit the most extreme margin deviations and applies a unified penalty, enabling fairness optimization across multiple demographic axes without requiring explicit intersectional constraints. Across two medical imaging datasets in realistic multi-label settings, our method consistently reduces disparities in Equalized Odds and Equalized Opportunity with minimal impact on AUC, preserving diagnostic performance while improving fairness.

</details>


### [210] [DeRegiME: Deep Regime Mixtures for Probabilistic Forecasting under Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19231)
*Kieran Wood,Stefan Zohren,Stephen J. Roberts*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DeRegiME——一种基于稀疏变分高斯过程的直接多视界概率预测器，通过分离潜在不确定性机制与基础信号，在10个基准上NLPD相比最强基线提升20.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经预测器存在关键局限：点预测丢弃残差不确定性，概率预测头（边际分布、混合、分位数集或扩散采样）很少揭示残差的机制结构。而噪声异方差时间序列中的分布偏移（突变、渐变或视界相关）常出现在残差不确定性而非条件均值中。

Method: 采用稀疏变分高斯过程，通过非平稳机制混合核和Student-t似然，利用共享门控将各机制子核和噪声过程结合。每个预测位置被软分配到学习到的周期性机制，生成单一稀疏GP后验而非GP专家混合。使用折棍法门控有效剪枝机制数量。

Result: 在10个基准数据集和3种编码器网格上验证，NLPD相比最强编码器匹配基线（DeepAR/GluonTS风格动态Student-t头）提升20.3%，CRPS提升3.0%，MSE提升4.7%。改进在涵盖突变、渐变和季节性偏移的所有数据集上均一致。

Conclusion: DeRegiME成功实现了可解释的均值-残差-噪声分解，机制作为残差相似性聚类出现，转换表现为隐式变点。证明了核有效性和预测密度适当性，为处理异方差时间序列中的分布偏移提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: We introduce DeRegiME -- Deep Regime Mixture of Experts -- a direct multi-horizon probabilistic forecaster that separates latent uncertainty regimes from the underlying signal and softly assigns each forecast location to learned recurring regimes using a sparse variational Gaussian process (GP) whose nonstationary regime-mixing kernel and Student-t likelihood combine per-regime sub-kernels and noise processes via a shared gate. This yields a single sparse-GP posterior, not a mixture of GP experts. DeRegiME addresses a key limitation of neural forecasters: point forecasts discard residual uncertainty, and probabilistic heads -- whether single marginals, uninterpreted mixtures, quantile sets, or diffusion samples -- rarely expose the regime structure of the residual. Yet distribution shift in noisy heteroskedastic time series may be abrupt, gradual, or horizon-dependent and often appears in residual uncertainty rather than the conditional mean. DeRegiME yields an interpretable mean-residual-noise decomposition with a direct-sum feature-space representation that anchors regimes as clusters of residual similarity whose transitions surface as implicit changepoints. The effective number of regimes is pruned by the stick-breaking gate. We prove kernel validity and predictive-density propriety, and across ten benchmarks and three encoder grids DeRegiME improves negative log predictive density (NLPD) by 20.3% over the strongest encoder-matched baseline, a DeepAR/GluonTS-style dynamic Student-t head, with parallel gains on CRPS (3.0%) and MSE (4.7%). Improvements are consistent across all datasets, which span abrupt, gradual, and seasonal shifts.

</details>


### [211] [GAE Falls Short in Imperfect-Information Self-Play Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19235)
*Zhiyuan Fan,Gabriele Farina*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了VRPO算法，通过Q-boosting方差缩减优势估计器解决不完全信息博弈中PPO的方差问题，在中大规模博弈中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在不完全信息博弈的多智能体强化学习中，标准PPO使用的广义优势估计(GAE)由于随机未来动作采样会产生额外方差。这种方差在均衡自博弈中被均衡策略的随机性进一步放大，即使评论家完全准确时依然存在，成为性能瓶颈。

Method: 提出基于集中式动作价值评论家的Q-boosting方差缩减优势估计器，并构建方差缩减策略优化(VRPO)算法。该方法用多步Expected SARSA(λ)迹替代采样多步备份，在每个步骤计算策略期望以平均消除动作采样噪声，同时保留PPO的裁剪目标和在策略演员更新。

Result: VRPO在中型到大规模博弈中持续取得强劲表现，包括斗地主和单挑无限注德州扑克等复杂博弈场景。

Conclusion: Q-boosting优势估计器有效解决了不完全信息博弈中PPO的方差瓶颈问题，VRPO算法在保持PPO核心优势的同时显著降低了方差，为竞争性多智能体强化学习提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Competitive multi-agent reinforcement learning in imperfect-information games requires agents to act under partial observability and against adversarial opponents, necessitating stochastic policies. While self-play reinforcement learning with Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) has achieved strong empirical success, its standard advantage estimator, generalized advantage estimation, suffers from additional variance due to the sampling of stochastic future actions. This variance is amplified in equilibrium self-play because of the stochastic nature of the equilibrium policy and persists even when the critic is exact. We address this bottleneck by introducing $Q$-boosting, a variance-reduced advantage estimator based on a centralized action-value critic, and propose Variance-Reduced Policy Optimization (VRPO), incorporating this new estimator. The algorithm replaces sampled multi-step backups with a multi-step Expected SARSA$(λ)$ trace, computing policy expectations at each step to average out action-sampling noise, while retaining PPO's clipped objective and on-policy actor updates. Empirically, VRPO consistently achieves strong performance from mid-sized to large-scale games including Dou Dizhu and Heads-Up No-Limit Texas Hold'em.

</details>


### [212] [Euclidean Embedding of Data Using Local Distances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19243)
*Dimitris Arabadjis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种仅需局部距离图即可恢复全局一致欧氏嵌入的方法，通过求解变分问题将局部图距离与欧氏度量匹配，实现了无特征向量的表示学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究如何在仅给定局部距离图（而非原始特征向量）的情况下，恢复数据的全局一致欧氏嵌入，这是一个在流形学习和降维领域中的重要问题。

Method: 提出变分优化方法，通过匹配局部图距离与欧氏度量来获得嵌入。推导出坐标无关形式的Euler-Lagrange方程，使其可直接从距离图评估。虽然方程具有非线性，但可通过迭代求解稀疏线性问题来求解。整个过程仅需局部图操作。

Result: 在合成流形和真实数据集上的实验评估表明，该方法能够一致地保持局部度量结构和邻域关系，同时近似实现全局等距嵌入。

Conclusion: 成功推导了连续域中最优欧氏嵌入的泛函方程，实现了完全表示无关的表述，仅需邻域距离图而无需特征向量，为流形学习提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: We study the problem of recovering a globally consistent Euclidean embedding of data, given only a local distance graph and propose a method that optimally represents these distances. The method operates solely on a neighborhood graph weighted by pairwise distances, without requiring any prior vector representation of the data. The embedding is obtained by solving a variational problem that matches local, on-graph distances to the Euclidean metric, induced by the differentials of the embedding functions. The resulting Euler-Lagrange equations are derived in a coordinate-free form, enabling direct evaluation of all operators from the distance graph alone. Though non-linear and missing an explicit expression for their non-linearity, these equations are shown to be resolved as an iteratively updated sparse linear problem. The main contributions of the proposed approach are (a) the derivation of the functional equations governing the optimal Euclidean embedding in the continuum, (b) a representation-free formulation that requires only a neighborhood distance graph and no feature vectors and (c) an estimation procedure based exclusively on local graph operations. We experimentally evaluate the resulting non-parametric algorithm on synthetic manifolds and real datasets, demonstrating consistent preservation of local metric structure and neighboring relations, while approximating the global isometric embedding.

</details>


### [213] [Beyond Extrapolation: Knowledge Utilization Paradigm with Bidirectional Inspiration for Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19249)
*Liu Chong,Yingjie Zhou,Hao Li,Pengyang Wang,Qingsong Wen,Ce Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出KUP-BI时间序列建模范式，通过从训练数据中提取“后目标延续”知识作为代理，将其与历史输入融合，实现双向预测，在6个数据集上持续提升SOTA模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时序预测方法主要依赖单向推理（历史→目标），忽视了“历史-目标-后目标延续”这一自然链结构中的关键信息。后目标延续记录了目标之后的轨迹演化，有助于稳定预测，但在推理时不可观测，因此需要获取其近似代理来引入结构化知识。

Method: 提出KUP-BI（知识利用范式与双向启发）：1）从仅训练阶段的历史库中蒸馏延续风格知识，作为后目标延续的近似代理；2）通过轻量级特征级门控模块，将输入流与延续代理流进行融合；3）该设计不引入训练轨迹之外的信息，而是提供结构化归纳偏置，帮助模型利用典型延续模式。

Result: 在六个公共数据集上的实验结果表明，KUP-BI能够持续提升最先进模型的预测性能，且仅带来较小的额外开销。

Conclusion: KUP-BI成功地将后目标延续的结构化知识融入标准预测骨干网络，实现了双向预测范式，为时间序列预测提供了一种有效的性能提升方案，证明了利用训练数据中的延续模式进行结构化建模的价值。

Abstract: Time-series forecasting is critical in various scenarios, such as energy, transportation, and public health. However, most existing forecasters rely primarily on one-way inference, \textit{i.e.}, mapping \textbf{history} to \textbf{target}, and overlook the structural information provided by a revised natural chain (``\textbf{history} (model input) -- \textbf{target} (ground-truth output) -- \textbf{post-target continuation}''). The post-target continuation records how trajectories evolve after the target, which can help stabilize forecasting, but it is not observable at inference time. In this work, we aim to obtain an approximate proxy of the post-target continuation for the current input, providing structural knowledge for bidirectional forecasting. This idea is instantiated as KUP-BI (Knowledge Utilization Paradigm with Bidirectional Inspiration), a new time-series modeling paradigm that distills continuation-style knowledge (as an approximate post-target continuation proxy) from a \emph{train-only} historical library and integrates it into standard forecasting backbones. The input stream and the continuation-proxy stream are fused via a lightweight feature-level gating module. This design does not introduce information beyond what is already contained in the training trajectories; instead, it provides a structured inductive bias that helps backbones exploit typical continuation patterns rather than relying solely on parametric extrapolation. Experimental results on six public datasets show that KUP-BI consistently improves the forecasting performance of state-of-the-art models, with small additional overhead.

</details>


### [214] [ExECG: An Explainable AI Framework for ECG models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19258)
*Jong-Hwan Jang,Yong-yeon Jo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了ExECG，一个用于ECG模型的可解释AI（XAI）Python框架，通过标准化的三阶段流程（封装器、解释器、可视化器）解决了现有研究中流程与报告规范不一致、难以复用和复现的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管深度学习在ECG诊断任务（如心律失常分类）中表现出色，但仅靠准确率不足以支持临床部署，因为缺乏可解释性限制了模型输出的理由说明、错误分析和可信度。此外，现有的ECG XAI研究在实用流程和报告规范上存在差异，阻碍了方法的复用与复现。

Method: 提出了名为ExECG的Python框架，包含三阶段流水线：(1) Wrapper（封装器）：标准化不同ECG格式和中间表示的访问；(2) Explainer（解释器）：在统一的执行协议下整合多种XAI方法；(3) Visualizer（可视化器）：在统一界面内支持一致的跨方法比较与可视化。

Result: 通过简洁的示例代码和两个案例研究展示了ExECG框架的端到端使用，验证了其在ECG可解释性分析中的互操作性与可复现性。

Conclusion: ExECG框架为ECG模型的可解释性分析提供了标准化、模块化且易于使用的解决方案，有助于推动ECG XAI研究的规范化发展与临床应用的信任建立。

Abstract: Deep learning has enabled ECG diagnostic models with strong performance in tasks such as arrhythmia classification and abnormality detection. However, accuracy alone is insufficient for clinical deployment because it does not explain why a specific output was produced, limiting justification, error analysis, and trust. Although ECG XAI has been extensively investigated and steadily improved, practical pipelines and reporting conventions vary across studies, hindering reuse and reproducibility. To address these issues, we present Explainable AI framework for ECG models (ExECG), a Python framework that provides a three-stage pipeline: Wrapper standardizes access across heterogeneous ECG formats and intermediate representations, Explainer unifies diverse XAI methods under a shared execution protocol, and Visualizer supports consistent cross-method comparison within a unified interface. We demonstrate end-to-end usage with concise examples and two case studies, highlighting interoperable and reproducible ECG explainability.

</details>


### [215] [Backdooring Masked Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19262)
*Daniel Yiming Cao,Chengzhong Wang,Sheng-Yen Chou,Chengyu Huang,Pin-Yu Chen,Shengwei An*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次针对掩码扩散语言模型（MDLM）提出了一种训练时后门攻击方法SHADOWMASK，通过修改前向腐蚀过程实现近100%攻击成功率，同时保持模型在正常任务上的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 掩码扩散语言模型（MDLM）作为一种新兴的文本生成范式，其训练时安全性尚未被探索。现有的针对高斯扩散模型或自回归语言模型的后门攻击方法无法直接应用于MDLM，因为MDLM依赖于离散状态腐蚀和迭代去噪，而非连续噪声或从左到右的预测。

Method: 提出SHADOWMASK后门攻击方法，通过修改MDLM的前向腐蚀过程，将标准全掩码终端分布替换为触发器-掩码混合先验。该方法创建了从触发器腐蚀状态到攻击者指定目标的专用去噪路径，同时保持正常去噪行为。论文还提供了数学公式化表述，包括定义后门前向过程、推导逆时后验概率以及获得连续时间训练目标。

Result: 在基于DiT的MDLM和LLaDA-8B-Instruct模型上，使用WikiText-103、OpenWebText和Alpaca数据集进行评估。SHADOWMASK实现了近100%的攻击成功率，显著优于标准数据投毒方法，基本保持正常任务性能，在全模型微调和参数高效微调下仍然有效，且对代表性防御方法具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了MDLM在训练时面临的后门攻击安全威胁，证明了SHADOWMASK攻击方法的有效性和鲁棒性，为MDLM的安全性研究提供了重要参考。

Abstract: Masked diffusion language models (MDLMs) are emerging as a compelling new paradigm for text generation, but their training-time security remains largely unexplored. Existing backdoor attacks on Gaussian diffusion models or autoregressive language models do not directly apply to MDLMs because MDLMs rely on discrete state corruption and iterative denoising rather than continuous noising or left-to-right prediction. In this work, we present the first systematic study of training-time backdoor attacks on MDLMs. We propose SHADOWMASK, a backdoor attack that modifies the MDLM forward corruption process by replacing the standard all-mask terminal distribution with a trigger-mask mixture prior. This creates a dedicated denoising pathway from trigger-corrupted states to attacker-specified targets while preserving clean denoising behavior. We further provide a principled mathematical formulation by defining the backdoored forward process, deriving the reverse-time posterior, and obtaining the continuous-time training objective. Evaluations on DiT-based MDLM and LLaDA-8B-Instruct across WikiText-103, OpenWebText, and Alpaca show that SHADOWMASK achieves near-100% attack success, substantially outperforms standard data poisoning, largely preserves clean utility, remains effective under full-model and parameter-efficient fine-tuning, and is robust against representative defenses.

</details>


### [216] [From Simple to Complex: Curriculum-Guided Physics-Informed Neural Networks via Gaussian Mixture Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19263)
*Jianan Yang,Yiran Wang,Shuai Li,Fujun Cao,Xuefei Yan,Junmin Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了CGMPINN方法，通过结合高斯混合模型和动态课程学习来解决PINN训练中的梯度病理、谱偏差和收敛问题，在六类PDE基准测试中相对L2误差最高降低97.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理信息神经网络(PINNs)在求解偏微分方程时面临梯度病理、谱偏差和收敛差等问题，特别是在处理强非线性、陡峭梯度或多尺度特征时表现不佳，需要一种能自适应调整学习难度的训练框架。

Method: 提出课程引导高斯混合物理信息神经网络(CGMPINN)：周期性地对PDE残差分布拟合高斯混合模型(GMM)以量化空间变化的学习难度；通过平滑的课程调度逐步将训练重心从简单区域转移到困难区域；采用基于精度的方差调制在早期优化中抑制不可靠聚类；这种双重课程机制由共享课程参数控制，并可结合自适应性损失平衡。

Result: 在涵盖椭圆型、抛物型、双曲型、对流主导型和非线性反应扩散型的六个PDE基准测试中，CGMPINN在所有对比方法中始终获得最低的相对L2误差和最大绝对误差，相比标准PINN最高降低97.8%的相对L2误差，且计算成本相当。

Conclusion: CGMPINN通过理论保证（梯度范数的次线性收敛、课程加权损失与标准PDE损失的均匀等价性、带显式权重诱导偏差的泛化界）和实验验证，证明了将课程学习与难度自适应采样相结合的有效性，为解决PINN的训练挑战提供了新思路。

Abstract: Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) offer a mesh-free framework for solving partial differential equations (PDEs), yet training often suffers from gradient pathologies, spectral bias, and poor convergence, especially for problems with strong nonlinearity, sharp gradients, or multiscale features. We propose the Curriculum-Guided Gaussian Mixture Physics-Informed Neural Network (CGMPINN), which integrates Gaussian mixture modeling with dynamic curriculum learning. Specifically, a GMM is periodically fitted to the PDE residual distribution to quantify spatially varying learning difficulty. A smooth curriculum schedule progressively shifts training focus from easy to harder regions, while precision-based variance modulation suppresses unreliable clusters during early optimization. This dual curriculum is governed by a shared curriculum parameter and can be combined with self-adaptive loss balancing. We further establish theoretical guarantees, including sublinear convergence of the gradient norm for the induced time-varying loss, uniform equivalence between the curriculum-weighted and standard PDE losses, and a generalization bound with an explicit weighting-induced bias characterization. Experiments on six benchmark PDEs spanning elliptic, parabolic, hyperbolic, advection-dominated, and nonlinear reaction-diffusion types show that CGMPINN consistently achieves the lowest relative $L_2$ and maximum absolute errors among all compared methods, reducing relative $L_2$ error by up to 97.8\% over the standard PINN at comparable cost. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/Mathematics-Yang/CGMPINN.

</details>


### [217] [CODA: Rewriting Transformer Blocks as GEMM-Epilogue Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19269)
*Han Guo,Jack Zhang,Arjun Menon,Driss Guessous,Vijay Thakkar,Yoon Kim,Tri Dao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CODA，一种将Transformer中访存密集型算子重新参数化为“GEMM+收尾程序”的GPU核抽象，通过在片上缓存期间执行计算，有效解决了数据移动瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer训练系统中，归一化、激活函数、残差连接等算子属于访存密集型，频繁在全局内存中搬运大量中间张量，成为日益严重的数据移动瓶颈，影响了高度优化训练栈的整体性能。

Method: 引入CODA抽象，将Transformer算子代数重参数化为GEMM-plus-epilogue程序。该抽象固定GEMM主循环，在输出块仍驻留在片上时，通过一组可组合的收尾原语（缩放、归约、成对变换、累加）来执行计算，从而避免昂贵的全局内存读写。

Result: 在代表性的Transformer工作负载中，由人类和LLM编写的CODA核均实现了高性能，该抽象足以覆盖标准Transformer块前向和反向传播中几乎所有的非注意力计算。

Conclusion: GEMM-plus-epilogue编程范式是一条切实可行的路径，能够在保持专家级GEMM性能结构的同时，实现框架级生产力与硬件级效率的结合。

Abstract: Transformer training systems are built around dense linear algebra, yet a nontrivial fraction of end-to-end time is spent on surrounding memory-bound operators. Normalization, activations, residual updates, reductions, and related computations repeatedly move large intermediate tensors through global memory while performing little arithmetic, making data movement an increasingly important bottleneck in otherwise highly optimized training stacks. We introduce CODA, a GPU kernel abstraction that expresses these computations as GEMM-plus-epilogue programs. CODA is based on the observation that many Transformer operators exposed as separate framework kernels can be algebraically reparameterized to execute while a GEMM output tile remains on chip, before it is written to memory. The abstraction fixes the GEMM mainloop and exposes a small set of composable epilogue primitives for scaling, reductions, pairwise transformations, and accumulation. This constrained interface preserves the performance structure of expert-written GEMMs while remaining expressive enough to cover nearly all non-attention computation in the forward and backward pass of a standard Transformer block. Across representative Transformer workloads, both human- and LLM-authored CODA kernels achieve high performance, suggesting that GEMM-plus-epilogue programming offers a practical path toward combining framework-level productivity with hardware-level efficiency.

</details>


### [218] [Rethinking Muon Beyond Pretraining: Spectral Failures and High-Pass Remedies for VLA and RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19282)
*Chongyu Fan,Gaowen Liu,Mingyi Hong,Ramana Rao Kompella,Sijia Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Pion优化器，通过引入高通谱滤波机制解决了Muon优化器在跨模态训练和强化学习中的不稳定性问题，在VLA训练和RLVR后训练中均显著优于Muon和AdamW。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon优化器通过Newton-Schulz迭代实现谱白化，在LLM预训练中表现优异，但在两个场景存在根本局限：(i)跨模态视觉-语言-动作(VLA)训练中，低秩动作模块梯度会导致噪声尾方向被放大；(ii)可验证奖励强化学习(RLVR)中，低信噪比梯度和保持预训练头部特异性的需求使白化过程不稳定。

Method: 提出Pion优化器，保留Muon的计算效率，将均匀谱白化替换为两阶段Promotion+Suppression机制（高通NS迭代）。该设计产生锐利的谱高通效应：将主导奇异值锚定在1，同时将噪声尾分量抑制至0，滤波强度可控。此外，Pion支持per-head模式，通过简单的reshape在各注意力头独立应用更新，以保持预训练头部异质性，且无额外开销。

Result: 在LIBERO和LIBERO-Plus的VLA训练中，Pion在l_1回归(VLA-Adapter)和流匹配(VLANeXt)架构上均优于基线。例如，VLA-Adapter在LIBERO Object上1500步后达到100%成功率，Muon为97.0%，AdamW仅32.2%。在DROID设置下的Franka Research 3真实机器人上，Pion在三个抓取放置任务中也表现优异。在Qwen3-1.7B/4B的RLVR后训练（GRPO和GMPO）中，Pion在MATH和GSM8K上优于AdamW，而Muon崩溃至零。

Conclusion: Pion作为Muon的即插即用替代品，通过高通谱滤波机制有效解决了均匀谱白化在非预训练场景的局限性，在VLA训练和RLVR后训练中均展现出卓越性能，证明了针对特定任务特性设计优化器谱特性控制的重要性。

Abstract: Muon is a matrix-aware optimizer that leverages Newton-Schulz (NS) iterations to enforce spectral gradient orthogonalization by driving all singular values of the momentum matrix toward 1. While this uniform spectral whitening enhances exploration and outperforms AdamW in LLM pretraining, we show it could lead to fundamental limitations beyond pretraining in two regimes: (i) cross-modality vision-language-action (VLA) training, where inherently low-rank action-module gradients cause amplification of noisy tail directions, and (ii) reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR), where low-SNR gradients and the need to preserve per-head specialization from prior training make whitening unstable. To address these challenges, we propose Pion, a drop-in replacement for Muon that preserves its computational efficiency while replacing uniform spectral whitening with a two-stage Promotion+Suppression mechanism, which we call the high-pass NS iteration. This design induces a sharp spectral high-pass effect, anchoring dominant singular values at 1 while suppressing noisy tail components toward 0, with controllable filter strength. To preserve pretrained per-head heterogeneity, Pion also supports a per-head mode that applies updates independently across attention heads via a simple reshape, at no extra cost. In VLA training on LIBERO and LIBERO-Plus, Pion consistently outperforms both baselines across l_1-regression (VLA-Adapter) and flow-matching (VLANeXt) architectures, e.g., reaching 100% success rate on LIBERO Object after 1,500 training steps with VLA-Adapter, vs. 97.0% for Muon and only 32.2% for AdamW. The advantage of Pion further extends to a real Franka Research 3 robot with a pi_0.5 backbone under the DROID setup on three grasp-and-place tasks. In RLVR post-training on Qwen3-1.7B/4B with GRPO and GMPO, Pion also outperforms AdamW on MATH and GSM8K while Muon collapses to zero.

</details>


### [219] [EviTrack: Selection over Sampling for Delayed Disambiguation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19283)
*Omer Haq*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了EviTrack框架，通过基于证据的轨迹假设选择而非增加采样，有效解决了序列预测中延迟消歧难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在延迟消歧的序列预测场景中，早期观测存在歧义，多种潜在解释在积累足够证据前均保持合理性。现有的基于边缘推断的方法在此设定下表现不佳，要么过早地坍缩不确定性，要么在获得有效证据后无法恢复。

Method: 引入EviTrack测试时推理框架。该框架在潜在轨迹而非边缘状态上进行操作，维护一组竞争性的轨迹假设，并应用基于证据和似然比的选择机制，借鉴了多假设跟踪和检测前跟踪中的假设管理思想，从而延迟决策直到数据支持为止。

Result: 构建了具有已知潜在真值的受控合成基准测试。在匹配的推理预算下，EviTrack大幅优于基于采样的基线方法，实现了更快的消歧后恢复速度。

Conclusion: 在延迟消歧场景中，适度的轨迹级选择比增加采样覆盖更有效；这表明“选择优于采样”是实现可靠序列推断的关键原则。

Abstract: Sequential prediction is challenging in regimes of delayed disambiguation, where early observations are ambiguous and multiple latent explanations remain plausible until sufficient evidence accumulates. Standard approaches based on marginal inference struggle in this setting, either collapsing uncertainty prematurely or failing to recover once informative evidence arrives.
  We introduce EviTrack, a test-time inference framework that operates over latent trajectories rather than marginal states. EviTrack maintains a set of competing trajectory hypotheses and applies evidence- and likelihood-ratio-based selection to delay commitment until supported by data, drawing inspiration from hypothesis management in multiple hypothesis tracking and track-before-detect.
  To evaluate this setting, we construct a controlled synthetic benchmark with known latent ground truth that explicitly exhibits delayed disambiguation. At matched inference budget, EviTrack substantially outperforms sampling-based baselines, achieving faster post-disambiguation recovery.
  These results show that, in delayed disambiguation regimes, moderate trajectory-level selection is more effective than increasing sampling coverage, highlighting selection over sampling as a key principle for reliable sequential inference.

</details>


### [220] [Cross-Paradigm Knowledge Distillation: A Comprehensive Study of Bidirectional Transfer Between Random Forests and Deep Neural Networks for Big Data Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19299)
*Mahdi Naser Moghadasi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次全面研究了随机森林(RF)与深度神经网络(DNN)之间的双向知识蒸馏，提出了多阶段蒸馏、多教师集成蒸馏等方法，在144个实验中证明了该方法能实现竞争性性能，并平衡了可解释性与表达能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大数据的指数级增长迫切需要高效且可解释的机器学习模型。现有知识蒸馏研究主要集中于神经网络到神经网络的迁移，跨范式的知识迁移（如树模型与神经网络之间）仍 largely 未探索， ensemble learning 和模型压缩在大数据应用中存在关键空白。

Method: 提出了三种新颖的方法论：1) 渐进式多阶段蒸馏；2) 来自多样化树模型的多教师集成蒸馏；3) 不确定性感知的跨范式迁移机制。在6个不同数据集上进行了144个综合实验，涵盖分类和回归任务。

Result: 双向RF-DL蒸馏实现了具有竞争力的性能。多教师集成蒸馏始终优于传统方法，其中NN-COMPACT在分类任务中达到98.13%的准确率，NN-WIDE在回归任务中达到92.6%的R²分数。该方法成功结合了树模型的可解释性和神经网络的表达能力。

Conclusion: 该框架为大数据环境提供了部署灵活性，允许根据计算约束和可解释性要求选择最优模型。本研究在跨范式知识迁移领域建立了新的研究方向，对可解释AI和资源受限大数据系统中的可扩展模型部署具有重要意义。

Abstract: The exponential growth of big data has intensified the need for efficient and interpretable machine learning models that can handle diverse data characteristics while maintaining computational efficiency. Knowledge distillation has primarily focused on neural network-to-neural network transfer, leaving cross-paradigm knowledge transfer largely unexplored. This paper presents the first comprehensive study of bidirectional knowledge distillation between Random Forests (RF) and Deep Neural Networks (DNN), addressing critical gaps in ensemble learning and model compression for big data applications. We propose novel methodologies including progressive multi-stage distillation, multi-teacher ensemble distillation from diverse tree models, and uncertainty-aware cross-paradigm transfer mechanisms. Through 144 comprehensive experiments across 6 diverse datasets encompassing classification and regression tasks, we demonstrate that bidirectional RF-DL distillation achieves competitive performance while providing complementary benefits: interpretability from tree models and expressiveness from neural networks. Our results show that multi-teacher ensemble distillation consistently outperforms traditional approaches, with NN-COMPACT achieving 98.13% classification accuracy and NN-WIDE reaching 92.6% R^2 score in regression tasks. The proposed framework enables deployment flexibility in big data environments, allowing optimal model selection based on computational constraints and interpretability requirements. This work establishes a new research direction in cross-paradigm knowledge transfer with significant implications for interpretable AI and scalable model deployment in resource-constrained big data systems.

</details>


### [221] [A Two-Phase Adaptive Balanced Penalty Method for Controllable Pareto Front Learning under Split Feasibility Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19306)
*Nguyen Viet Hoang,Dung D. Le,Tran Ngoc Thang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了自适应平衡惩罚（ABP）算法，用于解决分割可行性条件下的可控帕累托前沿学习问题，通过双层标量化分解和自适应惩罚机制，在理论上证明了全序列收敛性，并开发了ABP-HyperNet训练策略，在多任务学习中将可行性从36-49%提升至87-100%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决在分割可行性条件下训练超网络进行可控帕累托前沿学习这一开放问题，现有方法缺乏严格的理论保证，且难以同时保证解的质量和约束满足。

Method: 将约束帕累托问题重构为双层标量化分割问题（BSSP），提出自适应平衡惩罚（ABP）算法，通过自适应指示器融合最优性、集合可行性和图像可行性三个梯度分量；利用凸代理技术证明收敛性；设计两阶段可行性优先训练策略应用于Hyper-MLP和HyperTrans架构；引入期望可行超体积（EFHV）指标评估约束CPFL。

Result: 在五个多目标基准测试中验证了ABP求解器相对于真实值的有效性；在三个多任务学习数据集上，ABP-HyperNet相比无约束基线实现了高达2.3倍的EFHV提升，将可行性从36-49%提高到87-100%。

Conclusion: ABP算法为约束条件下的可控帕累托前沿学习提供了首个具有严格理论保证的解决方案，ABP-HyperNet在实际应用中显著提升了解的可行性和质量，为多任务学习等场景提供了有效的超网络训练方法。

Abstract: We address the open problem of training hypernetworks for Controllable Pareto Front Learning (CPFL) under split feasibility conditions with rigorous theoretical guarantees. We reformulate the constrained Pareto problem as a Bi-Level Scalarized Split Problem (BSSP) and propose the Adaptive Balanced Penalty (ABP) algorithm, whose three gradient components -- optimality, set feasibility, and image feasibility -- are blended through an adaptive indicator driven by a computable lower bound. Using a novel convex surrogate technique, we prove full-sequence convergence under standard convexity and Robbins-Monro step-size assumptions. The ABP penalty structure is then translated into a two-phase, feasibility-first training strategy for Hyper-MLP and HyperTrans architectures (ABP-HyperNet). To evaluate constrained CPFL, we introduce the Expected Feasible Hypervolume (EFHV), which jointly captures solution quality and constraint satisfaction. Experiments on five multi-objective benchmarks validate the ABP solver against ground truth, while three multi-task learning datasets demonstrate that ABP-HyperNet achieves up to 2.3x higher EFHV than unconstrained baselines by raising feasibility from 36-49% to 87-100%.

</details>


### [222] [An Objective Performance Evaluation of the LSTM Networks in Time Series Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19311)
*Sooraj Sunil,Balakumar Balasingam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过蒙特卡洛仿真对比了LSTM分类器和基于模型的EM分类器在二元时间序列分类任务上的表现，发现EM分类器在符合假设模型的数据上表现更优，而LSTM在仅测量噪声不同时性能受限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型在物理定律明确的领域中逐渐取代经典基于模型的算法，但其在结构化环境中相对于基于模型方法的性能很少被客观评估，因此需要系统地比较数据驱动方法与模型驱动方法的性能差异。

Method: 构建性能评估框架，比较LSTM分类器与基于期望最大化（EM）的分类器在二元时间序列分类任务上的表现。使用两个仅在噪声统计上不同的标量线性高斯状态空间模型，以真实参数的卡尔曼滤波似然比检验作为最佳分类性能参考。通过蒙特卡洛仿真，从任务难度（过程或测量噪声的分离程度）、序列长度和训练数据集大小三个维度进行评估。

Result: EM分类器利用已知模型结构，在数据符合假设模型类别时表现优异。LSTM分类器需要更大的噪声统计差异才能实现可靠分类；当模型仅在测量噪声上不同时，无论序列长度或训练数据集大小如何，LSTM分类器的性能均低于参考分类器且趋于饱和。

Conclusion: 在具有明确物理模型结构的环境中，基于模型的方法（如EM分类器）能够有效利用先验知识，性能优于数据驱动的LSTM方法，特别是在测量噪声差异较小的情况下，LSTM的性能局限性明显。

Abstract: The rapid adoption of deep learning has increasingly led to data-driven models replacing classical model-based algorithms, even in domains governed by well-understood physical laws. While data-driven models, such as long short-term memory (LSTM) networks, have become a popular choice for time-series analysis, their performance relative to model-based approaches in structured environments is rarely evaluated objectively. This paper presents a performance evaluation framework comparing an LSTM classifier against a model-based expectation maximization (EM) classifier for binary time-series classification. The evaluation is conducted on two scalar linear Gaussian state space models differing only in their noise statistics, where the Kalman filter likelihood ratio test with true parameters serves as a reference for the best achievable classification performance.Through Monte Carlo simulations, the classifiers are evaluated across three axes: task difficulty, controlled by the separation in process or measurement noise between the two models; sequence length; and training dataset size. The results show that the EM classifier, which exploits the known model structure, performs strongly when the data conform to the assumed model class. The LSTM classifier requires a larger separation in noise statistics to achieve reliable classification, and its performance saturates below the reference classifier when the models differ only in measurement noise, regardless of sequence length or training dataset size.

</details>


### [223] [Inference-Time Scaling in Diffusion Models through Iterative Partial Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19317)
*Taegu Kang,Jaesik Yoon,Sungjin Ahn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出迭代部分细化（IPR），一种无需外部验证器的扩散模型推理时缩放方法，通过重新加噪和重新生成样本的子区域来提升全局一致性，在MNIST数独任务上将有效解率从55.8%提升至75.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的扩散模型推理时缩放方法通常依赖外部验证器或奖励模型进行样本排序和选择，限制了其在评估器不可用或不可靠场景下的可扩展性；同时，针对具有区域级、混合噪声条件的顺序推理扩散模型，推理时缩放研究仍然相对不足。

Method: 提出迭代部分细化（IPR）方法，从已生成的样本出发，对一部分区域重新加噪，并以剩余区域为条件重新生成这些区域，使模型能够在比初始生成时更丰富的上下文中修正早期的决策。该方法无需外部验证器，通过迭代式的部分细化产生全局一致性更强的样本。

Result: 在需要全局约束满足的推理任务上，IPR一致地提升了性能。在MNIST数独任务上，有效解率从55.8%提高到75.0%。实验结果表明迭代部分细化可以作为顺序、混合噪声设置下扩散模型有效的推理时缩放策略。

Conclusion: 迭代部分细化（IPR）是一种有效的扩散模型推理时缩放策略，无需外部验证器即可提升顺序扩散模型的全局一致性，在需要全局约束满足的任务上取得了显著的性能提升。

Abstract: Inference-time scaling has emerged as a major approach for improving reasoning capabilities, and has been increasingly applied to diffusion models. However, existing inference-time scaling methods for diffusion models typically rely on external verifiers or reward models to rank and select samples, limiting their scalability to settings where such evaluators are available and reliable. Moreover, while recent diffusion models perform sequential inference with region-wise, mixed-noise conditioning, inference-time scaling tailored to this setting remains relatively underexplored. We propose Iterative Partial Refinement (IPR), an inference-time scaling method for sequential diffusion that requires no external verifier. Starting from an already-generated sample, IPR re-noises a subset of regions and regenerates them conditioned on the remaining regions, enabling the model to revise earlier decisions under a richer context than was available during the initial generation. This iterative partial refinement produces more globally consistent samples without external verification. On reasoning tasks requiring global constraint satisfaction, IPR consistently improves performance: on MNIST Sudoku, the valid solution rate increases from 55.8% to 75.0%. These results show that iterative partial refinement alone can serve as an effective inference-time scaling strategy for diffusion models in sequential, mixed-noise settings. Code is available at: https://github.com/ahn-ml/IPR

</details>


### [224] [BrainDyn: A Sheaf Neural ODE for Generative Brain Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19324)
*Siddharth Viswanath,Panayiotis Ketonis,Chen Liu,Michael Perlmutter,Dhananjay Bhaskar,Smita Krishnaswamy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了BrainDyn模型，一种基于层束神经ODE的连续时间大脑动力学建模方法，通过结合LSTM和层束理论在结构化脑图上进行动态建模，在多模态脑数据上展现出强大的预测能力和下游任务支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法存在局限性：大语言模型和标准RNN忽略解剖组织结构，无法产生与脑区对齐的组件；图神经网络的消息传递规则过于简单，表达能力不足以捕捉类脑动力学特征。需要一种能够同时兼顾大脑解剖结构和复杂动态特性的建模方法。

Method: BrainDyn采用层束神经ODE框架：(1) 使用LSTM在滑动时间窗口上编码各脑区的近期活动历史，生成隐藏状态（stalks）；(2) 通过可学习的限制映射将这些状态投影到边特定的共享空间；(3) 利用层束拉普拉斯算子刻画共享空间中相邻节点的差异，促进神经元单元间的消息传递；(4) 将消息传递结果输入神经ODE，控制神经元活动的连续时间演化。

Result: 在静息态fMRI（PNC数据集）、局灶性癫痫头皮EEG（TUSZ数据集）和NEST尖峰网络模拟器生成的模拟活动三个模态上进行了评估。BrainDyn展现出强大的跨模态预测能力，所学到的表示能够支持包括硅内扰动预测在内的下游任务。

Conclusion: BrainDyn成功地将层束理论与神经ODE相结合，实现了对大脑连续时间动力学的有效建模。该方法在保持与脑区解剖结构对齐的同时具备足够的表达能力，为生成合成数据、分析脑瞬态差异以及推断潜在生成动力学提供了有价值的工具。

Abstract: Efficient neural network models that generate brain-like dynamic activity can be a valuable resource for generating synthetic data, analyzing differences in brain transients under conditions such as testing perturbation activity or inferring the underlying generative dynamics. However, large language models (LLMs) or standard recurrent neural networks (RNNs) ignore the anatomical organization and therefore do not produce components that align with brain regions. On the other hand, graph-based networks often have very simple message passing rules that are not sufficiently expressive for brain-like dynamics. To address this, we introduce BrainDyn, a sheaf neural ordinary differential equation (neural ODE) model for continuous-time dynamics on structured brain graphs. BrainDyn encodes the recent activity history of each brain region using a long short-term memory (LSTM) model over a sliding temporal window to produce hidden states, or stalks, that are projected through learnable restriction maps into edge-specific shared spaces. Discrepancies between neighboring nodes in these shared spaces are characterized by a sheaf Laplacian that can facilitate message passing between neuronal units. The output of these messages is then fed to a neural ODE that governs the continuous-time evolution of neuronal activity. We evaluated BrainDyn on resting-state fMRI (PNC dataset), scalp EEG with focal epilepsy (TUSZ dataset), and simulated activity from the NEST spiking network simulator. BrainDyn achieves strong forecasting ability across modalities, and the resulting representations support downstream tasks including in silico perturbation prediction.

</details>


### [225] [An Exterior Method for Nonnegative Matrix Factorization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19325)
*Qiujing Lu,Tonmoy Monsoor,Ehsan Ebrahimzadeh,Kartik Sharma,Vwani Roychowdhury*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种外点优化框架eNMF，通过分离低秩逼近与非负约束，从无约束最优解出发旋转至非负象限边界，在收敛速度和重构误差上显著优于现有9种算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统NMF算法采用内点法在优化过程中强制维持非负可行性，作者发现这种约束驱动方式在非凸景观中会阻碍优化进程，导致收敛缓慢或陷入次优驻点，因此需要一种能够分离低秩逼近与非负约束的新框架。

Method: 提出外点框架eNMF：首先从最优无约束分解初始化，引入旋转过程将无约束因子映射至距离非负象限最近的外点，通过简单迭代更新收敛到满足KKT条件的边界驻点，同时揭示了因子矩阵在置换和正交变换下的等价类关系。

Result: 在3个真实数据集和2个合成数据集上，eNMF相较于9种SOTA算法和9种初始化方案（共81个对比项）全面领先：等时设置下重构误差降低最多30%，等误差设置下加速最多150%；400次实验中99%的情况下不同算法收敛到等价因子矩阵；下游音频处理和推荐任务也获得显著性能提升。

Conclusion: 外点优化框架为NMF提供了新的几何视角和算法范式，通过分离逼近与约束有效避免了内点法的局限性，在理论和实践中均展现出优越性，为NMF算法设计开辟了新方向。

Abstract: Nonnegative matrix factorization (NMF) seeks a low-rank approximation $X \approx UV^T$ with nonnegative factors and is commonly solved using interior methods that enforce feasibility throughout optimization. We show that such constraint-driven approaches can impede progress in the nonconvex landscape, leading to slow convergence or convergence to suboptimal stationary points. We propose an exterior framework for NMF (eNMF) that separates low-rank approximation from nonnegativity enforcement. Our method initializes from the optimal unconstrained factorization and introduces a rotation procedure that maps unconstrained factors to an exterior point closest to the nonnegative orthant. This viewpoint yields an algorithmic framework in which simple iterative updates converge to KKT-satisfying stationary points on the boundary of the positive orthant. The exterior formulation also enables a geometric interpretation of NMF solutions, clarifying equivalence classes of factorizations under permutation and orthogonal transformations. An intriguing numerical result, involving 400 NMF experiments across both real and synthetic datasets, show that in 99% of the cases, different algorithms tend to converge towards equivalent factor matrices. We benchmark eNMF against 9 state-of-the-art NMF algorithms with 9 initialization schemes across 3 real-world and 2 synthetic datasets. eNMF consistently outperforms all 81 competitors, achieving up to 30% lower reconstruction error under equal-time settings and up to 150% speedup under equal-error settings. The downstream experiments further demonstrate substantial performance gains in audio processing and recommendation tasks, corroborating the practical benefits of the proposed exterior optimization framework. Code is available at https://github.com/roychowdhuryresearch/eNMF

</details>


### [226] [What Makes a Representation Good for Single-Cell Perturbation Prediction?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19343)
*Wenkang Jiang,Yuhang Liu,Yichao Cai,Erdun Gao,Jiayi Dong,Ehsan Abbasnejad,Lina Yao,Javen Qinfeng Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了PerturbedVAE框架，通过显式分离显性扰动不变信息和稀疏扰动特定信号，解决了单细胞扰动建模中的信号不平衡问题，并在OOD组合预测上取得了SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法忽略了基因表达中扰动不变信息占主导地位、而扰动特定信号本质稀疏这一挑战，导致学习到的表征要么纠缠不变与特定信息造成虚假预测，要么完全抑制特定信号而无法有效预测。

Method: 提出PerturbedVAE框架，显式将扰动特定信息从主导的不变结构中分离，并恢复因果表征以有效利用这些信息进行预测；同时提供了可辨识性分析，刻画了在何种条件下可以可靠恢复稀疏扰动效应。

Result: 在广泛使用的基准数据集上，PerturbedVAE在多种评估设置下实现了最先进性能，在分布外组合预测上取得了显著提升，并揭示了可解释的扰动响应程序。

Conclusion: PerturbedVAE成功解决了单细胞扰动建模中的信号不平衡问题，为理解细胞对遗传扰动的响应提供了有效工具，具有重要的理论保证和实际应用价值。

Abstract: Single-cell perturbation modeling is fundamental for understanding and predicting cellular responses to genetic perturbations. However, existing approaches, from causal representation learning to foundation models, often struggle with an overlooked challenge: gene expression is dominated by perturbation-invariant information, while perturbation-specific signals are intrinsically sparse. As a result, learned representations either entangle invariant and perturbation-specific information, leading to spurious and non-generalizable predictors, or suppress perturbation-specific signals altogether, rendering them ineffective for prediction. To address this, we propose PerturbedVAE, a general framework designed to resolve this signal imbalance. The framework explicitly separates perturbation-specific information from dominant invariant structure and recovers causal representations to effectively utilize such information for prediction. We further provide an identifiability analysis that characterizes the conditions under which sparse perturbation effects can be reliably recovered, thereby clarifying how the framework can be concretely specified under such conditions. Empirically, PerturbedVAE achieves state-of-the-art performance on a widely used benchmark across multiple evaluation settings, yielding significant gains on out-of-distribution combinatorial predictions and uncovering interpretable perturbation-response programs.

</details>


### [227] [Accurate, Efficient, and Explainable Deep Learning Approaches for Environmental Science Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19366)
*Jimeng Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本论文开发了三种AI方法以实现环境智能：基于深度学习的洪水预测与管理模型（WaLeF和FIDLAr）、基于条件扩散模型的概率天气预报模型（CoDiCast），以及基于结构化文本立方体框架的科学问答系统（Hypercube-RAG），分别解决了传统方法计算成本高、缺乏不确定性量化以及知识检索的权衡问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 环境科学对于保护生态系统至关重要，该领域由大规模、异构数据驱动。在人工智能时代，AI已成为学习模式和辅助决策的变革性工具。然而，环境科学面临三个具体挑战：沿海河流系统的洪水预测与管理受限于传统物理模型计算密集、难以实时应用；全球天气预报面临海量数据规模挑战，传统方法确定性且计算繁重；环境科学领域的科学问答中，大语言模型常因知识过时或有限而产生幻觉，而现有检索增强生成方法难以同时兼顾准确性、效率和可解释性。

Method: 针对三个挑战分别提出解决方案：（1）提出基于深度学习的水位预测模型WaLeF和预报驱动的深度学习水位管理模型FIDLAr；（2）提出适用于概率天气预报的条件扩散模型CoDiCast，该方法改编自生成式AI以适应预测任务；（3）提出基于结构化文本立方体框架的Hypercube-RAG系统用于科学问答。所有方法均在南佛罗里达州一个以极端降雨和海平面波动为特征的洪水易发沿海系统中进行了评估。

Result: FIDLAr在准确性和效率方面均优于基线方法，同时提供可解释的输出；CoDiCast实现了准确、高效的预报，并具有显式的不确定性量化能力；Hypercube-RAG成功同时展现了准确性、效率和可解释性三种特性。

Conclusion: 本研究成功开发了针对环境科学复杂问题的AI方法，实现了环境智能。所提出的方法在各自领域均表现出优越性能：FIDLAr解决了实时洪水管理问题，CoDiCast提供了高效的概率天气预报，Hypercube-RAG实现了高质量的领域知识问答。这些成果证明了AI在处理大规模异构环境数据、支持决策制定方面的巨大潜力，为环境科学研究和实际应用提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: Environmental science plays a pivotal role in safeguarding ecosystems, a domain driven by large-scale, heterogeneous data. In the big data era, artificial intelligence (AI) has emerged as a transformative tool for learning patterns and supporting decision-making. This dissertation develops AI-based approaches tailored to complex environmental science problems to achieve Environmental Intelligence, studying three specific challenges. First, we focus on flood prediction and management in coastal river systems. Conventional physics-based models are computationally intensive, limiting real-time application. To overcome this, we propose a deep learning (DL)-based model, WaLeF, for water level forecasting, and a forecast-informed DL model, FIDLAr, to manage water levels. Evaluated in a flood-prone coastal system in South Florida characterized by extreme rainfall and sea level fluctuations, FIDLAr outperforms baselines in accuracy and efficiency while providing interpretable outputs. Second, we target global weather prediction, which is challenged by massive data scale. Traditional physics methods are deterministic and computationally heavy. We propose CoDiCast, a conditional diffusion model tailored for probabilistic weather forecasting. Adapted from generative AI for predictive tasks, experiments show CoDiCast achieves accurate, efficient forecasts with explicit uncertainty quantification. Lastly, we address scientific question-answering in environmental science. When answering in-domain questions, large language models (LLMs) often suffer from hallucinations due to out-of-date or limited knowledge. While retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) retrieves domain-specific knowledge, existing methods trade off accuracy, efficiency, or explainability. We propose Hypercube-RAG, built on a structured text cube framework, which successfully exhibits all three properties simultaneously.

</details>


### [228] [The Evaluation Game: Beyond Static LLM Benchmarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19377)
*Paul Wang,Jade Garcia-Bourrée,Anne-Marie Kermarrec,Vincent Corruble*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个博弈论框架来分析大语言模型中针对越狱攻击的鲁棒性微调，发现微调仅产生局部泛化，并指出对抗性评估基准应被视为评估者群作用下的轨道而非静态提示集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型中越狱攻击的不断发现，实践者越来越依赖微调作为防御策略，然而支撑这种鲁棒性微调的理论基础仍然缺乏探索。现有的评估方法忽略了训练者端的适应性，无法区分真正的修复与记忆化的补丁。

Method: 引入博弈论框架，将评估者（审计模型的越狱漏洞）与训练者之间的交互形式化为双人博弈。利用群作用这一数学结构来形式化表示数据增强，捕捉对称性和变换。以具有循环平移群的圆作为最简单的非平凡实例，根据训练者的泛化范围分析不同机制。

Result: 在临界阈值以下，评估者在线性轮数内保持恒定的遗漏率，而其他设置会产生截然不同的行为。对三个模型家族（Llama、Qwen和Mistral）的实验证据表明，对抗性提示的微调仅诱导局部泛化，测试样本的拒绝率与到微调提示的距离高度相关。

Conclusion: 该框架重新定义了对抗性评估的核心对象：基准不是静态的提示集，而是评估者群作用下的轨道；忽略训练者端适应性的审计协议无法区分真正的修复和记忆化的补丁。这为理解和改进大语言模型的安全性微调提供了理论基础。

Abstract: As jailbreaks, adversarially crafted inputs that bypass safety constraints, continue to be discovered in Large Language Models, practitioners increasingly rely on fine-tuning as a defensive strategy. Yet the theoretical foundations underlying this robustness fine-tuning remain underexplored. We introduce a game-theoretic framework in which the interaction between an evaluator (auditing the model for jailbreaks) and a trainer is formalized as a two-player game.
  A key feature of our approach is the use of group actions, a mathematical structure that captures symmetries and transformations, to formally represent data augmentation. The simplest non-trivial instance is the circle with cyclic translation groups, where we exhibit various regimes depending on the trainer's generalization range. Below a critical threshold, the evaluator maintains a constant miss ratio for linearly many rounds, whereas other settings can yield very different behaviors. We further provide empirical evidence supporting locality-dependence of the model: for the three model families we tested (Llama, Qwen and Mistral), we have significant evidence that fine-tuning on adversarial prompts induces only local generalization, with refusal rates on test examples highly correlated with the distance to the fine-tuning prompts. Our framework recasts the central object of adversarial evaluation: a benchmark is not a static set of prompts but an orbit under the evaluator's group action, and audit protocols that ignore trainer-side adaptation cannot distinguish a genuine fix from a memorized patch.

</details>


### [229] [Understanding Dynamics of Adam in Zero-Sum Games: An ODE Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19392)
*Yi Feng,Weiming Ou,Xiao Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文推导了Adam-DA在零和对博弈中的连续时间极限ODE，发现其一阶和二阶动量参数的作用与最小化问题中完全相反，并通过GAN实验验证了这一发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Adam优化器在训练神经网络中取得了巨大成功，其对应的博弈版本Adam-DA在零和对博弈中被广泛应用，但目前对Adam-DA缺乏严格的理论理解，尤其是对其动量参数在博弈环境下的行为尚不清楚。

Method: 推导出Adam-DA的连续时间极限常微分方程(ODEs)，这些ODE能紧密逼近Adam-DA的离散时间动态，提供了一个易于分析的框架；基于此ODE方法研究Adam-DA的局部收敛性和隐式梯度正则化。

Result: 理论分析揭示，在零和对博弈中，一阶和二阶动量参数的作用与它们在最小化问题中的已知效应完全相反；通过多个架构和数据集上的GAN实验验证了这些预测。

Conclusion: 该研究为理解Adam-DA在零和对博弈中的行为提供了新的理论视角，明确指出了动量参数在博弈优化中作用的逆转现象，对实际应用中Adam-DA的参数调节具有重要指导意义。

Abstract: The remarkable success of the Adam in training neural networks has naturally led to the widespread use of its descent-ascent counterpart, Adam-DA, for solving zero-sum games. Despite its popularity in practice, a rigorous theoretical understanding of Adam-DA still lags behind. In this paper, we derive ordinary differential equations (ODEs) that serve as continuous-time limits of the Adam-DA. These ODEs closely approximate the discrete-time dynamics of Adam-DA, providing a tractable analytical framework for understanding its behavior in zero-sum games. Using this ODE approach, we investigate two fundamental aspects of Adam-DA: local convergence and implicit gradient regularization. Our analysis reveals that the roles of the first- and second-order momentum parameters in zero-sum games are exactly the opposite of their well-documented effects in minimization problems. We validate these predictions through GAN experiments across multiple architectures and datasets, demonstrating the practical implications of this reversed momentum effect.

</details>


### [230] [TIDE: Asymmetric Neural Circuits for Stabilized Temporal Inhibitory-Excitatory Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19403)
*Alexander Kyuroson,Denis Kleyko,Marcus Liwicki*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了TIDE架构，利用非对称兴奋-抑制(E-I)网络对神经动力学进行建模，在ImageNet上准确率提升1.65%，训练时间减少超过50%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期的连续思维机(CTM)架构虽通过神经动力学将内部计算与外部输入解耦，但其依赖的多层感知机缺乏稳定性保证。为了解决这一问题，作者提出利用非对称兴奋-抑制(E-I)网络来建模神经动力学。

Method: 提出了时序抑制-兴奋动力学引擎(TIDE)，这是一种受神经科学启发的架构。该模型结合Wilson-Cowan动力学和侧抑制来稳定神经动力学计算内部表示；通过分层感受野和Dale原则确保80:20的E-I平衡比例，在保证生物真实性的同时实现端到端训练。

Result: TIDE在ImageNet数据集的各种扰动测试中，top-1准确率平均提升1.65%；训练时间减少至CTM的50%以下。论文还提供了收敛性、稳定性和复杂度界限的理论证明及消融实验。

Conclusion: TIDE成功将神经科学原理融入深度学习架构，在保证理论稳定性的前提下，显著提升了训练效率和模型性能，为神经启发式学习提供了新的研究方向。

Abstract: Recent Continuous Thought Machine architecture decouples internal computation from external inputs via neural dynamics, but relies on multi-layer perceptrons without stability guarantees. We propose to model neural dynamics using asymmetric Excitatory-Inhibitory (E-I) networks, which can be stabilized via principles from network theory and can be expressed as energy-based systems optimized through a game-theoretic loss. Building on this perspective, we introduce Temporal Inhibitory-Excitatory Dynamic Engine (TIDE), a neuro-inspired architecture that computes internal representations through neural dynamics stabilized by incorporating the Wilson-Cowan dynamics and lateral inhibition. TIDE balances biological realism by, for instance, using Hierarchical Receptive Fields and enforcing Dale's principle to ensure a realistic $80:20$ E-I balance ratio with an end-to-end trainable architecture.
