The aim of this paper is to introduce a new architecture that brings neuro-inspired learning to the forefront. We present proofs of convergence, stability, and complexity bounds, along with empirical ablation studies. Overall, TIDE surpasses CTM with under $50\%$ of the training time and improves $\texttt{top-1}$ accuracy by an average of $+1.65\%$ on ImageNet under various perturbations.

</details>


### [231] [A Bitter Lesson for Data Filtering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19407)
*Christopher Mohri,John Duchi,Tatsunori Hashimoto*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在高计算资源、数据稀缺的预训练场景下，研究表明最佳数据过滤策略是不使用过滤器；充分训练的大模型不仅能容忍低质量和干扰数据，反而能从中受益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对大模型预训练中的数据过滤问题，挑战了“必须过滤低质量数据”这一普遍认知，旨在探索在高计算、数据稀缺场景下数据过滤的真实作用。

Method: 通过设计针对高计算、数据稀缺场景的新型扩展律研究，对比有无数据过滤的模型表现，分析模型对低质量和干扰数据的容忍度与学习效果。

Result: 在计算资源充足时，最佳数据过滤策略是不使用过滤器；充分训练的大参数模型不仅能够容忍低质量和干扰数据，而且实际上能从名义上的“劣质”数据中获益。

Conclusion: 数据过滤并非大模型预训练的必要环节，在高计算条件下，保留包含低质量信息的完整数据集反而有助于提升模型性能，需重新审视数据过滤在预训练中的作用。

Abstract: We investigate data filtering for large model pretraining via new scaling studies that target the high compute, data-scarce regime. In spite of an apparently common belief that filtering data to include only high-quality information is essential, our experiments suggest that with enough compute, the best data filter is no data filter. We find that sufficiently trained large parameter models not only tolerate low-quality and distractor data, but in fact benefit from nominally ``poor'' data.

</details>


### [232] [Unlocking the Potential of Continual Model Merging: An ODE Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19409)
*Lihong Lin,Haidong Kang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了ODE-M方法，通过ODE驱动持续模型合并，在参数空间中追踪低损失连接路径并施加屏障约束，解决了现有方法在异构任务重要性场景下的严重遗忘问题，实现了最先进的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有持续模型合并方法缺乏对学习容量分配的显式可控性，在顺序合并任务时会累积严重遗忘问题，特别是在异构任务重要性场景下性能分配高度不一致。根本原因在于现有方法将每个任务模型视为孤立的参数点并应用固定代数组合，而非显式构建尊重独立训练模型在参数空间中如何连接的转换。

Method: 受模式连通性启发，假设理想的合并模型位于低损失连接路径上，持续合并应遵循此类路径而不跨越导致遗忘的损失屏障。提出ODE驱动的合并方法(ODE-M)，通过积分时间依赖的速度场并强制执行屏障约束来防止损失增加步骤，从而追踪这样的路径。

Result: 大量实验表明，ODE-M在主流持续模型合并基准测试中相比竞争对手达到了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: 通过显式构建参数空间中独立训练模型之间的连接转换，并遵循低损失路径进行持续合并，ODE-M有效解决了现有方法的遗忘问题，为持续模型合并提供了更优的解决方案。

Abstract: Continual Model Merging (CMM) enables rapid customization of foundation models across sequentially arriving tasks, offering a scalable alternative to repeated retraining. However, existing merging rules lack explicit controllability over the allocation of learning capacity between previously learned capabilities and newly merged models. Consequently, as tasks are merged sequentially, this deficiency accumulates into severe forgetting, particularly in scenarios with heterogeneous task importance, where performance allocation becomes highly inconsistent. The key reason can be attributed to the fact that previous methods treat each task model as an isolated parameter point and apply fixed algebraic combinations, rather than explicitly constructing a transition that respects how independently trained models can be connected in parameter space. Motivated by mode connectivity, we assume that desirable merged models lie on low loss connecting paths, and that continual merging should follow such paths without crossing loss barriers that induce forgetting. Grounded in these insights, we propose a novel ODE-driven Merging (ODE-M) tailored for CMM that traces such a path by integrating a time-dependent velocity field and enforcing barrier constraints to prevent loss-increasing steps. Extensive experiments demonstrate that ODE-M achieves state-of-the-art performance compared to its competitors across mainstream CMM benchmarks.

</details>


### [233] [When to Stop Reusing: Dynamic Gradient Gating for Sample-Efficient RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19425)
*Yuchun Miao,Sen Zhang,Yuqi Zhang,Yaorui Shi,Qi Gu,Xunliang Cai,Lefei Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现了RLVR中重复利用rollout数据导致性能下降与lm_head权重突变同步的现象(DWD)，据此提出动态梯度门控(DGG)方法，通过实时监控lm_head梯度范数来拦截有害梯度，实现了最高2.93倍的样本效率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习与可验证奖励(RLVR)是提升大语言模型推理能力的主流范式，但rollout样本获取成本高昂。虽然重复利用每个rollout批次进行多次梯度更新是经典RL的标准做法，但在RLVR中这会放大策略偏移，导致严重的性能下降，而如何早期检测并停止重用仍是一个开放问题。

Method: 研究首先识别并验证了"不成比例权重发散"(DWD)现象：性能下降与lm_head权重变化的急剧激增同步，而中间层保持稳定。理论上证明了有害梯度集中在lm_head且lm_head梯度范数是策略发散的下界。基于此洞察，提出了动态梯度门控(DGG)方法，实时监控lm_head梯度范数，在有害梯度破坏优化器之前进行拦截。

Result: DWD现象在多种LLM和任务中一致出现；理论上证明lm_head梯度范数可作为灾难性策略偏移的实时信号；DGG方法在数学、ALFWorld、WebShop和搜索增强QA任务中始终匹配或超越标准的单次使用基线，实现了最高2.93倍的样本效率和2.14倍的时钟加速。

Conclusion: 本研究成功解决了RLVR中rollout数据重用导致性能下降的早期检测难题，建立了lm_head梯度范数作为原则性的实时监控信号，所提出的DGG方法为提升RLVR样本效率提供了有效的轻量级解决方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) has become the dominant paradigm for advanced reasoning in Large Language Models (LLMs), but rollout samples are expensive to obtain, making sample efficiency a critical bottleneck. A natural remedy is to reuse each rollout batch for multiple gradient updates, a standard practice in classical RL. Yet in RLVR, this amplifies policy shift, leading to severe performance degradation. Detecting the onset of degradation early enough to stop reuse remains an open and challenging problem. We close this gap by identifying the \textit{Disproportionate Weight Divergence (DWD)} phenomenon: performance degradation is synchronized with a sharp surge in the \texttt{lm\_head} weight change, while intermediate layers remain stable. Empirically, we verify that DWD emerges consistently across diverse LLMs and tasks. Theoretically, we prove that (i) harmful gradients concentrate at the \texttt{lm\_head} while intermediate layers are structurally attenuated, and (ii) the \texttt{lm\_head} gradient norm lower-bounds the policy divergence. These results establish the \texttt{lm\_head} gradient norm as a principled, real-time signal of catastrophic policy shift. Guided by this insight, we propose \textit{Dynamic Gradient Gating (DGG)}, a lightweight intervention that monitors the \texttt{lm\_head} gradient norm in real time and intercepts harmful gradients before they corrupt the optimizer. DGG consistently matches or exceeds the standard single-use baseline, achieving up to $2.93\times$ sample efficiency and $2.14\times$ wall-clock speedup across math, ALFWorld, WebShop, and search-augmented QA tasks.

</details>


### [234] [CEPO: RLVR Self-Distillation using Contrastive Evidence Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19436)
*Ahmed Heakl,Abdelrahman M. Shaker,Youssef Mohamed,Rania Elbadry,Omar Fetouh,Fahad Shahbaz Khan,Salman Khan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CEPO提出对比证据策略优化方法，通过同时利用正确答案和错误答案作为教师信号，解决了RLVR中token奖励分配不均的问题，在多模态数学推理任务上显著优于GRPO基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在可验证奖励强化学习（RLVR）中，模型生成正确解时所有token都接收相同的奖励信号，无法区分关键推理步骤和语法填充词。现有方法通过条件化正确答案来识别token，但要么会将答案泄露到梯度中破坏训练，要么产生的信号太弱无法有效区分关键步骤。

Method: 提出对比证据策略优化（CEPO）：对每个token提出更尖锐的问题——"正确答案是否支持该token，同时错误答案是否反对该token？"满足两个条件的token是真正的推理步骤，都不满足的则是填充词。错误答案教师由训练批次中已拒绝的rollout构建，无需额外采样成本。方法继承了现有最先进方法的结构安全性保证，同时严格提升关键token的信用分配。

Result: 在五个多模态数学推理基准测试中，2B和4B规模模型分别达到43.43%和60.56%的平均准确率，在相同训练预算下优于GRPO的41.17%和57.43%。分布匹配自蒸馏方法（OPSD、SDPO）表现低于未训练基线，实证验证了理论预测的信息泄露问题。

Conclusion: CEPO通过对比正确和错误答案的教师信号，成功解决了token级别信用分配问题，理论证明其具有严格的安全保证且在填充token处改进恰好消失，实证结果表明其显著优于现有方法。

Abstract: When a model produces a correct solution under reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR), every token receives the same reward signal regardless of whether it was a decisive reasoning step or a grammatical filler. A natural fix is to condition the model on the correct answer as a teacher, identifying tokens it would have generated differently had it known the answer. Prior work shows this either corrupts training by leaking the answer into the gradient, or produces a weak signal that cannot distinguish decisive steps from filler, since both look equally surprising relative to the model's baseline. We propose Contrastive Evidence Policy Optimization (CEPO), which asks a sharper question at every token: not just "does the correct answer favor this token?" but "does the correct answer favor it while the wrong answer disfavors it?" A token satisfying both is a genuine reasoning step; one satisfying neither is filler. The wrong-answer teacher is constructed from rejected rollouts already in the training batch, incurring no additional sampling cost. We prove CEPO inherits all structural safety guarantees of the prior state of the art while strictly sharpening credit at decisive tokens, with the improvement vanishing exactly at filler positions. Empirically, CEPO achieves 43.43% and 60.56% average accuracy across five multimodal mathematical reasoning benchmarks at 2B and 4B scale, respectively, versus 41.17% and 57.43% for GRPO under identical training budgets. Distribution-matching self-distillation methods (OPSD, SDPO) fall below the untrained baseline, empirically confirming the information leakage our theory predicts. Our code is available at https://github.com/ahmedheakl/CEPO.

</details>


### [235] [When the Majority Votes Wrong, the Intervention Timing for Test-Time Reinforcement Learning Hides in the Extinction Window](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19444)
*Hongxiang Lin,Zhirui Kuai,Erpeng Xue,Lei Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示TTRL的伪标签信号存在系统性误判，提出“正确答案灭绝窗口”现象，并设计了TTRL-Guard框架来缓解该问题，在多个模型和基准测试上显著提升了准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者发现测试时强化学习（TTRL）在数学推理基准上报告的准确率提升被系统性误读：多数提升仅是对已解决问题答案的锐化，而非真正的学习；同时，因多数投票锁定错误答案而导致正确答案被永久压制的“正确答案灭绝窗口”现象，使得从正确变为错误的问题数量超过了真正学会的问题数量，且这种损害不可逆。

Method: 提出TTRL-Guard轻量级框架，包含三个针对灭绝窗口的机制：1) 翻转率感知奖励缩放（FRS），随着翻转率（FR）下降降低高风险更新的权重；2) 少数保留采样（MPS），保留来自少数正确答案的梯度信号；3) 风险条件化稀疏更新（RCSU），对极化问题暂停更新。

Result: 在三个模型和四个基准测试上的实验表明，TTRL-Guard在Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct和Qwen3-4B上取得了最佳的平均pass@1成绩；相较于TTRL，在AIME 2025上相对提升了+54%。

Conclusion: TTRL-Guard通过识别并缓解正确答案灭绝窗口问题，有效防止了测试时强化学习中的不可逆损害，显著提升了模型在复杂数学推理任务上的性能表现。

Abstract: Test-time reinforcement learning (TTRL) reports substantial accuracy gains on mathematical reasoning benchmarks using majority vote as a pseudo-label signal. We argue these gains are systematically misinterpreted: most reflect sharpening of already-solvable problems rather than genuine learning, while problems corrupted from correct to incorrect outnumber truly learned ones, and this damage is irreversible once majority vote locks onto a wrong answer. Per-problem tracking reveals that correct-answer signals in low-ability problems are briefly active before being permanently suppressed, a phenomenon we term the \textit{Correct-Answer Extinction Window}, with Flip Rate (FR) as its leading indicator. We thus propose \textbf{TTRL-Guard}, a lightweight framework with three mechanisms targeting the extinction window: Flip-Rate-Aware Reward Scaling (FRS) down-weights at-risk updates as FR declines, Minority-Preserving Sampling (MPS) retains gradient signal from minority correct answers, and Risk-Conditioned Sparse Updatings (RCSU) suspends updates on polarized problems. Experiments across three models and four benchmarks show that TTRL-Guard achieves the best average pass@1 on Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct and Qwen3-4B, improves relatively over TTRL by +54\% on AIME 2025. \footnote{Our code and implementation details are available at https://github.com/linhxkkkk/TTRL-Guard.

</details>


### [236] [Implicit Bias of Mirror Flow in Homogeneous Neural Networks: Sparse and Dense Feature Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19458)
*Tom Jacobs,Guido Montufar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了深度齐次神经网络中镜像流（mirror flow）的最大边际解，推导了平衡方程并建立了收敛率分析，揭示了不同镜像映射如何统一稀疏与密集特征学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典研究主要集中在梯度流上，而镜像流作为一种更广泛的优化框架，其在深度神经网络中的最大边际解特性尚不明确。作者旨在推广经典结果，理解镜像映射如何影响优化动态和特征学习的几何性质。

Method: 利用凸对偶性推导了镜像流的平衡方程，刻画了控制边际的“地平线函数”。在此基础上，建立了最大边际表征、收敛速率分析以及范数增长估计，并通过合成数据集和标准视觉任务进行实验验证。

Result: 发现：（1）不同的非齐次镜像映射可以诱导相同的最大边际解；（2）收敛速度可能极慢，存在指数级慢收敛的情况；（3）尽管所有镜像映射都具有特征学习能力，它们可以产生从稀疏到密集截然不同的神经元激活表示。

Conclusion: 研究结果为齐次神经网络中的稀疏和密集特征学习提供了统一视角，突显了镜像映射在塑造优化动力学和学习分类器几何结构中的关键作用。

Abstract: We study the max-margin solutions reached by mirror flow in deep neural networks with homogeneous activation functions. Extending classical results on gradient flow, we derive a novel balance equation for mirror flow from convex duality, enabling a characterization of the horizon function governing the induced margin. We further establish max-margin characterizations together with convergence rates and norm growth estimates. Finally, we support our theory through experiments on synthetic datasets and standard vision tasks. Concretely, we show that: (1) distinct non-homogeneous mirror maps can induce the same max-margin solution; (2) convergence can be extremely slow, including exponentially slow regimes; and (3) although all considered mirror maps exhibit feature learning, they can produce markedly different representations, ranging from sparse to dense neuron activations. Together, these results provide a unified perspective on sparse and dense feature learning in homogeneous neural networks, highlighting how mirror maps shape both optimization dynamics and the geometry of the learned classifiers.

</details>


### [237] [Quantifying the Pre-training Dividend: Generative versus Latent Self-Supervised Learning for Time Series Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19462)
*Noam Major,Kathy Razmadze,Yoli Shavit*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了时间序列自监督学习(SSL)的"预训练红利"，发现SSL对异常检测和分类任务提升高达375%，但对预测任务收益甚微，揭示了SSL表征效用的非通用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前时间序列自监督学习研究主要集中在生成范式和预测任务，缺乏对学习表征在多样化时序任务中广泛效用的量化评估，需要建立控制框架系统评估SSL预训练红利。

Method: 建立控制框架系统比较生成范式与潜在对齐架构，引入LeJEPA和DINO的时间序列适配版本，利用离散小波变换(DWT)增强强制局部波动不变性。

Result: 预训练红利高度不对称：SSL对异常检测和分类提升高达375%，但对预测任务收益有限；表征效用非通用，受精度-不变性权衡约束；表征质量独立于数据来源，在中等架构深度趋于饱和。

Conclusion: SSL表征效用依赖于任务所需信号分辨率与目标的匹配，这为通过大规模合成生成扩展SSL应用指明了方向。

Abstract: The success of self-supervised learning (SSL) in vision and NLP has motivated its rapid adoption for time series. However, research has focused primarily on Generative paradigms and forecasting tasks, leaving the broader utility of learned representations unquantified. We establish a controlled framework to evaluate the "pre-training dividend": the value added by SSL across diverse temporal tasks. We systematically compare Generative paradigms against Latent Alignment architectures, introducing adaptations of LeJEPA and DINO for time series. These adaptations utilize Discrete Wavelet Transform (DWT) augmentations to enforce invariance to local fluctuations. Our analysis reveals that the pre-training dividend is highly asymmetric: SSL yields gains of up to 375% for anomaly detection and classification, yet remains marginal for forecasting. We demonstrate that representational utility is non-universal, governed by a precision-invariance trade-off where the specific signal resolution required by the task must align with the objective. Finally, we show that representation quality is largely independent of data origin and saturates at moderate architectural depths, suggesting a path to scaling via massive synthetic generation. Our code is available at: https://github.com/noammajor/Models

</details>


### [238] [Sampling-Based Safe Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19469)
*Luca Vignola,Bruce D. Lee,Manish Prajapat,Manuel Wendl,Melanie Zeilinger,Andreas Krause,Yarden As*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了SBSRL算法，这是一种基于模型的RL算法，通过在有限的动力学样本集上联合执行约束来确保学习过程的安全性，并且提供了高概率安全性保证和有限样本复杂度界限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习中的安全探索问题限制了其在真实世界中的部署，因为传统方法难以在保持安全性的同时高效学习。

Method: 提出基于采样的安全强化学习（SBSRL）算法，在有限的动力学样本集上联合执行约束，近似处理不确定动力学下的难解最坏情况优化问题；引入基于约束认知不确定性的探索策略，无需显式的探索奖励。

Result: 理论上推导了学习过程中的高概率安全性保证和有限样本复杂度界限；实验上在仿真和真实机器人硬件上实现了安全高效的探索，并扩展到高维连续控制问题。

Conclusion: SBSRL成功解决了强化学习中的安全探索问题，提供了理论安全保证并在实际应用中表现出色，能够扩展到高维连续控制问题。

Abstract: Safe exploration remains a fundamental challenge in reinforcement learning (RL), limiting the deployment of RL agents in the real world. We propose Sampling-Based Safe Reinforcement Learning (SBSRL), a model-based RL algorithm that maintains safety throughout the learning process by enforcing constraints jointly across a finite set of dynamics samples. This formulation approximates an intractable worst-case optimization over uncertain dynamics and enables practical safety guarantees in continuous domains. We further introduce an exploration strategy based on constraining epistemic uncertainty, eliminating the need for explicit exploration bonuses. Under regularity conditions, we derive high-probability guarantees of safety throughout learning and a finite-time sample complexity bound for recovering a near-optimal policy. Empirically, SBSRL achieves safe and efficient exploration both in simulation and in real robotic hardware, and readily extends to practical deep-ensemble implementations that scale to high-dimensional continuous control problems.

</details>


### [239] [Adynamical systems view of training generativemodels and the memorization phenomenon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19483)
*Siva Athreya,Chiranjib Bhattacharya,Vivek S. Borkar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从动力系统视角出发，结合两时间尺度动态和坍缩现象的理论分析，解释了生成模型训练过程中的记忆化现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成模型在训练过程中会出现记忆化现象，即在较长时间内对不同输入产生相同或相似的输出。现有文献缺乏从动态系统角度对该现象及其与双下降、坍缩等现象相互关系的系统解释，因此需要建立统一的理论框架来理解这些现象。

Method: 基于Austin [2016]的结果构建损失函数的风格化模型，其中损失函数对部分变量具有强依赖性而对其他变量具有弱依赖性，从而在常数步长SGD中自然形成两个不同的时间尺度。结合Borkar [2025a]关于SGD中坍缩现象的数学模型，利用Azizian et al. [2024]的最新结果对常数步长SGD进行分析。

Result: 成功从纯动态角度解释了生成模型的记忆化现象，揭示了记忆化与双下降、坍缩等现象之间的内在联系，为理解这些机器学习现象提供了新的动力系统视角。

Conclusion: 通过动力系统理论可以统一解释生成模型训练中的多种现象，包括记忆化、双下降和坍缩，这些现象之间存在深层的相互关联，为深入理解生成模型的训练动态提供了新视角。

Abstract: Using recent works of one of the authors (VSB) on collapse in generative models and two time scale dynamics in stochastic gradient descent in high dimensions, we give a system theoretic explanation of the memorization phenomenon in generative models. This relies purely on the dynamic aspects of the training phase. Specifically, we use a result of Austin [2016] to motivate a stylized model for the loss function for stochastic gradient descent (SGD) wherein the loss function has a strong dependence on some variables and weak dependence on the rest in a precise sense. This naturally leads to two distinct time scales in the constant step size SGD that is commonly used in machine learning. This fact has been used to explain the double descent phenomenon in SGD in Borkar [2026]. In conjunction with a mathematical model for collapse phenomenon in SGD developed in Borkar [2025a], we analyze the constant step size SGD using the recent results of Azizian et al. [2024] in order to explain the phenomenon of memorization wherein a generative model that is concurrently being tuned yields the same or similar outputs for significant stretches of time. This gives a novel perspective on the aforementioned phenomena reported in machine learning literature and their interrelationships, using a dynamical systems viewpoint.

</details>


### [240] [The Silent Hyperparameter: Quantifying the Impact of Inference Backends on LLM Reproducibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19537)
*David Pape,Jonathan Evertz,Lea Schönherr*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究揭示了推理后端的选择会导致LLM基准测试结果出现高达16.6个百分点的差异，是影响模型评估可复现性的关键但常被忽视的因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM进展主要通过标准化基准测试衡量，分数差异极小；同时为降低计算成本，广泛采用了包含系统级优化的专用推理后端。作者旨在探究这些系统级优化是否会影响token概率并引入非确定性，从而导致生成结果的偏差。

Method: 首先调研推理生态，识别出200种引擎并分析35,000篇ML论文；随后在保持模型权重、解码参数和硬件不变的前提下，对vLLM、SGLang、llama.cpp等五种主流推理引擎进行系统性实证研究，通过隔离优化项和追踪执行流水线来定位差异根源。

Result: 推理后端的选择可导致基准测试分数发生高达16.6个百分点的偏移，并引发高比例的输出不一致。这种差异主要由前缀缓存、CUDA图、自定义内核以及引擎特定的对数几率处理默认设置等系统级优化驱动。

Conclusion: 推理后端是LLM评估中一个此前未被报告但影响重大的“超参数”。研究强烈呼吁在基准测试比较中标准化报告推理堆栈信息，以提高结果的可复现性和可解释性。

Abstract: Progress in LLMs is increasingly measured through standardized benchmarks, where state-of-the-art improvements are often separated by fractions of a percentage point. At the same time, the computational cost of evaluating modern LLMs has driven widespread adoption of specialized inference backends, software systems that execute trained models efficiently at inference time. While critical for scalability, system-level optimizations, such as custom CUDA kernels and reduced-precision arithmetic, can alter token probabilities and introduce non-determinism, possibly cascading into divergent generation. In this work, we first survey the inference landscape, identifying 200 distinct engines, and analyze 35,000 ML publications, finding that the specific inference stack is rarely reported despite this widespread diversity. We then present a systematic empirical study of how inference backends affect LLM benchmark results. Holding model weights, decoding parameters, and hardware constant, we evaluate five widely used inference engines, including vLLM, SGLang, and llama.cpp, across multiple open-weight models and established benchmarks. We show that the choice of backend alone can shift benchmark scores by up to 16.6 percentage points and induce high rates of output disagreement. By isolating backend optimizations and tracing the execution pipeline, we find this divergence is driven by system-level optimizations like prefix caching and CUDA graphs, custom kernels, and engine-specific defaults in logit processing. Our findings identify the inference backend as a previously unreported but consequential hyperparameter in the evaluation of LLM and advocate standardized reporting of inference stacks to improve the reproducibility and interpretability of benchmark comparisons.

</details>


### [241] [TORQ: Two-Level Orthogonal Rotation for MXFP4 Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19561)
*Zukang Xu,Xing Hu,Dawei Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了TORQ框架，一种无需训练的后训练量化方法，通过宏观和微观两级正交旋转解决MXFP4格式量化大语言模型时的结构性不平衡问题，显著提升了4位浮点量化的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MXFP4格式虽然能为下一代低位推理提供高动态范围和硬件效率，但直接应用于LLM激活量化会导致严重的精度下降。研究发现根本原因在于激活分布与MXFP4块浮点格式之间存在两种结构性不平衡：极端的块间方差不平衡和块内码本利用率不平衡。

Method: 提出TORQ（Two-level Orthogonal Rotation for MXFP4 Quantization）框架，通过最优坐标变换重塑激活空间的几何特性：宏观层面利用Schur-Horn定理通过块间正交旋转重新分配激活能量，防止高方差块推高共享缩放因子；微观层面采用最大熵引导的块内旋转缓解码本坍塌，最大化MXFP4码本的信息容量。

Result: 在LLaMA3和Qwen3等主流LLM上实验表明，TORQ显著优于现有方法。在Qwen3-32B上，WikiText困惑度降至8.43（BF16为7.61），平均准确率从直接RTN的38.40%提升至73.63%（BF16为74.82%），大幅缩小了4位浮点量化与全精度推理之间的差距。

Conclusion: TORQ成功解决了MXFP4激活量化中的结构性不平衡问题，无需训练即可显著提升量化精度，使4位浮点量化的性能接近全精度推理，为LLM的实际部署提供了有效的低位量化解决方案。

Abstract: As Large Language Models (LLMs) advance toward practical deployment, the Microscaling FP4 (MXFP4) format has emerged as a cornerstone for next-generation low-bit inference, owing to its ability to balance high dynamic range with hardware efficiency. However, directly applying MXFP4 to LLM activation quantization inevitably leads to significant accuracy degradation. In this paper, we theoretically analyze the error structure of MXFP4 activation quantization, revealing that the root cause of this performance drop lies in two structural imbalances between activation distributions and the MXFP4 block floating-point format: (1) extreme inter-block variance imbalance and (2) intra-block codebook utilization imbalance. To address these challenges, we propose TORQ (Two-level Orthogonal Rotation for MXFP4 Quantization), a training-free Post-Training Quantization (PTQ) framework designed to reshape the geometric properties of the activation space through optimal coordinate transformations. At the macroscopic level, TORQ leverages the Schur-Horn theorem to redistribute activation energy via inter-block orthogonal rotation, preventing high-variance blocks from driving up shared scaling factors and thereby preserving the precision of small-magnitude elements. At the microscopic level, TORQ employs maximum-entropy-guided intra-block rotation to alleviate codebook collapse and maximize the MXFP4 codebook's information capacity. Experiments on mainstream LLMs such as LLaMA3 and Qwen3 show that TORQ significantly improves the accuracy of MXFP4 activation quantization compared to existing methods: on Qwen3-32B, the perplexity on WikiText is reduced to 8.43 (vs. 7.61 for BF16), and the average accuracy increases from 38.40% with direct RTN to 73.63% (vs. 74.82% for BF16), substantially narrowing the gap between 4-bit floating-point quantization and full-precision inference.

</details>


### [242] [Online Market Making and the Value of Observing the Order Book](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19584)
*Davide Maran,Marcello Restelli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究在线做市问题，提出了一种基于动作相关反馈的新型模型。在随机设定下达到O(√T)遗憾界，在对抗性设定下达到O(T^{2/3})遗憾界，证明了观测限价订单簿信息的价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在线学习研究假设完全审查反馈，而现实限价订单簿中存在不同情况：交易发生时交易者估值隐藏，未交易时揭示供需信息。这种额外的动作相关信息可能根本改变问题的可学习性。

Method: 提出消除型算法处理随机i.i.d.市场价格；扩展至均值回归价格过程（包括局部自回归动态和基于累积偏差的全局漂移条件）；设计探索-扰动算法处理对抗性设定；开发新的集中不等式。

Result: 随机设定下无需交易者估值分布平滑性假设即可达到高概率O(√T)遗憾界；均值回归价格过程下同样达到O(√T)遗憾界；对抗性设定下达到期望O(T^{2/3})遗憾界。

Conclusion: 量化了观测订单簿在在线做市中的价值，证明即使有限、动作相关的反馈也能显著改善相比标准强盗反馈模型的遗憾保证。

Abstract: We study an online market-making problem in which a learner sequentially posts bid and ask prices for a single asset while interacting with traders holding private valuations. Unlike existing online learning formulations that assume fully censored feedback, we introduce an action-dependent feedback model inspired by real limit order books: when a trade occurs, the trader's valuation remains hidden, whereas when no trade occurs, informative feedback about supply and demand is revealed.
  We show that this additional information fundamentally changes the learnability of the problem. In the stochastic setting with i.i.d. market prices, we propose an elimination-based algorithm that achieves $O(\sqrt T)$ regret with high probability, without requiring any smoothness assumptions on the distribution of trader valuations. We then extend this result to a broad class of mean-reverting price processes by considering both local, autoregressive dynamics and a weaker global drift condition based on cumulative deviations from the mean. Under either assumption, we establish high-probability $O(\sqrt T)$ regret bounds, relying on a new concentration inequality of independent interest. Finally, in the adversarial setting with oblivious prices, we design an explore-then-perturb algorithm that guarantees $O(T^{2/3})$ regret in expectation.
  Our results quantify the value of observing the order book in online market making and demonstrate that even limited, action-dependent feedback can substantially improve regret guarantees compared to standard bandit feedback models.

</details>


### [243] [Physics-Informed Graph Neural Network Surrogates for Turbulent Nanoparticle Dispersion in Dental Clinical Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19589)
*Takshak Shende,Viktor Popov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ELGIN图神经网络代理模型，将牙科气溶胶扩散的CFD模拟加速37倍，同时提高预测精度，为实现实时临床感染风险评估奠定基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 牙科气溶胶操作产生的亚50微米颗粒可在封闭诊室内长期悬浮，构成空气传播病原体传播途径。传统RANS模拟结合欧拉-拉格朗日粒子追踪虽然准确，但每个场景需要极长运行时间，无法支持实时3D临床决策。

Method: 提出ELGIN（欧拉-拉格朗日图交互网络），这是一种物理信息图代理模型：将多头图Transformer与雅可比预条件可学习压力投影和湍流闭合头耦合，通过可微逆距离网格-粒子耦合连接到sigmoid门控拉格朗日交互网络，使用辛Störmer-Verlet积分器推进粒子。采用四阶段物理信息课程学习稳定260步自回归推演，避免梯度爆炸。使用foam-extend 4.1 OpenFOAM reactingParcelFoam在临床相关通风率和手机喷雾速度下生成CFD真值。

Result: 在单案例演示（20案例扫描中的Case_03）上，ELGIN相比纯拉格朗日基线(M0)表现更优：平均粒子位移误差从房间宽度的19.56%降至16.20%，回转半径误差从9.85%降至6.58%。在4GB GPU上26秒推演仅需约64秒完成，比foam-extend参考流程快约37倍。

Conclusion: ELGIN成功实现了高保真、快速的牙科气溶胶传输预测，在保持精度的同时大幅提升计算效率，为未来完成多案例训练后实现每次预约的感染风险筛查铺平道路。

Abstract: Dental aerosol procedures produce sub-50 micrometre nuclei that can remain airborne for long periods in enclosed clinics, creating pathways for airborne pathogen transmission. Reynolds-Averaged Navier-Stokes (RANS) simulations with Euler-Lagrange particle tracking capture this transport accurately but require very long run times per scenario, which precludes real-time clinical decision support in 3D. We present the Eulerian-Lagrangian Graph Interaction Network (ELGIN), a physics-informed graph surrogate that jointly predicts carrier-flow dynamics on the OpenFOAM polyhedral mesh and the per-parcel motion of the polydisperse spray cloud. ELGIN couples a multi-head Graph Transformer with Jacobi-preconditioned learnable pressure projection and a turbulence-closure head to a sigmoid-gated Lagrangian Interaction Network through differentiable inverse-distance mesh-parcel coupling, and advances parcels with a symplectic Stormer-Verlet integrator. A four-stage physics-informed curriculum stabilises 260-step autoregressive rollouts without gradient explosion. A parameter sweep with foam-extend 4.1 OpenFOAM reactingParcelFoam across clinically relevant ventilation rates and handpiece spray speeds provides CFD ground truth. This article reports a single-case demonstration in which both ELGIN and a Lagrangian-only baseline (M0) are trained and evaluated on Sweep_Case_03 of a twenty-case sweep; full 16/2/2 retraining is in progress and will replace all reported metrics. On this case, ELGIN tracks the foam-extend particle cloud much more closely than M0: mean parcel displacement error falls from 19.56% to 16.20% of room width and cloud radius-of-gyration error from 9.85% to 6.58%. A 26-second rollout completes in ~64 s on a 4 GB GPU, approximately 37x faster than the foam-extend reference pipeline, toward per-appointment infection-risk screening once the multi-case checkpoint is in place.

</details>


### [244] [A Family of Divergence Measures for Evaluating the Reconstruction Quality of Explainable Ensemble Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19618)
*Massimo Aria,Agostino Gnasso,Carmela Iorio*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一个基于一致性-关联性区分的统计框架，核心是标准化可解释性损失指标，用于验证集成学习器的可解释代理模型。该框架能检测到基于相关性方法无法发现的重建保真度梯度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于相关性的验证方法具有尺度不变性，无法检测共现结构中的系统性差异，仅测量关联性而非真正的一致性。因此需要一个新的统计框架来准确测量集成学习器内部表示与其代理近似之间的一致性。

Method: 提出基于Cressie-Read幂散度族（λ=2）的标准化可解释性损失指标，该指标具有闭式分解形式，可分解为节点内和节点间分量。框架包含四个互补测量指标，采用统一的置换检验程序在单次重采样中为所有测量提供有效推断。

Result: 蒙特卡洛模拟和实证评估证实了精确的I类错误控制，这些测量能够检测到基于相关性的替代方法无法察觉的重建保真度梯度。每个度量都建立了包括有界性和对称性在内的理论性质。

Conclusion: 该框架在可解释集成树(E2Tree)背景下开发，在三个基准数据集上的实证评估展示了框架的实用价值，为验证可解释代理模型提供了有效的诊断工具，能够精确定位重建失败的位置和原因。

Abstract: Validating interpretable surrogate models for ensemble learners requires measuring agreement between the ensemble's internal representation and its surrogate approximation, rather than mere association. Correlation-based approaches are scale-invariant and fail to detect systematic discrepancies in co-occurrence structure. We propose a statistical framework grounded in the agreement-association distinction, centered on the normalized Loss of Interpretability (nLoI). Rooted in the Cressie-Read power divergence family with lambda equal to 2, the nLoI admits a closed-form decomposition into within-node and between-node components, providing a unique diagnostic capability to identify precisely where and why reconstruction fails. The framework incorporates four complementary measures capturing distinct structural facets of approximation quality. A unified permutation testing procedure delivers valid inference for all measures within a single resampling pass. Theoretical properties, including boundedness and symmetry, are established for each metric. Monte Carlo simulations and empirical evaluations confirm exact Type I error control and demonstrate that these measures detect reconstruction fidelity gradients invisible to correlation-based alternatives. The framework is developed and illustrated in the context of Explainable Ensemble Trees (E2Tree), and empirical evaluation on three benchmark datasets illustrates the practical utility of the framework.

</details>


### [245] [MiMuon: Mixed Muon Optimizer with Improved Generalization for Large Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19619)
*Feihu Huang,Yuning Luo,Songcan Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次建立了Muon优化器的泛化误差上界，并提出混合优化器MiMuon，在保持相同收敛速率的同时显著降低了泛化误差，在大模型训练中表现出优越性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 矩阵结构参数广泛存在于大语言模型等AI模型中，Muon优化器针对此类参数展现出比向量优化算法更快的收敛速度。然而，现有工作仅关注Muon的收敛性质（优化误差），其泛化性质（泛化误差）尚未被研究。这一理论空白阻碍了对Muon优化器全面性能的理解和进一步改进。

Method: 1) 基于算法稳定性和数学归纳法，首次对Muon优化器的泛化误差进行理论分析；2) 提出混合Muon（MiMuon）优化器，通过谨慎使用梯度正交化技术，融合Muon和动量SGD优化器；3) 理论证明MiMuon的泛化性质和收敛性质；4) 在Qwen3-0.6B和YOLO26m等大规模模型上进行数值实验验证。

Result: 理论结果：1) 证明Muon优化器的泛化误差为$O\big(\frac{1}{Nκ^{T}}\big)$，其中$N$为训练样本数，$T$为迭代次数，$κ>0$为梯度估计奇异值的最小差异；2) MiMuon优化器达到更低的泛化误差$O\big(\frac{1}{N}\big)$，优于Muon的$O\big(\frac{1}{Nκ^{T}}\big)$（因$κ$通常很小）；3) MiMuon保持与Muon相同的收敛速率$O(\frac{1}{T^{1/4}})$。实验结果：在Qwen3-0.6B和YOLO26m等大模型训练中验证了MiMuon优化器的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文首次填补了Muon优化器泛化理论的研究空白，建立的泛化误差上界揭示了原始Muon优化器在泛化性能上的局限性。提出的MiMuon优化器通过混合策略成功降低了泛化误差阶数，同时保持了原有的收敛速率，在理论上和实验上都展现出明显优势，为大规模模型训练提供了更优的优化器选择。

Abstract: Matrix-structured parameters frequently appear in many artificial intelligence models such as large language models. More recently, an efficient Muon optimizer is designed for matrix parameters of large-scale models, and shows markedly faster convergence than the vector-wise algorithms. Although some works have begun to study convergence properties (i.e., optimization error) of the Muon optimizer, its generalization properties (i.e., generalization error) is still not established. Thus, in this paper, we study generalization error of the Muon optimizer based on algorithmic stability and mathematical induction, and prove that the Muon has a generalization error of $O\big(\frac{1}{Nκ^{T}}\big)$, where $N$ is training sample size, and $T$ denotes iteration number, and $κ>0$ denotes minimum difference between singular values of gradient estimate. To enhance generalization of the Muon, we propose an effective mixed Muon (MiMuon) optimizer by cautiously using orthogonalization of gradient, which is a hybrid of Muon and momentum-based SGD optimizers. Then we prove that our MiMuon optimizer has a lower generalization error of $O\big(\frac{1}{N}\big)$ than $O\big(\frac{1}{Nκ^{T}}\big)$ of Muon optimizer, since $κ$ generally is very small. Meanwhile, we also studied the convergence properties of our MiMuon algorithm, and prove that our MiMuon algorithm has the same convergence rate of $O(\frac{1}{T^{1/4}})$ as the Muon algorithm. Some numerical experimental results on training large models including Qwen3-0.6B and YOLO26m demonstrate efficiency of the MiMuon optimizer.

</details>


### [246] [Optimal Reconstruction from Linear Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19625)
*Yuval Filmus,Shay Moran,Elizaveta Nesterova*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了从近似线性查询重建未知点的问题，确定了最优重建误差的精确下界。在查询次数趋于无穷时，最优误差收敛于√(2d/(d+1))δ，且过剩误差以双指数速度衰减。维度增长时，需要O(exp(d))次查询才能消除过剩误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该问题在低维遥感、信号恢复、高维数据分析和隐私敏感推理等应用中自然产生。研究旨在刻画重建误差作为查询次数T、环境维度d和噪声参数δ的函数的最优值，填补了这一基础问题的理论空白。

Method: 技术上推广了Jung定理（1901）。经典定理界定了直径为1的集合的最大可能半径并刻画了极值体，而本文的推广提供了一个稳健变体来刻画近极值体。证明采用了几何和动力学方法，利用了对称性和李群作用。

Result: 主要结果包括：1) 当T→∞时，最优重建误差收敛于显式值√(2d/(d+1))δ，该值类似于监督学习中的贝叶斯最优误差；2) 维度固定时，过剩误差随T→∞以双指数速度衰减，显著快于学习曲线中的典型速率；3) 维度增长时，需要O(exp(d))次查询才能使过剩误差趋于零。

Conclusion: 本文完整刻画了近似线性查询重建问题的最优误差行为，揭示了固定维度下的双指数收敛速率和高维情形下的指数查询复杂度，并提供了Jung定理的稳健推广，为相关应用提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We study the problem of reconstructing an unknown point in $\mathbb{R}^d$ from approximate linear queries. This setting arises naturally in applications ranging from low-dimensional remote sensing and signal recovery to high-dimensional data analysis and privacy-sensitive inference. Our main goal is to characterize the optimal reconstruction error as a function of the number of queries $T$, the ambient dimension $d$, and the noise parameter $δ$.
  We first analyze the limit $T \to \infty$ and show that the optimal reconstruction error converges to the explicit value $\sqrt{2d/(d+1)} δ$, which plays a role analogous to the Bayes optimal error in supervised learning. When the dimension is fixed, we show that the excess error above this limit decays doubly exponentially fast as $T \to \infty$, a rate that is significantly faster than those typically encountered in learning curves. When the dimension grows, we show that a number of queries on the order of $\exp(d)$ is necessary and sufficient to achieve vanishing excess error. Finally, we introduce and analyze an improper variant of the reconstruction problem.
  From a technical perspective, our main contribution is a generalization of Jung's theorem (1901). The classical theorem bounds the maximum possible radius of a set of diameter 1 and characterizes extremal bodies. Our generalization provides a robust variant that characterizes near-extremal bodies and is proved via geometric and dynamical arguments exploiting symmetry and Lie group actions.

</details>


### [247] [OScaR: The Occam's Razor for Extreme KV Cache Quantization in LLMs and Beyond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19660)
*Zunhai Su,Rui Yang,Chao Zhang,Yaxiu Liu,Yifan Zhang,Wei Wu,Jing Xiong,Dayou Du,Xialie Zhuang,Yulei Qian,Yuchen Xie,Yik-Chung Wu,Hongxia Yang,Ngai Wong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出OScaR框架，通过Canalized Rotation和Omni-Token Scaling解决KV缓存在极低比特量化下的Token范数不平衡问题，在INT2量化下实现近乎无损性能，相比BF16基线内存减少5.3倍、解码速度提升3.0倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着长上下文推理和多模态智能的发展，KV缓存的内存占用已成为高效部署的主要瓶颈。现有的per-channel量化方法在极端压缩下效果下降，主要受Token范数不平衡(TNI)问题制约——当共享量化参数需要覆盖具有显著范数差异的token组时，会系统性地放大量化误差。

Method: 提出OScaR (Omni-Scaled Canalized Rotation)框架：1) 采用Canalized Rotation变换；2) 结合Omni-Token Scaling技术，有效缓解TNI引起的序列维度方差；3) 配合优化的系统设计和CUDA内核实现。该方法突破了传统per-channel量化范式的固有局限，适用于纯文本、多模态和全模态LLMs。

Result: 在X-LLMs上的广泛评估表明：1) OScaR在INT2量化下实现近乎无损性能，持续优于现有方法；2) 相比BF16 FlashDecoding-v2基线，解码速度提升高达3.0倍，内存占用减少5.3倍，吞吐量提升4.1倍；3) 定义了新的帕累托前沿。

Conclusion: OScaR作为一个稳健、低复杂度、通用的KV缓存压缩框架，成功解决了极低比特量化下的核心挑战，为长上下文和多模态LLM的高效部署提供了有效解决方案，具有重要的实用价值。

Abstract: The rapid advancement toward long-context reasoning and multi-modal intelligence has made the memory footprint of the Key-Value (KV) cache a dominant memory bottleneck for efficient deployment. While the established per-channel quantization effectively accommodates intrinsic channel-wise outliers in Key tensors, its efficacy diminishes under extreme compression. In this work, we revisit the inherent limitations of the per-channel quantization paradigm from both empirical and theoretical perspectives. Our analysis identifies Token Norm Imbalance (TNI) as the primary bottleneck to quantization fidelity. We demonstrate that TNI systematically amplifies errors when shared quantization parameters are required to span token groups exhibiting substantial norm disparities. Instead of relying on intricate quantization pipelines (e.g., TurboQuant), we propose OScaR (Omni-Scaled Canalized Rotation), an accurate and lightweight KV cache compression framework for X-LLMs (i.e., text-only, multi-modal, and omni-modal LLMs). Advancing the per-channel paradigm, OScaR employs Canalized Rotation followed by Omni-Token Scaling to mitigate TNI-induced sequence-dimensional variance both effectively and efficiently, further supported by our optimized system design and CUDA kernels. Extensive evaluations across X-LLMs show that OScaR consistently outperforms existing methods and achieves near-lossless performance under INT2 quantization, establishing it as a robust, low-complexity, and universal framework that defines a new Pareto front. Compared with the BF16 FlashDecoding-v2 baseline, our OScaR implementation achieves a notable up to 3.0x speedup in decoding, reduces memory footprint by 5.3x, and increases throughput by 4.1x. The code for OScaR is publicly available at https://github.com/ZunhaiSu/OScaR-KV-Quant.

</details>


### [248] [AR1-ZO: Topology-Aware Rank-1 Zeroth-Order Queries for High-Rank LoRA Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19767)
*Ziye Chen,Hongbin Lin,Chenyu Zhang,Xiangda Yan,Yongjie Yang,Yao Shu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出AR1-ZO方法解决零阶优化与LoRA结合时的秩矛盾问题，通过交替秩-1原子查询和拓扑感知缩放，在不增加查询成本的情况下使高秩LoRA有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零阶优化与LoRA结合存在秩矛盾：增加LoRA秩提升适配器容量，但标准两点ZO扰动维度依赖秩，而原子级更新会导致有限差分信号不可观测，FD-SNR随秩增加而衰减，引发方向坍塌。

Method: 将LoRA分解为匹配的秩-1原子，每步查询一个原子保持存储适配器秩r同时消除单查询扰动维度对r的依赖；引入拓扑感知缩放γ=αr替代标准LoRA缩放α/r，恢复秩不变的活跃信号。

Result: 理论证明原子最小性、秩无关的活跃查询维度、方向坍塌与恢复机制；在OPT和Qwen3模型上的实验验证了信号机制，AR1-ZO在匹配预算的ZO方法中使高秩LoRA有效。

Conclusion: 瓶颈是测量-拓扑问题而非外部子空间需求；AR1-ZO无需辅助基、激活钩子、曲率估计或额外前向查询即可解决秩矛盾，剩余秩依赖作为摊销覆盖成本存在。

Abstract: Zeroth-order (ZO) optimization enables large-language-model fine-tuning without storing backpropagation activations, while LoRA supplies compact trainable adapters. Combining them creates a rank paradox: increasing LoRA rank improves adapter capacity, but standard two-point ZO either perturbs a rank-dependent number of coordinates or, under atomwise updates, can make the finite-difference signal unobservable. This paper shows that the bottleneck is a measurement-topology problem rather than a need for an external subspace. LoRA already decomposes into matched rank-$1$ atoms, each a complete factor-coordinate block of dimension $d_\text{out}+d_\text{in}$. Querying one atom per step keeps the stored adapter rank $r$ while removing $r$ from the single-query perturbation dimension. The naive atomwise query is still miscalibrated: if it inherits canonical LoRA scaling $α/r$, the active finite-difference signal shrinks as $1/r$ and the active finite-difference signal-to-noise ratio (FD-SNR) as $1/r^2$, producing directional collapse under a fixed residual evaluation-noise floor. AR1-ZO pairs alternating rank-$1$ atom queries with topology-aware scaling $γ=αr$, restoring rank-invariant active signal without auxiliary bases, activation hooks, curvature estimates, or extra forward queries. Theory proves atom minimality, rank-independent active query dimension, directional collapse and restoration, and the remaining rank dependence as an amortized coverage cost. Experiments on OPT and Qwen3 models validate the signal mechanism and show that AR1-ZO makes high-rank LoRA effective among matched-budget ZO methods under the standard two-forward-pass query budget.

</details>


### [249] [Agentic Discovery of Cryomicroneedle Formulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19677)
*Hao Li,Lifu Du,Nurul Hameed,Shemonti Saha Authai,Zlata Stefanovic,Chenjie Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究开发了一种AI辅助的闭环工作流程，用于发现低温微针冷冻保护剂配方，通过10轮迭代验证，将预测误差从41.21降至6.86个百分点，最终获得了95.15%细胞存活率的低毒性配方。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低温微针技术可以实现微创真皮内活细胞递送，但其冷冻制剂必须同时解决细胞保护、毒性限制和装置制造约束之间的矛盾。传统的冷冻保护剂配方发现过程效率低下，需要大量的试错实验，因此需要一种数据高效的方法来加速配方优化。

Method: 研究构建了一个AI辅助的闭环工作流程，包含以下关键步骤：(1) 从42项研究中策划了198个间充质干细胞冷冻保存配方数据集，转化为21个成分特征；(2) 训练不确定性感知的文献先验模型（高斯过程代理模型）；(3) 结合贝叶斯优化进行配方推荐；(4) 通过10轮迭代湿实验室验证逐步修正模型，共获得106个湿实验室观测数据。

Result: 模型性能在迭代过程中显著提升：批RMSE从41.21降至6.86个百分点，后期阶段排名相关性持续为正，累计湿实验室预测与实测值R²达到0.942。最佳验证配方实现了95.15%的解冻后存活率，含有低浓度的DMSO、ectoin、乙二醇和胎牛血清。但研究发现高存活率并不能保证完整的低温微针形成，表明未来需要多目标优化。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了AI辅助的计算基础设施能够使数据效率高的配方发现更易于被缺乏内部数据专业知识的实验室所采用。研究代码已开源，为低温微针冷冻保护剂的系统化发现提供了可复制的方法论框架。

Abstract: Cryomicroneedles offer a route to minimally invasive intradermal delivery of living cells, but their cryogenic formulations must reconcile cell protection with constraints on toxicity and device fabrication. Here we report an AI-assisted, closed-loop workflow for cryomicroneedle cryoprotectant discovery that combines literature curation, Gaussian-process surrogate modelling, Bayesian optimization, and sequential wet-lab validation. A curated dataset of 198 mesenchymal stem-cell cryopreservation formulations from 42 studies was converted into 21 ingredient features and used to train an uncertainty-aware literature prior. This model captured moderate structure in the literature data but failed prospectively, motivating iterative wet-lab correction. Across ten validation iterations and 106 wet-lab observations, the model progressively adapted to cryomicroneedle-specific outcomes: batch RMSE decreased from 41.21 to 6.86 percentage points, later-stage rank correlations became consistently positive, and the cumulative wet-lab predicted-versus-measured summary reached $R^2 = 0.942$. The best validated formulation achieved 95.15\% post-thaw viability with low DMSO, ectoin, ethylene glycol, and fetal bovine serum. However, high viability alone did not ensure intact cryomicroneedle formation, highlighting the need for future multi-objective optimization. These results demonstrate that agent-assisted computational infrastructure can make data-efficient formulation discovery more accessible to labs with minimal data expertise in-house. Project code is available at https://github.com/baitmeister/ML-for-CryoMN.

</details>


### [250] [Latent Laplace Diffusion for Irregular Multivariate Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19805)
*Zinuo You,Jin Zheng,John Cartlidge*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了Latent Laplace Diffusion (LLapDiff)框架，用于非规则多变量时间序列的长时程预测。该方法将目标建模为低维潜在轨迹，实现了无需逐步物理时间积分的全时段生成，在长时程预测任务上优于基线方法，并支持缺失值填补。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非规则多变量时间序列的长时程预测存在权衡问题：离散方法通过重新网格化会扭曲时间结构，而连续时间模型通常需要易产生漂移的顺序求解器。研究旨在填补这一空白，开发既能保持时间结构又能避免漂移问题的方法。

Method: 提出LLapDiff生成框架：1)将目标建模为低维潜在轨迹，实现无需逐步时间积分的全时段生成；2)受随机端口哈密顿动力学启发，利用稳定的模态参数化指导反向过程；3)通过可学习的复共轭极点在拉普拉斯域参数化均值演化，实现对非规则时间戳的直接评估；4)通过更新平均分析将连续动力学与非规则观测关联，映射采样间隙到有效事件域极点，提出间隙感知历史摘要器。

Result: 大量实验表明，LLapDiff在长时程预测任务上优于基线方法。其连续时间生成特性支持通过在历史时间戳查询同一模型来完成缺失值填补任务。代码已在GitHub开源。

Conclusion: LLapDiff成功解决了非规则时间序列长时程预测中的核心挑战，通过拉普拉斯域参数化和潜在轨迹建模避免了传统方法的缺陷，同时扩展了模型应用范围至缺失值填补，为非规则时间序列建模提供了新思路。

Abstract: Irregular multivariate time series impose a trade-off for long-horizon forecasting: discrete methods can distort temporal structure via re-gridding, while continuous-time models often require sequential solvers prone to drift. To bridge this gap, we present Latent Laplace Diffusion (LLapDiff), a generative framework that models the target as a low-dimensional latent trajectory, enabling horizon-wide generation without step-by-step integration over physical time. We guide the reverse process utilizing a stable modal parameterization motivated by stochastic port-Hamiltonian dynamics, and parameterize its mean evolution in the Laplace domain via learnable complex-conjugate poles, enabling direct evaluation over irregular timestamps. We also link continuous dynamics to irregular observations through renewal-averaging analysis, which maps sampling gaps to effective event-domain poles and motivates a gap-aware history summarizer. Extensive experiments show that LLapDiff improves over baselines in long-horizon forecasting, and its continuous-time generative nature supports missing-value imputation by querying the same model at historical timestamps. Code is available at https://github.com/pixelhero98/LLapDiffusion.

</details>


### [251] [MSAlign: Aligning Molecule and Mass Spectra Foundation Models for Metabolite Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19752)
*Paul Krzakala,Gabriel Melo,Camille Lançon,Charlotte Laclau,Rémi Flamary,Etienne Thévenot,Florence d'Alché-Buc*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了MSAlign框架，通过对比学习对齐质谱和分子表示，在分子检索任务上取得最优性能，并系统分析了数据划分策略中数据泄漏与域偏移之间的权衡问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代谢组学中从质谱数据准确识别代谢物仍是一个核心挑战。尽管MassSpecGym等基准数据集推动了机器学习方法发展，但数据预处理流程复杂且缺乏统一实现，导致方法难以复现和比较。

Method: 1）提出了一个统一框架涵盖基于表示对齐和对比学习的现有方法；2）引入MSAlign方法，受视觉-语言模型多模态对齐启发，通过轻量级MLP投影将两个冻结的基础模型（质谱模型DreaMS和分子模型ChemBERTa）在共享表示空间中对齐，采用基于候选分子的对比目标进行训练；3）引入分布偏移的量化度量，系统评估数据划分策略。

Result: MSAlign实现简单、训练快速，在所有基准测试中一致优于现有方法。通过对数据划分策略的形式化分析，揭示了分子检索中数据泄漏与域偏移之间的内在权衡关系。

Conclusion: 公开了所有数据集、数据划分、候选集以及MSAlign和基线方法的统一实现，支持可复现研究，为代谢组学领域的分子检索任务提供了统一评估基准。

Abstract: Accurately identifying metabolites i.e. small molecules from mass spectrometry data remains a core challenge in metabolomics, with broad applications in drug discovery, environmental analysis, and clinical research. We address the Molecule Retrieval task, which consists in recovering the chemical structure of a metabolite from its MS/MS spectrum given a set of candidate molecules. While the recent release of benchmark datasets such as MassSpecGym and Spectraverse has considerably accelerated the development of novel machine learning approaches, the complexity of data preprocessing pipelines and the lack of unified implementations make methods and results difficult to reproduce and compare. We make three contributions. First, we propose a unified framework encompassing recent approaches based on representation alignment and contrastive learning. Second, we introduce MSAlign, inspired by multimodal alignment in vision-language models, which learns a shared representation space by aligning two frozen foundation models (DreaMS for mass spectra and ChemBERTa for molecules) through lightweight MLP projections trained with a candidate-based contrastive objective. MSAlign is simple to implement, fast to train and consistently outperforms existing approaches across all benchmarks. Third, we investigate a long-standing evaluation problem: data splitting strategies in molecule retrieval implicitly trade off data leakage against domain shift. We formalize this tension by introducing a quantitative measure of distribution shift, and use it to evaluate splitting strategies in existing benchmarks. All datasets, splits, candidate sets, and a unified implementation of MSAlign and baselines are publicly released to support reproducible research.

</details>


### [252] [FLUXtrapolation: A benchmark on extrapolating ecosystem fluxes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19812)
*Anya Fries,Jacob A Nelson,Martin Jung,Markus Reichstein,Jonas Peters*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了FLUXtrapolation基准，用于在分布偏移下进行生态系统通量外推，发现基线方法在尾部误差和多尺度评估下表现分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生态系统通量对理解碳、水和能量循环至关重要，但只能在稀疏的测量塔处直接测量，因此需要通过模型外推来生成全球通量估计。通量外推是一个受气候、生态系统类型和环境条件差异导致的协变量分布偏移及条件偏移影响的挑战性域泛化问题。

Method: 基于通量外推的领域专业知识设计了FLUXtrapolation基准，定义了时间、空间和基于温度的外推场景，并通过留一域、时间聚合和尾部误差进行性能评估。提供了对协变量分布偏移和条件偏移的定量分析。

Result: 在试点研究中发现基线方法在中位小时RMSE下表现相似，但在提出的尾部聚焦和多尺度评估下表现分离。

Conclusion: FLUXtrapolation为机器学习方法在分布偏移下提出了现实且相关的挑战，该基准的进展将直接支持改进通量外推的科学目标。

Abstract: We introduce FLUXtrapolation, a benchmark for extrapolating ecosystem fluxes under progressively harder distribution shifts. Ecosystem fluxes are central to understanding the carbon, water, and energy cycles, yet they can only be measured directly at sparsely located measurement towers. Producing global flux estimates therefore requires training models on observed sites using globally available covariates and predicting in unobserved regions, that is, upscaling. Flux upscaling is a challenging domain generalization problem that is affected by a shift in covariate distribution across climates, ecosystem types, and environmental conditions, as well as by conditional shift: important drivers remain unobserved at global scale. We provide a quantitative analysis of both these shifts in $P_X$ and $P_{Y\mid X}$. FLUXtrapolation is designed based on domain expertise on flux upscaling: it defines temporal, spatial, and temperature-based extrapolation scenarios and evaluates performance across held-out domains, temporal aggregations, and tail errors. In a pilot study, we find that baselines perform similarly under median hourly RMSE, but separate under the proposed tail-focused and multi-scale evaluation. FLUXtrapolation therefore poses a realistic and thus relevant challenge for machine learning methods under distribution shift; at the same time, progress on this benchmark would directly support the scientific goal of improving flux upscaling.

</details>


### [253] [ST-TGExplainer: Disentangling Stability and Transition Patterns for Temporal GNN Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19822)
*Hongjiang Chen,Xin Zheng,Pengfei Jiao,Huan Liu,Zhidong Zhao,Huaming Wu,Feng Xia,Shirui Pan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了ST-TGExplainer，一种自解释的时序图神经网络，通过解耦稳定性模式和转换模式，实现了更强的预测性能和更可信的解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的时序图神经网络（TGNNs）可解释性有限，大多数无法识别哪些历史交互对预测影响最大。现有的可解释TGNN方法主要关注已见过的历史交互（稳定性模式），而忽略了新出现的首次交互（转换模式），而这两类模式对于忠实的时间解释都至关重要。

Method: 提出了ST-TGExplainer，一种自解释的TGNN，通过解耦时序图中的稳定性模式和转换模式来提供更忠实的解释。该方法基于解耦信息瓶颈目标，学习一个紧凑的解释性子图，该子图能保持对事件标签的预测性，同时显式抑制稳定性模式和转换模式之间基于标签条件的冗余。

Result: 大量实验表明，ST-TGExplainer取得了很强的预测性能，并产生了更忠实的解释。

Conclusion: ST-TGExplainer成功解决了现有可解释TGNN方法的局限性，通过同时考虑稳定性模式和转换模式，提供了更全面和可信的时序图解释，为时序图神经网络的可解释性研究提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Temporal graph neural networks (TGNNs) have gained significant traction for solving real-world temporal graph tasks. However, their interpretability remains limited, as most TGNNs fail to identify which historical interactions most influence a given prediction. Despite promising progress on interpretable TGNNs, existing methods predominantly focus on previously seen historical interactions, which we term stability patterns, while overlooking newly emerging first-time interactions, which we term transition patterns. Both types of patterns are essential for faithful temporal explanations. To address this limitation, we propose ST-TGExplainer, a self-explainable TGNN that disentangles Stability and Transition patterns in temporal graphs for a more faithful Temporal GNN Explainer. Guided by a disentangled information bottleneck objective, ST-TGExplainer learns a compact explanatory subgraph that remains predictive of the event label while explicitly suppressing label-conditioned redundancy between stability and transition patterns. Extensive experiments demonstrate that ST-TGExplainer achieves strong predictive performance and yields more faithful explanations. Code is available at https://github.com/hjchen-hdu/ST-TGExplainer.

</details>


### [254] [B-cos GNNs: Faithful Explanations through Dynamic Linearity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19778)
*Joschka Groß,Mohammad Shaique Solanki,Verena Wolf*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了B-cos GNNs，一种天然可解释的图神经网络，通过单一的输入依赖线性映射将预测结果精确分解为节点级和特征级的贡献，无需额外的解释器即可快速生成高质量解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的图神经网络（GNN）通常是黑盒模型，缺乏内在的可解释性。现有的解释方法多为事后（post-hoc）方法，往往需要额外的解释器、修改训练目标或复杂的扰动过程，计算成本高且解释质量不稳定。因此，作者希望设计一种在保证预测性能的同时，能够内在地、高效地提供精确解释的GNN模型。

Method: 提出B-cos GNNs模型。该方法采用线性（基于求和）的聚合方式，并用B-cos变换替代传统的非线性消息传递和更新函数。B-cos变换能够诱导出有意义的、特定于任务的权重-输入对齐，并通过模型的“动态线性”特性，使得模型预测可以精确分解。解释过程仅通过一次前向和后向传播即可完成。该架构可以实例化为GIN（Graph Isomorphism Network）等常见GNN模型。

Result: 实验结果表明，B-cos GNNs在预测准确率上仅有轻微损失，但在多种合成和真实世界的基准测试中达到了最先进的可解释性。与事后解释方法相比，该方法生成解释的速度快了数个数量级。

Conclusion: B-cos GNNs成功地在预测精度和可解释性之间取得了良好的平衡，证明了设计内在可解释GNN架构的可行性。该方法避免了事后解释方法的弊端，为图神经网络的可靠性和透明度提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: We introduce B-cos GNNs, an inherently explainable class of graph neural networks whose predictions decompose exactly into per-node, per-feature contributions via a single input-dependent linear map. B-cos GNNs use linear (sum-based) aggregation and replace non-linear message and update functions with B-cos transforms. This induces meaningful, task-specific weight-input alignment that is directly accessible through the model's dynamic linearity. Instance-level explanations follow from a single forward and backward pass, requiring no auxiliary explainer, modified learning objective, or perturbation procedure. Instantiated as a GIN, our approach trades small losses in predictive accuracy for state-of-the-art explainability across diverse synthetic and real-world benchmarks, producing explanations orders of magnitude faster than post-hoc baselines.

</details>


### [255] [Smooth Piecewise Cutting for Neural Operator to Handle Discontinuities and Sharp Transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19823)
*Ha Dang,Sebastian Schmidt,Juergen Hesser*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Cut-DeepONet框架，通过提升策略将区域划分与不连续边界分离，显式建模不连续性，在低分辨率数据上以更少参数超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经算子在学习PDE解算子方面表现出色，但其固有的连续表示难以捕捉不连续性和急剧过渡。现有方法通常在连续函数空间内近似这些特征，往往需要增加模型容量和高分辨率数据。

Method: 提出Cut-DeepONet两阶段训练框架：通过提升策略将问题重构，将计算域划分为平滑子区域，同时将不连续性表示为高维空间中的边界。额外训练一个网络预测输入依赖的不连续位置，指导神经算子在各区域内生成平滑分量。

Result: 在基准PDE实验中，Cut-DeepONet即使在低分辨率数据集上训练，也优于现有最先进方法。该方法在不连续和急剧过渡问题上表现卓越，同时使用更少的可训练参数。

Conclusion: 研究结果突显了改变算子学习表示方式而非增加模型复杂性的优势，为处理不连续性提供了新思路。

Abstract: Neural operators have achieved strong performance in learning solution operators of partial differential equations (PDEs), but their inherently continuous representations struggle to capture discontinuities and sharp transitions. Existing approaches typically approximate such features within continuous function spaces, often requiring increased model capacity and high-resolution data. In this work, we propose Cut-DeepONet, a two-stage training framework that explicitly models discontinuities while reducing learning complexity. Our approach reformulates the problem via a lifting strategy, partitioning the domain into smooth subregions while representing discontinuities as boundaries in a higher-dimensional space. This separation aligns the operator learning task with the inductive bias of neural networks and avoids directly approximating discontinuities. An additional network predicts input-dependent discontinuity locations for unseen inputs, which are then used to guide the neural operator in generating smooth components within each region. Experiments on benchmark PDEs show that Cut-DeepONet outperforms state-of-the-art methods, even when trained on low-resolution datasets. The method excels on problems with discontinuities and sharp transitions, while using fewer trainable parameters. Our results highlight the benefits of changing the representation of operator learning rather than increasing model complexity.

</details>


### [256] [A Closed-loop, State-centric, Multi-agent Framework for Passenger Load Estimation from Heterogeneous Data Streams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19834)
*Yiyao Xu,Hao Zhou,Yuhang Wang,Jingran Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对公共交通乘客载量估计中传感器数据不完美、证据冲突和上下文依赖等问题，提出了一种闭环、以状态为中心的多智能体框架，通过物理可行性约束和动态信任分配实现鲁棒的载量估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公共交通机构需要可靠的乘客载量轨迹以支持运营和乘客服务，但目前的自动乘客计数（APC）系统受站点布局、客流强度和运营条件影响，存在精度波动、计数误差累积、证据冲突以及传感器可靠性依赖上下文等挑战，亟需一种能从异构数据流中稳健估计载量的方法。

Method: 提出闭环、以状态为中心的多智能体框架，核心架构包括：统一的站点事件骨干网络、耦合的感知-物理-融合循环（用于逐站推断）、可选的行程级宏观校正模块以及闭环校准模块。该方法在每一步强制物理可行性约束，动态分配对证据源的信任，并将物理推导的违规残差反馈到训练中以增强鲁棒性。

Result: 该方法能够有效处理增量计数误差、证据冲突和传感器可靠性变化问题，通过物理可行性约束和动态信任分配机制，提高了从异构数据流中进行乘客载量估计的准确性与鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架为不完美传感条件下的乘客载量估计提供了系统化解决方案，通过闭环反馈机制持续改进估计性能，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: To support operations and passenger-facing services, transit agencies need reliable passenger load trajectories. Currently, load estimates are typically inferred from imperfect sensing systems rather than fully observed, and the accuracy of modern automatic passenger counting (APC) systems still varies with station layout, flow intensity, and operating conditions. To address the challenges of robust passenger load estimation from heterogeneous data streams, including incremental count errors, evidence conflicts, and context-dependent sensor reliability, we propose a closed-loop, state-centric, multi-agent framework. This method enforces physical feasibility at every step, allocates trust dynamically among evidence sources, and feeds physics-derived violation residuals back into training for robustness improvement. The architecture consists of a unified stop-event backbone, a coupled Perception--Physical--Fusion loop for stop-by-stop inference, and optional trip-level macro-correction and closed-loop calibration modules.

</details>


### [257] [LionMuon: Alternating Spectral and Sign Descent for Efficient Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19811)
*Arman Bolatov,Artem Riabinin,Nikita Kornilov,Andrey Veprikov,Samuel Horváth,Martin Takáč,Aleksandr Beznosikov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LionMuon优化器，通过周期性交替Lion和Muon更新，在保持Muon强方向性的同时大幅降低计算成本，在多个规模上Pareto优于现有优化器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模优化中，更新步骤的廉价性和有效性是关键权衡：基于符号的优化器（如Lion）计算廉价但方向较弱，而Muon的谱矩阵符号更新方向更强但计算成本显著更高。作者旨在结合两者优势，在保持有效性的同时降低平均迭代成本。

Method: 提出LionMuon优化器，以固定周期P交替执行Lion和Muon更新，两者共享单一的双EMA动量缓冲区，使优化器状态内存与Lion相同（仅为AdamW的一半）。此外还提出简化版SignMuon（单EMA变体）。理论方面证明了在重尾噪声下的复杂度界限。

Result: 在P=2时，LionMuon在所有测试数据集和架构上Pareto优于Muon、Lion、Signum和AdamW（124M模型规模），以更低计算达到更低验证损失。该优势在355M和720M规模下持续存在。SignMuon单独使用已优于纯Muon。

Conclusion: LionMuon成功平衡了计算效率与优化效果，理论分析预测了计算最优周期及LionMuon优于Muон和Lion的条件，为大规模优化提供了新的有效解决方案。

Abstract: In large-scale optimization, the cheapness and effectiveness of update steps are the most crucial factors for a successful optimizer. Sign-based optimizers like Lion or Signum produce cheap per-step updates, whereas Muon's spectral matrix-sign update gives a much stronger direction at a substantially higher per-step cost. In this work, we propose LionMuon, which retains the effectiveness of Muon steps while considerably cutting the averaged iteration cost, similar to sign-based methods. It alternates between Lion's and Muon's updates on a fixed period P, sharing a single dual-EMA momentum buffer between them. The optimizer state memory therefore matches Lion and is exactly half of AdamW's. A simpler single-EMA variant, SignMuon, by itself already outperforms pure Muon. At P = 2, LionMuon Pareto-dominates Muon, Lion, Signum, and AdamW on every dataset and architecture we tested at 124M model size, reaching lower validation loss at lower compute, and the same advantage persists at 355M and 720M scale. On the theory side, we prove sharp complexity bounds under heavy-tailed noise which are governed by period-averaged smoothness and noise that interpolate between Muon's and Lion's constants. These bounds predict the compute-optimal period and the conditions under which LionMuon outruns Muon and Lion. Code: https://github.com/brain-lab-research/lion-muon

</details>


### [258] [StableGrad: Backward Scale Control without Batch Normalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19856)
*Jose I. Mestre,Alberto Fernández-Hernández,Cristian Pérez-Corral,Manuel F. Dolz,Enrique S. Quintana-Ortí*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了StableGrad，一种优化器级别的权重梯度尺度控制机制，可在不修改前向传播模型的情况下稳定训练深层网络，在物理信息神经网络(PINNs)和标准架构上均表现出色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练超深神经网络需要控制跨深度幅值的传播，否则激活和梯度可能消失、爆炸或进入不稳定状态。现代架构通常通过批归一化、残差连接等机制缓解此问题，但这些机制并不总是适用。在物理信息神经网络(PINNs)中，网络表示连续物理场，其输入导数定义训练目标，批依赖的归一化会在预测场及其导数中引入非局部依赖，导致问题。

Method: 提出StableGrad，一种优化器级别的尺度控制机制。该方法在前向传播后应用反向传播，在优化器更新前应用归一化，从而在不改变网络输出、其导数和物理残差的情况下，校正层级权重梯度不平衡。这种设计使得归一化仅在反向传播后、优化器更新前应用，保持了前向模型的完整性。

Result: 在PINN基准测试中，StableGrad提高了匹配深度的解精度，使深层模型在标准优化下更加可靠。在ResNet和EfficientNet架构上，当移除批归一化通常会导致训练崩溃时，StableGrad在不引入任何其他架构更改的情况下稳定了优化过程。

Conclusion: 优化器级别的权重梯度尺度控制在前向归一化不可用或不理想时可以提供实用的替代方案，证明了在不修改前向传播模型的情况下稳定深层网络训练的可行性。

Abstract: Training very deep neural networks requires controlling the propagation of magnitudes across depth. Without such control, activations and gradients may vanish, explode, or enter unstable regimes that make optimization fail. Modern architectures often mitigate this problem through Batch Normalization, residual connections, or other normalization layers, which repeatedly re-scale or bypass intermediate representations. However, these mechanisms are not always appropriate. In Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs), the network represents a continuous physical field and its input derivatives define the training objective, making batch-dependent normalization problematic because it can introduce non-local dependencies into the predicted field and its derivatives. We propose StableGrad, an optimizer-level scale-control mechanism that corrects layer-wise weight-gradient imbalances without modifying the forward model. Because the normalization is applied only after backpropagation and before the optimizer update, the network output, its derivatives, and the physical residual remain unchanged. We analyze the effective training dynamics induced by this rescaling and evaluate StableGrad on deep PINNs as the target application, with BatchNorm-free convolutional networks serving as a diagnostic stress test. On PINN benchmarks, StableGrad improves matched-depth solution accuracy and makes deeper models more reliable under standard optimization. On ResNet and EfficientNet architectures, where removing Batch Normalization normally leads to training collapse, StableGrad stabilizes optimization without introducing any other architectural change. These results show that optimizer-level control of weight-gradient scale can provide a practical alternative when forward normalization is unavailable or undesirable.

</details>


### [259] [General Lower Bounds for Differentially Private Federated Learning with Arbitrary Public-Transcript Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19813)
*Yicheng Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了差分隐私联邦学习协议的通用下界，建立了联邦van Trees界，为均值为平方ℓ₂损失下的参数估计提供了理论基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在联邦学习中，客户端数据需要在隐私保护的前提下进行协作学习。现有研究缺乏对具有任意公开记录交互的自适应多轮联邦学习协议的通用下界分析，特别是在客户端样本可跨轮次重用的场景下。因此，需要建立一个通用的理论框架来刻画差分隐私约束下联邦学习协议的性能极限。

Method: 提出了一种隐私-信息收缩不等式，用于分析完整的公开记录。基于该不等式，针对满足总客户端级样本级零集中差分隐私（zCDP）约束的估计器，建立了联邦van Trees下界理论框架。该方法适用于任意数量的自适应轮次，并允许客户端本地样本跨轮次重用。

Result: 成功建立了差分隐私联邦学习协议在平方ℓ₂损失下的通用下界。该下界适用于任意公开记录交互的自适应多轮协议。通过均值估计、线性回归和非参数回归三个具体应用场景验证了该下界的有效性和实用性。

Conclusion: 本文为差分隐私联邦学习提供了一个基础性的理论工具，即联邦van Trees下界。该工作不仅填补了自适应多轮联邦学习协议下界分析的理论空白，还为实际应用中的隐私-效用权衡提供了理论指导，有助于设计更优的联邦学习算法。

Abstract: We prove a general lower bound for differentially private federated learning protocols with arbitrary public-transcript interactions. The protocol may use any number of adaptive rounds, and each client's local samples may be reused across rounds. For parameter estimation under squared \(\ell_2\) loss, we establish a federated van Trees lower bound for every estimator satisfying a total clientwise sample-level zero-concentrated differential privacy (zCDP) constraint. The main technical ingredient is a privacy-information contraction inequality for complete public transcripts. We illustrate the bound through applications to mean estimation, linear regression, and nonparametric regression.

</details>


### [260] [Fast and Featureless Node Representation Learning with Partial Pairwise Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19916)
*Sujan Chakraborty,Saptarshi Bej*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了Contrastive FUSE框架，通过优化谱对比目标并引入轻量级模块化近似，实现了在无节点特征图上的快速、可扩展节点表示学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在处理仅有部分成对节点标签且无节点特征的图时，存在计算成本高、难以扩展到大规模图等问题，亟需一种既能捕捉结构信号又能高效训练的统一框架。

Method: 提出Contrastive FUSE框架，直接优化一个融合社区感知结构信号与有符号成对约束的谱对比目标；通过轻量级近似替代昂贵的模块化梯度计算，实现了自然梯度分解与自适应学习率缩放的高效优化方案。

Result: 在基准引用网络、大规模共同购买图和OGB数据集上的广泛实验表明，该方法在不依赖节点特征的情况下，达到了与现有方法相当或更优的对比分类性能，同时在运行时间上取得了显著优势，能够高效处理百万边规模的图。

Conclusion: 将模块化启发的结构学习与对比监督相结合，是实现高效、可扩展的无特征对比节点表示学习的有效途径。

Abstract: We introduce Contrastive FUSE, a fast and unified framework for scalable node representation learning in graphs with partially available pairwise node labels and no available node features. Unlike existing methods, we directly optimize a spectral contrastive objective that integrates community-aware structural signals with signed pairwise constraints. To support large-scale training, we replace the expensive modularity gradient with a lightweight approximation, which preserves the structure-seeking behavior of modularity while reducing the computational cost significantly. This yields an efficient optimization scheme with a natural gradient decomposition and adaptive learning-rate scaling, enabling fast iterative updates even on million-edge graphs. Extensive experiments on benchmark citation networks, large co-purchase graphs, and OGB datasets show that Contrastive FUSE achieves competitive or superior contrastive classification performance without relying on node features, while offering substantial runtime gains over existing baselines. These results highlight the effectiveness of coupling modularity-inspired structural learning with contrastive supervision for efficient and scalable contrastive node representation learning.

</details>


### [261] [A Measure-Theoretic Analysis of Reasoning: Structural Generalization and Approximation Limits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19944)
*Yuyang Zhang,Yifu Zhang,Xuehai Zhou,Xiaoyin Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过最优传输理论建立了大模型OOD泛化的理论框架，揭示了位置编码的移位等变性和电路深度是影响泛化性能的关键约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注LLM推理的经验性缩放定律，但缺乏对分布外(OOD)泛化机制的理论理解。本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 1. 将推理过程形式化为最优传输问题，通过Wasserstein-1距离量化域迁移；
2. 利用Kantorovich对偶性，通过架构Lipschitz连续性和函数逼近极限建立OOD泛化界；
3. 将序列回溯映射到Dyck-k语言以分析电路深度下界。

Result: 1. 位置相关注意力机制（如绝对位置编码）破坏移位不变性，导致Ω(1)的Lipschitz常数和期望风险，而旋转嵌入等移位不变机制可保持等变性并限制误差；
2. TC⁰ Transformer存在严格的电路深度下界，增加物理层深度对避免表示坍塌是必要的，且无法通过增加表示宽度来规避。

Conclusion: 通过54种Transformer配置的组合搜索实验验证了理论界限，证明泛化风险随Wasserstein域迁移单调下降，为LLM架构设计提供了理论指导。

Abstract: While empirical scaling laws for LLM reasoning are well-documented, the theoretical mechanisms governing out-of-distribution (OOD) generalization remain elusive. We formalize reasoning via optimal transport, projecting discrete trajectories into a continuous metric space to quantify domain shifts using the Wasserstein-1 distance. Invoking Kantorovich duality, we bound OOD generalization via architectural Lipschitz continuity and functional approximation limits. This exposes two primary constraints. First, position-dependent attention (e.g., Absolute Positional Encoding) fails to preserve shift invariance, yielding an $Ω(1)$ Lipschitz constant and expected risk, whereas shift-invariant mechanisms (e.g., Rotary Embeddings) preserve equivariance and bound the error. Second, by mapping sequential backtracking to a Dyck-$k$ language, we establish a strict circuit depth lower bound for $\text{TC}^0$ Transformers. Scaling physical layer depth is necessary to avert representation collapse -- a constraint that scaling representation width cannot bypass due to irreducible approximation bounds in Barron spaces. Evaluations across 54 Transformer configurations on combinatorial search corroborate these bounds, demonstrating that generalization risk degrades monotonically with the Wasserstein domain shift.

</details>


### [262] [Set-Valued Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19830)
*Laura Fuentes-Vicente,Mathieu Even,Gaëlle Dormion,Antoine Chambaz,Uri Shalit,Julie Josse*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多治疗方案下的集合值策略学习范式，通过输出一组合理的治疗方案而非单一推荐，实现内在的不确定性量化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的治疗方案推荐通常将患者协变量映射为单一干预措施，但这种点值推荐对估计不确定性、模型设定和有限样本变异性高度敏感，且无法提供对推荐行为的置信度指导。

Method: 将学习-延迟（learning-to-defer）框架扩展至多治疗场景，提出最大下界方法；引入保形策略学习（conformal policy learning）桥接未观测的真实最优治疗与估计的最优治疗规则；借鉴噪声标签文献，开发随机注入方法以保证边际覆盖率。

Result: 在合成数据和体外受精（IVF）真实应用实验中，所提方法能够生成稳健且可操作的策略，自然融入临床考量，有效平衡性能与可靠性。

Conclusion: 集合值策略学习范式为临床决策提供了一种新的思路，通过预测集合的大小反映决策模糊程度，在保证覆盖率的同时实现了性能与可靠性的平衡，具有重要的临床应用价值。

Abstract: Conventional treatment policies map patient covariates to a single recommended intervention in order to maximize expected clinical outcomes. Although a rich body of causal inference methods has been developed to estimate such policies, point-valued recommendations can be highly sensitive to estimation uncertainty, model specification, and finite-sample variability, while typically providing little guidance about how confident one should be in the recommended action. In this work, we propose a set-valued policy learning paradigm for the multiple-treatment setting, in which policies output a set of plausible treatments rather than a single recommendation. This formulation enables intrinsic uncertainty quantification, with the size of the predicted set reflecting the degree of decision ambiguity. We extend the learning-to-defer framework to multiple treatments via a novel \textit{greatest Lower Bound} method, and introduce \textit{conformal policy learning}, which bridges the gap between unobserved ground-truth optimal treatments and estimated optimal treatment rules. Drawing on insights from the noisy-label literature, we develop a randomness-injection approach that guarantees marginal coverage without requiring assumptions on underlying black-box optimal treatment rules. Through experiments on synthetic data and a real-world application to In-Vitro Fertilization (IVF), we demonstrate that our methods produce robust and actionable policies that naturally incorporate clinical considerations while effectively balancing performance and reliability.

</details>


### [263] [Detecting Fluent Optimization-Based Adversarial Prompts via Sequential Entropy Changes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19966)
*Mohammed Alshaalan,Miguel R. D. Rodrigues*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于在线变点检测（CUSUM）的对抗性后缀检测方法CPD，通过监控token级熵流来识别优化型对抗攻击，在多个模型上显著优于现有的困惑度基线，并能精确定位攻击起始位置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的优化型对抗后缀攻击（如GCG、AutoDAN等）能够绕过对齐的大语言模型，同时保持文本流畅性，使得基于静态和滑动窗口困惑度的检测器效果减弱。需要一种更有效的检测方法来应对这类攻击。

Method: 将对抗后缀检测建模为token级下一词元熵流的在线变点检测问题。利用LLM系统提示词估计稳健的基线，对用户token熵进行标准化处理，并应用单侧CUSUM统计量。该方法无需训练、模型无关、可在线运行，并能定位对抗后缀的起始位置。

Result: 在包含1,012个优化型后缀攻击和1,012个困惑度控制的良性提示词的基准测试中，CPD在六个开源聊天模型上的F1分数均优于最强的滑动窗口困惑度基线。在LLaMA-2-7B上达到AUROC 0.88和F1 0.82。CPD能将79.6%的触发器定位在对抗后缀内（滑动窗口困惑度仅为17-46%）。作为LLaMA Guard的轻量级门控，CPD可减少17-22%的守护调用次数，同时保持检测质量。

Conclusion: CPD是一种有效的对抗性后缀检测方法，不仅能提升检测性能，还能精确定位攻击位置，并可作为轻量级门控模块优化现有安全系统的部署效率。

Abstract: Optimization-based adversarial suffixes can jailbreak aligned large language models (LLMs) while remaining fluent, weakening static and windowed perplexity-based detectors. We cast adversarial suffix detection as an online change-point detection problem over the token-level next-token entropy stream. Using the LLM system prompt to estimate a robust baseline, we standardize user-token entropies and apply a one-sided CUSUM statistic. The resulting detector, CPD Online (CPD), is model-agnostic, training-free, runs online, and localizes the adversarial suffix onset. On a benchmark of 1,012 optimization-based suffix attacks (GCG, AutoDAN, AdvPrompter, BEAST, AutoDAN-HGA) and 1,012 perplexity-controlled benign prompts, CPD improves F1 over the strongest windowed-perplexity baseline on all six open-weight chat models (LLaMA-2-7B/13B, Vicuna-7B/13B, Qwen2.5-7B/14B). On LLaMA-2-7B at the canonical CUSUM setting ($k=0$), CPD reaches AUROC $0.88$ and F1 $0.82$. Beyond prompt-level detection, CPD concentrates 79.6% of its triggers inside the adversarial suffix, versus 17-46% for windowed perplexity. Finally, when used as a lightweight gate for LLaMA Guard, CPD reduces guard calls by 17-22% on a high-volume, benign-dominated deployment while preserving guard-level detection quality

</details>


### [264] [Block-Sphere Vector Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19972)
*Heesang Ann,Joongkyu Lee,Min-hwan Oh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首先统一比较了现有旋转量化方法（EDEN、RabitQ、TurboQuant），揭示了它们在不同失真准则下的相对优势；其次提出了Block-Sphere Quantization（BlockQuant），这是一种基于球形几何的旋转块量化新算法，在理论和实验上均优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 向量量化是可扩展机器学习系统的基础组件。近年来出现的旋转量化方法（EDEN、RabitQ、TurboQuant）各具优势，但由于它们基于不同的失真准则、概率假设和实现条件，难以进行公平比较。因此需要统一的框架来理解这些方法的相对性能，并在此基础上设计更好的量化算法。

Method: 1. 提供了旋转量化方法（EDEN、RabitQ、TurboQuant）的统一理论比较框架，基于不同失真准则（MSE、期望内积失真、高概率控制）分析各自优势。
2. 提出Block-Sphere Quantization（BlockQuant）算法，该算法针对随机旋转向量的球形几何特性进行设计，在球面上对块进行量化，而非采用坐标级量化，从而更好地保持旋转嵌入的几何结构。

Result: 理论分析表明：EDEN和TurboQuant在MSE失真方面表现优越；EDEN在期望内积失真方面有效；RabitQ提供强高概率控制。BlockQuant在重建MSE和期望内积失真两方面理论上均优于所考虑的基线方法。在真实嵌入数据集和长上下文LLM推理任务上的实验结果与理论改进一致，展示了实际性能提升。

Conclusion: 本文通过统一的理论框架澄清了现有旋转量化方法的相对优势，表明这些方法的优势是依赖于准则而非绝对的。提出的BlockQuant算法利用块球形设计，在保持旋转向量几何特性方面更具优势，理论和实验均证明了其在重建误差和内积失真方面的优越性，为向量量化领域提供了新的技术选择。

Abstract: Vector quantization is a fundamental primitive for scalable machine learning systems, enabling memory-efficient storage, fast retrieval, and compressed inference. Recent rotation-based quantizers such as EDEN, RabitQ, and TurboQuant have introduced strong guarantees and empirical performance, but the surrounding comparisons have been difficult to interpret because they rely on different distortion criteria, probability regimes, and implementation assumptions. As our first contribution, we provide a unified theoretical comparison of these methods and show that their relative advantages are criterion-dependent rather than absolute: EDEN and TurboQuant are favorable for MSE distortion, EDEN is also effective for expected inner-product distortion, and RabitQ provides strong high-probability control. This comparison further clarifies that EDEN provides particularly strong guarantees for expected distortion measures. As our second contribution, we introduce Block-Sphere Quantization (BlockQuant), a new rotation-based block quantization algorithm designed around the spherical geometry of randomly rotated vectors. Unlike coordinate-wise quantizers, BlockQuant quantizes blocks on the sphere, preserving the geometry of rotated embeddings more faithfully. We prove that this block-spherical design theoretically improves over the baselines considered in this paper for both reconstruction MSE and expected inner-product distortion. Our experiments on real embedding datasets and long-context LLM inference tasks show practical gains that are consistent with our theoretical improvements.

</details>


### [265] [Fast Tensorization of Neural Networks via Slice-wise Feature Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19842)
*Safa Hamreras,Sukhbinder Singh,Román Orús*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于切片特征蒸馏的可扩展神经网络张量化压缩框架，通过模块化策略在减少数据和训练成本的同时实现了接近无损的压缩效果，并成功应用于GPT-2 XL等大规模模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的张量分解压缩方法依赖于昂贵的全局微调（global finetuning），这在处理大规模模型时面临优化困难、数据需求大、难以并行化等挑战。为了解决这些问题，本文旨在开发一种高效、可扩展且模块化的压缩方法。

Method: 提出了一种切片特征蒸馏框架。该方法将预训练网络分解为独立的切片（slice），例如单层、块（如卷积层或MLP）或连续层小组。不同于全局优化，该方法对每个切片独立进行张量化，目标是复现原始模型在对应位置的中间表示。这种模块化策略避免了全局微调，支持并行优化，并显著降低了数据需求。

Result: 在ResNet-34上的实验表明，该方法在中等压缩率下实现了接近无损的压缩，相比传统全局张量化方法在精度恢复和优化速度上均有显著提升。在GPT-2 XL上的实验验证了该方法的可扩展性及其在分布式环境下处理大规模模型的适用性。

Conclusion: 切片特征蒸馏框架为神经网络压缩提供了一种高效、可扩展的解决方案，克服了传统全局张量化方法的瓶颈，证明了其在从视觉模型到大语言模型等多种架构中的有效性和实用价值。

Abstract: We propose a scalable tensorization framework for neural network compression based on slice-wise feature distillation. Unlike conventional tensor decomposition methods that rely on costly global finetuning, our approach decomposes the network into slices consisting of either individual layers or blocks (e.g., convolutional layers or MLPs), or small groups of consecutive layers, and tensorizes each slice independently to reproduce the intermediate representations of the original pretrained model. This modular strategy improves accuracy recovery, reduces data requirements, and enables efficient parallel optimization. Experiments on ResNet-34 show significant gains over conventional global tensorization, achieving near-lossless compression at moderate compression rates with faster optimization. Results on GPT-2 XL further demonstrate the scalability of the method and its applicability to large-scale models, particularly in distributed settings.

</details>


### [266] [Learning with Foresight: Enhancing Neural Routing Policy via Multi-Node Lookahead Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19975)
*Xia Jiang,Yaoxin Wu,Yew-Soon Ong,Yingqian Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了多节点前瞻预测（MnLP）训练策略，通过预测多个未来节点解决神经策略在车辆路径问题中的短视决策问题，在保持推理效率的同时显著提升了泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经策略训练范式主要关注下一节点预测，导致决策短视，削弱了长期规划能力，限制了车辆路径问题的求解效果。

Method: 提出多节点前瞻预测（MnLP）训练策略，扩展监督学习范式以同时预测多个未来节点；引入因果性和可丢弃的MnLP模块，仅在训练阶段运行；通过在损失函数中引入多深度辅助监督，赋予模型长期上下文理解能力。

Result: MnLP在多种问题规模、分布和真实基准测试中均优于现有训练方法，显著提升了神经策略的泛化能力；可无缝集成到多种神经架构中，不增加额外推理开销。

Conclusion: MnLP通过多节点预测有效解决了神经策略的短视问题，在保持推理效率的同时实现了长期规划能力，为车辆路径问题的神经求解提供了更优的训练范式。

Abstract: Neural policies have shown promise in solving vehicle routing problems due to their reduced reliance on handcrafted heuristics. However, current training paradigms suffer from a fundamental limitation: they primarily focus on next-node prediction for solution construction, resulting in myopic decision-making that undermines long-horizon planning capacity. To this end, we introduce Multi-node Lookahead Prediction (MnLP), a novel training strategy that extends the supervised learning paradigm to predict multiple future nodes simultaneously.
