We incorporate causal and discardable MnLP modules that operate exclusively during training, facilitating models to anticipate multi-step decisions while preserving inference-time efficiency. By incorporating multi-depth auxiliary supervision into the loss function, MnLP equips neural policies with the ability of long-range contextual understanding. Experimentally, MnLP outperforms existing training methods, improving the generalization capability of neural policies across various problem sizes, distributions, and real-world benchmarks. Moreover, MnLP can be seamlessly integrated into diverse neural architectures without introducing additional inference overhead.

</details>


### [267] [LLM Benchmark Datasets Should Be Contamination-Resistant](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19999)
*Ali Al-Lawati,Jason Lucas,Dongwon Lee,Suhang Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出LLM基准数据集被污染的普遍性问题，提出构建抗污染（不可学习但支持推理）基准数据集的方案，利用Transformer架构的推理-训练不对称性实现数据集在不同架构间的互操作性，呼吁社区关注此问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究表明许多基准数据集包含在大语言模型的预训练语料库中（即被污染），削弱了其作为模型泛化能力可靠衡量指标的价值，因此需要开发抗污染的基准数据集。

Method: 首先揭示基准数据集污染的广泛存在并概述抗污染数据集的特性；其次利用Transformer架构中推理与训练管线的不对称性实现抗污染能力；最后提出数学方法使数据集能在不同LLM架构间互操作。

Result: 提出了构建抗污染基准数据集的理论框架，即数据集应具有“不可学习”特性但仍支持“推理”，并基于Transformer架构不对称性设计了实现方案。

Conclusion: 呼吁社区通过推进新型抗污染方法论、开发支持方法和平台、以及在现有评估流程中采用抗污染基准数据集三方面措施，确保LLM基准测试的可靠性。

Abstract: Benchmark datasets are critical for reproducible, reliable, and discriminative evaluation of LLMs. However, recent studies reveal that many benchmark datasets are included in pretraining corpora, i.e., $\textit{contaminated}$, which diminishes their value as reliable measures of model generalization. In this paper, we argue that benchmark datasets should be $\textit{contamination-resistant}$, i.e., $\textit{unlearnable}$, but support $\textit{inference}$. To accomplish this, we first highlight the wide prevalence of benchmark dataset contamination and outline the properties of contamination-resistant datasets. Second, we highlight how the asymmetry between the inference and training pipelines in the Transformer architecture can be leveraged to support contamination-resistance. Third, we outline mathematical advancements to make these datasets interoperable across various LLM architectures. Based on the above, we call on the community to ensure the reliability of LLM benchmarking by: (i) advancing novel contamination-resistant methodologies, (ii) developing supporting methods and platforms, and (iii) adopting contamination-resistant benchmarks into existing evaluation pipelines.

</details>


### [268] [Hierarchical Contrastive Learning for Multi-Domain Protein-Ligand Binding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19902)
*Shuo Zhang,Rongqi Hong,Huifeng Zhang,Jian K. Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了HCLBind框架，通过自监督学习和分层诱饵策略解决多域蛋白质结合亲和力预测难题，显著提升了预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有几何深度学习方法将蛋白质视为整体静态图，受刚体假设和柔性区域噪声限制，难以处理域间动力学调控分子识别的多域蛋白质结合亲和力预测问题。

Method: 提出自监督框架HCLBind，采用从一般到特定的预训练范式；设计分层诱饵策略（单域蛋白坐标扰动学习局部约束，多域复合物域间旋转学习全局构象）；集成域门控图注意力网络和跨模态注意力机制；在基础模型上应用LoRA进行高效优化。

Result: 在PDBBind数据集上的实验表明，HCLBind有效学习了判别性界面特征，提供了鲁棒的不确定性估计，克服了标准监督学习的局限性。

Conclusion: HCLBind通过解耦几何表示学习与亲和力回归，成功建模域间动力学，为多域蛋白质结合亲和力预测提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Predicting protein-ligand binding affinity remains intractable for multi-domain proteins, where inter-domain dynamics govern molecular recognition. Existing geometric deep learning methods typically treat proteins as monolithic static graphs, suffering from rigid-body assumptions and aleatoric noise in flexible regions. To address this, we introduced HCLBind, a self-supervised framework that decouples geometric representation learning from affinity regression. HCLBind leverages a general-to-specific pre-training paradigm on the Q-BioLiP database to learn a robust physical grammar of binding. We propose a novel hierarchical decoy strategy: the model learns local physicochemical constraints through protein coordinate perturbation in single-domain proteins and global conformational geometry through inter-domain rotation in multi-domain complexes. Our hybrid architecture integrates a domain-gated graph attention network and cross-modal attention to explicitly prioritize domain interfaces. Furthermore, we employ LoRA on protein and ligand foundation models, ensuring efficient optimization while preserving evolutionary knowledge. Experiments on PDBBind demonstrate that HCLBind effectively learns discriminative interface features and provides robust uncertainty estimation, overcoming the limitations of standard supervised learning. The code is available at https://github.com/jiankliu/HCLBind.

</details>


### [269] [Training Neural Networks with Optimal Double-Bayesian Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20009)
*Vy Bui,Hang Yu,Karthik Kantipudi,Ziv Yaniv,Stefan Jaeger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于双重贝叶斯决策机制的概率框架，用于自动推导随机梯度下降中的理论最优学习率，解决了传统超参数选择依赖经验的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在神经网络训练中，基于梯度的反向传播优化策略需要设定超参数，尤其是学习率。然而，寻找最优超参数极具挑战性，目前主要依赖经验实验，缺乏理论指导。不恰当的参数选择可能导致过拟合或偏差，因此亟需一种有理论依据的方法来确定学习率。

Method: 提出了一种新的学习率概率框架，将经典贝叶斯统计发展为包含两个对抗性贝叶斯过程的“双重贝叶斯”决策机制，并从中推导出理论最优的学习率，用于指导随机梯度下降训练。

Result: 在多种分类、分割和检测任务的实验中，验证了该理论推导出的学习率具有实际有效性，证明了该框架在不同任务上的适用性和性能提升。

Conclusion: 该双重贝叶斯框架为网络训练提供了理论指导，能够自动推导最优学习率，减少了对经验调参的依赖，对提升模型训练效率和性能具有重要意义。

Abstract: Backpropagation with gradient descent is a common optimization strategy employed by most neural network architectures in machine learning. However, finding optimal hyperparameters to guide training has proven challenging. While it is widely acknowledged that selecting appropriate parameters is crucial for avoiding overfitting and achieving unbiased outcomes, this choice remains largely based on empirical experiments and experience. This paper presents a new probabilistic framework for the learning rate, a key parameter in stochastic gradient descent. The framework develops classic Bayesian statistics into a double-Bayesian decision mechanism involving two antagonistic Bayesian processes. A theoretically optimal learning rate can be derived from these two processes and used for stochastic gradient descent. Experiments across various classification, segmentation, and detection tasks corroborate the practical significance of the theoretically derived learning rate. The paper also discusses the ramifications of the proposed double-Bayesian framework for network training and model performance.

</details>


### [270] [When Critics Disagree: Adaptive Reward Poisoning Attacks in RIS-Aided Wireless Control System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20037)
*Deemah H. Tashman,Soumaya Cherkaoui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对RIS辅助认知无线电网络中SAC智能体的自适应奖励投毒攻击方法(DGRP)，通过利用评论家网络的分歧来诱导策略学习次优动作，显著降低了系统性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于学习的无线控制系统面临奖励投毒攻击的显著风险，现有研究缺乏针对RIS辅助网络中深度强化学习智能体的攻击方法研究，需要揭示此类系统的脆弱性以促进鲁棒性设计。

Method: 提出分歧引导的奖励投毒攻击(DGRP)，在SAC双评论家网络表现出显著分歧的状态(特别是高杠杆、高不确定性状态)下，针对性地破坏奖励信号，扭曲价值估计并引导策略向次优动作方向发展。

Result: 实验表明DGRP显著降低了RIS带来的性能提升并恶化传输质量；相比周期性和探索触发式攻击基线，DGRP造成更大损害；攻击参数对学习过程有重要影响。

Conclusion: 分歧感知的攻击威胁对RIS辅助网络中的深度强化学习系统构成严重挑战，在评估DRL鲁棒性时必须考虑此类攻击，突出了设计安全强化学习算法的必要性。

Abstract: Reward-poisoning attacks present a significant risk to learning-based wireless control systems. Given this, we propose a Disagreement-Guided Reward Poisoning (DGRP) adaptive attack on a Soft Actor-Critic (SAC) agent. In a Cognitive Radio Network (CRN) environment assisted by Reconfigurable Intelligent Surfaces (RIS), the SAC agent is tasked with maximizing the long-term secondary users' (SUs) rate by simultaneously optimizing the transmission power of the SU transmitter and the RIS phase shifts. DGRP corrupts rewards, particularly when the SAC dual critics exhibit substantial disagreement-especially in high-leverage, high-uncertainty states-resulting in distorted value estimations and guiding the policy towards suboptimal actions. Our findings demonstrate that DGRP substantially diminishes the performance improvements typically provided by RIS and degrades transmission quality. We further investigate key attack parameters and determine their impact on learning. In comparison to periodic-timing and exploration-triggered baselines, DGRP consistently causes greater damage, highlighting the necessity of considering disagreement-aware threats when evaluating the robustness of Deep Reinforcement Learning (DRL) in RIS-assisted networks.

</details>


### [271] [JAXenstein: Accelerated Benchmarking for First-Person Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19926)
*Ruo Yu Tao,George Konidaris*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文推出了JAXenstein，一个基于JAX的开源基准测试平台，通过实现Wolfenstein 3D渲染引擎，为视觉第一人称任务提供快速且可扩展的实验环境，填补了JAX强化学习生态系统中此类基准的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习算法的快速发展依赖于具有挑战性的基准测试，而研究者在问题设置上的迭代速度直接影响算法开发效率。尽管现代机器学习工具（如JAX库）支持快速且可扩展的算法开发，但JAX强化学习生态系统缺乏用于测试视觉第一人称任务的基准。此类任务对于评估智能体的探索能力及其在部分可观测环境中的表现至关重要，因此迫切需要填补这一空白。

Method: 开发了JAXenstein，一个基于JAX的开源基准测试平台。该平台实现了Wolfenstein 3D渲染引擎，专门用于视觉第一人称任务的快速且可扩展的实验，并且可以轻松扩展到更复杂的第一人称任务领域。

Result: JAXenstein在运行速度上比同类基于视觉的基准测试快数倍，同时具备良好的扩展性，能够支持更复杂的第一人称任务领域的实验需求。

Conclusion: JAXenstein有效解决了JAX强化学习生态系统中视觉第一人称任务基准缺失的问题，为研究者提供了一个高速、可扩展的实验平台，有助于加速强化学习算法在复杂视觉环境中的开发与评估。

Abstract: The progression of reinforcement learning algorithms have been driven by challenging benchmarks. The rate in which a researcher can iterate on a problem setting directly impacts the speed of algorithm development. Modern machine learning has produced tools that allow for fast and scalable algorithm development like the JAX library. With the availability of these tools, a serious bottleneck in algorithm development is the availability of large and complex domains for experimentation. Most notably, the JAX reinforcement learning ecosystem does not have any benchmarks that test visual first-person tasks; these domains are crucial for testing both exploration and an agent's ability to overcome partial observability. We introduce JAXenstein: an open-source JAX-based benchmark that implements the Wolfenstein 3D rendering engine for fast and scalable experimentation in visual first-person tasks. JAXenstein is several times faster than comparable vision-based benchmarks, and is easily extensible to more complex first-person domains.

</details>


### [272] [INSHAPE: Instance-Level Shapelets for Interpretable Time-Series Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20088)
*Seongjun Lee,Seokhyun Lee,Changhee Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了INSHAPE框架，通过发现实例特定的变长判别性时间模式(shapelets)并建模其时间依赖关系，解决了现有方法中群体级shapelets与实例特征不匹配以及忽视模式间交互的问题，在分类性能和可解释性上均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的时间序列分类方法主要关注在整体数据集上优化的群体级shapelets，存在两个局限性：(1) 群体级模式往往与实例特定特征不一致，导致性能欠佳和潜在误导性解释；(2) 大多数方法将shapelets视为独立实体，忽视了多个模式之间的重要时间依赖性和交互关系。

Method: 提出了INSHAPE框架，发现每个时间序列特定的变长判别性时间模式。该方法将这些模式识别为非重叠片段，并对其时间依赖关系进行建模，提供清晰的实例级解释。同时采用自底向上的方法，将实例级shapelets聚合为原型（群体级）shapelets，从而连接局部和全局可解释性。

Result: 在128个UCR和30个UEA基准数据集上的广泛实验表明，INSHAPE在分类性能上持续优于最先进的基于shapelet的方法，同时提供更直观和可解释的见解。

Conclusion: INSHAPE成功解决了传统shapelet方法中群体级模式与实例特征不匹配的问题，通过实例级shapelet发现和时间依赖建模，实现了强预测性能与良好可解释性的统一，为时间序列分类提供了一种更有效且透明的解决方案。

Abstract: Discovering shapelets -- i.e., discriminative temporal patterns within time series -- has been widely studied to address the inherent complexity of time-series classification (TSC) and to make model decision-making processes more transparent. However, existing methods primarily focus on population-level shapelets optimized across the entire dataset, which leads to two fundamental limitations: (i) population-level patterns often misalign with instance-specific features, resulting in suboptimal performance and potentially misleading interpretations, and (ii) most methods treat shapelets as independent entities, overlooking important temporal dependencies and interactions among multiple patterns. To address these limitations, we propose INSHAPE, an interpretable TSC framework that discovers variable-length, discriminative temporal patterns specific to each time series. INSHAPE identifies these patterns as non-overlapping segments and models their temporal dependencies, thereby providing clear instance-level interpretations while achieving strong predictive performance. Furthermore, INSHAPE bridges local and global interpretability through a bottom-up approach, aggregating instance-level shapelets into prototypical (population-level) shapelets. Extensive experiments on 128 UCR and 30 UEA benchmark datasets show that INSHAPE consistently outperforms state-of-the-art shapelet-based methods while providing more intuitive and interpretable insights.

</details>


### [273] [Draft Less, Retrieve More: Hybrid Tree Construction for Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20104)
*Yuhao Shen,Tianyu Liu,Xinyi Hu,Quan Kong,Baolin Zhang,Jun Dai,Jun Zhang,Shuang Ge,Lei Chen,Yue Li,Mingcheng Wan,Cong Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Graft框架，通过"剪枝-检索"的协同机制解决推测解码中草稿树扩展带来的开销问题，在保持无损和无训练的前提下实现了端到端加速的显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推测解码通过草稿-验证范式加速大语言模型推理。为最大化接受率，近期方法构建大规模草稿树，但带来了严重的显存带宽和计算开销，成为端到端加速的瓶颈。虽然动态深度剪枝能通过移除边缘分支减少延迟，但也丢弃了潜在有效候选，阻碍了接受率达到稠密树的上界。作者发现从稠密到剪枝草稿的转换释放了大量计算预算，这为打破帕累托权衡提供了关键机遇。

Method: 提出Graft补偿框架，将剪枝和检索作为相互增强的操作耦合。剪枝为检索提供充足预算，检索补偿剪枝引起的覆盖损失并恢复接受长度。采用顺序的"先剪枝后嫁接"机制，将高预测性的检索token附加到剪枝开放的位置，以近乎零开销填补拓扑缺口。该框架完全无需训练且无损。

Result: 在短上下文、长上下文和大规模模型等实际部署场景中建立了新的帕累托前沿。在短上下文基准测试中，实现了最高5.41倍的加速；在大规模Qwen3-235B模型上，相比EAGLE-3的平均加速提升高达21.8%。同时提供了将Graft应用于DFlash风格块草稿范式的初步探索。

Conclusion: Graft成功打破了推测解码中速度与接受率之间的帕累托权衡，通过创新的剪枝-检索协同机制在多种实际部署场景中实现了显著性能提升，为推测解码技术的发展提供了新思路。

Abstract: Speculative decoding (SD) accelerates large language model inference by leveraging a draft-then-verify paradigm. To maximize the acceptance rate, recent methods construct expansive draft trees, which unfortunately incur severe VRAM bandwidth and computational overheads that bottleneck end-to-end speedups. While dynamic-depth pruning can reduce this latency by removing marginal branches, it also discards potentially valid candidates, preventing the acceptance rate from reaching the upper bound of dense trees. In this paper, we identify a critical opportunity in resource allocation: the transition from dense to pruned drafting frees up significant computational budget. To break this Pareto tradeoff, we introduce Graft, a compensation framework that couples pruning and retrieval as mutually reinforcing operations. Pruning supplies sufficient budget for retrieval, while retrieval compensates for pruning-induced coverage loss and recovers accepted length. By employing a sequential `prune-then-graft' mechanism, Graft attaches highly predictive retrieved tokens into positions opened by pruning, filling the topological gaps with near-zero overhead. Graft is entirely training-free and lossless. Comprehensive evaluations show that Graft establishes a new Pareto frontier across practical deployment settings, including short-context generation, long-context generation, and large-scale models. On short-context benchmarks, it achieves up to 5.41$\times$ speedup and improves average speedup over EAGLE-3 by up to 21.8% on the large-scale Qwen3-235B. We also provide a preliminary exploration of applying Graft to the DFlash-style block drafting paradigm, offering initial evidence and insights for extending grafting beyond autoregressive draft trees.

</details>


### [274] [Exploiting Non-Negativity in DAG Structure Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19947)
*Samuel Rey,Madeline navarro,Gonzalo Mateos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对非负权重DAG学习问题，提出了一种新的连续优化方法，通过乘子法求解正则化问题，证明了在总体情形下真实DAG是唯一的全局极小值点，且优化景观良好，在合成和真实数据上优于现有连续DAG学习方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DAG学习是信号处理、机器学习和因果推断的核心任务，但无环性是全局组合性质，导致学习具有挑战性。现有的连续无环性约束方法仍面临困难的非凸优化景观和退化的一阶最优性条件问题。本文旨在利用非负边权重这一额外结构来简化无环性表征，获得更良好的优化景观。

Method: 1. 将注意力限制在具有非负边权重的DAG上，利用该结构获得更简单的无环性表征；2. 基于此表征，构建正则化非负DAG学习问题；3. 采用乘子法（Method of Multipliers）开发求解算法；4. 分析非负性带来的良性优化景观。

Result: 在总体情形下证明了：真实DAG是所提增广拉格朗日形式的唯一全局极小值点；优化景观中不存在伪内部驻点；真实DAG是唯一的无环KKT点。在合成数据和真实数据上的数值实验表明，所提方法优于现有的连续DAG学习方法。

Conclusion: 通过利用非负边权重约束，本文成功简化了DAG学习中的无环性表征，建立了具有良性优化景观的连续优化框架，从理论和实验两个层面验证了方法的有效性，为DAG学习提供了新的思路和工具。

Abstract: This work addresses the problem of learning directed acyclic graphs (DAGs) from nodal observations generated by a linear structural equation model. DAG learning is a central task in signal processing, machine learning, and causal inference, but it remains challenging because acyclicity is a global combinatorial property. Continuous acyclicity constraints have led to important algorithmic advances by replacing the discrete DAG constraint with smooth equality constraints. However, existing formulations still involve difficult non-convex optimization landscapes and may suffer from degenerate first-order optimality conditions. Here, we restrict attention to DAGs with non-negative edge weights and exploit this additional structure to obtain a simpler characterization of acyclicity. Building on this characterization, we formulate a regularized non-negative DAG learning problem and develop an algorithm based on the method of multipliers. We further analyze the benign optimization landscape induced by non-negativity. In the population regime, we show that the true DAG is the unique global minimizer of the proposed augmented-Lagrangian formulation; moreover, the landscape contains no spurious interior stationary points, and the true DAG is the only acyclic KKT point. Numerical experiments on synthetic and real-world data show that the proposed method improves over state-of-the-art continuous DAG-learning alternatives.

</details>


### [275] [Beyond Isotropy in JEPAs: Hamiltonian Geometry and Symplectic Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20107)
*Robert Jenkinson Alvarez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了JEPAs中使用欧几里得各向同性正则化的缺陷，提出HamJEPA方法，通过哈密顿相空间表示和辛映射跨视角耦合显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: JEPAs通常将单视图嵌入正则化为各向同性高斯分布，隐式地引入了欧几里得对称性。作者发现这并非无害默认设置：对于已知结构的下游几何H，各向同性会带来闭式代价；当下游几何未知时，不存在与几何无关的固定边际目标是规范的，任何固定协方差形状都可能对某些结构化几何产生最大错位。此外，即使oracle单视图边际也无法识别JEPA视图间预测耦合。

Method: 提出HamJEPA方法，将每个视图编码为相空间状态(q,p)，使用学习的哈密顿蛙跳映射(leapfrog map)预测视图间转换，同时通过非各向同性尺度和谱下限防止坍塌。核心原则是将结构偏差引入跨视图耦合而非固定编码器边际。

Result: 在CIFAR-100上，HamJEPA在30 epochs时相比SIGReg提升+4.89 kNN@20和+3.52线性探测点，在80 epochs时提升+6.45 kNN@20和+10.64线性探测点。匹配的MLP预测器消融实验证明辛耦合是邻域几何增益的关键因素。在ImageNet-100上，HamJEPA-q在45 epochs时提升+4.82 kNN@20和+7.52线性探测点。

Conclusion: JEPAs中的结构偏差应进入跨视图耦合而非固定编码器边际，HamJEPA通过哈密顿力学的辛对称性实现了更好的邻域几何表示学习，显著优于传统各向同性正则化方法。

Abstract: JEPAs often regularize one-view embeddings toward an isotropic Gaussian, implicitly baking Euclidean symmetry into the representation. We show that this is not merely a benign default. For a known structured downstream geometry $H\succ0$, the minimax and maximum-entropy covariance under a Hamiltonian energy budget is $(c/d)H^{-1}$, and Euclidean isotropy incurs a closed-form price of isotropy. More importantly, when the downstream geometry is unknown, no geometry-independent fixed marginal target is canonical: every fixed covariance shape can be maximally misaligned for some structured geometry. We further show that even oracle one-view marginals do not identify the JEPA view-to-view predictive coupling. These results suggest that the structural bias in JEPAs should enter the cross-view coupling rather than a fixed encoder marginal. We instantiate this principle with \textbf{HamJEPA}, which encodes each view as a phase-space state $(q,p)$ and predicts view-to-view transitions with a learned Hamiltonian leapfrog map, while non-isotropic scale and spectral floors prevent collapse. In a deliberately headless token protocol, HamJEPA improves over SIGReg on CIFAR-100 by $+4.89$ kNN@20 and $+3.52$ linear-probe points at 30 epochs, and by $+6.45$ kNN@20 and $+10.64$ linear-probe points at 80 epochs, while a matched MLP predictor ablation shows that the symplectic coupling is the ingredient driving the neighborhood-geometry gain. On ImageNet-100, HamJEPA-$q$ improves by $+4.82$ kNN@20 and $+7.52$ linear-probe points at 45 epochs.

</details>


### [276] [Learning Orthonormal Bases for Function Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19959)
*Hamidreza Kamkari,Mohammad Sina Nabizadeh,Justin Solomon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用神经网络在李群流形上通过ODE路径来参数化和优化无限维正交基的方法，证明了有限秩生成子的通用性，并展示了其在函数数据主成分、线性算子特征函数等场景中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的无限维正交基（如傅里叶或小波基）是固定的，无法根据特定问题或数据集的结构进行自适应调整。为了解决这一问题，作者希望利用神经网络来表示并优化这些正交基，使其能够适应特定的任务需求。

Method: 将目标正交基视为正交群李流形上的点或从参考基（如傅里叶基）出发的连续路径的终点。路径上的演化由斜伴随积分算子控制的常微分方程（ODE）描述。作者使用神经网络定义有限秩生成子来参数化这些ODE，从而在函数空间中优化正交基。

Result: 证明了方法的通用性：即使使用秩为2的生成子，ODE积分解在适当的算子拓扑下在正交群中是稠密的。这意味着对于任何目标正交基，都存在由有限秩生成子驱动的路径，能从参考基出发无限逼近该目标基。实验成功将傅里叶基转换为函数数据的主成分、线性算子的特征函数以及物理仿真的动态模态。

Conclusion: 该框架为无限维正交基的自适应表示提供了一种理论基础扎实且灵活的方法，通过有限秩生成子在李流形上的路径积分实现了对任意正交基的逼近，拓展了正交基在数据驱动问题中的应用潜力。

Abstract: Infinite-dimensional orthonormal basis expansions play a central role in representing and computing with function spaces due to their favorable linear algebraic properties. However, common bases such as Fourier or wavelets are fixed and do not adapt to the structure of a given problem or dataset. In this paper, we aim to represent these bases with neural networks and optimize them. Our key idea is that any target infinite-dimensional orthonormal basis can be viewed either as a point on the Lie manifold of the orthogonal group, or equivalently, as the endpoint of a continuous path on that manifold that connects a reference basis, e.g. Fourier, to that target. Paths on the Lie manifold satisfy ordinary differential equations (ODEs) governed by skew-adjoint integral operators. Using neural networks to define finite-rank generators of such ODEs allows us to parameterize and optimize orthonormal bases in function space. While relying on finite-rank generators to model infinite operators might seem restrictive, we prove a universality result: even with a rank-2 generator, the integrated solutions of the ODE are dense in the orthogonal group under the appropriate operator topology. In other words, for any target orthonormal basis, there exists a path originating from a reference basis and driven by finite-rank generators that gets arbitrarily close to that target basis. We demonstrate the flexibility of our framework by transforming the Fourier basis into the principal components of a functional dataset, eigenfunctions of linear operators, or dynamic modes of energy-preserving physical simulations.

</details>


### [277] [Toto 2.0: Time Series Forecasting Enters the Scaling Era](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20119)
*Emaad Khwaja,Chris Lettieri,Gerald Woo,Eden Belouadah,Marc Cenac,Guillaume Jarry,Enguerrand Paquin,Xunyi Zhao,Viktoriya Zhukov,Othmane Abou-Amal,Chenghao Liu,Ameet Talwalkar,David Asker*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了时序基础模型具有缩放性，发布了包含五个模型的 Toto 2.0 家族，在三个基准测试上达到新 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索并验证时序预测模型是否具有随着参数规模增加（从400万到25亿参数）而持续提升预测质量的缩放能力。

Method: 提出了一套统一的训练配方，设计了 Toto 2.0 架构，采用了 u-muP 超参数迁移管道，并在特定数据集上训练了五个不同规模的模型。

Result: 在 BOOM（可观测性基准）、GIFT-Eval（通用基准）以及 TIME（抗污染基准）三个预测基准测试上均取得了新的最先进（SOTA）性能。

Conclusion: 成功验证了时序基础模型的缩放定律，并以 Apache 2.0 协议开源了五个基础模型权重，为社区提供了高性能的预测工具。

Abstract: We show that time series foundation models scale: a single training recipe produces reliable forecast-quality improvements from 4M to 2.5B parameters. We release Toto 2.0, a family of five open-weights forecasting models trained under this recipe. The Toto 2.0 family sets a new state of the art on three forecasting benchmarks: BOOM, our observability benchmark; GIFT-Eval, the standard general-purpose benchmark; and the recent contamination-resistant TIME benchmark. This report describes our experimental results and details the design decisions behind Toto 2.0: its architecture and training recipe, training data, and the u-muP hyperparameter transfer pipeline. All five base checkpoints are released under Apache 2.0.

</details>


### [278] [Normative Networks for Source Separation via Local Plasticity and Dendritic Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19965)
*Bariscan Bozkurt,Efe Ali Gorguner,Francesco Innocenti,Rafal Bogacz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为“预测熵最大化”的生物 plausible 在线盲源分离算法，仅依赖局部权重更新，在源相关性和观测噪声下表现稳健，且优于现有生物可行方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于熵最大化的盲源分离在线算法涉及复杂且非局部的循环动力学，难以在生物神经网络中实现。研究旨在开发一种仅需局部权重更新、生物可行的在线算法，以处理结构化且潜在相关的源信号。

Method: 提出预测熵最大化方法，通过最小化一个近似熵的目标函数实现盲源分离。算法采用预测式神经架构：前馈突触遵循误差驱动规则（可通过树突机制实现），侧向抑制连接通过局部赫布可塑性学习，并利用简单输出非线性施加源域约束。理论推导了代理误差的谱界限。

Result: 实验表明，该方法在源相关性增加和观测噪声条件下保持鲁棒性；性能优于依赖更强独立性或去相关假设的生物可行算法，并与基于精确行列式和相关信息的基线方法具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 局部可塑性和自适应侧向抑制可以从在结构化源域上最大化正则化二阶熵的过程中自然涌现，为生物神经系统执行盲源分离提供了新的计算原理和机制解释。

Abstract: Blind source separation (BSS) is a natural framework for studying how latent causes may be recovered from sensory mixtures, but deriving online and biologically plausible algorithms for structured (i.e., constrained to known domains) and potentially correlated sources remains challenging. Recent work has derived neural networks for BSS from maximization of an entropy measure, yet its online implementations involve complex and nonlocal recurrent dynamics. Motivated by this perspective, we propose Predictive Entropy Maximization, which achieves competitive performance in BSS, using only local weight updates. The method employs a close approximation of an entropy measure, yielding an objective function with easily interpretable components. Minimizing this objective leads to a predictive neural architecture in which feedforward synapses follow an error-driven rule (that can be realized through dendritic mechanisms), lateral inhibitory connections are learned with local Hebbian plasticity, and source-domain constraints are enforced through simple output nonlinearities. We derive explicit spectral bounds on the surrogate error, characterizing when the approximation is accurate. Empirically, Predictive Entropy Maximization remains robust under increasing source correlation and observation noise, outperforms biologically plausible algorithms that rely on stronger independence or decorrelation assumptions, and remains competitive with exact determinant- and correlative-information-based baselines. These results show how local plasticity and adaptive lateral inhibition can emerge from maximizing a regularized second-order entropy over structured source domains. Our implementation code is available at https://github.com/BariscanBozkurt/Predictive-Entropy-Maximization.

</details>


### [279] [Atoms of Thought: Universal EEG Representation Learning with Microstates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20182)
*Xinyang Tian,Ruitao Liu,Ziyi Ye,Siyang Xue,Xin Wang,Xuesong Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于微状态的EEG通用表示学习方法，通过将连续EEG信号聚类为离散微状态序列，在睡眠分期、情感识别和运动想象分类等下游任务中表现优于传统时域和频域特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的EEG表示学习主要将EEG视为多变量时间序列，提取时域或频域特征，缺乏对脑活动模式微观结构的建模。本文旨在探索微状态作为一种简单有效的EEG表示方法，以实现跨任务的通用表示学习。

Method: 构建一个通用微状态分词器，通过在大规模医学EEG数据集上进行聚类，将连续EEG信号转换为离散微状态序列。该微状态分词器可通用于多个下游任务，包括睡眠分期、情感识别和运动想象分类。

Result: 实验结果表明，基于微状态的EEG表示学习在不同模型和不同任务下均优于传统时域和频域特征。微状态方法具有更高的可解释性和可扩展性。

Conclusion: 微状态作为EEG的通用表示方法，不仅提升了表示学习性能，还增强了可解释性和可扩展性，为认知神经科学和临床研究开辟了新的应用前景。

Abstract: Learning universal representations from electroencephalogram (EEG) signals is a cutting-edge approach in the field of neuroinformatics and brain-computer interfaces (BCIs). Conventionally, EEG is treated as a multivariate temporal signal, where time- or frequency-domain features are extracted for representation learning. This paper investigates a simple yet effective EEG representation, i.e., microstates. Microstates represent the building blocks of brain activity patterns at a microscopic time scale. We build a universal microstate tokenizer from a large medical EEG dataset by clustering continuous EEG signals into sequences of discrete microstates. The microstate tokenizer is then adopted universally across a series of downstream tasks, including sleep staging, emotion recognition, and motor imagery classification. Experimental results show that EEG representation learning with microstates outperforms traditional time-domain and frequency-domain features under different models and across different tasks. Further analysis shows that microstates offer greater interpretability and scalability, thereby opening up applications in both cognitive neuroscience and clinical research.

</details>


### [280] [Your Neighbors Know: Leveraging Local Neighborhoods for Backdoor Detection in Decentralized Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19969)
*Sayan Biswas,Antoine Boutet,Davide Frey,Romaric Gaudel,Rachid Guerraoui,Maxime Jacovella,Anne-Marie Kermarrec,Dimitri Lerévérend,François Taïani,Martijn de Vos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Argus，一种专为去中心化学习设计的后门检测框架。该方法无需中央协调器或触发器先验知识，通过节点间协作分析和结构相似性度量区分真实后门与误报，在保持模型效用的同时将攻击成功率降低高达90个百分点，并提供了首个针对DL后门检测机制的理论收敛保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 去中心化学习（DL）的协作特性使其容易受到后门攻击，攻击者可植入隐藏恶意行为。然而，现有防御方法往往忽视DL的约束条件（如无中央服务器），导致DL场景下的后门攻击防御研究不足。

Method: 提出Argus框架：诚实节点本地分析接收的模型更新以识别潜在后门触发器；通过结构相似性度量区分真实后门（跨节点一致）和由数据异质性引起的误报（跨节点不一致）；拒绝未通过协作测试的模型更新，并驱逐持续恶意节点。

Result: 在三个标准数据集和三个最先进基线上评估，Argus将攻击成功率降低高达90个百分点，同时保持模型效用与全知oracle相差在5个百分点以内；随着数据异质性增加，Argus相比基线的有效性提升。

Conclusion: Argus是首个为去中心化学习量身定制的后门检测框架，提供了理论收敛保证，证明了过滤可疑模型更新可保持与标准DL相当的收敛速率，且实验验证了其在实际场景中的有效性。

Abstract: Decentralized learning (DL) is an emerging machine learning paradigm where nodes collaboratively train models without a central server. However, the collaborative nature of DL makes it vulnerable to backdoor attacks, where a model is taught to behave normally on standard inputs while executing hidden, malicious actions when encountering data with specific triggers. Backdoor attacks in DL remain understudied and existing defenses often overlook DL constraints. We introduce Argus, a novel backdoor detection framework native to DL that requires neither a central coordinator nor prior knowledge of the trigger. In Argus, honest nodes locally analyze received model updates to identify potential backdoor triggers. Nodes then collectively share their triggers with their neighbors and use a structural similarity metric to separate true backdoors from false alarms induced by data heterogeneity. A key insight is that false positive triggers exhibit inconsistencies across participants while true positive ones show consistent patterns. Model updates that fail this collaborative test are rejected, and persistently malicious senders are eventually evicted. We provide the first theoretical convergence guarantees for a DL-specific backdoor detection mechanism, showing that filtering out suspicious model updates with high probability preserves a convergence rate comparable to standard DL. We implement and evaluate Argus on three standard datasets and against three state-of-the-art baselines. Across settings, Argus reduces attack success rates by up to 90 points compared to no defense, while preserving model utility within 5 percentage points of an omniscient oracle. Furthermore, the effectiveness of Argus compared to baselines improves as data heterogeneity increases.

</details>


### [281] [Fine-Tuning Without Forgetting via Loss-Adaptive Learning Rates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20005)
*Parjanya Prajakta Prashant,Jiongli Zhu,Aldan Creo,Babak Salimi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了FINCH，一种损失自适应的学习率调度策略，通过在训练损失高时降低学习率来显著缓解大语言模型微调中的灾难性遗忘问题，同时保持新任务的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在新数据上进行微调虽然能提升任务性能，但会导致预训练阶段学习到的能力退化，即灾难性遗忘。现有方法通常通过修改微调目标来抑制高损失token，但作者指出这些高损失token对于学习新任务（特别是预训练覆盖不足的任务）至关重要，不应被抑制。因此，需要一种新机制在不抑制高损失token的情况下控制遗忘。

Method: 作者首先从理论上发现单步遗忘程度受学习率与当前训练损失平方根的乘积限制，这表明高损失批次特别容易引发遗忘。基于此观察，作者提出了FINCH（Fast Incremental Learning with No Catastrophic Forgetting），一种损失自适应的学习率调度策略：在高损失批次上降低学习率，随着模型收敛逐步增加学习率，同时保持微调目标函数不变。

Result: 在知识获取、科学领域和低资源语言适应基准测试中，FINCH平均减少了93%的遗忘，同时达到了标准微调的任务性能。在Qwen3-4B知识获取任务上，FINCH将TruthfulQA的性能退化减少了5倍，并逆转了HaluEval的退化，同时更好地保持了置信度校准。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，学习率调度不仅是优化目标任务的工具，更是塑造微调过程中模型行为、平衡新旧知识保留的有效手段。FINCH为解决大语言模型微调中的灾难性遗忘问题提供了一种简单而有效的方法。

Abstract: Fine-tuning large language models on new data improves task performance but degrades capabilities learned during pretraining, a phenomenon known as catastrophic forgetting. Existing methods mitigate this by modifying the fine-tuning objective to suppress high-loss tokens or sequences, but these tokens are essential for learning new tasks, especially those with poor pretraining coverage. In such settings, hard tokens should still contribute to learning, so forgetting must be controlled without suppressing them. We identify a simple mechanism for doing so: per-step forgetting is bounded by the product of the learning rate and the square root of the current training loss. This suggests that high-loss batches are especially prone to inducing forgetting. Motivated by this observation, we introduce FINCH, a loss-adaptive learning-rate schedule that reduces the learning rate on high-loss batches and increases it as the model converges, while leaving the fine-tuning objective unchanged. Across knowledge acquisition, science, and low-resource language adaptation benchmarks, FINCH reduces forgetting by 93% on average while matching the task performance of standard fine-tuning. On Qwen3-4B knowledge acquisition, FINCH cuts TruthfulQA degradation by 5x and reverses HaluEval degradation, while better preserving confidence calibration. Overall, our results show that learning-rate schedules are an effective tool to shape model behavior during fine-tuning, beyond just target-task optimization.

</details>


### [282] [Training-Free Bayesian Filtering with Generative Emulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20028)
*Thomas Savary,François Rozet,Gilles Louppe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于扩散模型的粒子滤波方法，成功将粒子滤波扩展到高维场景，解决了传统方法在高维非线性动力系统中的可扩展性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贝叶斯滤波旨在从观测中估计动态系统的合理状态。虽然粒子滤波对非线性和观测在理论上是精确的，但在高维场景下存在可扩展性差的问题。

Method: 利用基于扩散的动态系统模拟器，实现了一种无需额外训练的最优粒子滤波变体，该方法克服了传统数值求解器在实现上的挑战。

Result: 在包括大气动力学在内的非线性混沌系统实验中，所提出的方法成功地将粒子滤波扩展到高维场景，证明了其有效性和可扩展性。

Conclusion: 基于扩散模型的模拟器为实现最优粒子滤波提供了新途径，解决了高维场景下的可扩展性问题，具有重要的理论和应用价值。

Abstract: Bayesian filtering is a well-known problem that aims to estimate plausible states of a dynamical system from observations. Among existing approaches to solve this problem, particle filters are theoretically exact for non-linear dynamics and observations, but suffer from poor scalability in high dimensions. In this work, we show that diffusion-based emulators of dynamical systems can be used to implement, without additional training, an optimal variant of particle filters that has remained largely unexplored due to implementation challenges with classical numerical solvers. Experiments on nonlinear chaotic systems, including atmospheric dynamics, demonstrate that the proposed approach successfully scales particle filtering to high-dimensional settings.

</details>


### [283] [Take It or Leave It: Intent-Controlled Partial Optimal Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20030)
*Salil Parth Tripathi,Bertrand Chapron,Fabrice Collard,Nicolas Courty,Ronan Fablet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出意图控制部分最优传输（IC-POT），用逐点拒绝成本替代全局拒绝范式，在正无标记学习和开放部分域适应等任务中提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统部分最优传输采用全局拒绝机制（预算、标量返还款或统一拒绝规则），但许多实际应用需要结构化的逐点拒绝机制，其中是否保留未匹配质量取决于侧边可靠性、支持几何形状或外部信息。

Method: 引入IC-POT，为两个测度定义逐点拒绝成本，将优化问题转化为对偶形式（局部接受阈值），并通过在增广支持上重构为平衡Kantorovich最优传输问题求解。

Result: 在正无标记学习和开放部分域适应任务中，融入编码统计结构的逐点拒绝规则改进了基准流程；在地球物理多模态卫星海洋测量案例中，物理和传感器先验自然指导拒绝机制并定义可比较的信号信息。

Conclusion: IC-POT成功将部分最优传输从全局拒绝推广到逐点拒绝，理论上有对偶解释和有效算法，实践中在多个应用场景证明其有效性。

Abstract: While optimal transport (OT) enforces a rigid constraint by requiring two measures to be matched exactly, partial optimal transport relaxes this requirement by allowing mass to remain unmatched through a global budget, scalar rebate, or uniform rejection rule. However, many applications call for more structured, pointwise rejection mechanisms, where the decision to leave mass unmatched depends on side-specific reliability, support geometry, or external information about which components should participate in the comparison. We introduce \emph{intent-controlled partial optimal transport} (IC-POT), a targeted generalization of partial transport that replaces the global rejection paradigm with pointwise rejection costs over both measures. We show that the resulting optimization problem admits a dual interpretation in terms of local acceptance thresholds and can be solved by recasting it as a balanced Kantorovich OT problem on an augmented support. Beyond theoretical analysis, we demonstrate the practical relevance of IC-POT in settings where rejection is driven by side information. In positive-unlabeled learning and open-partial domain adaptation, incorporating pointwise rejection rules that encode statistical structure improves fixed baseline pipelines. Finally, we motivate the use of IC-POT with a geophysical practical case: multi-modal satellite ocean measurements, for which physical and sensors priors naturally inform the rejection mechanism and define the retrieved comparable signal information.

</details>


### [284] [CAMERA: Adapting to Semantic Camouflage in Unsupervised Text-Attributed Graph Fraud Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20032)
*Junjun Pan,Yixin Liu,Yu Zheng,Lianhua Chi,Alan Wee-Chung Liew,Shirui Pan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对欺诈者通过语义伪装逃避检测的问题，提出CAMERA框架，通过混合专家架构自适应融合多种欺诈线索，并结合无监督单类学习，在语义伪装场景下显著提升欺诈检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本属性图欺诈检测（TAGFD）面临新的挑战：欺诈者采用语义伪装策略，故意模仿正常用户的文本行为，使得传统依赖结构和属性特征的检测方法失效，导致无监督检测难以识别伪装的欺诈者。

Method: 提出案例自适应多线索专家框架（CAMERA），采用自中心节点解耦的混合专家架构，每个专家专门建模一种欺诈指示线索；引入上下文感知门控模型，联合考虑节点自身表示及其局部邻域上下文，自适应整合各专家学习的线索；利用欺诈者稀少特性，设计专家级目标函数支持无监督单类学习，鼓励建模良性模式。

Result: 在4个具有挑战性的数据集上进行实验，CAMERA在所有数据集上均一致地超越竞争对手，证明了其在对抗语义伪装欺诈者方面的有效性。

Conclusion: CAMERA框架有效解决了语义伪装下的无监督欺诈检测难题，通过自适应融合多种线索和建模良性模式，实现了对伪装欺诈者的可靠检测。

Abstract: Text-attributed graph fraud detection (TAGFD) plays a critical role in preventing fraudulent activities on online social and e-commerce platforms. However, to evade detection, fraudsters continuously evolve their camouflaging strategies by deliberately mimicking textual responses of benign users, thereby concealing their malicious purposes. This phenomenon, referred to as semantic camouflage, fundamentally undermines commonly relied assumptions on how structural and attribute cues can be exploited to identify fraudsters, and makes it difficult to spot fraudsters with unsupervised TAGFD. To bridge the gaps, we propose a Case-Adaptive Multi-cue Expert fRAmework (CAMERA) for unsupervised TAGFD. CAMERA employs an ego-decoupled mixture-of-experts architecture, where each expert specializes in modeling a distinct type of fraud-indicative cue. A context-informed gating model is introduced to jointly consider the ego node representation and its local neighborhood context for adaptive integration of cues learned by different experts. Furthermore, CAMERA leverages the inherent rarity of fraudsters to support unsupervised one-class learning with expert-level objectives that encourage modeling dominant benign patterns, thereby enabling reliable unsupervised detection of camouflaged fraudsters. Experiments on 4 challenging datasets show that CAMERA consistently outperforms competitors, showing its effectiveness against semantically camouflaged fraudsters. Code available at https://github.com/CampanulaBells/CAMERA

</details>


### [285] [D$^3$-Subsidy: Online and Sequential Driver Subsidy Decision-Making for Large-Scale Ride-Hailing Market](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20036)
*Taijie Chen,Rui Su,Siyuan Feng,Laoming Zhang,Hongyang Zhang,Haijiao Wang,Zhaofeng Ma,Jintao Ke*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了D$^3$-Subsidy框架，一个基于扩散模型的城市级司机端补贴控制系统，用于网约车平台的供需平衡，在满足补贴率上限约束的同时提升了订单完成量和GMV。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网约车平台（如滴滴出行）需要在高度动态的环境中平衡司机供给和乘客需求。司机端补贴是主要的调节杠杆，但在生产环境中优化补贴需要同时满足三个约束条件：（i）对随机冲击的快速响应，（ii）严格的补贴率上限，以及（iii）城市规模的低延迟执行。传统的逐订单优化方法计算成本过高，无法满足这些要求，因此需要一个前向的、约束感知的城市级在线控制器。

Method: 提出了D$^3$-Subsidy（Dynamic Driver-side Diffusion-based Subsidy）分层扩散框架：1）使用前缀条件扩散模型从历史观测中采样合理的未来轨迹，解决了训练与推理之间的差距；2）通过上下文条件逆模块将生成的计划解码为低维城市级控制信号；3）利用拉格朗日对偶推导的映射将补贴率上限直接嵌入订单-司机激励中，实现可扩展执行；4）采用多城市预训练与参数高效微调策略实现跨城市迁移。

Result: 离线评估表明D$^3$-Subsidy在提升订单完成量（Rides）和GMV的同时增强了对补贴上限的合规性；真实A/B测试证实了显著的指标提升，且预算相关的违规指标保持在运营阈值之内。

Conclusion: D$^3$-Subsidy成功解决了网约车平台城市级补贴控制的三大约束挑战，实现了高效、合规且可扩展的在线决策，为工业部署提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Ride-hailing platforms like DiDi Chuxing operate in highly dynamic environments where balancing driver supply and passenger demand is critical. Although driver-side subsidies serve as a primary lever to align these forces and improve key KPIs like completed rides (\texttt{Rides}) and gross merchandise value (\texttt{GMV}), optimizing them in production requires simultaneously meeting three constraints: (i) responsiveness to stochastic shocks, (ii) strict subsidy-rate caps, and (iii) low-latency execution at city scale. These requirements rule out expensive per-order optimization, calling for a forward-looking, constraint-aware city-level controller for online sequential decision making. To meet these requirements, we introduce D$^3$-Subsidy (Dynamic Driver-side Diffusion-based Subsidy), a hierarchical diffusion-based framework for deployable city-wide subsidy control. To bridge the train-inference gap, D$^3$-Subsidy employs a prefix-conditioned diffusion model that samples plausible future trajectories from immutable historical observations, ensuring the training protocol aligns with the fixed-history nature of online deployment. These generated plans are then decoded by a context-conditioned inverse module into low-dimensional city-level control signals. For scalable execution, we bridge the gap between city-level planning and fine-grained dispatch via a Lagrangian-dual-derived mapping, which embeds subsidy-rate caps directly into order-driver incentives without iterative optimization. Additionally, a multi-city pretraining strategy with parameter-efficient fine-tuning enables robust transfer across heterogeneous cities. Extensive offline evaluations demonstrate that D$^3$-Subsidy improves \texttt{Rides} and \texttt{GMV} while enhancing cap compliance, and a real-world A/B test confirms significant uplift while keeping budget-related violation metrics within operational thresholds.

</details>


### [286] [Active Context Selection Improves Simple Regret in Contextual Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20040)
*Mohammad Shahverdikondori,Jalal Etesami,Negar Kiyavash*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了有限上下文空间的多臂老虎机问题，发现主动采样相比被动采样可将后悔值提升Θ(k^{1/4})倍，并提出EETC算法在未知上下文分布时达到与已知分布相同的后悔值率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在上下文多臂老虎机问题中，传统被动采样在处理多个上下文（子群体）时效率较低。受实验设计问题启发（种群固定但可控制采样子群体），作者希望研究主动选择采样上下文是否能提升学习效率，并量化其理论改进上限。

Method: 1. 定义上下文加权简单后悔值评估指标，建立最坏情况与实例相关的理论保证框架；2. 针对已知上下文分布p，分析被动采样（随机揭示上下文）与主动采样（按q_j∝p_j^{2/3}分配）的最优策略；3. 扩展至预算约束的主动采样场景；4. 针对未知p，提出Explore-Explore-Then-Commit (EETC)算法，平衡估计上下文分布与切换到主动分配的时机。

Result: 1. 被动采样后悔值率为√(n/T)‖p‖_{1/2}，主动采样可达√(n/T)‖p‖_{2/3}；2. 主动采样改进幅度最高达Θ(k^{1/4})；3. 刻画了预算约束主动采样的紧率，并确定有限预算何时足以恢复完全主动采样率；4. EETC算法在大视界下匹配已知p的主动采样率（至常数级）。

Conclusion: 主动采样策略在上下文多臂老虎机问题中具有显著理论优势，本文首次给出了紧的后悔值界，证明合理的采样分配可大幅提升性能。所提EETC算法解决了分布未知的实际难题，理论与实验结果一致，为实际应用提供了有效方法。

Abstract: We study the contextual multi-armed bandit problem with a finite context space (a.k.a. subpopulations), where the learner recommends a best action for each context and is evaluated by context-weighted simple regret. Our guarantees are worst-case over the reward distributions, while remaining instance-dependent with respect to the context distribution vector $p$. Akin to experimental design problems where the population of interest is fixed but the sampled subpopulation can be controlled, we allow the learner to actively choose which context to sample from. For a known $p$, we characterize tight regret rates: passive sampling where contexts are randomly revealed achieves regret of order $\sqrt{n/T \, \lVert p \rVert_{1/2}}$, whereas active sampling with allocation $q_j \propto p_j^{2/3}$ achieves the tight rate $\sqrt{n/T} \, \lVert p \rVert_{2/3}$. The resulting improvement can be as large as $Θ(k^{1/4})$, where $k$ is the number of contexts. We further extend the analysis to budgeted active sampling, characterize the corresponding tight rate, and identify when a limited active budget suffices to recover the fully active rate. When $p$ is unknown, we propose the Explore-Explore-Then-Commit (EETC) algorithm, which optimally balances estimating the context distribution and the time to switch to active allocation, such that for large horizons, it matches the known-$p$ active rate up to constants. Experiments on synthetic and real-world data support our theoretical findings.

</details>


### [287] [Smooth Partial Lotteries for Stable Randomized Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20069)
*Alexander Goldberg,Giulia Fanti,Nihar B. Shah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了平滑性作为部分抽签选择机制的设计原则，引入了截断线性抽签机制，在理论和实验上都证明了其相比现有方法能实现更好的平滑性-效用权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的部分抽签选择机制存在固有不稳定性，即单个候选人分数的微小变化会导致其选择概率的大幅波动，这破坏了抽签机制的核心目标：减少决策边界附近细粒度分数差异的影响。

Method: 提出平滑性作为设计原则，将其形式化为从候选人评审分数到选择概率映射的Lipschitz条件；引入截断线性抽签机制，该机制中选择概率在上阈值（以上必定接受）和下阈值（以下必定拒绝）之间随估计质量线性变化。

Result: 证明了截断线性抽签的最坏情况遗憾与任何平滑选择规则的下界匹配（相差因子1-k/n）；在ICLR 2025、NeurIPS 2024和瑞士国家科学基金会的真实同行评审数据上的实验表明，现有抽签设计在实践中高度不稳定，而截断线性抽签实现了更好的平滑性-效用权衡。

Conclusion: 截断线性抽签机制在保持平滑性的同时实现了接近最优的效用，为科学资助、招生和招聘等竞争性选择过程提供了一个更稳定、更公平的选择机制设计方案。

Abstract: Competitive selection processes, from scientific funding to admissions and hiring, use evaluations to score candidates, and eventually choose a subset of them based on those scores. Recently, many organizations have adopted partial lotteries, which randomize selection based on evaluation scores. However, existing lottery designs are inherently unstable, as a small change to a single candidate's score can cause large shifts in their selection probabilities. This instability undermines a key goal of lotteries: reducing the influence of fine-grained score distinctions near the decision boundary. We propose smoothness as a design principle for partial lotteries, formalizing it as a Lipschitz condition on the mapping from review scores over candidates to selection probabilities. We introduce the Clipped Linear Lottery, a simple mechanism in which selection probabilities scale linearly with estimated quality between an upper threshold, above which we always accept, and a lower threshold, below which we always reject. We prove that the Clipped Linear Lottery's worst-case regret matches a lower bound for any smooth selection rule up to a factor of $(1 - k/n)$, where $k/n$ is the acceptance rate. We compare smooth selection to other stability notions like Individual Fairness and Differential Privacy, showing that the Clipped Linear Lottery achieves a better smoothness-regret tradeoff than alternatives. Experiments on real peer review data from ICLR 2025, NeurIPS 2024, and the Swiss National Science Foundation demonstrate that existing lottery designs are highly unstable in practice even under perturbations to a single score. Our experiments also confirm the tightness of our theoretical analysis and show that our proposed Clipped Linear Lottery achieves a better smoothness-utility tradeoff than alternatives in practice.

</details>


### [288] [Towards Distillation Guarantees under Algorithmic Alignment for Combinatorial Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20074)
*Thien Le,Melanie Weber*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在算法对齐框架下，证明了当源模型满足线性表示假设且目标GNN架构与任务的动态规划算法对齐时，组合优化任务的蒸馏问题可被高效求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蒸馏技术可将大模型知识迁移至小模型，但在结构化预测与组合优化任务中，缺乏关于蒸馏何时成功的理论保障。现有研究已关注决策树蒸馏，本文旨在填补针对图神经网络（GNN）作为目标模型、并结合动态规划算法对齐的理论空白。

Method: 以算法对齐思想为核心，设定目标模型为架构与特定动态规划（DP）算法对齐的图神经网络（GNN）。假设源模型足够丰富并满足线性表示假设（LRH），将DP转移函数表示为决策树，并基于此分析蒸馏问题的计算复杂度。

Result: 在源模型满足线性表示假设的条件下，证明了蒸馏问题可在DP转移函数（以决策树表示）的复杂度参数范围内被高效解决，给出了蒸馏成功的充分条件。

Conclusion: 研究为组合优化任务中GNN的蒸馏提供了严格的理论依据，阐明了算法对齐在知识蒸馏中的关键作用，为构建高效、可解释的结构化预测模型提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Distillation transfers knowledge from a large model trained on broad data to a smaller, more efficient model suitable for deployment. In structured prediction settings, prior knowledge about the task can guide the choice of a target architecture that is algorithmically aligned with the underlying problem. Building on recent learning-theoretic analyses of decision-tree (DT) distillation (Boix-Adsera, 2024), we study when distillation succeeds for combinatorial optimization tasks. We focus on the case where the target model is a graph neural network whose architecture is aligned with a dynamic programming (DP) algorithm for the task. Assuming that the source model is sufficiently rich, formalized through the linear representation hypothesis (LRH) (Elhage et al., 2022; Park et al., 2024), we show that the distillation problem can be solved efficiently in the complexity parameters of the DP transition function, represented as a DT. Our results provide a rigorous sufficient condition for successful distillation in the flavour of algorithmic alignment.

</details>


### [289] [Optimal Representation Size: High-Dimensional Analysis of Pretraining and Linear Probing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20105)
*Valentina Njaradi,Clémentine Dominé,Rachel Swanson,Marco Mondelli,Andrew Saxe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文建立了预训练+微调范式的解析模型，发现表示维度的选择至关重要：预训练数据充足但下游数据稀缺时，压缩表示最优；预训练数据有限时，高维表示泛化更好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从有限数据学习并泛化是人工智能和生物系统的核心挑战。当前主流的两阶段训练范式（预训练+微调）缺乏解析理解，特别是预训练表示如何影响下游泛化、表示维度如何优化等关键问题尚无理论指导。

Method: 建立了一个可解析的数学模型：将结构提取形式化为对无标签数据的主成分分析（PCA），将下游学习形式化为对独立有标签数据集的线性回归。在高维情形下推导了训练误差和泛化误差的精确表达式，并进一步在自编码器和预训练大语言模型中验证。

Result: （1）推导了误差关于表示维度、无标签和有标签样本量、任务对齐度的精确依赖关系；（2）刻画了最优表示维度：预训练数据充足但下游数据稀缺时，最大化压缩的表示最优；预训练数据有限时，高维表示泛化更好；（3）建立了预训练与监督信息之间的精确权衡，量化了一个有标签样本可由多少无标签数据替代。

Conclusion: 预训练表示对下游泛化有决定性影响，优化表示维度至关重要。研究给出了预训练阶段压缩表示能改善泛化的具体条件，为实际训练中预训练与微调的权衡提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Learning to generalise from limited data is a fundamental challenge for both artificial and biological systems. A common strategy is to extract reusable structure from abundant unlabelled data, enabling efficient adaptation to new tasks from limited labelled data. This two-stage paradigm is now standard in modern training pipelines, where pretraining is followed by fine-tuning or linear probing. We provide an analytical model of this process: structure extraction is formalized as principal component analysis on unlabelled data, and downstream learning as linear regression on a separate labelled dataset. In the high-dimensional regime, we derive exact expressions for training and generalisation error showcasing their dependence on representation dimensionality, unlabelled and labelled sample sizes, and task alignment. Our results show that pretrained representations strongly influence downstream generalisation, and we characterize the optimal representation size as a function of task parameters: with abundant pretraining data but scarce downstream data, maximally compressed representations are optimal, whereas with limited pretraining data, higher-dimensional representations generalise better. Furthermore, we establish an exact trade-off between pretraining and supervision, quantifying how much unlabelled data is required to replace a single labelled sample. Beyond our idealised model, we observe similar phenomenology in autoencoders and pretrained LLMs. Altogether, we highlight that optimising representation size is critical, giving conditions for when compression during pretraining improves generalisation.

</details>


### [290] [TrajTok: Adaptive Spatial Tokenization for Trajectory Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20134)
*Zhen Xiong,Shang-Ling Hsu,Cyrus Shahabi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出TrajTok，一种基于多分辨率六边形空间划分和掩码标记预训练的轨迹编码器，能够生成可迁移的轨迹嵌入，在多项下游任务中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从原始GPS轨迹学习可泛化的表示具有挑战性，因为数据具有连续、嘈杂和采样不规则的特点。现有的空间标记化方法面临两难：细网格导致稀疏单元和弱嵌入，粗网格则将异构的运动模式合并到同一标记中。

Method: TrajTok首先从GPS点的空间分布中学习多分辨率六边形单元划分，将噪声GPS序列转换为离散单元标记。为捕获几何和运动学特征，采用分解式Transformer编码器，包含早期模态内自注意力块、交叉注意力融合层和时空旋转位置嵌入（ST-RoPE）。预训练采用掩码标记建模，从部分轨迹观测中恢复几何结构和运动模式。

Result: 在Porto数据集上，冻结的TrajTok编码器配合轻量级任务适配器，在轨迹相似性搜索、分类、到达时间估计和全程行程时间回归等任务中均取得优异性能，优于多种特定任务方法。同一冻结编码器能同时支持几何主导和运动学主导的任务。

Conclusion: 结果表明，学习的多分辨率空间标记化结合掩码标记预训练是构建通用轨迹基础模型的有前途的方向，TrajTok学习到了可迁移的轨迹结构而非任务特定的捷径。

Abstract: Learning generalizable trajectory representations from raw GPS traces remains difficult because the data is continuous, noisy, and irregularly sampled. Spatial tokenization is also challenging: fine grids yield sparse cells with weak embeddings, while coarse grids merge heterogeneous movement patterns into the same token. We present TrajTok, a trajectory encoder with a simple pretraining recipe for transferable trajectory embeddings. TrajTok first learns a multi-resolution hexagonal cell partition from the spatial distribution of GPS points, converting noisy GPS sequences into discrete cell tokens. To capture both geometry and kinematics, it uses a factorized transformer encoder with early per-modality self-attention blocks, cross-attention fusion layers, and spatiotemporal rotary position embeddings, ST-RoPE, to encode where and when each token occurs. TrajTok is pretrained with masked-token modeling that recovers both geometric structure and kinematic patterns from partial trajectory observations. On the Porto dataset, a frozen TrajTok encoder with lightweight task adapters achieves strong performance across trajectory similarity search, classification, estimated time of arrival, and full travel-time regression, outperforming multiple task-specific methods. The same frozen encoder supports both geometry-dominated and kinematics-dominated tasks, suggesting that TrajTok learns transferable trajectory structure rather than task-specific shortcuts. These results indicate that learned multi-resolution spatial tokenization combined with masked-token pretraining is a promising direction for general-purpose trajectory foundation models.

</details>


### [291] [When Does Model Collapse Occur in Structured Interactive Learning?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20151)
*Yuchen Wu,Kangjie Zhou,Weijie Su*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将生成式AI的交互学习形式化为有向图结构，推导出了模型崩溃发生的充要条件：当且仅当交互图的拓扑结构满足特定条件时发生崩溃，为多模型交互环境提供了理论保障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI的广泛应用催生了交互式学习环境，模型参数不仅由真实数据更新，还受到其他模型合成输出的影响。这带来了两大挑战：训练数据不再独立于目标总体（违背经典统计学习假设），以及模型训练过程因交互而相互关联。现有关于模型崩溃的研究仅局限于单一模型自训练场景，无法捕捉多模型交互设置下的模型性能表现，因此亟需建立多模型交互环境下的统计推断理论。

Method: 作者提出使用有向图来形式化模型间的交互模式，建立了通用的交互学习环境理论框架。通过对交互图拓扑结构的分析，推导出了模型崩溃发生的显式充要条件。针对线性回归模型建立了有限样本理论结果，对一般M估计量建立了渐近理论保证，并通过大量数值实验验证了理论发现。

Result: 研究发现模型崩溃的发生关键性地取决于交互图的拓扑结构。作者给出了明确的充要条件来判定何时会发生模型崩溃：当且仅当交互图的拓扑特性满足特定条件时，模型性能才会发生渐进式退化。针对线性回归获得了有限样本界，对一般M估计量建立了渐近收敛保证。数值实验充分支持了理论结论。

Conclusion: 本研究填补了多模型交互环境下模型崩溃理论分析的空白，证明了交互图拓扑结构是决定模型性能的关键因素。所提出的充要条件为理解和预测交互学习环境中的模型行为提供了理论基础，为设计稳定的交互式学习系统指明了方向。

Abstract: The proliferation of generative artificial intelligence has given rise to an interactive learning environment, where model parameters are continuously updated using not only data generated by natural processes, but also synthetic outputs produced by other models. This paradigm introduces two major challenges: (1) training data are no longer drawn exclusively from the target population, undermining a core assumption of classical statistical learning, and (2) model training processes become inherently correlated, as models interact with one another through repeated exposure to each other's synthetic outputs in a potentially complex manner. Establishing reliable statistical inference in such structured interactive learning environments therefore remains an important open problem. In particular, there is growing concern about model collapse, a phenomenon in which the performance of generative models progressively degrades as they are trained on synthetic data produced by earlier model generations.
  Prior work on model collapse primarily focuses on a single model trained on its own output, failing to capture model performance in multi-model interactive settings. In this work, we fill this gap by investigating the performance of generative models in an interactive learning environment with general interaction patterns. In particular, we formalize model interactions using directed graphs and show that the occurrence of model collapse depends critically on the topology of the interaction graph. We further derive an explicit necessary and sufficient condition characterizing when model collapse occurs, and establish finite-sample results for linear regression and asymptotic guarantees for general M-estimators. We support our theoretical findings through extensive numerical experiments.

</details>


### [292] [SAGE: Scalable Automatic Gating Ensemble for Confident Negative Harvesting in Fraud Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20157)
*Sudheer Tubati,Amit Goyal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了SAGE，一种基于反事实感知的负样本挖掘方法，通过SimHash分层采样和模块化门控集成来识别高置信度的负样本，有效解决了音乐流媒体欺诈检测中合法边缘情况（如超级粉丝）与欺诈行为难以区分的难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 音乐流媒体欺诈通过人为虚增播放量来操纵排名和版税，对平台和创作者构成威胁。现有的检测方法面临一个关键挑战：许多合法的边缘案例（如超级粉丝和睡眠音乐场景）的行为模式与协同欺诈高度相似，导致传统方法难以区分，存在严重的假阳性风险。

Method: 提出了SAGE框架，结合了基于SimHash的分层采样与模块化门控集成。该方法通过地板约束采样确保对稀有行为群体的全面覆盖，解决了正样本-无标签（PU）学习中的表示偏差问题。集成架构采用可插拔的统计门控（当前实例化为马氏距离和k-NN密度）及可配置的投票阈值，以实现精确率与召回率之间的自适应权衡。

Result: 评估结果显示，该方法在保留数据集上实现了较高的精确率和召回率。该方法具有良好的泛化能力，在不修改核心方法论的前提下，在客户级和艺术家级欺诈检测中均取得了优异表现。

Conclusion: SAGE成功解决了PU学习中的表示偏差问题，能够从无标签数据中有效识别负样本，为区分合法边缘行为与欺诈行为提供了可靠的解决方案，具有广泛的跨领域应用潜力。

Abstract: Music streaming fraud, where bad actors artificially inflate stream counts to manipulate chart rankings and royalty payments, poses a significant threat to streaming services and legitimate content creators. Traditional fraud detection approaches struggle with a critical challenge: many legitimate edge cases, including super-fans and sleep-music sessions, exhibit activity patterns that closely mimic those of coordinated fraud. We present SAGE, a novel counterfactual-aware negative harvesting approach that combines SimHash-based stratified sampling with a modular gating ensemble for confident negative identification from unlabeled data. Our ensemble architecture employs pluggable statistical gates (currently instantiated with Mahalanobis distance and k-NN density) with configurable voting thresholds enabling adaptive precision-recall trade-offs. This addresses the representation bias problem in Positive-Unlabeled learning by ensuring comprehensive coverage of rare behavioral cohorts through floor-constrained sampling. Evaluation demonstrates strong precision and recall on held-out data. The approach generalizes across fraud detection domains, achieving strong performance on both customer-level and artist-level fraud without modification to the core methodology.

</details>


<div id='cs.AR'></div>

# cs.AR [[Back]](#toc)

### [293] [HSCO-Bench: An Agent-Driven End-to-End Hardware-Software Co-design Benchmark for Systems-on-Chip](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19399)
*Pei-Huan Tsai,Kuan-Lin Chiu,William Baisi,Pin-Yu Chen,Luca P. Carloni*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了HSCO-Bench，这是首个端到端硬件-软件协同设计基准，用于评估大语言模型在异构SoC生成中的系统能力。实验表明当前前沿模型在完整协同设计流程中仍面临重大挑战，仅有两个模型能生成有效SoC原型，且设计远未达到最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM评估基准存在明显局限性：软件基准假设固定硬件目标，硬件基准仅关注组件级优化而忽略完整硬件-软件栈。缺乏能够评估LLM智能体执行端到端、系统级硬件-软件协同设计能力的基准。这种协同设计过程需要分析应用程序识别需要加速的内核、在资源约束下设计异构加速器并集成到SoC中，以及将内核映射到生成的加速器上。

Method: 提出HSCO-Bench基准，构建于开源SoC平台，采用精心策划的存储库结构。该基准评估LLM联合优化软件和硬件栈的能力，要求模型完成完整的硬件-软件协同设计流程，最终在AMD Virtex-7 FPGA VC708评估套件上部署生成的SoC原型。基准涵盖了从应用分析、加速器设计集成到内核映射的完整流程。

Result: 评估了五个前沿模型，仅两个模型能成功生成有效SoC原型。即使成功案例中，生成的设计也远非最优。观察到16.22X的峰值加速效果，但最大额外资源利用率仅达23.67%，表明当前模型虽然展现出硬件加速能力，但严重未充分利用可用硬件容量。端到端集成对当前模型仍是重大挑战。

Conclusion: HSCO-Bench是首个针对完整协同设计流程的基准，使LLM能够联合推理和修改异构SoC的软件和硬件栈。研究表明最先进模型展现出新兴的硬件加速能力，但在系统级协同设计方面仍有很大优化空间，为未来研究指明了方向。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are adopted for software and hardware design, yet these domains are still evaluated separately. Software benchmarks typically assume fixed hardware targets, while hardware benchmarks focus on component-level optimization without considering the full hardware-software stack. Consequently, no existing benchmark evaluates whether an LLM agent can perform end-to-end, system-level hardware-software co-design. Such a process requires: 1) analyzing applications to identify kernels requiring acceleration, 2) designing and integrating heterogeneous accelerators into a System-on-Chip (SoC) under resource constraints, and 3) mapping kernels onto the generated accelerators. We present HSCO-Bench, an end-to-end hardware-software co-design benchmark for accelerator-rich heterogeneous SoC generation. Built upon an open-source SoC platform with a curated repository structure, HSCO-Bench evaluates the ability of LLMs to jointly optimize software and hardware stacks, producing SoC prototypes deployed on the AMD Virtex-7 FPGA VC707 Evaluation Kit. Experimental results show that end-to-end integration remains challenging for current models. Among the five frontier models evaluated, only two of them could successfully generate valid SoC prototypes. Yet, even in these successful instances, the generated designs are far from optimal. While we observe a promising peak speedup of 16.22X, the maximum additional resource utilization reaches only 23.67%. This highlights that while state-of-the-art models demonstrate an emerging capability for hardware acceleration, they still heavily underutilize the available hardware capacity, leaving room for future optimization. To the best of our knowledge, HSCO-Bench is the first benchmark targeting this complete co-design flow, enabling LLMs to jointly reason about and modify both the software and hardware stacks of heterogeneous SoCs.

</details>


### [294] [A complete discussion on fully reconfigurable, digital, scalable, graph and sparsity-aware near-memory accelerator for graph neural networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19405)
*Siddhartha Raman Sundara Raman,Lizy John,Jaydeep P. Kulkarni*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于图神经网络加速的无DAC/ADC的可扩展存内计算架构（NEM-GNN），通过早停机制、可重构预计算和图稀疏感知的近存聚合方法，显著提升了性能与能效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图神经网络（GNN）在引用网络分析和药物发现等领域应用广泛，但其两阶段执行流水线（组合与聚合）具有异构的内存访问模式：组合阶段内存访问相对规则，而聚合阶段针对稀疏图数据的访问高度不规则。这种特性导致传统CPU和GPU执行时数据移动开销巨大，能效低下。现有的专用加速器虽然采用PIM等技术，但仍存在可扩展性受限、面积开销大、并行度受限以及模拟计算带来的能效问题。

Method: 提出NEM-GNN架构，一种无DAC/ADC（纯数字）的可扩展存内计算方案。主要技术包括：1）早停计算机制；2）利用可重构片上系统组件进行预计算；3）基于“计算即就绪”（CAR）和广播执行模型的图稀疏感知近存聚合。

Result: 实验结果表明，与现有最先进方法相比，NEM-GNN实现了约80-230倍的性能提升、80-300倍的吞吐量提升、850-1134倍的能效提升以及7-8倍的计算密度提升。

Conclusion: NEM-GNN通过纯数字存内计算架构和针对GNN异构内存特性的优化设计，有效解决了现有加速器的可扩展性和能效瓶颈，为GNN加速提供了一种高效、可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Graph neural networks (GNNs) have gained significant interest for applications such as citation network analysis and drug discovery due to their ability to apply machine learning techniques on graph-structured data. GNNs typically employ a two-stage execution pipeline consisting of combination and aggregation kernels. The combination stage performs data-intensive convolution operations with relatively regular memory access patterns, whereas the aggregation stage operates on sparse graph data with highly irregular accesses. These heterogeneous memory behaviors make conventional CPU- and GPU-based execution energy inefficient due to substantial data movement overheads.
  Existing accelerators attempt to mitigate these challenges using specialized architectures and processing-in-memory (PIM) techniques. However, prior approaches often suffer from scalability limitations, area overheads, restricted parallelism, and energy inefficiencies associated with analog compute and dedicated accelerator structures.
  This paper presents NEM-GNN, a scalable DAC/ADC-less processing-in-memory architecture for graph neural network acceleration. The proposed design introduces early compute termination mechanisms, pre-computation using reconfigurable system-on-chip components, and graph- and sparsity-aware near-memory aggregation using a compute-as-soon-as-ready (CAR) and broadcast-based execution model. Experimental results demonstrate that NEM-GNN achieves approximately 80--230x higher performance, 80--300x higher throughput, 850--1134x better energy efficiency, and 7--8x higher compute density compared to prior state-of-the-art approaches.

</details>


### [295] [A Hardware-Based Multi-Stage Dynamic Power Management Architecture for Autonomous Low-Light Operation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19879)
*Charalampos S. Kouzinopoulos,Marcel L. Meli,Martin Schellenberg,Philip J. Poole,Mathieu Bellanger,Matthias Kauer,Julien De Vos,Dimosthenis Ioannidis,Dimitrios Tzovaras*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种硬件编排的动态电源管理架构，通过完全关闭微控制器和非必要外设，将静态电流降至452nA，显著优于传统软件睡眠模式，解决了物联网传感器节点在低光环境下的能耗瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对由光伏供电的自主智能传感器网络和物联网嵌入式系统，在低光照等能量匮乏环境中，传统的基于软件的动态电源管理技术依赖处理器管理的低功耗状态，会产生持续的静态电流消耗，该电流成为主要的能量损耗来源，严重限制了系统的能源自给能力。

Method: 提出了一种鲁棒的、由硬件编排的动态电源管理架构。该架构利用超低功耗PMIC、RTC以及一个专门开发的 novel latch circuit（新型锁存电路）来协调唤醒过程，完全关断微控制器和所有非必要外设的电源，从而消除处理器管理的待机损耗。

Result: 提出的架构实现了极低的静态电流消耗，仅为452nA。评估结果表明，该动态电源管理架构在能效方面显著优于传统的基于软件的睡眠模式。

Conclusion: 通过引入硬件编排的电源门控机制，该研究成功克服了传统软件管理技术在静态功耗上的局限性，为电池供电的传感器节点提供了一种更高效的能源管理方案，显著提升了能量收集系统在恶劣条件下的自主性。

Abstract: The advance of autonomous Smart Sensor Networks and embedded systems for the Internet of Things, powered by photovoltaic energy harvesting, is severely limited by energy efficiency, especially in low-light environments. While Dynamic Power Management is essential for energy conservation, conventional software-based techniques that rely on processor-managed low-power states incur a persistent quiescent current drain. This current becomes the dominant energy sink in energy-scarce conditions, limiting autonomy. The work of this paper addresses this limitation by introducing a robust, hardware-orchestrated dynamic power management architecture that improves existing configurations for battery-based sensor nodes. The proposed architecture achieves a minimal quiescent drain of 452nA, by completely power-gating the microcontroller and all non-essential peripherals, with wake-up orchestrated by an ultra-low-power PMIC, RTC and a novel latch circuit developed specifically for this work. Our evaluation demonstrates that the dynamic power management architecture is significantly more efficient than traditional software-based sleep modes.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [296] [CASPIAN: Online Detection and Attribution of Cascade Attacks in LLM Multi-Agent Systems via Cross-Channel Causal Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19240)
*Kavana Venkatesh,Jafar Isbarov,Saad Amin,Murat Kantarcioglu,Jiaming Cui*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出CASPIAN框架，通过跨通道因果分析解决LLM多智能体系统中的级联攻击检测问题，实现了高精度、低延迟的在线监测与溯源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM多智能体系统（MAS）中存在级联攻击风险，即对抗性影响在智能体间传播并导致系统级故障。此类攻击信号具有分布式、跨通道耦合、时序协调等特性，在局部看似良性但会快速或渐进式扩散。现有的防御方法多为局部且以文本为中心，无法捕捉跨通道的动态传播过程，因此亟需一种能够统一建模和监测此类级联行为的新方法。

Method: 提出CASPIAN框架，首先构建统一、动态的跨通道因果影响矩阵，利用后期交互条件传递熵（LI-CTE）公式高效估计因果影响。该方法从系统级涌现结构出发检测级联攻击的起始，而非依赖孤立的异常检测。进一步实现在线因果归因，识别驱动级联的起源、桥接和放大智能体，并重构主要传播路径。

Result: 在多种多智能体框架和基准测试中，CASPIAN在检测准确性和早期级联识别方面均持续优于语义护栏、LLM判断器和基于图的异常检测器。同时，该框架运行时的相对开销延迟低于1%，证明了其高效性。

Conclusion: 统一的跨通道因果建模对于可靠检测和理解LLM多智能体系统中的级联故障至关重要。CASPIAN为该领域提供了首个有效解决方案，填补了现有方法在跨通道、时序协调动态分析方面的空白。

Abstract: Cascade attacks in LLM multi-agent systems (MAS) arise when adversarial influence propagates across agents and leads to escalated system-level failures through complex agent interactions. Detecting such cascades is challenging, as their signals are distributed, tightly coupled across interaction channels, and often appear plausibly benign locally but may unfold quickly either within a single turn or gradually across multiple turns. Existing defenses, being largely local and text-centric, fail to capture such cross-channel, temporally coordinated dynamics of cascade propagation. Therefore, we propose CASPIAN, the first framework that provides a unified, cross-channel causal analysis of cascade behavior in LLM-MAS through online monitoring of dynamic influence propagation across agents. CASPIAN models multi-agent interactions using a unified, dynamic causal influence matrix across channels, estimated efficiently via a late-interaction conditional transfer entropy (LI-CTE) formulation, thereby enabling the detection of cascade onset from emergent system-level structure rather than isolated anomalies. It further performs online causal attribution, identifying the origin, bridge, and amplifier agents driving the cascade and reconstructing its principal propagation pathways, capabilities not supported by existing methods. Across diverse multi-agent frameworks and benchmarks, CASPIAN consistently outperforms semantic guardrails, LLM-based judges, and graph-based anomaly detectors in both detection accuracy and early cascade identification while operating with sub-1% relative overhead latency. These results demonstrate that unified cross-channel causal modeling is essential for reliably detecting and understanding cascade failures in LLM multi-agent systems.

</details>


### [297] [STAR-PólyaMath: Multi-Agent Reasoning under Persistent Meta-Strategic Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19338)
*Jiaao Wu,Xian Zhang,Hanzhang Liu,Sophia Zhang,Fan Yang,Yinpeng Dong*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: STAR-PólyaMath是一个多智能体框架，通过元级监督和结构化的推理器-验证器交互解决长程推理中的幻觉累积、记忆碎片化等问题，在八项顶级数学竞赛基准上达到SOTA，多项获得满分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿AI模型在长程推理任务中存在三个根本性可靠性问题：幻觉累积、记忆碎片化以及推理-工具权衡失衡。现有系统在面对需要扩展的长程推理问题时，缺乏有效的机制来处理这些挑战，容易陷入非生产性循环或过度依赖工具。

Method: 提出STAR-PólyaMath多智能体框架：1) 构建带有嵌套挑战-步骤-重规划循环的状态机架构；2) 使用无推理的Python协调器分离控制与推理，通过追溯和重规划限制错误传播；3) 引入持久性Meta-Strategist维护跨尝试记忆并执行元级控制，通过发布高层战略指导或强制性指令帮助系统摆脱非生产性循环。

Result: 在八项顶级竞赛基准上达到SOTA：AIME 2025-2026、MathArena Apex Shortlist、MathArena Apex 2025、Putnam 2025、IMO 2025、HMMT February 2026和USAMO 2026。在AIME、Putnam和HMMT上获得满分；在Apex 2025上得分93.75%，显著超越最强基线GPT-5.5的80.21%。消融实验证明性能提升来自框架协调而非模型级多样性。

Conclusion: STAR-PólyaMath通过系统性框架设计成功解决了长程数学推理中的关键可靠性问题，证明了元级监督和结构化协调的有效性。框架的编排机制而非单一模型能力是性能提升的关键因素，为构建可靠的长程推理系统提供了新范式。

Abstract: Frontier AI models and multi-agent systems have led to significant improvements in mathematical reasoning. However, for problems requiring extended, long-horizon reasoning, existing systems continue to suffer from fundamental reliability issues: hallucination accumulation, memory fragmentation, and imbalanced reasoning-tool trade-offs. In this paper, we introduce STAR-PólyaMath, a multi-agent framework that systematically addresses these challenges through meta-level supervision and structured Reasoner-Verifier interaction. STAR-PólyaMath is structured as an orchestrated state machine with nested challenge-step-replan loops, governed by a reasoning-free Python orchestrator that separates control from inference and bounds error propagation through trace-back and re-planning. Our key innovation is a persistent Meta-Strategist that maintains cross-attempt memory and exercises meta-level control by issuing high-level strategic guidance or mandatory directives, so the system can escape unproductive loops rather than stagnate or over-rely on tools. STAR-PólyaMath achieves state-of-the-art results on all eight top-tier competition benchmarks: AIME 2025-2026, MathArena Apex Shortlist, MathArena Apex 2025, Putnam 2025, IMO 2025, HMMT February 2026, and USAMO 2026. It obtains perfect scores on AIMEs, Putnam, and HMMT, and shows its largest margin on Apex 2025, scoring 93.75% compared with 80.21% by the strongest baseline GPT-5.5. Ablation studies show that the gains arise from the framework's orchestration rather than from model-level diversity since removing key components or substituting in mixed backbones consistently weakens performance. Code is available at https://github.com/Julius-Woo/STAR-PolyaMath.

</details>


### [298] [PAVE: A Cognitive Architecture for Legitimate Violation in Generative Agent Societies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19351)
*Ahmad Yehia,Abduallah Mohamed,Kun Qian,Tianyi Wang,Jiseop Byeon,Omar Hassanin,Christian Claudel*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出了PAVE认知架构，使生成式智能体能够在需要违反规则的情况下（如火灾疏散或权威监督下的紧急情况）做出合理决策，在合法违规、权威服从、范围限制和恢复四个方面表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于大语言模型的生成式智能体在合作场景中能复现可信的人类行为，但在可能需要违反规则的情况下（如火灾疏散或权威监督的紧急情况）如何进行推理仍然缺乏研究。

Method: 提出PAVE四模块认知架构：（i）感知模块提取结构化上下文，包含显式的权威距离、同伴行为和严重性标记的情境线索；（ii）评估模块沿五个标量对上下文评分，包括检查必要性、比例性和缺乏替代方案的显式合法性判断；（iii）裁决模块在硬合法性门控下决定遵守或违反规则，使用从人设中引出的每智能体阈值；（iv）模拟模块执行裁决并将违规范围限定在触发因素证实的规则上。在基于Smallville衍生的Voville交通环境中实例化PAVE。

Result: PAVE智能体同时满足四个属性：合法违规（仅在触发因素证实时）、权威服从（执法人员指令覆盖即使高合法性）、范围限制（违规仅限于目标规则）和恢复能力（触发因素结束后恢复基线）。PAVE智能体比原始方法在所有四个属性上做出更结构化和可解释的决策，人类评估者认为其更合理。消融合法性门控会重现类似原始方法的失败。

Conclusion: PAVE架构成功解决了生成式智能体在规则违反场景中的推理问题，在三个场景、四个LLM骨干网络和消融实验中验证了其有效性，为构建更可信的智能体行为提供了新方法。

Abstract: Generative agents based on large language models reproduce believable human behavior in cooperative settings, but how they should reason in situations where rule-breaking may be required, such as fire evacuation or authority-supervised emergency, remains poorly characterized. We propose PAVE (Perception, Assessment, Verdict, Emulation), a novel four-module cognitive architecture that addresses this gap end to end: (i) Perception extracts a structured context with explicit authority distance, peer behaviors, and severity-tagged situational cues; (ii) Assessment scores the context along five scalars including an explicit legitimacy judgment that checks necessity, proportionality, and absence of alternatives; (iii) Verdict decides to comply or violate under a hard legitimacy gate, with a per-agent threshold elicited from the persona; (iv) Emulation enacts the verdict and scopes the violation to the rule the trigger justifies. We instantiate PAVE in Voville, a tile-based traffic environment forked from Smallville, and evaluate across three scenarios, four LLM backbones, and a focused ablation. PAVE agents satisfy four properties simultaneously: legitimate violation (only when a trigger justifies it), authority deference (officer instructions override even high legitimacy), bounded scope (violations confined to the targeted rule), and recovery (baseline restored once the trigger ends). PAVE agents make more structured and interpretable decisions than vanilla across all four properties, and human evaluators rate them as more plausible. Ablating the legitimacy gate reproduces vanilla-like failures. We release Voville, the PAVE prompts and code, and the evaluation pipeline.

</details>


### [299] [LLM Agents Make Collective Belief Dynamics Programmable: Challenges and Research Directions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19915)
*Xin He,Junxi Shen,Yuchen Mou,David M. Bossens,Caishun Chen,Ivor W. Tsang,Yew Soon Ong*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: LLM代理使集体信念动态变得可编程，能够被有意操控，且难以检测和防御。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典观点动力学模型假设参与者具有有限理性和协调能力，而基于LLM的代理能够大规模参与在线讨论、保持一致的劝说策略并进行系统性协调，这导致了集体信念动态的质变。作者旨在揭示这一新兴问题——可编程集体信念控制，并强调其紧迫性。

Method: 通过受控的多代理仿真实验，提供了概念验证证据。研究识别了使得检测和防御变得困难的四个结构属性：不可区分性、持久性、情境性和可配置性。

Result: 概念验证表明，协调的AI代理可以引发可测量的信念转变，并且这种转变在少量交互轮次内即可稳定。

Conclusion: 该研究并未提出完整解决方案，而是阐明了问题的重要性并提供了概念基础，呼吁建立涵盖对抗性信念动力学理论基础、系统级检测与干预方法以及可扩展实验仿真基础设施的研究议程。

Abstract: Classical models of opinion dynamics assume human participants with bounded rationality and limited coordination. The rise of LLM-based agents introduces a qualitative shift: agents can now participate in online discussions at scale, maintain consistent persuasion strategies, and coordinate systematically. This paper argues that LLM agents make collective belief dynamics programmable, enabling deliberate steering of population-level beliefs. We term this emerging problem programmable collective belief control. Through controlled multi-agent simulations, we provide proof-of-concept evidence that coordinated AI agents can induce measurable belief shifts that stabilize within a few interaction rounds. We identify four structural properties (indistinguishability, persistence, contextuality, and configurability) that make detection and defense fundamentally difficult. Based on these findings, we outline a research agenda spanning theoretical foundations for adversarial belief dynamics, operational methods for system-level detection and intervention, and simulation infrastructure for scalable experimentation. Our goal is not to present a complete solution, but to articulate why this problem demands urgent attention and to provide a conceptual foundation for future work.

</details>
